Method: 1. 从“模块分配”和“更新方向”两个维度分析OPD的参数动力学，揭示其早期更新轨迹对最终模型的拟合能力（即“预见性”）。2. 提出EffOPD方法：通过自适应选择外推步长，在当前更新方向上进行加速，无需额外参数，无需复杂的超参数调优。

Result: 1. 揭示了OPD在高训练效率下的两个表现：识别低边际效用模块以集中更新，以及在训练早期表现出极强的低秩集中度与最终 subspace 对齐。2. EffOPD方法实现了平均3倍的训练加速，且性能与标准OPD持平。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了在线策略蒸馏（OPD）在参数动力学层面的效率机制（“预见性”），并基于此提出了EffOPD方法，成功在保持性能的前提下实现了3倍的训练加速，为大模型的高效后训练设计提供了理论与实践依据。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has emerged as an efficient post-training paradigm for large language models. However, existing studies largely attribute this advantage to denser and more stable supervision, while the parameter-level mechanisms underlying OPD's efficiency remain poorly understood. In this work, we argue that OPD's efficiency stems from a form of ``foresight'': it establishes a stable update trajectory toward the final model early in training. This foresight manifests in two aspects. First, at the \textbf{Module-Allocation Level}, OPD identifies regions with low marginal utility and concentrates updates on modules that are more critical to reasoning. Second, at the \textbf{Update-Direction Level}, OPD exhibits stronger low-rank concentration, with its dominant subspaces aligning closely with the final update subspace early in training. Building on these findings, we propose \textbf{EffOPD}, a plug-and-play acceleration method that speeds up OPD by adaptively selecting an extrapolation step size and moving along the current update direction. EffOPD requires no additional trainable modules or complex hyperparameter tuning, and achieves an average training acceleration of $3\times$ while maintaining comparable final performance. Overall, our findings provide a parameter-dynamics perspective for understanding the efficiency of OPD and offer practical insights for designing more efficient post-training methods for large language models.

</details>


### [38] [Training-Inference Consistent Segmented Execution for Long-Context LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11744)
*Xianpeng Shang,Jiang Li,Zehua Duo,Qianyi Cai,Xiangdong Su*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对长上下文LLM训练与推理不一致的问题，本文提出了一种分段生成框架，通过强制训练与推理路径的一致性，有效缓解了内存与计算挑战，并在极长序列上实现了显著的效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的高效长上下文方法在推理时采用分段执行，但在训练时仍使用全上下文注意力计算，导致训练与推理在执行语义及状态转换上存在不匹配问题。

Method: 提出了一种训练-推理一致的分段生成（segment-level generation）框架。在训练时，限制梯度传播仅限于前一个分段携带的KV状态，同时在正向传递中允许访问过去的KV状态，且不涉及这些状态的梯度反向计算。

Result: 在长上下文基准测试上，该方法性能与全上下文注意力相当，且在极长上下文（如128K）下的预填充阶段（prefill）内存占用比FlashAttention低约6倍。

Conclusion: 提出了一种训练与推理保持一致的分段生成框架，在保证长上下文推理性能的同时，显著提升了模型的扩展性和内存效率，解决了现有长上下文方法存在的训练与推理不匹配问题。

Abstract: Transformer-based large language models face severe scalability challenges in long-context generation due to the computational and memory costs of full-context attention. Under practical computation and memory constraints, many inference-efficient long-context methods improve efficiency by adopting bounded-context or segment-level execution only during inference, while continuing to train models under full-context attention, resulting in a mismatch between training and inference execution and state-transition semantics. Based on this insight, we propose a training-inference consistent segment-level generation framework, in which training and inference follow the same segment-level forward execution semantics. During training, consistency with inference is enforced by restricting gradient propagation to KV states carried over from the immediately preceding segment, while permitting head-specific access to past KV states during the forward pass without involving them in gradient propagation. Across long-context benchmarks, our approach achieves performance comparable to full-context attention, while achieving competitive latency-memory trade-offs against strong inference-efficient baselines, and substantially improving scalability at very long context lengths (e.g., approximately 6x lower peak prefill memory at 128K compared to full-context attention with FlashAttention).

</details>


### [39] [Safety-Oriented Evaluation of Language Understanding Systems for Air Traffic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11769)
*Yujing Chang,Yash Guleria,Duc-Thinh Pham,Nhut-Huy Pham,Ningli Wang,Vu N. Duong,Sameer Alam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究指出大语言模型在空中交通管制（ATC）应用中存在严重的安全性盲区：虽宏观指标表现尚可，但在处理关键指令时的“风险感知力”极差，现有评估体系无法有效预警此类高危错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法过度依赖宏观聚合指标（如F1分数），无法区分低风险错误与可能导致灾难性后果的高风险错误，导致大模型在ATC高危场景下的实际可靠性评估存在空白。

Method: 提出了一套面向安全、感知后果（Consequence-aware）的评估框架，专门针对航空管制（ATC）任务，重点考查模型在处理高风险语义实体（如跑道标识、运动约束）时的错误。

Result: 实验表明大模型在ATC任务中“高宏观精度、低风险可靠性”。即使在干净的转录本上，巅峰风险评分也仅为0.69，多数模型低于0.6；错误主要集中在影响重大的实体识别上，揭示了模型在关键指令执行上的结构性基础缺陷。

Conclusion: 当前大模型在ATC领域的综合指标与实际安全性严重脱节，现有的评估方式未能反映高风险语义错误的严重性。研究强调，在部署AI辅助ATC系统时，必须引入感知后果（Consequence-aware）的评估协议。

Abstract: Air Traffic Control (ATC) is a safety-critical domain in which incorrect interpretation of instructions may lead to severe operational consequences. While large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong general performance, their reliability in operational ATC environments remains unclear. Existing evaluation approaches, largely based on aggregate metrics such as F1 or macro accuracy, treat all errors uniformly and fail to account for the asymmetric consequences of high-risk semantic mistakes (e.g., incorrect runway identifiers or movement constraints). To address this gap, we propose a safety-oriented, consequence-aware evaluation framework tailored to ATC operations. Our results reveal that while current LLMs achieve reasonable aggregate accuracy, their operational reliability is severely limited. Evaluated on clean transcripts, the peak Risk Score reaches only 0.69, with most models scoring below 0.6 despite high macro-F1 performance. Further analysis shows that errors concentrate in high-impact entities despite relatively stable action-type classification, indicating structural grounding deficiencies. These findings highlight the necessity of consequence-aware evaluation protocols for the responsible deployment of AI-assisted ATC systems.

</details>


### [40] [From Token to Token Pair: Efficient Prompt Compression for Large Language Models in Clinical Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11774)
*Mingcheng Zhu,Zhiyao Luo,Yu Liu,Tingting Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 处理医疗 EHR 数据时长序列带来的高成本和低效问题，MedTPE 通过无损的标记对合并技术，在大幅降低序列长度和推理延迟的同时，提升了预测性能并具备良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录（EHR）产生的长序列导致 LLM 推理成本高、延迟长；现有压缩方法存在额外延迟或信息丢失问题，亟需一种无损且高效的压缩方案。

Method: 提出 Medical Token-Pair Encoding (MedTPE)，一种分层扩展标记化方法。它通过依赖感知（dependency-aware）替换策略，将频繁共现的医学 Token 对合并为复合 Token，并仅对极小比例（0.5-1.0%）的模型参数进行自监督微调。

Result: 在真实数据集上，MedTPE 将输入 Token 长度降低了高达 31%，推理延迟降低了 34-63%；在保持或提升预测性能的同时，兼容多种 LLM 并能扩展至金融、科研及多语言场景。

Conclusion: MedTPE 是一种高效、通用的无损压缩方法，通过合并频繁出现的医学 Token 对，在不损失性能的前提下显著降低了 EHR 处理的计算成本和推理延迟，并具备跨领域和跨语言的通用性。

Abstract: By processing electronic health records (EHRs) as natural language sequences, large language models (LLMs) have shown potential in clinical prediction tasks such as mortality prediction and phenotyping. However, longitudinal or highly frequent EHRs often yield excessively long token sequences that result in high computational costs and even reduced performance. Existing solutions either add modules for compression or remove less important tokens, which introduce additional inference latency or risk losing clinical information. To achieve lossless compression of token sequences without additional cost or loss of performance, we propose Medical Token-Pair Encoding (MedTPE), a layered method that extends standard tokenisation for EHR sequences. MedTPE merges frequently co-occurring medical token pairs into composite tokens, providing lossless compression while preserving the computational complexity through a dependency-aware replacement strategy. Only the embeddings of the newly introduced tokens of merely 0.5-1.0% of the LLM's parameters are fine-tuned via self-supervised learning. Experiments on real-world datasets for two clinical scenarios demonstrate that MedTPE reduces input token length by up to 31% and inference latency by 34-63%, while maintaining or even improving both predictive performance and output format compliance across multiple LLMs and four clinical prediction tasks. Furthermore, MedTPE demonstrates robustness across different input context lengths and generalisability to scientific and financial domains and different languages.

</details>


### [41] [Choosing features for classifying multiword expressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11779)
*Eric Laporte*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对多词表达（MWEs）分类体系混乱的问题，通过甄选可靠特征并整合多语言研究成果，提出了一种更具计算适用性的增强型分类方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多词表达（MWEs）内部差异巨大，现有分类标准尚不完善；由于许多特征在分类可靠性上存在差异，需要一套更具计算实用性且适用于多语言的分类标准。

Method: 综合前人针对多种语言的研究成果，评估并筛选出特征，通过分析特征的可靠性来构建分类体系，以提升其计算实用价值。

Result: 构建了一个改进的MWE分类框架，该框架通过优化特征选择，增强了在跨语言任务中的应用效能。

Conclusion: 提出了一种增强型的多词表达（MWE）分类方案，旨在提高其在不同语言环境下的通用性和计算适用性。

Abstract: Multiword expressions (MWEs) are a heterogeneous set with a glaring need for classifications. Designing a satisfactory classification involves choosing features. In the case of MWEs, many features are a priori available. Not all features are equal in terms of how reliably MWEs can be assigned to classes. Accordingly, resulting classifications may be more or less fruitful for computational use. I outline an enhanced classification. In order to increase its suitability for many languages, I use previous works taking into account various languages.

</details>


### [42] [Probabilistic Calibration Is a Trainable Capability in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11845)
*Davide Baldelli,Sruthi Kuriakose,Maryam Hashemzadeh,Amal Zouaq,Sarath Chandar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究表明通过微调可以有效提升语言模型的概率校准能力（即满足特定的随机性约束），其中硬目标与软目标微调在不同场景下各有优劣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在需要满足特定随机性约束的场景下使用日益增加，但其生成概率往往无法很好地校准到目标分布，现有研究探讨是否通过微调直接改善这一能力。

Method: 提出了两种校准微调（Calibration Fine-Tuning）方案：1. 软目标方法（Soft-target）：将目标输出分布转换为词缀树（trie）导出的下一个词元目标；2. 硬目标方法（Hard-target）：在从目标分布采样得到的补全序列上进行训练。

Result: 在12种模型中，两种微调方法均显著提升了在未见过的分布族和参数设置下的结构化采样保真度，验证了改进可行性及泛化能力。

Conclusion: 概率校准是一种可以通过微调习得的能力。硬目标微调在结构化数值采样中表现更优，而软目标微调在广泛的随机生成任务中更具优势，但可能会对算术推理等下游能力产生一定影响。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly used in settings where outputs must satisfy user-specified randomness constraints, yet their generation probabilities are often poorly calibrated to those targets. We study whether this capability can be improved directly through fine-tuning. Concretely, we fine-tune language models on synthetic prompts that require sampling from mathematical distributions, and compare two Calibration Fine-Tuning variants: a soft-target method that converts the desired output distribution into trie-derived next-token targets, and a hard-target method that trains on sampled completions from the same target distribution. Across 12 models spanning four families, both methods substantially improve structured-sampling fidelity on held-out distribution families and unseen parameter settings, showing that probabilistic calibration is a trainable capability. Under our selected training configurations, the two methods exhibit different empirical profiles: hard-target fine-tuning is often strongest on structured numeric sampling, while soft-target fine-tuning performs better on broader stochastic generation benchmarks, including open-ended random generation, multiple-choice answer-position balancing, and NoveltyBench. The gains sometimes reduce downstream capability, especially arithmetic reasoning, with costs varying by model. Overall, our results show that probabilistic calibration can be improved through fine-tuning, with our hard-target configuration favoring exact numeric fidelity and our soft-target configuration favoring broader stochastic transfer. Code is available at https://github.com/chandar-lab/calibration-finetuning.

</details>


### [43] [Self-Distilled Trajectory-Aware Boltzmann Modeling: Bridging the Training-Inference Discrepancy in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11854)
*Kecheng Chen,Ziru Liu,Xijia Tao,Hui Liu,Yibing Liu,Xinyu Fu,Shi Wu,Suiyun Zhang,Dandan Tu,Lingpeng Kong,Rui Liu,Haoliang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对扩散语言模型（DLM）训练与推理不一致的问题，本文提出了TABOM框架。它通过将模型推理时的“由易到难”解掩码轨迹转化为一种基于玻尔兹曼分布的排序优化目标，从而有效利用自蒸馏轨迹提升DLM的生成能力与知识获取效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散语言模型（DLM）微调方法（基于NELBO）存在训练（单步重构）与推理（多步去噪）不一致的问题；现有的轨迹自蒸馏主要关注推理加速，而非利用轨迹进行深度知识获取。作者旨在探讨如何利用自蒸馏轨迹提升DLM的基础生成能力。

Method: 提出了TABOM（Trajectory-Aligned optimization via Boltzmann Modeling）框架：将推理过程中的逐步解掩码偏好建模为预测熵上的玻尔兹曼分布，并推导出一个成对排序目标，强制模型在训练时对齐推理时的“由易到难”置信度轨迹。

Result: TABOM在多个新领域实现了显著的性能提升，扩展了DLM的有效知识边界，并表现出优于标准有监督微调（SFT）的抗灾难性遗忘能力。

Conclusion: TABOM通过将推理过程中的置信度排序与训练目标对齐，成功利用自蒸馏轨迹实现了扩散语言模型（DLM）的知识获取与能力提升，并有效缓解了微调过程中的灾难性遗忘问题。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive language models, offering stronger global awareness and highly parallel generation. However, post-training DLMs with standard Negative Evidence Lower Bound (NELBO)-based supervised fine-tuning remains inefficient: training reconstructs randomly masked tokens in a single step, whereas inference follows a confidence-guided, multi-step easy-to-hard denoising trajectory. Recent trajectory-based self-distillation methods exploit such inference trajectories mainly for sampling-step compression and acceleration, often improving decoding efficiency without substantially enhancing the model's underlying capability, and may even degrade performance under full diffusion decoding. In this work, we ask whether self-distilled trajectories can be used not merely for faster inference, but for genuine knowledge acquisition. Although these trajectories lie on the pretrained DLM's own distributional manifold and thus offer a potentially lower optimization barrier, we find that naively fine-tuning on them with standard NELBO objectives yields only marginal gains. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{T}rajectory-\textbf{A}ligned optimization via \textbf{Bo}ltzmann \textbf{M}odeling (\textbf{TABOM}), a self-distilled trajectory-based post-training framework that aligns training with the easy-to-hard structure of inference. TABOM models the inference unmasking preference as a Boltzmann distribution over predictive entropies and derives a tractable pairwise ranking objective to align the model's certainty ordering with the observed decoding trajectory. Empirically, TABOM achieves substantial gains in new domains, expands the effective knowledge boundary of DLMs, and significantly mitigates catastrophic forgetting compared with standard SFT.

</details>


### [44] [Concordance Comparison as a Means of Assembling Local Grammars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11862)
*Juliana Pirovani,Elias de Oliveira,Eric Laporte*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过自动比较和优化组合局部语法（LG）的方法来提升葡萄牙语人名命名实体识别性能的技术，在HAREM数据集上实现了6个F-Measure点的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人名命名实体识别在信息抽取中具有重要性且面临挑战，旨在克服单一局部语法在覆盖范围和准确性上的局限性，从而提升信息抽取的质量。

Method: 提出了一种利用差异分析工具比较不同局部语法（LG）结果的方法，通过识别语法间的包含、交集和不相交关系，选取并组装性能最优的语法集合。

Result: 在第二届HAREM黄金数据集上的测试显示，改进后的语法达到了76.86的F-Measure，较葡萄牙语领域当前最佳水平提升了6个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过比较和组合多种局部语法，可以有效提升葡萄牙语人名命名实体识别（NER）的性能，所提出的方法在测试集上取得了显著的性能增益。

Abstract: Named Entity Recognition for person names is an important but non-trivial task in information extraction. This article uses a tool that compares the concordances obtained from two local grammars (LG) and highlights the differences. We used the results as an aid to select the best of a set of LGs. By analyzing the comparisons, we observed relationships of inclusion, intersection and disjunction within each pair of LGs, which helped us to assemble those that yielded the best results. This approach was used in a case study on extraction of person names from texts written in Portuguese. We applied the enhanced grammar to the Gold Collection of the Second HAREM. The F-Measure obtained was 76.86, representing a gain of 6 points in relation to the state-of-the-art for Portuguese.

</details>


### [45] [Enhancing Target-Guided Proactive Dialogue Systems via Conversational Scenario Modeling and Intent-Keyword Bridging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11964)
*Maodong Li,Yancui Li,Fang Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过动态建模对话场景和预测意图关键词来引导目标导向对话系统的方法，解决了现有模型响应僵化的问题，显著增强了系统在真实对话中的主动性和交互质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的目标导向对话系统未能充分动态建模对话场景与意图关键词，导致系统生成的回复难以适应真实对话的动态变化，缺乏主动性和引导性。

Method: 提出了一种联合建模框架：1. 将用户画像与领域知识建模为对话场景（Conversational Scenarios），引入动态场景偏置影响生成；2. 采用意图关键词桥接（Intent-keyword Bridging）预测后续对话轮次的意图关键词，提供灵活的引导。

Result: 在自动评测和人工评测中表现优异，显著提升了系统的主动性、流畅度和信息量。

Conclusion: 通过联合建模用户画像与领域知识并引入意图关键词桥接，能有效提升目标导向对话系统在主动性、流畅性及信息量方面的表现，使其更贴近真实对话场景。

Abstract: A target-guided proactive dialogue system aims to steer conversations proactively toward pre-defined targets, such as designated keywords or specific topics. During guided conversations, dynamically modeling conversational scenarios and intent keywords to guide system utterance generation is beneficial; however, existing work largely overlooks this aspect, resulting in a mismatch with the dynamics of real-world conversations. In this paper, we jointly model user profiles and domain knowledge as conversational scenarios to introduce a scenario bias that dynamically influences system utterances, and employ intent-keyword bridging to predict intent keywords for upcoming dialogue turns, providing higher level and more flexible guidance. Extensive automatic and human evaluations demonstrate the effectiveness of conversational scenario modeling and intent keyword bridging, yielding substantial improvements in proactivity, fluency, and informativeness for target-guided proactive dialogue systems, thereby narrowing the gap with real world interactions.

</details>


### [46] [On Predicting the Post-training Potential of Pre-trained LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11978)
*Xiaoyuan Li,Yubo Ma,Kexin Yang,Moxin Li,Keqin Bao,Wenie Wang,Fuli Feng,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 为了解决评估基座模型后训练潜力困难的问题，本文提出了 RuDE 框架，通过判别式评估方法，在不进行完整后训练的情况下，高精度预测模型未来的性能表现，从而优化模型选择并提升开发效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基准测试（如 MMLU）无法有效反映基座模型在复杂开放场景下的潜在演化能力（即“塑性”），导致在后训练（如 RLHF）前难以高效筛选适合的模型，造成计算资源的严重浪费。

Method: 提出 RuDE (Rubric-based Discriminative Evaluation) 框架。该框架利用“响应判别”（response discrimination）而非文本生成来评估模型，并通过 4C 分类法构建细粒度的受控对比样本对，进行判别式评估。

Result: 实验表明，RuDE 的预测结果与模型后训练后的实际表现相关性超过 90%。在使用强化学习验证中，RuDE 成功识别出了性能超越更大参数模型的小型高潜力模型。

Conclusion: RuDE 通过预测基座模型在后训练后的性能，提供了一种高效的模型选择机制。该方法证明了能够识别出具备高潜力的小参数模型，从而为基础模型开发节省了大量的计算资源。

Abstract: The performance of Large Language Models (LLMs) on downstream tasks is fundamentally constrained by the capabilities acquired during pre-training. However, traditional benchmarks like MMLU often fail to reflect a base model's plasticity in complex open-ended scenarios, leading to inefficient model selection. We address this by introducing a new task of predicting post-training potential - forecasting a base model's performance before post-training. We propose RuDE (Rubric-based Discriminative Evaluation), a unified framework that bypasses the generation gap of base models by leveraging response discrimination. Guided by our systematic 4C Taxonomy, RuDE constructs controlled contrastive pairs across diverse domains by fine-grained rubric violations. Extensive experiments demonstrate a correlation greater than 90% with post-training performance. Crucially, validation via Reinforcement Learning (RL) confirms that RuDE effectively identifies high-potential smaller models that outperform larger counterparts, offering a compute-efficient mechanism for foundation model development.

</details>


### [47] [Towards Visually-Guided Movie Subtitle Translation for Indic Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11993)
*Tarun Chintada,Kshetrimayum Boynao Singh,Asif Ekbal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对电影字幕翻译中视觉信息缺失问题，研究引入轻量级视觉定位策略。发现针对低质量翻译段落进行选择性视觉增强（结构化属性法最佳），可有效提升低资源语种在复杂影音场景下的翻译准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的字幕翻译系统仅依赖文本，难以处理视频中蕴含的情感、动作及社交语境等视觉线索，特别是在低资源语种（如印地语、孟加拉语等）的翻译中表现受限。

Method: 提出了两种轻量级视觉定位策略：基于5分钟滑动窗口的“结构化属性摘要”和基于字幕间隙的“自由文本摘要”。通过对比实验，分析了时间对齐问题对视觉接地（Visual Grounding）的影响，并提出了仅替换低质量段落（20-30%）的“选择性增强”优化策略。

Result: 研究发现，视频帧与字幕的时间不对齐是长视频任务中的主要技术瓶颈。实验证明，仅对质量最低的20-30%字幕段落进行视觉辅助处理，即可在维持较低计算成本的前提下，实现比纯文本模型更高的COMET评分。

Conclusion: 在长视频字幕翻译任务中，选择性（Oracle）地利用视觉信息能显著提升翻译质量。相比自由文本摘要，基于粗粒度属性的视觉上下文摘要更具备鲁棒性，能够有效捕捉场景级的复杂情感与语境。

Abstract: Movie subtitle translation is inherently multimodal, yet text-only systems often miss visual cues needed to convey emotion, action, and social nuance, especially for low-resource Indic languages (English to Hindi, Bengali, Telugu, Tamil and Kannada). We present a case study on five full-length films and compare two lightweight visual grounding strategies: structured attribute summaries from a 5-minute sliding window and free-text summaries of inter-subtitle visual gaps. Our analysis shows that temporal misalignment between subtitles and frames is a major obstacle in long-form video, often rendering indiscriminate visual grounding ineffective. However, oracle selective grounding, which replaces only the lowest-quality 20-30\% of baseline segments with visual-enhanced outputs, consistently improves COMET over the text-only baseline while requiring far less visual processing. Among the two approaches, coarse attribute-based visual context summarization is more robust, capturing scene-level emotion and contextual subtle cues that text alone often misses

</details>


### [48] [SAGE: Scalable Automated Robustness Augmentation for LLM Knowledge Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12022)
*Xiaoyuan Li,Yuzhe Wang,Moxin Li,Keqin Bao,Rui Men,Yichang Zhang,Dayiheng Liu,Wenjie Wang,Fuli Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对现有 LLM 知识评价鲁棒性不足且测评方案成本高昂的问题，本文提出了 SAGE 框架，通过微调小模型进行高效的自动化变体生成与验证，以低成本构建了高质量、可扩展的鲁棒性测评基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型（LLM）在标准知识评价中表现优异，但在面对同一知识的不同提问变体时表现脆弱。现有的 LLM 辅助生成验证流程存在成本高、扩展性差、生成收益低及验证不可靠等瓶颈，亟需一种可扩展的鲁棒性增强方案。

Method: 提出 SAGE 框架，包含两个核心组件：1. VariantQual：基于准则的验证器，利用人工标注的种子数据进行训练；2. VariantGen：变体生成器，先进行有监督微调（SFT），随后利用 VariantQual 作为奖励模型进行强化学习优化。

Result: 在 HellaSwag 数据集上的实验表明，SAGE 构建的鲁棒性增强基准在质量上与人类标注的 HellaSwag-Pro 相当，且成本大幅降低。此外，该方法无需特定任务微调即可泛化到 MMLU 数据集。

Conclusion: SAGE 证明了利用经微调的较小模型进行鲁棒性增强是高效且可扩展的，其生成的基准质量可媲美人类标注，且具有良好的跨任务泛化能力，为解决 LLM 知识评估的脆性问题提供了高性价比的方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve strong performance on standard knowledge evaluation benchmarks, yet recent work shows that their knowledge capabilities remain brittle under question variants that test the same knowledge in different forms. Robustness augmentation of existing knowledge evaluation benchmarks is therefore necessary, but current LLM-assisted generate-then-verify pipelines are costly and difficult to scale due to low-yield variant generation and unreliable variant verification. We propose SAGE (Scalable Automated Generation of Robustness BEnchmarks), a framework for scalable robustness augmentation of knowledge evaluation benchmarks using fine-tuned smaller models. SAGE consists of VariantQual, a rubric-based verifier trained on human-labeled seed data, and VariantGen, a variant generator initialized with supervised fine-tuning and further optimized with reinforcement learning using VariantQual as the reward model. Experiments on HellaSwag show that SAGE constructs a large-scale robustness-augmented benchmark with quality comparable to the human-annotated HellaSwag-Pro at substantially lower cost, while the fine-tuned models further generalize to MMLU without benchmark-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [49] [SkillGraph: Skill-Augmented Reinforcement Learning for Agents via Evolving Skill Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12039)
*Xiaoyuan Li,Moxin Li,Keqin Bao,Yubo Ma,Wenjie Wang,Dayiheng Liu,Fuli Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SKILLGRAPH通过将技能组织成有向结构化图（Skill Graph）而非孤立条目，解决了Agent在复杂任务中技能组合与维护的难题，实现了性能的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大模型Agent技能库多为孤立条目，仅通过语义相似度检索，难以处理复杂组合任务中技能间的依赖关系，且缺乏结构化指导，导致技能库维护困难。

Method: 提出SKILLGRAPH框架，将技能表示为有向图中的节点，并使用带类型的边（前提、增强、共现关系）编码技能间的结构化关系。基于新任务动态检索有序技能子图，并利用Agent轨迹和强化学习反馈进行图结构的持续迭代优化。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop及七个搜索增强QA任务中达到SOTA效果，尤其在需要多技能组合的复杂任务上表现出显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: SKILLGRAPH通过构建技能依赖图，有效地解决了大模型Agent在技能复用中的组合性与管理难题，通过动态更新机制实现了策略与技能库的双重优化，在复杂任务中表现出显著优势。

Abstract: Skill libraries enable large language model agents to reuse experience from past interactions, but most existing libraries store skills as isolated entries and retrieve them only by semantic similarity. This leads to two key challenges for compositional tasks. Firstly, an agent must identify not only relevant skills but also how they depend on and build upon each other. Secondly, it also makes library maintenance difficult, since the system lacks structural cues for deciding when skills should be merged, split, or removed. We propose SKILLGRAPH, a framework that represents reusable skills as nodes in a directed graph, with typed edges encoding prerequisite, enhancement, and co-occurrence relations. Given a new task, SKILLGRAPH retrieves not just individual skills, but an ordered skill subgraph that can guide multi-step decision making. The graph is continuously updated from agent trajectories and reinforcement learning feedback, allowing both the skill library and the agent policy to improve together. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and seven search-augmented QA tasks show that SKILLGRAPH achieves state-of-the-art performance against memory-augmented RL methods, with especially large gains on complex tasks that require composing multiple skills.

</details>


### [50] [Is Child-Directed Language Optimized for Word Learning? A Computational Study of Verb Meaning Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12047)
*Francesca Padovani,Jaap Jumelet,Yevgen Matusevych,Arianna Bisazza*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究通过计算建模发现，CDL并不具备某种独特的语言习得优化机制，其表现出的优势实际上是口语语体的通用特征；语言学习过程中遵循“语义先于句法”的发展轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究儿童导向语言（CDL）是否针对语言学习进行了优化，以及它究竟促进了哪些语言发展维度，特别是针对动词意义的习得机制。

Method: 利用神经语言模型（Neural language models）进行对比实验，通过选择性剔除训练数据中的句法或词汇共现信息，观察其对模型习得动词意义的影响，并追踪模型在训练过程中的语义与句法习得进度。

Result: 1. 破坏句法信息对所有数据集的学习均有损害，但CDL和口语ADL模型比书面语模型表现出更强的鲁棒性；2. 观察到“语义优先”的习得轨迹，即动词含义的习得早于句法能力的成熟；3. 这种先语义后句法的异步现象在口语领域（尤其是CDL）中最为显著。

Conclusion: 与成人导向语言（ADL）相比，针对儿童导向语言（CDL）的独特优化优势并不存在；所谓CDL在动词学习上的优势，更多源于口语语体（spoken register）的普遍特性。

Abstract: Is child-directed language (CDL) optimized to support language learning, and which aspects of linguistic development does it facilitate? We investigate this question using neural language models trained on CDL versus adult-directed language (ADL). We selectively remove syntactic or lexical co-occurrence information from the model training data, and evaluate the impact of these manipulations on verb meaning acquisition. While disrupting syntax impairs learning across all datasets, models trained on CDL and spoken ADL show significantly higher resilience than those trained on written input. Tracking semantic and syntactic performance over training, we observe a semantic-first trajectory, with verb meanings emerging prior to robust syntactic proficiency, an asynchrony most pronounced in the spoken domain, especially CDL. These results suggest that the advantage for verb learning previously attributed to CDL may instead reflect broader properties of the spoken register, rather than a uniquely CDL-specific optimization.

</details>


### [51] [Sign Language Recognition and Translation for Low-Resource Languages: Challenges and Pathways Forward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12096)
*Nigar Alishzade,Gulchin Abdullayeva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对低资源手语（以阿塞拜疆手语为例）面临的数据匮乏困境，提出了以社区共建为基础、迁移学习为手段、数据驱动为核心的低资源手语技术发展路线及三个范式转移建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球300多种手语因缺乏文档和数据处于资源匮乏状态，亟需一套针对低资源手语的计算工具与方法论，以实现文化真实性与技术适用性。

Method: 通过系统综述（Systematic Review）分析全球低资源手语识别与翻译研究，并以阿塞拜疆手语（AzSL）作为案例研究；探讨了利用突厥语系手语间的语言学亲缘关系进行迁移学习的可行性。

Result: 梳理了8项关键行动准则；提出了三个范式转变（数据中心化、自适应化、任务指标化）；制定了AzSL的技术实现路线图，重点在于社区参与的标注与轻量化模型开发。

Conclusion: 构建低资源手语技术需要从以模型为中心转向以数据为中心、从独立于手语者系统转向自适应系统，并建立以社区为核心的跨学科协作机制。技术路线建议采用轻量化架构（如MediaPipe）和离线部署，以确保护理与可用性。

Abstract: Sign languages are natural, visual-gestural languages used by Deaf communities worldwide. Over 300 distinct sign languages remain severely low-resource due to limited documentation, sparse datasets, and insufficient computational tools. This systematic review synthesizes literature on sign language recognition and translation for under-resourced languages, using Azerbaijan Sign Language (AzSL) as a case study. Analysis of global initiatives extracts eight actionable lessons, including community co-design, dialectal diversity capture, and privacy-preserving pose-based representations. Turkic sign languages (Kazakh, Turkish, Azerbaijani) receive special attention, as linguistic proximity enables effective transfer learning. We propose three paradigm shifts: from architecture-centric to data-centric AI, from signer-independent to signer-adaptive systems, and from reference-based to task-specific evaluation metrics. A technical roadmap for AzSL leverages lightweight MediaPipe-based architectures, community-validated annotations, and offline-first deployment. Progress requires sustained interdisciplinary collaboration centered on Deaf communities to ensure cultural authenticity, ethical governance, and practical communication benefit.

</details>


### [52] [Metaphor Is Not All Attention Needs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12128)
*Olga Sorokoletova,Francesco Giarrusso,Giacomo De Luca,Piercosma Bisconti,Matteo Prandi,Federico Pierucci,Marcello Galisai,Vincenzo Suriani,Daniele Nardi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过可解释性实验揭示了文学类越狱的机制：模型并非“被忽悠”识别不出诗歌，而是复杂的文体风格改变了模型的内部处理模式，使其逃脱了后训练阶段建立的安全屏障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探讨“文学越狱”（如以诗歌形式绕过模型安全限制）为何能有效奏效，特别是这种效果是否源于对特定修辞的依赖、模型对格式的识别失效，或是由于文体异常导致的深层处理机制变化。

Method: 采用可解释性分析方法：1. 进行输入级消融研究以评估各种诗歌修辞手法的影响；2. 构建注意力图的可解释向量表示；3. 对表示进行聚类并训练线性探测器（Linear Probes），用于预测安全结果和文学格式。以Qwen3-14B为案例研究。

Result: 研究发现模型能准确区分诗歌与散文格式，但难以在格式内部预测越狱成功与否；聚类分析显示格式划分清晰，但与安全性标签无关；结论指出越狱成功是由于累积的文体不规则性改变了提示处理路径并规避了词汇触发器。

Conclusion: 文学类越狱的成功并非因为模型无法识别诗歌格式，而是因为非正统的文体导致模型内部处理模式发生偏移，从而绕过了针对常规格式训练的词汇触发机制。为了提升鲁棒性，安全机制必须纳入对文体引发的行为偏移的考量。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in safety-critical applications, where their ability to resist harmful instructions is essential. Although post-training aims to make models robust against many jailbreak strategies, recent evidence shows that stylistic reformulations, such as poetic transformation, can still bypass safety mechanisms with alarming effectiveness. This raises a central question: why do literary jailbreaks succeed? In this work, we investigate whether their effectiveness depends on specific poetic devices, on a failure to recognize literary formatting, or on deeper changes in how models process stylistically irregular prompts. We address this problem through an interpretability analysis of attention patterns. We perform input-level ablation studies to assess the contribution of individual and combinations of poetic devices; construct an interpretable vector representation of attention maps; cluster these representations and train linear probes to predict safety outcomes and literary format. Our results show that models distinguish poetic from prose formats with high accuracy, yet struggle to predict jailbreak success within each format. Clustering further reveals clear separation by literary format, but not by safety label. These findings indicate that jailbreak success is not caused by a failure to recognize poetic formatting; rather, poetic prompts induce distinct processing patterns that remain largely independent of harmful-content detection. Overall, literary jailbreaks appear to misalign large language models not through any single poetic device, but through accumulated stylistic irregularities that alter prompt processing and avoid lexical triggers considered during post-training. This suggests that robustness requires safety mechanisms that account for style-induced shifts in model behavior. We use Qwen3-14B as a representative open-weight case study.

</details>


### [53] [Latent Causal Void: Explicit Missing-Context Reconstruction for Misinformation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12156)
*Hui Li,Zhongquan Jian,Jinsong Su,Junfeng Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对通过“遗漏关键背景事实”进行误导的错误信息，本研究提出了一种LCV检测模型，通过大模型显式重构缺失事实并将其整合进图推理中，有效提升了跨源错误信息检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的错误信息检测方法在处理“局部连贯但因掩盖事实而误导”的案例时表现不佳，且现有方法通常仅将背景证据作为辅助信息或仅推断分类标签，未能明确显式缺失的具体事实。

Method: 提出“潜在因果空白”（Latent Causal Void, LCV）框架：1. 检索时间对齐的背景文章；2. 利用冻结的指令微调大模型（LLM）为每个句文对生成缺失的上下文描述；3. 将该描述作为文本跨源关系构建异构图并进行推理。

Result: 在Sheng等人提出的双语基准数据集上，LCV在英语和中文拆分集上分别较最强基线提升了2.56和2.84个宏观F1值。

Conclusion: 通过显式重构缺失事实并将其作为图推理中的文本关系，能够显著提高对基于事实遗漏（omission）的错误信息的检测能力。

Abstract: Automatic misinformation detection performs well when deception is visible in what an article explicitly states. However, some misinformation articles remain locally coherent and only become misleading once compared with contemporaneous reports that supply background facts the article omits. We study this omission-relevant setting and observe that current omission-aware approaches typically either attach retrieved context as auxiliary evidence or infer a categorical omission signal, leaving the specific missing fact implicit. We propose \emph{Latent Causal Void} (LCV), a retrieval-guided detector that explicitly reconstructs the missing fact for each target sentence and uses it as a textual cross-source relation in graph reasoning. Concretely, LCV retrieves temporally aligned context articles, asks a frozen instruction-tuned large language model to generate a short missing-context description for each sentence--article pair, and feeds the resulting relation text into a heterograph over target sentences and context articles. On the bilingual benchmark of Sheng et al., LCV improves over the strongest omission-aware baseline by $2.56$ and $2.84$ macro-F1 points on the English and Chinese splits, respectively. The results indicate that modeling the missing cross-source fact itself, rather than only attaching retrieved evidence or predicting an omission signal, is a useful representation for omission-aware misinformation detection.

</details>


### [54] [Correcting Selection Bias in Sparse User Feedback for Large Language Model Quality Estimation: A Multi-Agent Hierarchical Bayesian Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12177)
*Andrea Morandi,Mahesh Viswanathan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM生产环境用户反馈的非随机选择偏差问题，提出了一种基于分层贝叶斯建模的自动修正框架，通过结合主题聚类和领域先验信息，实现了比朴素统计方法更精准的系统质量评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的LLM用户反馈存在严重的“选择偏差”（非随机性），直接使用用户反馈生成的平均分往往与真实的系统质量偏离高达40-50个百分点。

Method: 提出了一种三智能体分层贝叶斯处理流水线：1. 主题聚类智能体（UMAP+HDBSCAN）将交互流分区；2. 偏差建模智能体（分层Beta-Binomial+NUTS）推断各主题的选择率和质量；3. 合成智能体根据真实主题比例重加权并输出修正后的质量评估及可信区间。

Result: 通过UltraFeedback数据集模拟验证，在极端偏差比例（30:1）下，该Hierarchical-Informed方法能够将误差控制在4-13个百分点内；而缺乏先验信息的变体误差则高达22-33个百分点。

Conclusion: 在处理生产环境LLM反馈的选择性偏差问题时，引入简单的先验信息（基于通道频率的先验）是打破模型参数简并性并获得准确系统质量估计的关键，优于传统的朴素平均或IPW方法。

Abstract: [Abridged] Production LLM deployments receive feedback from a non-random fraction of users: thumbs sit mostly in the tails of the satisfaction distribution, and a naive average over them can land 40-50 percentage points away from true system quality. We treat this as a topic- and sentiment- stratified selection-bias problem and propose a three-agent hierarchical Bayesian pipeline that does not require ground-truth labels on individual interactions. A Topic Clustering Agent partitions the stream via UMAP + HDBSCAN over text embeddings; a Bias Modeling Agent fits a two-stage hierarchical Beta-Binomial under NUTS, inferring per-topic selection rates $s_c$ and quality $q_c$ with partial pooling; a Synthesis Agent reweights $q_c$ by true topic prevalence $\hatπ_c = n_c/N$ to report a bias-corrected aggregate posterior $\bar Q = \sum_c \hatπ_c q_c$ with credible interval, plus drift signals for online recalibration. Validation uses UltraFeedback (N=10,232 retained interactions, $C=18$ clusters, $Q^\star=0.6249$) with simulated topic- and sentiment-dependent selection biases. We compare five Bayesian variants against Naive and IPW baselines. A mild prior on the feedback channel (typical positive-feedback rate and negative-to-positive ratio, both readable from any production dashboard without labels) keeps Hierarchical-Informed within 4-13 pp of $Q^\star$ as the bias ratio sweeps from 1:1 to 30:1, with 95% credible intervals covering $Q^\star$ in 50/50 random-seed replicates at $κ_{\max}=10$. Without channel-side priors, every weak-prior variant misses $Q^\star$ by 22-33 pp: the per-cluster sufficient statistics admit a one-parameter family of equally good fits, and the prior on the bias channel (not on latent quality) is what breaks the degeneracy.

</details>


### [55] [Mitigating Context-Memory Conflicts in LLMs through Dynamic Cognitive Reconciliation Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12185)
*Yigeng Zhou,Wu Li,Yifan Lu,Yequan Wang,Xuebo Liu,Wenya Wang,Jun Yu,Min Zhang,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对大语言模型知识冲突的动态译码框架DCRD，通过预测冲突并动态调整译码路径，实现了在解决知识冲突的同时兼顾效率与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解决大语言模型参数知识与上下文外部知识冲突的方法存在局限性：静态方法在无冲突场景下会扰动输出，而现有动态译码方法难以处理复杂的真实冲突场景。

Method: 提出了一种双阶段译码方法DCRD：第一阶段通过分析注意力图评估上下文保真度并预测冲突；第二阶段根据预测结果动态选择路径——无冲突时使用贪婪译码，冲突时使用基于上下文保真度的动态译码。此外，构建了ConflictKG基准测试集。

Result: 在四个大语言模型和六个QA数据集上的实验表明，DCRD优于所有基线方法，在处理冲突与保持效率之间取得了更好的平衡。

Conclusion: DCRD是一种有效缓解LLM知识冲突的译码策略，能够在保持无冲突场景性能的同时，显著提高冲突场景下的回答准确性，确立了新的SOTA性能。

Abstract: Large language models accumulate extensive parametric knowledge through pre-training. However, knowledge conflicts occur when outdated or incorrect parametric knowledge conflicts with external knowledge in the context. Existing methods address knowledge conflicts through contrastive decoding, but in conflict-free scenarios, static approaches disrupt output distribution. Other dynamic decoding methods attempt to measure the degree of conflict but still struggle with complex real-world situations. In this paper, we propose a two-stage decoding method called Dynamic Cognitive Reconciliation Decoding (DCRD), to predict and mitigate context-memory conflicts. DCRD first analyzes the attention map to assess context fidelity and predict potential conflicts. Based on this prediction, the input is directed to one of two decoding paths: (1) greedy decoding, or (2) context fidelity-based dynamic decoding. This design enables DCRD to handle conflicts efficiently while maintaining high accuracy and decoding efficiency in conflict-free cases. Additionally, to simulate scenarios with frequent knowledge updates, we constructed ConflictKG, a knowledge conflict QA benchmark. Experiments on four LLMs across six QA datasets show that DCRD outperforms all baselines, achieving state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [56] [Mechanistic Interpretability of ASR models using Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12225)
*Dan Pluth,Zachary Nicholas Houghton,Yu Zhou,Vijay K. Gurbani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究首次成功将稀疏自编码器（SAE）应用于语音识别模型Whisper，通过在帧级嵌入上训练高维稀疏空间，揭示了模型内部丰富的单语义特征，并验证了特征引导的可行性，填补了语音模型可解释性研究的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于Transformer的NLP模型中SAE技术已取得进展，但针对音频处理模型（如ASR）的内部可解释性研究尚属空白。为了揭示自动语音识别模型的内部运作机制，亟需探索适用于音频领域的特征可解释性方法。

Method: 将稀疏自编码器（SAE）应用于Transformer架构的语音识别模型Whisper；通过在Whisper编码器提取的帧级嵌入（frame-level embeddings）上训练高维稀疏潜在空间，以实现对模型内部表示的特征拆解。

Result: 成功提取出跨越语言与非语言边界的多样化单语义特征（monosemantic features），并实现了跨语言的特征引导（feature steering），展示了模型内部表示的粒度化控制能力。

Conclusion: 本研究证实了稀疏自编码器（SAE）在音频处理模型（如Whisper）解释性研究中的有效性，揭示了Whisper编码器中包含丰富的语言学信息，为理解音频模型内部机制提供了可靠的分析路径。

Abstract: Understanding the internal machinations of deep Transformer-based NLP models is more crucial than ever as these models see widespread use in various domains that affect the public at large, such as industry, academia, finance, health. While these models have advanced rapidly, their internal mechanisms remain largely a mystery. Techniques such as Sparse Autoencoders (SAE) have emerged to understand these mechanisms by projecting dense representations into a sparse vector. While existing research has demonstrated the viability of the SAE in interpreting text-based Large Language Models (LLMs), there are no equivalent studies that demonstrate the application of a SAE to audio processing models like Automatic Speech Recognizers (ASRs). In this work, a SAE is applied to Whisper, a Transformer-based ASR, training a high-dimensional sparse latent space on frame-level embeddings extracted from the Whisper encoder. Our work uncovers diverse monosemantic features across linguistic and non-linguistic boundaries, and demonstrates cross-lingual feature steering. This work establishes the viability of a SAE model and demonstrates that Whisper encodes a rich amount of linguistic information.

</details>


### [57] [Combining On-Policy Optimization and Distillation for Long-Context Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12227)
*Miguel Moura Ramos,Duarte M. Alves,André F. T. Martins*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为dGRPO的新方法，通过结合强化学习（GRPO）的奖励优化与知识蒸馏（OPD）的密集引导，有效解决了大模型长上下文任务中训练不稳定和样本效率低的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长上下文对齐方法存在局限：SFT/KD存在暴露偏差；GRPO因奖励稀疏导致不稳定和样本效率低；OPD虽提供密集信号但无法优化任意奖励。因此需要一种既能提供密集引导又能优化复杂奖励的方法。

Method: 提出了Distilled Group Relative Policy Optimization (dGRPO)算法，将GRPO（离线策略强化学习）与来自强Teacher模型的OPD（在线策略蒸馏）相结合；同时构建了包含多跳推理、上下文关联和长文本生成的合成数据集LongBlocks以辅助训练。

Result: dGRPO显著提升了模型在长上下文任务中的性能，在推理质量与稳定性上优于纯SFT、纯KD或纯GRPO方法，且没有牺牲模型原有的短上下文能力。

Conclusion: 将基于结果的策略优化（强化学习）与知识蒸馏（监督信号）相结合，是在保持短上下文能力的同时，实现稳定高效的长上下文推理对齐的最佳路径。

Abstract: Adapting large language models (LLMs) to long-context tasks requires post-training methods that remain accurate and coherent over thousands of tokens. Existing approaches are limited in several ways: 1) off-policy methods such as supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and knowledge distillation (KD) suffer from exposure bias and limited recovery from model-generated errors over long horizons; 2) on-policy reinforcement learning methods such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) better align training with model-generated states, but are unstable and sample-inefficient due to sparse rewards; 3) on-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense token-level guidance, but does not directly optimize arbitrary reward signals. In this paper, we propose Distilled Group Relative Policy Optimization (dGRPO), a method for long-context reasoning that augments GRPO with dense guidance from a stronger teacher via OPD. We also introduce LongBlocks, a synthetic long-context dataset spanning multi-hop reasoning, contextual grounding, and long-form generation. We conduct extensive experiments and ablations comparing off-policy training, sparse-reward GRPO, and our combined approach, leading to an improved recipe for long-context alignment. Overall, our results show that combining outcome-based policy optimization with knowledge distillation in a single objective provides a more stable and effective path to long-context reasoning, while preserving short-context capabilities.

</details>


### [58] [Mind the Pause: Disfluency-Aware Objective Tuning for Multilingual Speech Correction with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12242)
*Deepak Kumar,Baban Gain,Asif Ekbal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对ASR转录中的不连贯问题，本研究提出了一种结合序列标注、LLM指令微调与对比学习的多语言修正方案，在保持语义连贯的同时有效提升了文本流利度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动语音识别（ASR）转录中普遍存在的填充词、重复和更正等不连贯现象，严重影响了可读性及下游任务的可靠性。现有方法多侧重于简单的令牌移除，往往导致句子结构破碎，且现有LLM应用多局限于检测而非全面修正。

Method: 提出了一种多语言修正流水线：首先利用序列标注器标记不连贯的令牌，随后利用这些信号指导大语言模型（LLM）进行指令微调以重写转录内容，并引入对比学习目标以抑制不连贯令牌的生成，从而在保持语法和语义的同时去除冗余成分。

Result: 在印地语、孟加拉语和马拉地语三种语言的实验中，该方法相比多语言序列到序列（Seq2Seq）基线模型均表现出显著且一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 单纯的检测策略不足以解决口语不连贯问题。通过将令牌级线索与指令微调及对比学习相结合，可为多语言语音驱动的自然语言处理系统提供一种高效且具可扩展性的不连贯修正方案。

Abstract: Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) transcripts often contain disfluencies, such as fillers, repetitions, and false starts, which reduce readability and hinder downstream applications like chatbots and voice assistants. If left unaddressed, such disfluencies can significantly degrade the reliability of downstream systems. Most existing approaches rely on classical models that focus on identifying disfluent tokens for removal. While this strategy is effective to some extent, it often disrupts grammatical structure and semantic coherence, leading to incomplete or unnatural sentences. Recent literature explored the use of large language models (LLMs); however, these efforts have primarily focused on disfluency detection or data augmentation, rather than performing comprehensive correction. We propose a multilingual correction pipeline where a sequence tagger first marks disfluent tokens, and these signals guide instruction fine-tuning of an LLM to rewrite transcripts into fluent text. To further improve reliability, we add a contrastive learning objective that penalizes the reproduction of disfluent tokens, encouraging the model to preserve grammar and meaning while removing disfluent artifacts. Our experiments across three Indian languages, namely Hindi, Bengali, and Marathi show consistent improvements over strong baselines, including multilingual sequence-to-sequence models. These results highlight that detection-only strategies are insufficient. Combining token-level cues with instruction tuning and contrastive learning provides a practical and scalable solution for multilingual disfluency correction in speech-driven NLP systems. We make the codes publicly available at https://github.com/deepak-kumar-98/Mind-the-Pause.

</details>


### [59] [PreScam: A Benchmark for Predicting Scam Progression from Early Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12243)
*Weixiang Sun,Shang Ma,Yiyang Li,Tianyi Ma,Zehong Wang,Colby Nelson,Xusheng Xiao,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过构建 PreScam 数据集，本研究发现现有大模型在检测诈骗进展和心理操纵逻辑方面仍存在挑战，难以准确预测实时诈骗的终结阶段或诈骗者的下一步动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注静态诈骗检测，缺乏对真实世界中诈骗如何通过多轮对话进行心理操纵和随时间演变的深入理解，亟需对其进展建模进行评估。

Method: 构建了一个名为 PreScam 的基准数据集，包含 11,573 个真实对话诈骗实例，并定义了“诈骗杀伤链”（scam kill chain）以结构化诈骗生命周期；通过监督学习编码器与零样本大语言模型（LLM）进行对比，执行实时终止预测和诈骗者行为预测两项任务。

Result: 实验结果表明，监督学习编码器在实时终止预测任务上大幅领先于零样本 LLM；即使是强大的 LLM 在预测诈骗者的后续动作上也仅取得了中等程度的成功，暴露了模型在把握诈骗进程演变方面的能力鸿沟。

Conclusion: 当前模型虽能捕捉对话中零星的诈骗线索，但仍难以有效追踪风险升级过程以及操纵手段随对话轮次的演变，显示出在诈骗进展建模方面的局限性。

Abstract: Conversational scams, such as romance and investment scams, are emerging as a major form of online fraud. Unlike one-shot scam lures such as fake lottery or unpaid toll messages, they unfold through multi-turn conversations in which scammers gradually manipulate victims using evolving psychological techniques. However, existing research mainly focuses on static scam detection or synthetic scams, leaving open whether language models can understand how real-world scams progress over time. We introduce PreScam, a benchmark for modeling scam progression from early conversations. Built from user-submitted scam reports, PreScam filters and structures 177,989 raw reports into 11,573 conversational scam instances spanning 20 scam categories. Each instance is hierarchically structured according to the scam lifecycle defined by the proposed scam kill chain, and further annotated at the turn level with scammer psychological actions and victim responses. We benchmark models on two tasks: real-time termination prediction, which estimates whether a conversation is approaching the termination stage, and scammer action prediction, which forecasts the scammer's subsequent actions. Results show a clear gap between surface-level fluency and progression modeling: supervised encoders substantially outperform zero-shot LLMs on real-time termination prediction, while next-action prediction remains only moderately successful even for strong LLMs. Taken together, these results show that current models can capture some scam-related cues, yet still struggle to track how risk escalates and how manipulation unfolds across turns.

</details>


### [60] [What makes a word hard to learn? Modeling L1 influence on English vocabulary difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12281)
*Jonas Mayer Martins,Zhuojing Huang,Aaricia Herygers,Lisa Beinborn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究利用机器学习评估不同母语（西、德、中）在英语词汇学习中的难度，发现学习难度受“词汇熟悉度”与“跨语言迁移能力”的共同制约，且母语特征显著影响该迁移机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究词语学习难度及其背后的语言学根源，并分析学习者母语（西班牙语、德语或中文）如何影响这种难度差异。

Method: 采用基于梯度的提升树模型（Gradient-boosted models），结合词汇熟悉度（如频率）、语义、表层形式及跨语言迁移等多维特征进行训练，并使用Shapley值量化特征重要性。

Result: 词汇熟悉度在所有语言中均为核心预测因子；但对于西语和德语学习者，正字法迁移起显著作用，而中文学习者仅受熟悉度与表层特征影响。

Conclusion: 模型展示了词汇难度预测的L1差异性，为个性化课程设计提供了可解释的工具。跨语言迁移（或其缺失）是决定学习难度的核心因素。

Abstract: What makes a word difficult to learn, and how does the difficulty depend on the learner's native language? We computationally model vocabulary difficulty for English learners whose first language is Spanish, German, or Chinese with gradient-boosted models trained on features related to a word's familiarity (e.g., frequency), meaning, surface form, and cross-linguistic transfer. Using Shapley values, we determine the importance of each feature group. Word familiarity is the dominant feature group shared by all three languages. However, predictions for Spanish- and German-speaking learners rely additionally on orthographic transfer. This transfer mechanism is unavailable to Chinese learners, whose difficulty is shaped by a combination of familiarity and surface features alone. Our models provide interpretable, L1-tailored difficulty estimates that can be used to design vocabulary curricula.

</details>


### [61] [TokenRatio: Principled Token-Level Preference Optimization via Ratio Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12288)
*Truong Nguyen,Tien-Phat Nguyen,Linh Ngo Van,Duy Minh Ho Nguyen,Khoa Doan,Trung Le*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的对齐方法 TBPO，通过将 token 级的偏好建模引入 Bregman 框架，克服了传统 DPO 序列级建模的局限性，实现了更细粒度且稳定的语言模型对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 DPO 等强化学习无关（RL-free）对齐方法将整条序列视为单位建模偏好，而忽略了语言模型生成过程本质上是基于每个 token 的自回归决策，导致偏好建模精度不足。

Method: 提出 Token-level Bregman Preference Optimization (TBPO)。该方法将序列级偏好分解为状态相关的 token 级 Bradley-Terry 模型，通过 Bregman 散度密度比匹配进行优化。包含两种实现：TBPO-Q（引入轻量级状态基线）和 TBPO-A（通过优势归一化消除基线）。

Result: 在指令跟随、有用性/无害性和摘要生成等多个基准测试中，TBPO 相比标准的 DPO 及其他 token 级基线方法，在对齐质量、训练稳定性和模型输出多样性方面均表现出显著提升。

Conclusion: TBPO 通过建模 token 级的 Bradley-Terry 偏好，解决了 DPO 无法处理序列执行中细粒度 token 决策的问题。实验证明 TBPO 在各种基准测试中均优于传统的序列级和现有的 token 级对齐方法，且具有更高的训练稳定性和输出多样性。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) is a widely used RL-free method for aligning language models from pairwise preferences, but it models preferences over full sequences even though generation is driven by per-token decisions. Existing token-level extensions typically decompose a sequence-level Bradley-Terry objective across timesteps, leaving per-prefix (state-wise) optimality implicit. We study how to recover token-level preference optimality using only standard sequence-level pairwise comparisons. We introduce Token-level Bregman Preference Optimization (TBPO), which posits a token-level Bradley-Terry preference model over next-token actions conditioned on the prefix, and derive a Bregman-divergence density-ratio matching objective that generalizes the logistic/DPO loss while preserving the optimal policy induced by the token-level model and maintaining DPO-like simplicity. We introduce two instantiations: TBPO-Q, which explicitly learns a lightweight state baseline, and TBPO-A, which removes the baseline through advantage normalization. Across instruction following, helpfulness/harmlessness, and summarization benchmarks, TBPO improves alignment quality and training stability and increases output diversity relative to strong sequence-level and token-level baselines.

</details>


### [62] [Output Composability of QLoRA PEFT Modules for Plug-and-Play Attribute-Controlled Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12345)
*Michela Lorandi,Anya Belz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了PEFT模块的泛化与组合方法，发现通过对不同任务训练的多个PEFT模块进行“输出求和”，不仅能高效处理多任务组合，还能在性能上超越单任务微调模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的PEFT技术虽能降低微调成本，但每个新任务仍需进行独立的微调，导致资源消耗和存储压力随任务增加而线性增长。作者旨在探索通用的泛化方法，使PEFT模块能够通过组合处理多种任务，摆脱单任务限制。

Method: 1. 多任务联合训练：在相关数据集组合上进行训练；2. 权重矩阵组合：对单独训练的PEFT模块权重进行线性组合；3. 输出组合：对单独训练的PEFT模块输出进行直接求和。通过在三种LLM和QLoRA基础上进行实验评估。

Result: 输出求和（Summing outputs）方法表现最强，在所有模型和任务上均优于或等同于其他组合方式。在情感控制任务中，三模块输出组合甚至在单任务数据集上实现了平均2个百分点的性能提升。

Conclusion: 提出了一种有效的PEFT模块组合方法——输出求和（Summing PEFT module outputs）。该方法在多任务场景下，甚至在单任务测试中，均能表现出卓越的性能，证明了在无需重新训练的前提下通过组合现有模块实现零样本或少样本任务迁移的可行性。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) techniques offer task-specific fine-tuning at a fraction of the cost of full fine-tuning, but require separate fine-tuning for every new task (combination). In this paper, we explore three ways of generalising beyond single-task training/inference: (i) training on combinations of multiple, related datasets; (ii) at inference, composing the weight matrices of separately trained PEFT modules; and (iii) at inference, composing the outputs of separately trained PEFT modules. We test these approaches on three different LLMs, QLoRA as the PEFT technique, and three sets of controlled text generation datasets for sentiment control, topic control, and multi-attribute control. We find that summing PEFT module outputs is a particularly strong composition method, which consistently either outperforms or matches the performance of alternative approaches. This is the case even when comparing against single-task specialised modules on the single-task test set, where three-module output composition achieves an average 2% point performance increase across all models for sentiment control.

</details>


### [63] [Pretraining Exposure Explains Popularity Judgments in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12382)
*Jamshid Mozafari,Bhawna Piryani,Adam Jatowt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究通过分析OLMo和Dolma语料发现，大模型的流行度偏差主要由预训练数据中的频率分布决定，而非外部世界的真实流行度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM存在明显的流行度偏差，但此前研究受限于预训练语料不可见，难以确定这种偏差是源于真实世界流行度，还是源于训练数据中的统计暴露分布。本研究旨在首次基于完全可观察的语料库验证这一机制。

Method: 利用开源的OLMo模型及其完整的预训练语料库Dolma，对7.4万亿token中的2000个实体进行了精确的暴露统计分析，并将其实验结果与维基百科浏览量及模型诱导出的流行度信号（标量估计和成对比较）进行对比。

Result: 1. 预训练暴露与维基百科流行度高度相关；2. LLM的流行度判断（特别是成对比较）与预训练暴露的相关性高于与维基百科的相关性；3. 模型越大，这种一致性越强；4. 在维基百科数据不可靠的长尾区域，LLM依然强烈依赖预训练暴露。

Conclusion: 大语言模型中的“流行度偏差”主要源于预训练数据中的统计暴露分布，而非外部世界的流行度（如维基百科页面浏览量）。训练数据中对实体的暴露频率是模型形成流行度先验的核心驱动因素。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit systematic preferences for well-known entities, a phenomenon often attributed to popularity bias. However, the extent to which these preferences reflect real-world popularity versus statistical exposure during pretraining remains unclear, largely due to the inaccessibility of most training corpora. We provide the first direct, large-scale analysis of popularity bias grounded in fully observable pretraining data. Leveraging the open OLMo models and their complete pretraining corpus, Dolma, we compute precise entity-level exposure statistics across 7.4 trillion tokens. We analyze 2,000 entities spanning five types (Person, Location, Organization, Art, Product) and compare pretraining exposure against Wikipedia pageviews and two elicited LLM popularity signals: direct scalar estimation and pairwise comparison. Our results show that pretraining exposure strongly correlates with Wikipedia popularity, validating exposure as a meaningful proxy for real-world salience during the training period. More importantly, we find that LLM popularity judgments align more closely with exposure than with Wikipedia, especially when elicited via pairwise comparisons. This alignment is strongest for larger models and persists in the long tail, where Wikipedia popularity becomes unreliable. Overall, our findings demonstrate that popularity priors in LLMs are primarily shaped by pretraining statistics rather than external popularity signals, offering concrete evidence that data exposure plays a central role in driving popularity bias.

</details>

### [64] [Sieve: Dynamic Expert-Aware PIM Acceleration for Evolving Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11277)
*Jungwoo Kim,Rubens Lacouture,Genghan Zhang,Gina Sohn,Qizheng Zhang,Swapnil Gandhi,Christos Kozyrakis,Kunle Olukotun*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 为解决 MoE 模型中不均匀的专家负载与 PIM 系统的不匹配问题，提出了一种名为 Sieve 的运行时调度框架，通过动态划分专家计算任务并优化计算与通信重叠，显著提升了多 GPU HBM-PIM 系统下的 MoE 推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 MoE 模型中 token-to-expert 分布呈现明显的双峰特性，导致现有 PIM 系统中负载不均衡，无法有效利用多 GPU+HBM-PIM 架构的算力。

Method: 提出了一种针对 MoE 模型调度的框架 Sieve。该方法基于运行时 token 分布，动态划分 GPU 和 PIM 之间的专家执行任务，并联合考虑互联开销、内存带宽、GPU 和 PIM 的吞吐量，实现计算与通信的重叠。

Result: 在 Qwen3.5-397B-A17B、GPT-OSS-120B 和 Qwen3-30B-A3B 等模型上，相比现有最优的 MoE PIM 系统，Sieve 的吞吐量和交互性分别提升了 1.3 倍、1.3 倍和 1.6 倍。

Conclusion: Sieve 框架能够有效应对现代 MoE 模型中日益严重的 token 分布双峰性问题，通过综合考量计算、通信和内存性能，在多 GPU 和 HBM-PIM 系统上实现了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) has become a dominant architecture for scaling large language models (LLMs). However, the execution characteristics of MoE inference are changing rapidly and increasingly mismatch the assumptions underlying existing Processing-in-Memory (PIM) systems. Prior PIM systems for LLMs rely on static rules to offload memory-bound operations to PIM, without accounting for the combined effects of load imbalance and inter-GPU communication. Meanwhile, modern MoE models activate fewer experts out of increasingly many, creating a bimodal expert distribution: a small set of experts receives many tokens, while a long tail of experts receives only one or a few.
  We identify a trend in modern MoE models toward increasingly bimodal token-to-expert distributions, quantify the resulting disparity in arithmetic intensity across experts, and show that this disparity dramatically reduces the efficiency of state-of-the-art PIM systems for LLMs. To address this problem, we propose a scheduler for serving MoE models on multi-GPU systems with attached HBM-PIM stacks. Our scheduler partitions expert execution between GPU and PIM based on runtime token-to-expert distributions, while jointly considering interconnect overhead, memory bandwidth, GPU throughput, and PIM throughput. Moreover, we propose Sieve, a runtime framework that employs the scheduler to coordinate execution across GPUs and their attached HBM-PIM stacks. Sieve overlaps GPU computation, PIM computation, and intra- and inter-device communication while preserving cross-device dependencies induced by expert parallelism. Sieve is evaluated on our cycle-accurate simulator based on Ramulator 2.0. Compared to state-of-the-art PIM systems for MoE, Sieve improves both throughput and interactivity by 1.3x, 1.3x, and 1.6x on Qwen3.5-397B-A17B, GPT-OSS-120B, and Qwen3-30B-A3B, respectively.

</details>


### [65] [Enhancing Instruction Prefetching via Cache and TLB Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12433)
*Alexandre Valentin Jamet,Georgios Vavouliotis,Marti Torrents,Dimitrios Chasapis,Marc Casas*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 针对 L1I 预取中翻译延迟和预取行重用异构的问题，IP-CaT 通过联合优化 TLB 和缓存置换机制，实现了服务器工作负载指令预取性能的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代服务器工作负载指令足迹巨大，L1I 预取至关重要，但现有预取器面临两大瓶颈：跨页翻译带来的时延限制，以及预取指令行重用行为的高度异构性导致缓存利用率低下。

Method: 提出了 IP-CaT（Instruction Prefetch-Centric Cache and TLB Management）框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) tPB（translation Prefetch Buffer）：置于二级 TLB 处，缓存跨页预取的页表项以降低翻译延迟；(2) TIPRP（Trimodal Instruction Prefetch Replacement Policy）：基于决策树的 L2 缓存置换策略，用于识别并处理缓存中预取指令行的高重用异构性。

Result: 在 105 个服务器工作负载上表现优异，IP-CaT+EPI 相比单纯使用 EPI 性能提升 8.7%。且在与先进的 TLB 预取及缓存置换策略（如 CHiRP、Emissary、SHiP++、Mockingjay）对比中均表现更佳。

Conclusion: IP-CaT 通过协同优化 TLB 和缓存管理，有效地解决了现代服务器工作负载在 L1I 预取中的翻译延迟和缓存污染问题，显著提升了指令预取的性能。

Abstract: Modern server workloads exhibit massive instruction footprints that heavily pressure the processor front-end, making L1 instruction (L1I) prefetching critical for sustaining performance. However, this paper shows that current L1I prefetchers fail to reach their full potential due to two key limitations. First, L1I prefetches crossing page boundaries require address translation before issuance, and translation latency reduces prefetch timeliness. Second, the reuse behavior of code lines fetched by L1I prefetches is highly heterogeneous: while some lines are reused many times, others are dead-on-arrival.
  This paper introduces Instruction Prefetch-Centric Cache and TLB Management (IP-CaT), the first microarchitectural framework jointly optimizing TLB and cache management for L1I prefetching. IP-CaT consists of two components: (i) the translation Prefetch Buffer (tPB), a small structure colocated with the second-level TLB (sTLB) that stores page table entries fetched by page-crossing L1I prefetches, reducing translation overheads; and (ii) the Trimodal Instruction Prefetch Replacement Policy (TIPRP), a decision-tree-based L2 cache replacement policy specialized for lines fetched by L1I prefetches.
  We evaluate IP-CaT with three state-of-the-art L1I prefetchers: EPI, FNL+MMA, and Barca. Across 105 contemporary server workloads, IP-CaT consistently improves performance. For example, IP-CaT+EPI achieves an 8.7% geomean speedup over EPI alone. We further show that IP-CaT outperforms state-of-the-art instruction TLB prefetching, advanced TLB replacement (CHiRP), and state-of-the-art code-aware, prefetch-aware, and general-purpose cache replacement policies, including Emissary, SHiP++, and Mockingjay.

</details>

### [66] [PROTECT-DB: Protecting Data using Replicated State Machines: Efficient Corruption Detection & Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11953)
*Anant Utgikar,S. Sudarshan*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于确定性PostgreSQL扩展的拜占庭容错（BFT）数据库系统，实现了高效的数据损坏检测与并发自动修复，为构建安全的数据库复制状态机提供了实用方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织机构对数据完整性需求日益增长，现有系统难以有效抵御威胁数据库状态的攻击；旨在解决在数据库场景下实现高效的拜占庭容错及自动化状态恢复的问题。

Method: 基于确定性扩展的PostgreSQL，结合拜占庭容错（BFT）复制状态机架构。事务被记录在共享日志/区块链中，所有副本均进行确定性执行，以支持快速的数据损坏检测及并发修复。

Result: 性能评估表明，该系统在保证数据安全的同时，实现了高效的损坏检测与并发修复机制，验证了其在实际数据库应用中的实用性和高效性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了将拜占庭容错（BFT）复制状态机技术应用于数据库是切实可行的，为实现高可用、可验证且具备自愈能力的数据库系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Data is critical for the operation of any organization and needs to be protected, especially against attacks that compromise the state of the database. In this paper, we explore an approach based on Byzantine-fault tolerant replicated state machines, built on top of a deterministic extension of PostgreSQL. Each replica deterministically executes transactions recorded in a shared log/blockchain. Our focus is on creating a practical system that is designed for efficient and quick detection of corruption, as well as quick repair concurrent with execution of transactions. We also present a performance study showing the efficiency and practicality of our approach. We believe our work lays the foundations for the practical use of the BFT replicated state machine approach in the context of databases.

</details>


### [67] [Data-aware candidate selection in NL2SQL translation via small separating instances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12319)
*Stanislav Kikot,Alexander Shulgin,Yanwei Xu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SISelection的NL2SQL候选选择方法，通过区分实例和来源，在低候选规模和缺失一致性指标的情境下实现了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决NL2SQL翻译中，当候选集较小（如仅2-3个）且无法利用一致性评分（consistency score）进行筛选时的准确率优化问题。

Method: 提出了一种数据感知的候选选择方法（data-aware candidate selection），核心机制是分离实例（instances）与来源（provenance）。

Result: 在BIRD-DEV数据集的子集上进行的实验表明，该方法显著优于三种自然基线（baselines）。

Conclusion: 通过分离实例和来源（instances and provenance）的候选选择方法，能有效提升NL2SQL在有限候选条件下的翻译质量，特别是在缺乏一致性得分的情况下。

Abstract: We propose a data-aware candidate selection method for NL2SQL translation based on separating instances and provenance. We implement this approach and evaluate it against three natural baselines on a subset of BIRD-DEV. Experiments show that our method significantly outperforms baselines when only two or three candidates are given and no consistency score is available. The code of our prototype can be found at https://github.com/staskikotx/SISelection

</details>

### [68] [Improving the Performance and Learning Stability of Parallelizable RNNs Designed for Ultra-Low Power Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11855)
*Julien Brandoit,Arthur Fyon,Damien Ernst,Guillaume Drion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了累积内存递归单元（CMRU），通过改进梯度流动机制，解决了传统低功耗BMRU在复杂任务上的性能瓶颈，使其在保持硬件模拟电路友好性的同时，具备了与主流RNN相媲美的长程依赖处理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前序列学习中，Transformer和状态空间模型在处理长程依赖时功耗较高。尽管BMRU支持低功耗硬件实现，但在复杂任务上性能不足。研究旨在在保持BMRU硬件友好性的前提下，提升其处理复杂序列任务的能力。

Method: 针对BMRU存在的梯度阻塞限制，提出了一种累积更新公式（cumulative update formulation），通过在时间维度上创建跳跃连接（skip-connections）来改善梯度流，并在此基础上衍生出CMRU和αCMRU两种架构。

Result: 实验表明，CMRU/αCMRU显著提高了收敛稳定性并降低了对初始化权重的敏感度；在小模型尺寸下，其性能匹配或优于LRU和minGRU，特别是在需要离散长程记忆的任务中表现优异。

Conclusion: 提出的CMRU及其变体αCMRU有效解决了BMRU中的梯度阻塞问题，在保持硬件友好的持久化存储与量化特性的同时，实现了与LRU和minGRU相当甚至更优的性能，证明了其在低功耗模拟计算场景下的应用潜力。

Abstract: Sequence learning is dominated by Transformers and parallelizable recurrent neural networks (RNNs) such as state-space models, yet learning long-term dependencies remains challenging, and state-of-the-art designs trade power consumption for performance. The Bistable Memory Recurrent Unit (BMRU) was introduced to enable hardware-software co-design of ultra-low power RNNs: quantized states with hysteresis provide persistent memory while mapping directly to analog primitives. However, BMRU performance lags behind parallelizable RNNs on complex sequential tasks. In this paper, we identify gradient blocking during state updates as a key limitation and propose a cumulative update formulation that restores gradient flow while preserving persistent memory, creating skip-connections through time. This leads to the Cumulative Memory Recurrent Unit (CMRU) and its relaxed variant, the $α$CMRU. Experiments show that the cumulative formulation dramatically improves convergence stability and reduces initialization sensitivity. The CMRU and $α$CMRU match or outperform Linear Recurrent Units (LRUs) and minimal Gated Recurrent Units (minGRUs) across diverse benchmarks at small model sizes, with particular advantages on tasks requiring discrete long-range retention, while the CMRU retains quantized states, persistent memory, and noise-resilient dynamics essential for analog implementation.

</details>


### [69] [Search Your Block Floating Point Scales!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12464)
*Tanmaey Gupta,Hayden Prairie,Xiaoxia Wu,Reyna Abhyankar,Qingyang Wu,Austin Silveria,Pragaash Ponnusamy,Jue Wang,Ben Athiwaratkun,Leon Song,Tri Dao,Daniel Y. Fu,Chris De Sa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于细粒度搜索的缩放因子优化策略 ScaleSearch，通过改进块浮点（BFP）的缩放选择，显著降低了生成式模型的量化误差并提升了推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的块浮点（BFP）量化算法通常采用基于块内最大幅值的固定缩放策略，这种方法在最小化量化误差方面往往不是最优的。

Method: 提出了一种名为 ScaleSearch 的缩放因子选择策略，利用微缩放格式中的尾数位进行细粒度搜索以最小化量化误差；并将该方法应用于注意力机制，开发了 ScaleSearchAttention 算法。

Result: ScaleSearch 将 NVFP4 的量化误差降低了 27%；在 Qwen3-8B 模型上将 MATH500 的 PTQ 性能提升了 15 个点；ScaleSearchAttention 将 Llama 3.1 70B 在 Wikitext-2 上的困惑度（PPL）改善了 0.77 点。

Conclusion: ScaleSearch 证明了通过在块浮点（BFP）格式中进行细粒度的缩放因子优化，可以显著降低量化误差并提升生成式模型在低位宽（如 NVFP4）下的性能，同时通过 ScaleSearchAttention 实现了近乎无损的模型推理。

Abstract: Quantization has emerged as a standard technique for accelerating inference for generative models by enabling faster low-precision computations and reduced memory transfers. Recently, GPU accelerators have added first-class support for microscaling Block Floating Point (BFP) formats. Standard BFP algorithms use a fixed scale based on the maximum magnitude of the block. We observe that this scale choice can be suboptimal with respect to quantization errors. In this work, we propose ScaleSearch, an alternative strategy for selecting these scale factors: using a fine-grained search leveraging the mantissa bits in microscaling formats to minimize the quantization error for the given distribution. ScaleSearch can be integrated with existing quantization methods such as Post Training Quantization and low-precision attention, and is shown to improve their performance. Additionally, we introduce ScaleSearchAttention, an accelerated NVFP4-based attention algorithm, which uses ScaleSearch and adapted prior techniques to ensure near-0 performance loss for causal language modeling. Experiments show that ScaleSearch reduces quantization error by 27% for NVFP4 and improves language model PTQ by up to 15 points for MATH500 (Qwen3-8B), while ScaleSearchAttention improves Wikitext-2 PPL by upto 0.77 points for Llama 3.1 70B. The proposed methods closely match baseline performance while providing quantization accuracy improvements.

</details>


### [70] [Enabling Performant and Flexible Model-Internal Observability for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11093)
*Nengneng Yu,Sixian Xiong,Yibo Zhao,Wei Wang,Zaoxing Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DMI-Lib 是一个高效率的深度模型监控库，通过软硬件解耦与高效内存缓冲机制，在极小性能损耗的前提下，实现了对模型内部状态的低延迟访问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前推理工作负载越来越依赖于对模型内部状态的及时访问，但现有的观测手段会对推理性能造成过大干扰。需要在保证推理优化和受限内存预算前提下，实现高效的内部状态监控。

Method: 提出了DMI-Lib库，利用异步可观测性基底（asynchronous observability substrate）将监控逻辑与推理热路径解耦；基于Ring^2内存抽象技术在GPU和CPU间 staging 张量；并引入策略控制的后端用于导出数据。

Result: 在离线批处理推理中仅引入0.4%–6.8%的开销，在线服务中平均开销仅为6%，相比同类方案，延迟开销降低了2至15倍。

Conclusion: DMI-Lib 通过将模型内部可观测性作为系统原语，成功实现了低开销、高性能的深度模型实时状态监控，解决了个性化推理任务对内部状态访问的需求。

Abstract: Today's inference-time workloads increasingly depend on timely access to a model's internal states. We present DMI-Lib, a high-speed deep model inspector that treats internal observability as a first-class systems primitive, decoupling it from the inference hot path via an asynchronous observability substrate built from Ring^2, a GPU-CPU memory abstraction for capturing and staging tensors, and a policy-controlled host backend that exports them. DMI-Lib enables the placement of observation points across a rich space of internal signals and diverse inference backends while preserving serving optimizations and adhering to tight GPU memory budgets. Our experiments demonstrate that DMI-Lib incurs only 0.4%--6.8% overhead in offline batch inference and an average of 6% in moderate online serving, reducing latency overhead by 2x-15x compared to existing baselines with similar observability features. DMI-Lib is open-sourced at https://github.com/ProjectDMX/DMI.

</details>


### [71] [Steering Without Breaking: Mechanistically Informed Interventions for Discrete Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10971)
*Hanhan Zhou,Shamik Roy,Rashmi Gangadharaiah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对离散扩散语言模型(DLMs)的属性控制，本文提出了一种自适应调度算法。研究发现属性生成具有特定的时间规律，而传统的均匀干预会导致质量下降。新方法根据属性形成的动态规律优化干预时机，大幅提升了多属性联合控制的精准度与生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离散扩散语言模型(DLMs)受控生成大多沿用自回归模型的“均匀干预”策略，这种方法不仅会导致生成质量下降，且在多属性联合控制时性能恶化严重，原因是该方法忽略了不同属性在去噪过程中独特的形成规律。

Method: 1. 利用稀疏自动编码器(Sparse Autoencoders)深入分析了多个DLMs中属性生成的动态过程（时间、尖锐度、幅度）；2. 提出了“自适应调度器(Adaptive Scheduler)”，仅在属性形成的活跃阶段进行干预；3. 推导了描述自适应与均匀调度权衡关系的闭式分析方法（基于承诺分布的离散统计量）。

Result: 在包含124M至8B参数的四种模型及七项控制任务中，自适应调度器表现优异；在挑战性的三属性联合控制任务中，达到了高达93%的控制强度，比最佳基线方法提升了15个百分点，且高质量保持了生成效果。

Conclusion: 针对离散扩散语言模型(DLMs)，自适应调度算法通过利用属性生成的动态特性，能够在实现精准属性控制的同时避免模型生成质量的下降，显著优于传统的均匀干预方法。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion language models (DLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising all positions in parallel, offering an alternative to autoregressive models. Controlled generation methods for DLMs, imported from autoregressive models, apply uniform intervention at every denoising steps. We show this uniform schedule degrades quality, and the damage compounds when multiple attributes are steered jointly. To diagnose the failure, we train sparse autoencoders on four DLMs (124M-8B parameters) and find that different attributes commit on distinct schedules, varying in timing, sharpness, and magnitude. For instance, topic commits within the first 2\% of denoising, whereas sentiment emerges gradually over 20\% of the process. Consequently, uniform intervention wastes steering capacity on steps where the target attribute has already solidified or has yet to emerge. We propose a novel adaptive scheduler that concentrates interventions on the steps where an attribute is actively forming and leaves the rest of generation untouched. The cost-control trade-off admits a closed-form characterization: the advantage of adaptive over uniform scheduling is governed by a single dispersion statistic of the commitment distribution. Across four DLMs and seven steering tasks, our method achieves precise control without the degradation typical of uniform interventions. Especially on challenging simultaneous three-attribute control, it reaches up to 93\% steering strength, beating the strongest baseline by up to 15\% points while preserving generation quality.

</details>


### [72] [Rotation-Preserving Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10973)
*Hangzhan Jin,Tianwei Ni,Lu Li,Pierre-Luc Bacon,Mohammad Hamdaqa,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RPSFT 提出了一种高效的参数约束方法，通过限制预训练权重奇异子空间的旋转变化，解决了大型语言模型在微调过程中产生域外泛化退化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督微调（SFT）虽然能增强领域内性能，但往往会导致域外（OOD）泛化能力下降。研究指出这种退化与预训练权重矩阵主奇异子空间的变化有关，但现有的基于 Hessian 或 Fisher 信息的限制方法在大型语言模型规模上计算成本过高。

Method: 提出了旋光保留监督微调（RPSFT）方法。该方法通过惩罚权重矩阵中预训练前 k 个奇异向量块的投影旋转变化，作为 Hessian 或 Fisher 信息计算的计算高效替代方案，从而在微调时保留预训练的表示能力。

Result: 在多种模型架构和规模的数学推理任务测试中，RPSFT 在领域内性能与 OOD 泛化能力之间取得了更好的平衡，优于标准的 SFT 和其他强基准方法，同时实现了对预训练表示的更好保留。

Conclusion: RPSFT 是一种有效的方法，通过在监督微调（SFT）过程中限制预训练权重矩阵主要奇异子空间的过度旋转，成功在保持任务适应性的同时，提升了模型在大语言模型（LLM）规模下的域外（OOD）泛化能力，并为后续的强化学习微调提供了更优的初始化基础。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) improves in-domain performance but can degrade out-of-domain (OOD) generalization. Prior work suggests that this degradation is related to changes in dominant singular subspaces of pretrained weight matrices. However, directly identifying loss-sensitive directions with Hessian or Fisher information is computationally expensive at LLM scale. In this work, we propose preserving projected rotations in pretrained singular subspaces as an efficient proxy for Fisher-sensitive directions, which we call Rotation-Preserving Supervised Fine-Tuning (RPSFT). RPSFT penalizes changes in the projected top-$k$ singular-vector block of each pretrained weight matrix, limiting unnecessary rotation while preserving task adaptation. Across model families and sizes trained on math reasoning data, RPSFT improves the in-domain/OOD trade-off over standard SFT and strong SFT baselines, better preserves pretrained representations, and provides stronger initializations for downstream RL fine-tuning. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/jinhangzhan/RPSFT.git}{https://github.com/jinhangzhan/RPSFT}.

</details>


### [73] [Vertex-Softmax: Tight Transformer Verification via Exact Softmax Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10974)
*Navid Rezazadeh,Arash Gholami Davoodi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 Vertex-Softmax 的高效验证算子，通过理论证明在 Softmax 最优化问题中仅需考察线性数量的候选点，从而在形式化验证中实现了最优界限，在提升 Transformer 模型验证效果的同时大幅降低了计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对 Transformer 注意力的形式化验证方法在松弛（relax）Softmax 函数时过于独立，导致过多的近似误差（slack），降低了界限的紧致度（tightness）。

Method: 提出了 Vertex-Softmax 算子，证明了 Softmax 最优化问题在约束盒的顶点处取得。利用阈值结构定理，将搜索候选点从指数级降至线性级（对序列长度呈对数线性复杂度）；并将其集成到 CROWN 验证框架中。

Result: Vertex-Softmax 在 MNIST、Fashion-MNIST 和 CIFAR-10 上显著提高了认证鲁棒性（certified rates）和下界紧致度，同时在极低计算成本下超越了 alpha-CROWN 和分支定界（branch-and-bound）等基准方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入 Vertex-Softmax 原始算子，该研究实现了在得分区间约束下对 Softmax 函数的最紧致界限，在保持计算效率的同时，显著提升了 Transformer 注意力机制的验证性能，并为后续的鲁棒性验证提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Certified verification of transformer attention requires bounding the softmax function over interval constraints on the pre-softmax scores. Existing verifiers relax softmax ndependently of the downstream objective, leaving avoidable slack. We prove that the exact optimum of this score-box problem is attained at a vertex of the constraint box, and establish a threshold structure theorem showing that, after sorting the objective coefficients, the optimum lies among only linearly many candidates, yielding the Vertex-Softmax primitive with log-linear complexity in the sequence length. We further prove a formal optimality result showing that Vertex-Softmax is the tightest sound bound obtainable from score intervals alone, characterizing precisely what additional structure (score correlations, score-value coupling) is needed for further improvement. Integrated into a CROWN Convex Relaxation based Optimization for Worst-case Neurons)-style verifier with a formal soundness guarantee, Vertex-Softmax significantly improves certified rates and substantially tightens lower bounds across MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and CIFAR-10 attention models, while consistently matching or outperforming alpha-CROWN and branch-and-bound baselines at a fraction of their cost.

</details>


### [74] [FERMI: Exploiting Relations for Membership Inference Against Tabular Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11527)
*Abtin Mahyar,Masoumeh Shafieinejad,Yuhan Liu,Xi He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对扩散模型生成的表格数据，研究提出了 FERMI 攻击框架，通过有效利用多表关系间的辅助信息，显著增强了成员推理攻击在多表数据场景下的隐私风险探测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的隐私评估多集中在单表设置，忽视了现实中敏感数据广泛存在的“多表关系”结构。研究旨在解决如何利用多表辅助信息，更准确地评估基于扩散模型生成的合成表格数据的隐私泄露风险。

Method: 提出了一种名为 FERMI (FEature-mapping for Relational Membership Inference) 的方法。该方法在攻击训练阶段整合目标记录及其关联表（parent tables）的辅助信息，通过特征映射增强单表特征表达，从而在推理阶段仅利用目标表属性值即可实现更高效的成员推理攻击。

Result: 在三种表格扩散模型和三个真实关系数据集上的实验表明，FERMI 相比单表基线具有显著优势：在白盒设置下，TPR@0.1FPR 最高提升了 53%；在黑盒设置下提升了 22%。

Conclusion: 现有的单表成员推理攻击在处理具有多表关系的数据时存在显著局限。FERMI 框架通过引入特征映射机制，有效利用了表间的关联信息，显著提升了针对表格生成模型（特别是扩散模型）的隐私风险评估能力。

Abstract: Diffusion models are the leading approach for tabular data synthesis and are increasingly used to share sensitive records. Whether they actually protect privacy has become a pressing question. Membership inference attacks are the standard tool for this purpose, yet existing attacks assume a single-table setting and ignore the multi-relational structure of real sensitive data. A core challenge in assessing privacy risks from membership inference attacks in multi-table settings is how to leverage auxiliary information from relations associated with the target table, such as its parent tables. Particularly, we study a practical setting in which such auxiliary information is available only when training the attack model. At inference time, the attacker observes only the attribute values of the target record from the target table. We propose FERMI (FEature-mapping for Relational Membership Inference), which resolves this gap by enriching single-table features with relational membership signal. Across three tabular diffusion architectures and three real-world relational datasets, FERMI consistently improves attack performance over single-table baselines, with TPR@$0.1$FPR rising by up to 53% over the single-table baseline in the white-box setting and 22% in the black-box setting.

</details>


### [75] [EpiCastBench: Datasets and Benchmarks for Multivariate Epidemic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11598)
*Madhurima Panja,Danny D'Agostino,Huitao Li,Tanujit Chakraborty,Nan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 EpiCastBench，一个包含 40 个高质量多变量传染病数据集的大规模基准测试框架，通过统一的评估标准对 15 种主流预测模型进行了综合对比分析，旨在解决流行病预测领域缺乏评价标准的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
