Motivation: 现有的流行病预测研究缺乏高质量、覆盖多种传染病与地理区域的基准数据集，制约了多变量预测模型的发展与公平评估。

Method: 构建了包含 40 个多样化、具有相关性的多变量疫情数据集的基准；建立了包含统一预测视野、预处理流程和评估指标的标准化评估体系；对比了涵盖统计学模型、深度学习模型及基础模型在内的 15 种多变量预测方法。

Result: 发布了 EpiCastBench 数据集与代码库，并基于该框架对 15 种主流预测模型进行了综合评估与性能分析，通过多维度分析揭示了数据集的全局特征与结构模式。

Conclusion: EpiCastBench 为流行病预测研究提供了首个大规模、标准化且多样化的基准测试框架，填补了该领域高质量数据集和统一评估标准的空白，能够有效促进多变量预测模型的发展与比较。

Abstract: The increasing adoption of data-driven decision-making in public health has established epidemic forecasting as a critical area of research. Recent advances in multivariate forecasting models better capture complex temporal dependencies than conventional univariate approaches, which model individual series independently. Despite this potential, the development of robust epidemic forecasting methods is constrained by the lack of high-quality benchmarks comprising diverse multivariate datasets across infectious diseases and geographical regions. To address this gap, we present EpiCastBench, a large-scale benchmarking framework featuring 40 curated (correlated) multivariate epidemic datasets. These publicly available datasets span a wide range of infectious diseases and exhibit diverse characteristics in terms of temporal granularity, series length, and sparsity. We analyze these datasets to identify their global features and structural patterns. To ensure reproducibility and fair comparison, we establish standardized evaluation settings, including a unified forecasting horizon, consistent preprocessing pipelines, diverse performance metrics, and statistical significance testing. By leveraging this framework, we conduct a comprehensive evaluation of 15 multivariate forecasting models spanning statistical baselines to state-of-the-art deep learning and foundation models. All datasets and code are publicly available on Kaggle (https://www.kaggle.com/datasets/aimltsf/epicastbench) and GitHub (https://github.com/aimltsf/EpiCastBench).

</details>


### [76] [The tractability landscape of diffusion alignment: regularization, rewards, and computational primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11361)
*Ankur Moitra,Andrej Risteski,Dhruv Rohatgi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了扩散模型推理时奖励对齐的理论基础，指出对齐效果依赖于距离度量的选择，并提出了基于KL散度的指数倾斜法和基于Wasserstein距离的近端传输法，为不同需求提供了对应的算法原语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时奖励对齐依赖于对“原始模型与对齐模型距离”的选择，但缺乏统一标准。作者旨在探究哪些算法原语足以实现特定奖励类的对齐，并揭示距离选择与计算复杂度之间的关系。

Method: 1. 针对KL散度，利用线性指数倾斜（linear exponential tilts）作为实现奖励对齐的原语；
2. 针对Wasserstein距离，定义近端传输预言机（proximal transport oracle）作为实现原语。

Result: 1. 在KL散度下，证明了线性指数倾斜足以对齐各种凸低维奖励；
2. 在Wasserstein距离下，证明了近端传输预言机能够有效处理凹或低维Lipschitz奖励；
3. 阐明了距离度量选择直接决定了所需的计算原语及可处理的奖励类别。

Conclusion: 论文证明了推理时奖励对齐的效果取决于分布距离的选择（KL散度与Wasserstein距离），并为不同距离定义了相应的计算原语，从而明确了不同算法下的可计算奖励类。

Abstract: Inference-time reward alignment asks how to turn a pre-trained diffusion model with base law $p$ into a sampler that favors a reward $r$ while remaining close to $p$. Since there is no canonical distributional distance for this closeness constraint, different choices lead to different "reward-aligned" laws and, just as importantly, different algorithmic problems. We develop a primitive-based approach to reward alignment: rather than assuming arbitrary reward-aligned laws can be sampled, we ask which simple algorithmic primitives suffice to implement alignment for non-trivial reward classes. If closeness is measured in KL distance, the target law is $q(x) \propto p(x) \exp(λ^{-1}r(x))$. For this setting, we show that linear exponential tilts of the form $q(x)\propto p(x)\exp(\langle θ, x \rangle)$ -- which according to recent work [MRR26] can be efficiently sampled from -- are a sufficient primitive for aligning to a very broad class of convex low-dimensional rewards. If closeness is measured in Wasserstein distance, the corresponding primitive is a proximal transport oracle: given $x$, solve $\mbox{argmax}_y \{r(y)- λc(x,y)\}$. This oracle can be efficiently implemented for concave or low-dimensional Lipschitz rewards $r(x)=f(Ax)$. Together, these results illustrate that the choice of distribution distance for alignment affects the computational primitive and the tractable reward class.

</details>


### [77] [$ξ$-DPO: Direct Preference Optimization via Ratio Reward Margin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10981)
*Zhengyuan Fan,Zhonghua Wu,Yuxuan Du,Qun Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对SimPO中超参数调优困难的问题，本文提出了ξ-DPO，通过将偏好目标重构为比率奖励边际形式，实现了模型参数的自动推导与高效调优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SimPO作为无参考偏好优化的代表，其超参数β和γ在不同数据集上缺乏可解释性，导致协同调优困难。

Method: 1. 分析SimPO中超参数β（样本过滤控制）和γ（与奖励间隙结构相关）的耦合机制；2. 将原偏好目标重构为奖励间隙与最优边际之间的距离最小化；3. 提出比率奖励形式，通过数学转换抵消β的影响，引入具有解释力的边际参数ξ。

Result: 提出的ξ-DPO方法实现了参数的解耦，使得模型边际参数ξ可直接由数据集初始奖励间隙分布自动确定，避免了繁琐的试错式超参数搜索。

Conclusion: 通过将偏好目标重构为基于比率奖励边际（ξ-DPO）的形式，成功消除了超参数β的依赖，使得边际ξ具有明确的物理意义且可直接从原始数据分布中推导，从而解决了SimPO中超参数难以协同调优的问题。

Abstract: Reference-free preference optimization has emerged as an efficient alternative to reinforcement learning from human feedback, with Simple Preference Optimization(SimPO) demonstrating strong performance by eliminating the explicit reference model through a simple objective. However, the joint tuning of the hyperparameters $β$ and $γ$ in SimPO remains a central challenge. We argue that this difficulty arises because the margin formulation in SimPO is not easily interpretable across datasets with different reward gap structures. To better understand this issue, we conduct a comprehensive analysis of SimPO and find that $β$ implicitly controls sample filtering, while the effect of $γ$ depends on the reward gap structure of the dataset. Motivated by these observations, we propose $ξ$-DPO: Direct preference optimization via ratio reward margin. We first reformulate the preference objective through an equivalent transformation, changing the optimization target from maximizing the likelihood of reward gaps to minimizing the distance between reward gaps and optimal margins. Then, we redefine the reward in a ratio form between the chosen and rejected, which effectively cancels the effect of $β$ and yields a bounded and interpretable margin. This margin is called the ratio reward margin and is denoted by $ξ$. Unlike the margin $γ$ in SimPO, $ξ$ explicitly represents the desired relative separation between chosen and rejected responses and can be determined from the initial reward gap distribution, avoiding repeated trial-and-error tuning. ....

</details>


### [78] [TMPO: Trajectory Matching Policy Optimization for Diverse and Efficient Diffusion Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10983)
*Jiaming Li,Chenyu Zhu,Zhiyuan Ma,Nanxi Yi,Youjun Bao,Li Sun,Quanying Lv,Xiang Fang,Daizong Liu,Jianjun Li,Kun He,Bowen Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对扩散模型在强化学习对齐中易出现“奖励欺骗”导致模型崩溃的问题，本文提出TMPO算法，通过轨迹级分布匹配代替奖励最大化，在显著提升生成多样性的同时兼顾了奖励性能与训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习扩散模型对齐方法普遍存在“奖励欺骗（Reward Hacking）”问题，即通过追求期望奖励最大化导致视觉模式崩溃（Mode Collapse）和多样性丧失。研究旨在解决这种模式追求（Mode-seeking）倾向带来的性能劣化。

Method: 1. 提出 Trajectory Matching Policy Optimization (TMPO)，将奖励最大化转化为轨迹级奖励分布匹配；2. 引入 Softmax Trajectory Balance (Softmax-TB) 目标函数，利用前向 KL 散度的模式覆盖特性避免模型崩溃；3. 结合动态随机树采样（Dynamic Stochastic Tree Sampling），通过共享去噪前缀和动态分支策略，优化了大模型在多轨迹训练中的计算效率。

Result: 在人类偏好、组合生成和文本渲染等多种对齐任务中，TMPO 的生成多样性较现有最先进方法提升了 9.1%，并在所有指标及训练效率上均表现优异。

Conclusion: TMPO 通过引入 Softmax Trajectory Balance 和动态随机树采样，成功解决了扩散模型在 RL 对齐中存在的奖励欺骗问题，在保持高性能的同时显著提升了生成的多样性，实现了奖励与多样性之间的最优权衡。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has shown extraordinary potential in aligning diffusion models to downstream tasks, yet most of them still suffer from significant reward hacking, which degrades generative diversity and quality by inducing visual mode collapse and amplifying unreliable rewards. We identify the root cause as the mode-seeking nature of these methods, which maximize expected reward without effectively constraining probability distribution over acceptable trajectories, causing concentration on a few high-reward paths. In contrast, we propose Trajectory Matching Policy Optimization (TMPO), which replaces scalar reward maximization with trajectory-level reward distribution matching. Specifically, TMPO introduces a Softmax Trajectory Balance (Softmax-TB) objective to match the policy probabilities of K trajectories to a reward-induced Boltzmann distribution. We prove that this objective inherits the mode-covering property of forward KL divergence, preserving coverage over all acceptable trajectories while optimizing reward. To further reduce multi-trajectory training time on large-scale flow-matching models, TMPO incorporates Dynamic Stochastic Tree Sampling, where trajectories share denoising prefixes and branch at dynamically scheduled steps, reducing redundant computation while improving training effectiveness. Extensive results across diverse alignment tasks such as human preference, compositional generation and text rendering show that TMPO improves generative diversity over state-of-the-art methods by 9.1%, and achieves competitive performance in all downstream and efficiency metrics, attaining the optimal trade-off between reward and diversity.

</details>


### [79] [AESOP: Adversarial Execution-path Selection to Overload Deep Learning Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10987)
*Tingxi Li,Mingfang Ji,Ravishka Shemal Rathnasuriya,Simin Chen,Yitao Hu,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AESOP 是一种针对现代动态机器学习流水线的新型攻击框架。它通过精准控制输入以操纵路径，比现有的单模型攻击方法效率高出约 20 倍，能够有效引发推理流水线的性能崩溃或被迫导致系统性数据丢失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习部署常采用动态推理流水线，且受硬实时约束。现有攻击主要针对单一模型，未考虑流水线中组件协作带来的“计算量与调用路径耦合”的特性，导致攻击效率存在巨大提升空间。

Method: 提出了“对抗性路径选择问题”（Adversarial Path-Selection Problem）的概念。开发了 AESOP 框架，通过“漏洞引导的路径排序”（vulnerability-guided path ranking）和“自适应损失加权”（adaptive loss weighting）策略，专门针对模型调用路径进行攻击优化。

Result: 在白盒设置下，AESOP 可实现高达 2,407 倍的 FLOPs 膨胀和 419 倍的延迟膨胀；在灰盒设置下分别达到 58 倍和 17 倍。测试表明 AESOP 能够高效绕过或利用现有的生产级防御措施。

Conclusion: AESOP 证明了动态推理流水线在面对路径感知的攻击时具有极高的脆弱性。即使存在系统级防御，攻击者仍能迫使系统在吞吐量崩溃和数据丢失之间做出艰难权衡，从而实现高效的拒绝服务攻击。

Abstract: Modern machine learning deployments increasingly compose specialized models into dynamic inference pipelines, where upstream components produce intermediate predictions that determine the workload and inputs of downstream components. The cost of processing an input is therefore not determined by any single model, but by two coupled factors: the per-inference cost of each invoked component and its workload volume. Because these pipelines run under hard real-time constraints, efficiency is a fundamental requirement for system availability. We show that this structure creates an efficiency-attack surface that existing methods targeting single models cannot exploit: on identical inputs and budgets, path-aware targeting inflates FLOPs by $2,407\times$ while the strongest single-model baseline achieves $117\times$ -- a $20\times$ gap attributable entirely to where the attack is directed. We formalize this as the adversarial path-selection problem and present AESOP, a framework combining vulnerability-guided path ranking with adaptive loss weighting. We evaluate AESOP on five pipelines plus a production-realistic deployment variant with batching, bounded buffering, and confidence-threshold defenses. AESOP achieves up to $2,407\times$ FLOPs and $419\times$ latency inflation in white-box setting and 58$\times$ FLOPs / 17$\times$ latency in gray-box settings. Under system-level defenses, the attack is not neutralized but redirected: pipelines are forced to choose between throughput collapse ($0.578 \to 0.006$ input/s) and $96.7\%$ data loss to sustain throughput.

</details>


### [80] [Test-Time Personalization: A Diagnostic Framework and Probabilistic Fix for Scaling Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10991)
*Linhai Zhang,Yulan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了测试时个性化（TTP）框架，通过采样与个性化奖励模型选择实现推理性能优化。作者推导了描述扩展边界的统一定律，诊断了现有奖励模型的失效模式，并提出了一种概率奖励模型方案来确保性能随计算量有效扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM个性化主要集中在模型微调或提示工程上，忽略了推理阶段的计算扩展能力。研究旨在探讨如何通过推理时采样和个性化奖励模型选择，实现测试时性能的系统性提升。

Method: 1. 提出了Test-Time Personalization (TTP)框架：通过采样N个候选结果并利用个性化奖励模型进行选择来提升性能；
2. 推导了统一扩展定律（Unified Scaling Law）：将Best-of-N曲线分解为四个可测量量，识别出用户级崩溃和查询级奖励黑客行为两种失效模式；
3. 提出概率个性化奖励模型（Probabilistic Personalized Reward Model）：利用学习到的方差来缓解上述失效模式。

Result: 1. 证明了理论上采样N个候选结果时，预期效用随采样数量呈对数增长；
2. 实验验证了TTP框架在多种策略模型和任务中均能实现稳定的性能扩展；
3. 扩展定律准确拟合了不同奖励模型变体的观测曲线。

Conclusion: 该论文通过引入概率个性化奖励模型，成功克服了传统奖励模型在测试时扩展（Best-of-N）中的失效模式，证明了通过增加推理计算量（采样与选择策略）可以实现可预测的性能增益。

Abstract: Existing approaches to LLM personalization focus on constructing better personalized models or inputs, while treating inference as a single-shot process. In this work, we study Test-Time Personalization (TTP) along an unexplored axis: scaling inference-time computation by sampling N candidates from a personalized policy model and selecting the best with a personalized reward model. We prove that oracle selection yields expected utility growing logarithmically with the number of sampled candidates, establishing a theoretical ceiling for test-time scaling. However, standard reward models fail to realize this potential. To diagnose why, we derive a unified scaling law that decomposes any reward model's Best-of-N curve into four measurable quantities and reveals two failure modes, user-level collapse (near-constant prediction for some users) and query-level reward hacking (negative correlation with true quality for some queries). Guided by this law, we propose a probabilistic personalized reward model whose learned variance effectively mitigates both failure modes. Experiments confirm both elements of our framework: TTP delivers consistent scaling across multiple policy models and personalized text generation tasks, and our scaling law closely matches observed scaling curves across reward-model variants.

</details>


### [81] [Simpson's Paradox in Behavioral Curves: How Aggregation Distorts Parametric Models of User Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11017)
*Chao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 聚合行为数据会导致严重的“幸存者偏差”，表现为聚合曲线显著偏离个体真实行为（最高达 5 倍以上）。研究揭示了其机制并提出了“合成零点校准”方法，旨在修正各类依赖行为曲线建模应用中的系统性误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了揭示在推荐系统、广告和临床剂量等领域中，将个体行为建模直接应用到聚合数据时所产生的系统性失真问题，特别是探讨“聚合”行为是否会导致类似于辛普森悖论的偏差。

Method: 通过对 Goodreads、Amazon Electronics 和 MovieLens-25M 数据集进行分析，量化个体曲线与聚合曲线之间的参数差异；识别出幸存者偏差是导致该现象的根本原因；开发了“合成零点校准（Synthetic Null Calibration）”技术来优化个体行为参数的估计。

Result: 研究发现聚合产生的影响极其显著（如 Goodreads 上存在 3 倍偏差，Amazon 上存在 5.3 倍偏差）；确认了幸存者偏差是导致该失真的核心机制；所提出的校准方法有效降低了 32% 的个体分类误报率。

Conclusion: 数据聚合会导致严重的幸存者偏差，使得聚合出的行为曲线无法准确反映个体行为模式。研究提出使用“合成零点校准（Synthetic Null Calibration）”方法来缓解个体分类中的误报问题，并强调在处理存在差异化流失（differential attrition）的行为数据时，必须考虑这种系统性的结构偏差。

Abstract: Behavioral curve modeling -- fitting parametric functions to engagement-versus-exposure data -- is standard practice in recommendation, advertising, and clinical dosing. We show that aggregation introduces a systematic distortion: Simpson's paradox in behavioral curves. On Goodreads (3.3M users, 9 genres), individual users peak at n* approximately 11 exposures while the aggregate peaks at n* approximately 34 -- a 3x gap driven by survival bias. Amazon Electronics (18M reviews) shows a 5.3x distortion. MovieLens-25M (D approximately 1) serves as a negative control, confirming that survival bias -- not aggregation per se -- is the operative mechanism. The distortion is robust to category granularity, engagement operationalization, and classifier calibration. We develop Synthetic Null Calibration to address a 32% false positive rate in per-user classification. Our findings apply wherever individual behavioral parameters are estimated from aggregate curves under differential attrition.

</details>


### [82] [Muon is Not That Special: Random or Inverted Spectra Work Just as Well](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11181)
*Zakhar Shumaylov,Nathaël Da Costa,Peter Zaika,Bálint Mucsányi,Alex Massucco,Yoav Gelberg,Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb,Yarin Gal,Philipp Hennig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究推翻了“精确几何结构是优化器性能核心”的范式，通过实验证明 Muon 的成功主要归功于其对步长最优性的保证，而非复杂的几何几何建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对近期 Muon 优化器及其背后的非欧几里得几何、二阶优化方法的理论解释提出质疑，试图探究几何结构是否真是决定优化性能的关键因素。

Method: 1. 引入 Freon 优化器家族（基于 Schatten 准范数并采用 QDWH 迭代近似）；2. 提出 Kaon 优化器（使用随机噪声替代奇异值）；3. 在 GPT-2 等任务上进行对比实验，并分析对齐度和下降潜力。

Result: 1. Freon 的最优参数处于准范数区域，无法用单位不变 LMO 表示；2. 缺乏几何结构的 Kaon 能够达到与 Muon 相当的性能；3. 实验证实优化性能主要由对齐度和下降潜力控制。

Conclusion: 优化器的性能并不直接取决于精确的几何结构，而是由“对齐度（alignment）”和“下降潜力（descent potential）”这两个局部量决定。Muon 的成功核心在于如何保证步长的最优性，而非其对全局几何结构的拟合能力。

Abstract: The recent empirical success of the Muon optimizer has renewed interest in non-Euclidean optimization, typically justified by similarities with second-order methods, and linear minimization oracle (LMO) theory. In this paper, we challenge this geometric narrative through three contributions, demonstrating that precise geometric structure is not the key factor affecting optimization performance. First, we introduce Freon, a family of optimizers based on Schatten (quasi-)norms, powered by a novel, provably optimal QDWH-based iterative approximation. Freon naturally interpolates between SGD and Muon, while smoothly extrapolating into the quasi-norm regime. Empirically, the best-performing Schatten parameters for GPT-2 lie strictly within the quasi-norm regime, and thus cannot be represented by any unitarily invariant LMO. Second, noting that Freon performs well across a wide range of exponents, we introduce Kaon, an absurd optimizer that replaces singular values with random noise. Despite lacking any coherent geometric structure, Kaon matches Muon's performance and retains classical convergence guarantees, proving that strict adherence to a precise geometry is practically irrelevant. Third, having shown that geometry is not the primary driver of performance, we demonstrate it is instead controlled by two local quantities: alignment and descent potential. Ultimately, each optimizer must tune its step size around these two quantities. While their dynamics are difficult to predict a-priori, evaluating them within a stochastic random feature model yields a precise insight: Muon succeeds not by tracking an ideal global geometry, but by guaranteeing step-size optimality.

</details>


### [83] [RT-Transformer: The Transformer Block as a Spherical State Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11007)
*Peter Racioppo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过几何概率视角证明，Transformer 的各种核心机制是解决超球面几何估计问题的自然数学解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示 Transformer 架构各组件背后的理论基础，解释为什么这些特定的操作组合在一起能如此有效地进行深度学习，而非仅仅依赖经验性的架构设计。

Method: 将潜在状态建模为超球面上的方向，并在当前估计的切平面上定义噪声；通过在该几何框架下推导精度加权的方向推断过程，映射出 Transformer 的各个组件。

Result: 成功推导出注意力机制对应于证据聚合，残差连接对应于增量状态更新，归一化对应于将更新后的状态重投影（Retract）回超球面。

Conclusion: Transformer 的核心组件（注意力、残差连接、归一化）并非人为设计的架构选择，而是源于球面流形上的几何估计问题的数学必然结果。

Abstract: We show that the core components of the Transformer block -- attention, residual connections, and normalization -- arise naturally from a single geometric estimation problem. Modeling the latent state as a direction on the hypersphere, with noise defined in the tangent plane at the current estimate, yields a precision-weighted directional inference procedure in which attention aggregates evidence, residual connections implement incremental state updates, and normalization retracts the updated state back onto the hypersphere. Together, these components follow from the geometry of the estimation problem rather than being introduced as independent architectural choices.

</details>


### [84] [Extending Kernel Trick to Influence Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11239)
*Zhenhuan Sun,Shahrokh Valaee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种影响力函数（Influence Functions）的对偶表示法，在处理大规模模型时，通过将计算复杂度从参数规模转移至数据集规模，显著提升了计算效率，尽管其使用受限于线性化模型且受矩阵存储空间限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的影响函数计算复杂性与模型参数量成线性比例，当面对当前超大规模模型（Large Models）时，直接在参数空间计算变得高昂甚至不可行。作者旨在降低对模型参数量的依赖，提升大规模模型下影响度分析的计算效率。

Method: 通过推导影响函数的对偶表示（dual representation），将计算重心从模型参数空间（parameter space）转移至数据集空间。该方法利用模型训练过程中的线性化特性，通过处理与数据集规模和模型输出维度相关的矩阵来计算影响度。

Result: 理论与实验分析结果显示，该方法在参数规模较大且数据集相对较小时具有显著的计算效率优势。成功实现了对数据点删除后参数变化、模型输出变化以及损失变化的有效估计。

Conclusion: 所提出的对偶影响函数（dual representation of influence functions）是处理大规模模型时的一种高效替代方案。虽然受限于线性化模型（linearizable models）且存在计算矩阵存储开销，但在大数据集、模型参数量远超数据集规模的情境下，其计算复杂性呈现出显著优势。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a dual representation of the influence functions, whose computational complexity scales with dataset size rather than model size. Both analytically and experimentally, we show that this representation can be an efficient alternative to the original influence functions for estimating changes in parameters, model outputs and loss due to data point removal, when model size is large relative to dataset size, or when evaluating the original influence functions in parameter space is infeasible. The dual representation, however, is limited to linearizable models, which are models whose behavior can be approximated by their linearizations throughout training, and requires materializing a matrix, whose size grows with the product of model output dimension and dataset size.

</details>


### [85] [Localization Boosting for Growth Markets: Mitigating Cross-Locale Behavioral Bias in Learning-to-Rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11272)
*Suryaa Veerabathiran Seran,Ashwin Naresh Kumar,Tracy Holloway King,Jing Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对国际化推荐系统中因数据分布不均导致的区域偏差问题，本文提出了一种整合VLM语义反馈与区域感知增权的排序模型，在提升相关性的同时有效保障了本地内容的曝光。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adobe Express在国际化扩张中面临严重的市场供需偏差，即美国市场的海量行为数据导致LTR（学习排序）模型在非美地区过度推荐美国流行模板，压制了本地化内容的发现，降低了非美地区的排序质量。

Method: 提出了一种多目标学习框架：1. 使用VLM（视觉-语言模型）生成的相关性评分作为辅助监督信号，以解决仅靠点击反馈导致的语义特征丢失；2. 增加区域感知的加权机制（locale-aware boosting），在优化相关性的同时，显式调控不同区域的内容分发权重。

Result: 在五个国际站点进行测试，该模型显著提升了推荐内容的相关性，同时成功恢复了本地化内容的稳定可见性，证明了将模型暴露偏差与语义监督分离的重要性。

Conclusion: 在国际化推荐系统中，单纯依赖行为反馈会导致跨区域暴露偏差（exposure bias）。通过引入多目标优化框架——结合行为反馈指标、VLM语义相关性信号及区域感知加权，能够有效缓解该偏差，实现推荐相关性提升与本地内容可见性的平衡。

Abstract: Adobe Express is expanding internationally, but the US has a disproportionately large content supply and interaction volume. Learning-to-rank (LTR) models trained primarily on behavioral feedback inherit this imbalance: templates popular in US are over-served in non-US locales. This cross-locale exposure bias suppresses local content discoverability and degrades ranking quality in growth locales.
  We show that click-only training suppresses semantically informative localization features. Adding vision-language model (VLM) graded relevance labels as auxiliary supervision alongside clicks improves semantic alignment but does not preserve local content visibility. We propose a multi-objective framework combining behavioral supervision, VLM-derived relevance signals, and locale-aware boosting. Across five locales, the resulting model improves relevance while restoring stable localization, demonstrating the importance of disentangling exposure from semantic supervision.

</details>


### [86] [Couple to Control: Joint Initial Noise Design in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11311)
*Jing Jia,Liyue Shen,Guanyang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过打破传统扩散模型初始噪声的独立性假设，引入了一种通用的“噪声耦合”框架，利用精心设计的样本间相关性在不增加采样成本的情况下，显著提升了图像生成的多样性与可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的扩散模型采样通常假设初始噪声是独立同分布（i.i.d.）的，这种做法限制了对生成样本集合整体特性的直接控制，且在提升多样性等需求上往往需要额外的计算代价。作者旨在通过重新审视初始噪声的依赖结构，在不增加采样开销的情况下改善生成效果。

Method: 定义了一种用于扩散模型的一般性初始噪声设计框架，通过保持单样本的边缘高斯分布不变，人为设计样本间的联合分布（耦合）。具体包括：1. 提出“斥力高斯耦合”（Rep repulsive Gaussian coupling）策略以增强多样性；2. 使用“子空间耦合”（Subspace couplings）实现固定主体背景下的多变背景生成。

Result: 实验表明，“斥力高斯耦合”在SD1.5、SDXL和SD3模型上显著提高了生成画廊的多样性，同时保持了良好的文本对齐与图像质量，在无额外推理成本的条件下超越了现有的噪声优化基准。此外，子空间耦合在固定主体背景生成任务上也展现出优异的灵活性和效果。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于“耦合噪声”（Coupled Noise）的扩散模型图像生成通用框架。通过设计多样本间的初始噪声依赖结构，在不增加推理成本的前提下，实现了对生成多样性、背景控制等方面的有效提升和灵活调控。

Abstract: Diffusion models typically generate image batches from independent Gaussian initial noises. We argue that this independence assumption is only one choice within a broader class of valid joint noise designs. Instead, one can specify a coupling of the initial noises: each noise remains marginally standard Gaussian, so the pretrained diffusion model receives the same single-sample input distribution, while the dependence across samples is chosen by design. This reframes initial-noise control from selecting or optimizing individual seeds to designing the dependence structure of a multi-sample gallery. This view gives a general framework for initial-noise design, covering several existing methods as special cases and leading naturally to new coupled-noise constructions. Coupled noise can improve generation on its own without adding sampling cost, and it is flexible enough to serve as a structured initialization for optimization-based pipelines when additional computation is available. Empirically, repulsive Gaussian coupling improves gallery diversity on SD1.5, SDXL, and SD3 while largely preserving prompt alignment and image quality. It matches or outperforms recent test-time noise-optimization baselines on several diversity metrics at the same sampling cost as independent generation. Subspace couplings also support fixed-object background generation, producing diverse, natural backgrounds compared with specialized inpainting baselines, with a tunable trade-off in foreground fidelity.

</details>


### [87] [ACSAC: Adaptive Chunk Size Actor-Critic with Causal Transformer Q-Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11009)
*Qian Chen,Junqiao Zhao,Hongtu Zhou,Hang Yu,Yanping Zhao,Chen Ye,Guang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为解决强化学习中长程任务的动作分块局限性，本文提出了 ACSAC，利用因果 Transformer 自适应选择动作分块长度，从而在不进行任务特定调优的情况下，实现了更好的长程任务决策与执行效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决深度强化学习在长程、稀疏奖励任务中，单步 TD 学习导致的引导误差累积问题。现有的固定动作分块（Action Chunking）方法无法在动作的可塑性（Reactivity）与时间一致性（Temporal Consistency）之间进行自适应平衡，且通常需要针对特定任务进行超参数调整。

Method: 提出 Adaptive Chunk Size Actor-Critic (ACSAC) 框架：1. 使用因果 Transformer (Causal Transformer) 作为 Critic，评估不同长度动作分块的期望回报；2. 在每个分块边界，根据状态动态选择最优分块大小；3. 理论证明了 ACSAC Bellman 算子是压缩映射，并存在唯一不动点。

Result: 在 OGBench 数据集上的实验结果表明，ACSAC 在离线强化学习（Offline RL）和离线转在线（Offline-to-online RL）设置下均实现了长程稀疏奖励操作任务的 SOTA 性能。

Conclusion: ACSAC 通过引入因果 Transformer 评估不同分块大小的期望回报，实现了根据环境状态动态调整动作分块长度（Action Chunking），有效解决了长程稀疏奖励任务中固定分块大小导致的反应性与时间一致性难以平衡的问题，并在多个基准测试中达到了 SOTA 性能。

Abstract: Long-horizon, sparse-reward tasks pose a fundamental challenge for reinforcement learning, since single-step TD learning suffers from bootstrapping error accumulation across successive Bellman updates. Actor-critic methods with action chunking address this by operating over temporally extended actions, which reduce the effective horizon, enable fast value backups, and support temporally consistent exploration. However, existing methods rely on a fixed chunk size and therefore cannot adaptively balance reactivity against temporal consistency. A large fixed chunk size reduces responsiveness to new observations, while a small one produces incoherent motions, forcing task-specific tuning of the chunk size. To address this limitation, we propose Adaptive Chunk Size Actor-Critic (ACSAC). ACSAC leverages a causal Transformer critic to evaluate expected returns for action chunks of different sizes. At each chunk boundary, it adaptively selects the chunk size that maximizes the expected return, supporting flexible, state-dependent chunk sizes without task-specific tuning. We prove that the ACSAC Bellman operator is a contraction whose unique fixed point is the action-value function of the adaptive policy. Experiments on OGBench demonstrate that ACSAC achieves state-of-the-art performance on long-horizon, sparse-reward manipulation tasks across both offline RL and offline-to-online RL settings.

</details>


### [88] [$\varepsilon$-Good Action Identification in Fixed-Budget Monte Carlo Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11324)
*Yinan Li,Tuan Nguyen,Kwang-Sung Jun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度-2 max-min 树中的固定预算动作识别问题，提出了一种不需要预设 $\varepsilon$ 的算法，并证明了其在识别 $\varepsilon$-好子树时的错误概率界，填补了该领域固定预算算法保证的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）的重要特例——深度-2 max-min 树中，解决固定预算下的最大-最小动作识别问题。研究重点在于 $\varepsilon$-好子树的识别，旨在改进近似规划的性能。

Method: 提出了一种 $\varepsilon$-不可知（$\varepsilon$-agnostic）算法，该算法无需预先输入 $\varepsilon$ 即可在不同 $\varepsilon$ 下实现实例依赖的误差界。推导了错误识别概率随 $\exp(-\widetilde{\Theta}(T/H_2(\varepsilon)))$ 衰减的理论界。

Result: 实现了对 $\varepsilon$-好子树识别的实例依赖误差界。当退化为标准最佳臂识别问题时，该分析恢复了 halving 类方法的相关保证，并为 Successive Rejects 提供了新的 $\varepsilon$-好保证。同时给出了相应的下界分析。

Conclusion: 本文首次为深度-2 max-min 树的固定预算动作识别问题提供了可证明的算法保证，证明了其难度结构与标准 K-armed bandit 不同。

Abstract: We study the fixed-budget max-min action identification problem in depth-2 max-min trees, an important special case of Monte Carlo Tree Search. A learner sequentially allocates $T$ samples to leaves and then recommends a subtree whose minimum leaf value is largest. Motivated by approximate planning, we focus on $\varepsilon$-good subtree identification, where any subtree whose min value is within $\varepsilon$ of the optimal maximin value is acceptable.
  Our main contribution is an $\varepsilon$-agnostic algorithm: it does not require $\varepsilon$ as input, but achieves instance-dependent error bounds for every meaningful $\varepsilon$. We show that the misidentification probability decays as $\exp(-\widetildeΘ(T/H_2(\varepsilon)))$, where $H_2(\varepsilon)$ captures both cross-subtree and within-subtree gaps. When each subtree has a single leaf, the problem reduces to standard fixed-budget best-arm identification, and our analysis recovers, up to accelerating factors, known $\varepsilon$-good guarantees for halving-style methods while giving a new $\varepsilon$-good guarantee for Successive Rejects.
  On the lower-bound side, we provide complementary positive and negative results showing that max-min identification has a different hardness structure from standard $K$-armed bandits. To our knowledge, this is the first provable fixed-budget algorithmic guarantee for max-min action identification.

</details>


### [89] [A Comparative Study of Federated Learning Aggregation Strategies under Homogeneous and Heterogeneous Data Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11010)
*Antonios Makris,Christos Dousis,Emmanouil Kritharakis,Stavros Bouras,Konstantinos Tserpes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过实验评估分析了联邦学习中不同聚合策略在多种数据分布下的表现，指出聚合策略的选择必须根据具体数据集特性与性能需求进行权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习的性能在很大程度上取决于服务器端的聚合策略，但目前缺乏针对不同数据分布条件下各主流聚合策略影响的系统性实验研究。

Method: 在基准图像分类数据集上，针对同质与异质数据分布，对主流联邦学习聚合策略进行了全面的实验对比与基准测试。评估指标涵盖了中心化准确率、损失值以及系统级效率（聚合时间、训练时间、通信时间）。

Result: 实验结果显示，各聚合策略在不同的数据集和数据分布下展现出明显的性能权衡，其有效性高度依赖于具体的数据特征和系统运行条件。

Conclusion: 联邦学习中不存在“万能”的聚合策略；不同策略在不同数据集特征与数据分布（同质或异质）下表现出显著的权衡差异，没有一种单一方法在所有场景下均占优。

Abstract: Federated Learning has emerged as a transformative paradigm for collaborative machine learning across distributed environments. However, its performance is strongly influenced by the aggregation strategy used to combine local model updates at the server, which directly affects learning performance, robustness, and system behavior. This work presents a comprehensive experimental comparison of widely used federated aggregation strategies under both homogeneous and heterogeneous data distributions. Using benchmark image classification datasets, we analyze how different aggregation mechanisms respond to varying degrees of data heterogeneity, examining their impact on centralized accuracy and loss, and system-level efficiency metrics, including aggregation, training, and communication time. The results demonstrate that aggregation strategies exhibit distinct trade-offs across datasets and data distributions, with their effectiveness varying according to dataset characteristics and operating conditions.

</details>


### [90] [Causal Fairness for Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11362)
*Drago Plecko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于生存/时间事件（TTE）分析的公平性因果框架，通过分解直接、间接和虚假路径，不仅能在时间维度上解释不公平现象的成因，还通过非参数化方法验证了在ICU种族差异分析中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗等高风险领域，现有的公平机器学习研究多局限于静态场景；而在生存分析（TTE）领域，现有方法仅依赖统计公平定义，无法剥离产生偏差的因果机制，导致无法有效解释不公平现象的根源。

Method: 1. 利用图形模型形式化处理删失（censoring）和混杂因素（confounding）的假设；2. 恢复给定协变量的条件生存函数；3. 应用因果缩减定理（Causal Reduction Theorem）重构问题以进行因果路径分解；4. 基于非参数方法高效估计各路径效应。

Result: 成功应用于ICU入院后种族差异的演变分析，定性且定量地分解了直接、间接和虚假路径对生存差异的贡献。

Conclusion: 该因果框架能够通过因果路径分解，揭示生存分析中差异性的来源及其随时间演变的机制，克服了传统统计公平定义在因果推理上的不足，为高风险决策领域提供了可解释的公平性工具。

Abstract: In the data-driven era, large-scale datasets are routinely collected and analyzed using machine learning (ML) and artificial intelligence (AI) to inform decisions in high-stakes domains such as healthcare, employment, and criminal justice, raising concerns about the fairness behavior of these systems. Existing works in fair ML cover tasks such as bias detection, fair prediction, and fair decision-making, but largely focus on static settings. At the same time, fairness in temporal contexts, particularly survival/time-to-event (TTE) analysis, remains relatively underexplored, with current approaches to fair survival analysis adopting statistical fairness definitions, which, even with unlimited data, cannot disentangle the causal mechanisms that generate disparities. To address this gap, we develop a causal framework for fairness in TTE analysis, enabling the decomposition of disparities in survival into contributions from direct, indirect, and spurious pathways. This provides a human-understandable explanation of why disparities arise and how they evolve over time. Our non-parametric approach proceeds in four steps: (1) formalizing the necessary assumptions about censoring and lack of confounding using a graphical model; (2) recovering the conditional survival function given covariates; (3) applying the Causal Reduction Theorem to reframe the problem in a form amenable to causal pathway decomposition; (4) estimating the effects efficiently. Finally, our approach is used to analyze the temporal evolution of racial disparities in outcome after admission to an intensive care unit (ICU).

</details>


### [91] [LoopUS: Recasting Pretrained LLMs into Looped Latent Refinement Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11011)
*Taekhyun Park,Yongjae Lee,Dohee Kim,Hyerim Bae*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoopUS 是一种无需重训的 LLM 后处理框架，通过将预训练模型重构为“编码器-循环模块-解码器”架构，在不增加推理生成长度的前提下，通过循环计算显著提升了推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环计算方法存在重训成本高、需要破坏性改造或损害预训练性能等缺陷，缺乏一种高效、通用的后处理方法来提升 LLM 的测试时计算能力。

Method: 提出 LoopUS 框架，将预训练模型重构为“编码器-循环推理模块-解码器”架构。包含四大核心机制：1. 基于阶段性表征动态的块分解；2. 用于缓解隐藏状态漂移的输入依赖选择门；3. 用于长递归环境下内存高效学习的随机深度监督；4. 用于自适应提前退出的置信度头。

Result: LoopUS 能够使标准预训练模型实现稳定的潜在循环（latent looping），在不增加生成轨迹长度的情况下显著改善推理任务表现，同时避免了计算瓶颈和表征坍缩。

Conclusion: LoopUS 通过循环架构成功将预训练大模型转化为推理更强的模型，在无需重训或改变生成长度的情况下，有效提升了推理性能并解决了循环计算中的表征漂移与坍缩问题。

Abstract: Looped computation shows promise in improving the reasoning-oriented performance of LLMs by scaling test-time compute. However, existing approaches typically require either training recurrent models from scratch or applying disruptive retrofits, which involve substantial computational costs and may compromise pretrained capabilities. To address these limitations, we introduce \textbf{Looped Depth Up-Scaling} (LoopUS), a post-training framework that converts a standard pretrained LLM into a looped architecture. As a key technical contribution, LoopUS recasts the pretrained LLM into an encoder, a looped reasoning block, and a decoder. It operationalizes this latent-refinement architecture through four core components: (1) block decomposition, guided by staged representation dynamics; (2) an input-dependent selective gate to mitigate hidden-state drift; (3) random deep supervision for memory-efficient learning over long recursive horizons; and (4) a confidence head for adaptive early exiting. Collectively, these mechanisms transform a standard non-looped model into a looped form while stabilizing it against both computational bottlenecks and representation collapse. Through stable latent looping, LoopUS improves reasoning-oriented performance without extending the generated traces or requiring recurrent training from scratch. For more details, see https://thrillcrazyer.github.io/LoopUS

</details>


### [92] [Test-Time Compute for Dense Retrieval: Agentic Program Generation with Frozen Embedding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11374)
*Han Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究发现通过简单的“查询向量与Top-K文档质心插值”这一测试时计算策略，无需重训练即可显著提升各类小型嵌入模型的检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为测试时间计算仅对大型推理模型有益。本文旨在探究小型嵌入模型是否也能通过额外的推理计算（而非重新训练）来提升性能。

Method: 使用智能体程序搜索循环（agentic program-search loop），在90个世代中评估了259个候选推理程序，最终发现了一个参数无关的默认策略：将查询向量与本地Top-K文档的Softmax加权质心进行插值。

Result: 该方法在7个嵌入模型家族（参数范围跨度10倍）上均显著提升了nDCG@10指标，并在BEIR基准测试中得到了验证。

Conclusion: 推特时间推理（Test-time compute）同样能够显著提升小型嵌入模型（embedding models）的性能。研究发现，简单的代数方法（加权质心插值）即能作为一种通用的测试时优化手段，跨模型家族实现一致的性能增益。

Abstract: Test-time compute is widely believed to benefit only large reasoning models. We show it also helps small embedding models. Most modern embedding checkpoints are distilled from large LLM backbones and inherit their representation space; a frozen embedding model should therefore benefit from extra inference compute without retraining. Using an agentic program-search loop, we explore 259 candidate inference programs over a frozen embedding API across ninety generations. The entire Pareto frontier collapses onto a single algebra: a softmax-weighted centroid of the local top-K documents interpolated with the query. This parameter-free default lifts nDCG@10 statistically significantly across seven embedding-model families spanning a tenfold parameter range, with held-out full-BEIR validation confirming the lift on every model tested.

</details>


### [93] [Backbone-Equated Diffusion OOD via Sparse Internal Snapshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11014)
*Yadang Alexis Rouzoumka,Jean Pinsolle,Eugénie Terreaux,Christèle Morisseau,Jean-Philippe Ovarlez,Chengfang Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为了解决扩散模型OOD检测评估不一致的问题，本文提出了MBE评估协议和极轻量级的CFS检测方法，证明了扩散主干的内部激活中蕴含着高度集中的OOD识别信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的OOD检测研究缺乏公平的基准（受主干选择、腐蚀参数化和测试时间预算影响），导致结论不一致。

Method: 1. 提出MBE（Mutualized Backbone-Equated）协议，通过对齐腐蚀级别和测试时间成本，实现公平比较；2. 引入CFS（Canonical Feature Snapshots）检测器，仅利用冻结扩散主干在低噪声水平下的少量内部激活进行推理。

Result: 在CIFAR基准测试中，CFS(1x2)变体表现最强，且其更小的纯解码器变体同样具有高度竞争力，极大降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: 证实了冻结扩散模型中的OOD检测信号主要集中在少数稀疏的内部状态中，无需完整的去噪轨迹或复杂的下游分类器。提出了有效的理论解释，并证明了轻量级检测器CFS的强竞争性。

Abstract: Fair comparison between diffusion-based OOD detectors is challenging, as conclusions can vary with backbone choice, corruption parameterization, and test-time budget. We address this issue through a Mutualized Backbone-Equated (MBE) protocol that aligns canonical corruption levels and logical test-time cost across diffusion backbones. Within this setting, we introduce Canonical Feature Snapshots (CFS), a family of detectors that probes a frozen diffusion backbone using only a tiny number of native internal activations at canonical low-noise levels. On a controlled CIFAR-scale benchmark, the strongest one-forward CFS variant is CFS(1x2), while an even smaller decoder-only variant remains highly competitive. This shows that much of the relative-OOD signal exposed by frozen diffusion backbones is concentrated in a small number of sparse internal states, rather than requiring full denoising trajectories or high-capacity downstream heads. We further provide a local diagnostic theory explaining these observations through conditional encoder-decoder complementarity, diagonal-score separation, and low-noise corruption stability. The official implementation is available at https://github.com/RouzAY/cfs-diffusion-ood/.

</details>


### [94] [Efficient LLM Reasoning via Variational Posterior Guidance with Efficiency Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11019)
*Zizhao Chen,Yuying Li,Siting Lin,Lianxi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大模型“过度思考”带来的推理低效问题，本文提出 VPG-EA 框架，通过引入变分推理将高质量后验路径蒸馏至先验策略，有效突破了强化学习探索高质量样本的瓶颈，显著提升了推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型推理中的“过度思考”（overthinking）现象严重降低了推理效率，而传统深度强化学习方法在稀疏奖励下难以高效探索高质量推理路径。

Method: 提出 VPG-EA 框架：(1) 将高效推理形式化为变分推理问题，引入效率感知证据下界 (ELBO)；(2) 采用参数共享的双流架构实例化后验分布与先验策略；(3) 利用跨视图评估过滤伪高效路径，并通过变分蒸馏实现知识迁移。

Result: 在 DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B 和 7B 模型上，VPG-EA 使得综合效率指标（epsilon cubed）分别提升了 8.73% 和 12.37%。

Conclusion: VPG-EA 通过变分蒸馏有效地将后验推理模式迁移至先验策略，在保持推理质量的前提下显著提升了长文本推理的效率，在多个模型规模上均优于现有基线。

Abstract: Although large language models rely on chain-of-thought for complex reasoning, the overthinking phenomenon severely degrades inference efficiency. Existing reinforcement learning methods compress reasoning chains by designing elaborate reward functions, which renders high-quality samples extremely sparse in the exploration space and creates a sampling bottleneck for the prior policy. Inspired by cognitive science, we theoretically prove that a posterior distribution guided by reference answers achieves higher expected utility than the prior distribution, thus capable of breaking through the sampling bottleneck of high-quality samples. However, the posterior distribution is unavailable during inference. To this end, we formalize efficient reasoning as a variational inference problem and introduce an efficiency-aware evidence lower bound as the theoretical foundation. Based on this, we propose the VPG-EA framework. It adopts a parameter-shared dual-stream architecture to instantiate both the posterior distribution and the prior policy; after filtering out pseudo-efficient paths via cross-view evaluation, it unidirectionally transfers the posterior's efficient patterns to the prior policy through variational distillation. Experiments on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B and 7B scales demonstrate that VPG-EA improves the comprehensive efficiency metric epsilon cubed by 8.73% and 12.37% over the strongest baselines on each model size, respectively.

</details>


### [95] [Trust Region Inverse Reinforcement Learning: Explicit Dual Ascent using Local Policy Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11020)
*Anish Diwan,Davide Tateo,Christopher E. Mower,Haitham Bou-Ammar,Jan Peters,Oleg Arenz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 TRIRL 算法，通过信赖域方法解决了 IRL 中计算开销与训练稳定性之间的矛盾，实现了更稳健、高效且具有单调提升性质的策略与奖励函数学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逆强化学习（IRL）需要每轮完全求解强化学习问题，计算开销巨大；而对抗式 IRL 虽然降低了计算成本，但存在训练不稳定及无法保证对偶目标单调提升的问题。本文旨在桥接这两种方法，实现稳定、单调的性能提升。

Method: 提出 Trust Region Inverse Reinforcement Learning (TRIRL) 算法，通过利用“针对奖励函数更新的信赖域最优策略对于同方向的较小更新也是全局最优”这一核心理论，通过局部搜索优化对偶目标，避免了每轮迭代完全求解强化学习子问题的需求。

Result: 在多个挑战性任务中，TRIRL 的性能优于现有的模仿学习方法，在聚合四分位均值（IQM）方面提升了 2.4 倍，且能够学习到在系统动力学变化下仍具有泛化能力的奖励函数。

Conclusion: TRIRL 通过信赖域策略更新实现了逆强化学习的单调性能提升，克服了传统方法计算开销大和对抗式方法不稳定的问题，并展现了良好的奖励函数泛化能力。

Abstract: Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) is typically formulated as maximizing entropy subject to matching the distribution of expert trajectories. Classical (dual-ascent) IRL guarantees monotonic performance improvement but requires fully solving an RL problem each iteration to compute dual gradients. More recent adversarial methods avoid this cost at the expense of stability and monotonic dual improvement, by directly optimizing the primal problem and using a discriminator to provide rewards. In this work, we bridge the gap between these approaches by enabling monotonic improvement of the reward function and policy without having to fully solve an RL problem at every iteration. Our key theoretical insight is that a trust-region-optimal policy for a reward function update can be globally optimal for a smaller update in the same direction. This smaller update allows us to explicitly optimize the dual objective while only relying on a local search around the current policy. In doing so, our approach avoids the training instabilities of adversarial methods, offers monotonic performance improvement, and learns a reward function in the traditional sense of IRL--one that can be globally optimized to match expert demonstrations. Our proposed algorithm, Trust Region Inverse Reinforcement Learning (TRIRL), outperforms state-of-the-art imitation learning methods across multiple challenging tasks by a factor of 2.4x in terms of aggregate inter-quartile mean, while recovering reward functions that generalize to system dynamics shifts.

</details>


### [96] [Adaptive Calibration in Non-Stationary Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11490)
*Junyan Liu,Haipeng Luo,Lillian J. Ratliff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一系列自适应在线校准算法，能在未知环境非平稳程度的情况下，通过多尺度预测空间划分，在平稳与对抗环境下自动折中并实现最优的校准误差边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线预测算法多针对完全对抗环境，过于保守，在平稳或近似平稳环境下性能不佳。研究目标是设计一种能根据环境非平稳程度（以C衡量）自动适应的算法，在维持对抗鲁棒性的同时，提高在更平稳环境下的性能。

Method: 提出了基于纪元（epoch）调度的算法框架，并结合了一种对预测空间进行非均匀划分的新方法，通过在预期地面真值附近分配更细的分辨率来优化校准误差。

Result: 针对长度为T的序列，在未知非平稳性程度C的情况下，对于$\ell_1$校准误差实现了$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{T}+(TC)^{\frac{1}{3}})$的界，对于$\ell_2$和伪KL校准误差实现了$\widetilde{O}((1+C)^{\frac{1}{3}})$的界。这些结果在平稳（C=0）和完全对抗（C=T）极端情况下均达到了最优或既定界限。

Conclusion: 作者提出了一套在线校准算法，成功实现了在i.i.d.和完全对抗环境之间平滑过渡的自适应校准误差界。该工作闭合了理论上限，证明了算法在非平稳环境下无需预知环境条件即可达到最优性能。

Abstract: Making calibrated online predictions is a central challenge in modern AI systems. Much of the existing literature focuses on fully adversarial environments where outcomes may be arbitrary, leading to conservative algorithms that can perform suboptimally in more benign settings, such as when outcomes are nearly stationary. This gap raises a natural question: can we design online prediction algorithms whose calibration error automatically adapts to the degree of non-stationarity in the environment, smoothly interpolating between i.i.d. and adversarial regimes? We answer this question in the affirmative and develop a suite of algorithms that achieve adaptive calibration guarantees under multiple calibration measures. Specifically, with $T$ being the number of rounds and $C\in[0,T]$ being an unknown non-stationary measure defined as the minimal $\ell_1$ deviation of the mean outcomes, our algorithms attain $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{T}+(TC)^{\frac{1}{3}})$ for $\ell_1$ calibration error and $\widetilde{O}((1+C)^{\frac{1}{3}})$ for both $\ell_2$ and pseudo KL calibration error. These bounds match the optimal rates in the stationary case ($C=0$) and recover known guarantees in the fully adversarial regime ($C=T$). Our approach builds on and extends prior work [Hu et al., 2026, Luo et al., 2025], introducing an epoch-based scheduling together with a novel non-uniform partition of the prediction space that allocates finer resolution near the underlying ground truth.

</details>


### [97] [ASD-Bench: A Four-Axis Comprehensive Benchmark of AI Models for Autism Spectrum Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11091)
*Shubhankit Singh,Hassan Shaikh,Kuldeep Raghuwanshi,Keshav Bulia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了ASD-Bench基准，通过多架构、多维度和多年龄段的系统性评估，揭示了ASD筛查中存在的性能与校准断层，并发现不同年龄段的关键诊断特征存在显著差异，为精准的临床ASD筛查提供了模型部署建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ASD自动筛查工具存在架构评估单一、评估维度匮乏以及过度关注成人群体的问题，导致早期干预所需的年龄特异性诊断模式被掩盖。

Method: 1. 构建了ASD-Bench：涵盖机器学习、深度学习及基础模型，针对三个年龄段（1-11岁、12-16岁、17-64岁）进行多维评估；
2. 评价指标：引入启发式聚合惩罚（HAP）指标，注重降低假阴性率并保证临床部署稳定性；
3. 数据集：基于4,068条AQ-10记录的数据集；
4. 综合评估：在预测性能、校准度、模型可解释性（特征重要性）及对抗鲁棒性四个轴向进行全面基准测试。

Result: 1. 性能差异：成人组分类表现最优（部分模型F1和AUC达1.0），而青少年组任务难度最高（F1上限0.837）；
2. 特征演变：儿童组主要依赖社交动机（A9），青少年组依赖模式识别（A5），成人组特征权重趋于平缓（可能与社交掩饰有关）；
3. 指标解耦：性能与校准度存在差异（如AdaBoost在成人组F1虽为1.0，但ECE高达0.302，说明其可靠性差），证实了使用单一指标评估的局限性。

Conclusion: 现有的自闭症谱系障碍（ASD）筛查模型评估过于单一，忽视了年龄特异性的诊断模式。本研究证明仅依靠单一指标（如F1分数）不足以评估临床AI，必须要进行多维度分析（性能、校准、解释性、鲁棒性）。研究明确了不同年龄段特征重要性的演变，强调了跨年龄段筛查工具部署的差异化策略。

Abstract: Automated ASD screening tools remain limited by single-architecture evaluations, axis-restricted assessment, and near-exclusive focus on adult cohorts, obscuring age-specific diagnostic patterns critical for early intervention. We introduce ASD-Bench, a systematic tabular benchmark evaluating ML, deep learning, and foundation model configurations across three age cohorts (children 1-11 yr, adolescents 12-16 yr, adults 17-64 yr) on four axes: predictive performance, calibration, interpretability, and adversarial robustness. Applied to a curated v3 dataset of 4,068 AQ-10 records, our benchmark spans classical models (XGBoost, AdaBoost, Random Forest, Logistic Regression), neural networks (MLP), deep tabular transformers (TabNet, TabTransformer, FT-Transformer), and TabPFN v2. We introduce the Heuristic Aggregate Penalty (HAP): a cost-sensitive metric penalising false negatives more heavily and incorporating cross-validation variance for deployment stability. Adult classification yields high performance (10/17 models achieve perfect F1 and AUC), while adolescents present a harder task (F1 ceiling 0.837 vs. 0.915 for children). Feature hierarchies shift across cohorts: A9 (social motivation) dominates for children, A5 (pattern recognition) leads for adolescents, and adults exhibit a flatter importance profile consistent with developmental social masking. Accuracy and calibration are dissociated: AdaBoost achieves F1=1.000 on adults with ECE=0.302, confirming single-metric evaluation is insufficient for clinical AI. Cohort-specific deployment recommendations are provided. All findings should be interpreted as proof-of-concept evidence on questionnaire-derived labels rather than clinically validated diagnostic performance.

</details>


### [98] [LOFT: Low-Rank Orthogonal Fine-Tuning via Task-Aware Support Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11872)
*Lanxin Zhao,Bamdev Mishra,Pratik Jawanpuria,Lequan Lin,Dai Shi,Junbin Gao,Andi Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 LOFT 框架，规范化了正交 PEFT 的设计维度，并证明通过根据任务训练信号进行“任务感知支持选择”，可以显著提升正交微调的效率与效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的正交参数高效微调（PEFT）方法模糊了“适应发生的子空间”与“子空间内的变换”这两个设计选择，缺乏对支持向量选择机制的深入研究。

Method: 提出 LOFT 框架，将正交 PEFT 分解为子空间旋转和内部变换；基于此框架统一了现有方法（坐标变换、蝴蝶变换、Householder 变换等）；开发了一阶分析方法，提出基于下游训练信号的任务感知支持选择策略。

Result: LOFT 成功复现了现有代表性方法；实验表明，梯度引导的支持向量选择在语言、视觉、数学和跨语言任务中，在同等参数、内存和计算预算下，实现了更优的性能与效率平衡。

Conclusion: LOFT 通过明确划分“子空间”与“变换”两个设计维度，揭示了支持向量选择（Support Selection）的核心地位。基于梯度信息的任务感知选择策略证明了原则性的支持选择是优化正交 PEFT 的关键方向。

Abstract: Orthogonal parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) adapts pretrained weights through structure-preserving multiplicative transformations, but existing methods often conflate two distinct design choices: the subspace in which adaptation occurs and the transformation applied within that subspace. This paper introduces LOFT, a low-rank orthogonal fine-tuning framework that explicitly separates these two components. By viewing orthogonal adaptation as a multiplicative subspace rotation, LOFT provides a unified formulation that recovers representative orthogonal PEFT methods, including coordinate-, butterfly-, Householder-, and principal-subspace-based variants. More importantly, this perspective exposes support selection as a central design axis rather than a byproduct of a particular parameterization. We develop a first-order analysis showing that useful adaptation supports should be informed by the downstream training signal, motivating practical task-aware support selection strategies. Across language understanding, visual transfer, mathematical reasoning, and multilingual out-of-distribution adaptation, LOFT recovers principal-subspace orthogonal adaptation while gradient-informed supports improve the efficiency-performance trade-off under matched parameter, memory, and compute budgets. These results suggest that principled support selection is an important direction for improving orthogonal PEFT.

</details>


### [99] [QDSB: Quantized Diffusion Schrödinger Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11983)
*Tobias Fuchs,Florian Kalinke,Nadja Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对薛定谔桥模型在无配对数据下训练成本高且易产生局部失真的问题，本文提出 QDSB 方法，通过锚点量化近似求解全局最优传输计划，实现了高效且高质量的生成模型训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无模拟薛定谔桥（simulation-free SBs）方法依赖于最优传输（OT）耦合，但大规模全局最优传输计算不可行，而 minibatch 迭代法不仅计算成本高，还可能导致全局传输几何结构的扭曲。

Method: 提出“量化扩散薛定谔桥”（QDSB）算法：通过对端点分布进行锚点量化，在量化空间中计算最优传输计划，随后通过胞元采样（cell-wise sampling）将耦合计划回传至原始数据点，从而避免了大规模的 minibatch 迭代计算。

Result: 理论上证明了正则化最优耦合对锚点量化具有稳定性，误差受限于锚点近似质量；在真实数据集实验中，QDSB 的样本质量与现有基线方法相当，但计算速度显著提升。

Conclusion: QDSB 证明了在锚点量化分布上计算最优传输耦合是可行的，且该方法在保持采样质量的同时，显著降低了计算开销，克服了 minibatch 迭代求解带来的局部失真与高昂算力成本问题。

Abstract: Learning generative models in settings where the source and target distributions are only specified through unpaired samples is gaining in importance. Here, one frequently-used model are Schrödinger bridges (SB), which represent the most likely evolution between both endpoint distributions. To accelerate training, simulation-free SBs avoid the path simulation of the original SB models. However, learning simulation-free SBs requires paired data; a coupling of the source and target samples is obtained as the solution of the entropic optimal transport (OT) problem. As obtaining the optimal global coupling is infeasible in many practical cases, the entropic OT problem is iteratively solved on minibatches instead. Still, the repeated cost remains substantial and the locality can distort the global transport geometry. We propose quantized diffusion Schrödinger bridges (QDSB), which compute the endpoint coupling on anchor-quantized endpoint distributions and lift the resulting plan back to original data points through cell-wise sampling. We show that the regularized optimal coupling is stable w.r.t. anchor quantization, with an error controlled by the quality of the anchor approximation. In real-world experiments, QDSB matches the sample quality of existing baselines, requiring substantially less time. Code and data are available at github.com/mathefuchs/qdsb.

</details>


### [100] [GRAFT-ATHENA: Self-Improving Agentic Teams for Autonomous Discovery and Evolutionary Numerical Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11117)
*Juan Diego Toscano,Zhaojie Chai,George Em Karniadakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRAFT-ATHENA是一个通过将决策空间因子化并结合经验迁移机制，能够从过往科学问题中学习并自主扩展行动空间的自我改进型AI框架，在复杂物理与工程求解中展现了优于人类专家的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的科学发现AI系统通常孤立地处理问题，缺乏跨领域积累方法论经验的能力；且面对复杂科学问题时，组合式的方法选择空间巨大，难以优化。

Method: 引入GRAFT（Graph Reduction to Adaptive Factored Trees）方法，将组合决策空间投影为因子化概率树，使参数规模从指数级降至线性级；利用概率路径将方法嵌入度量空间，通过相似度实现跨问题的经验迁移；采用LLM驱动的智能体架构进行自主求解、评估及正则化约束的发现。

Result: 在物理信息机器学习（PIML）基准测试中优于人类和现有基准；成功解决诸如Apollo指令舱Mach-10流动重建等复杂工程问题；自主发现了新型数值方法（如具有指数收敛性的谱PINN）。

Conclusion: GRAFT-ATHENA提供了一个可自我改进的AI框架，通过跨领域积累经验和自主扩展行动空间，实现了科学发现的自动化。该系统证明了AI能有效处理复杂的物理与工程逆问题，并展现了发现新型数值方法的能力，为自主实验室的发展奠定了基础。

Abstract: Scientific discovery can be modeled as a sequence of probabilistic decisions that map physical problems to numerical solutions. Recent agentic AI systems automate individual scientific tasks by orchestrating LLM-driven planners, solvers, and evaluators. Each method is a combination of methodological actions, with many viable combinations for any given problem and structural dependencies between choices. However, existing frameworks treat each problem in isolation, with no shared substrate to accumulate methodological experience across domains. Here we show that GRAFT-ATHENA, a self-improving agentic framework, learns from past problems and autonomously expands its own action space across diverse domains. GRAFT (Graph Reduction to Adaptive Factored Trees) projects combinatorial decision spaces into factored probabilistic trees in which each method is a single path, taking the parameter footprint from exponential to linear. In the lineage of classical Bayesian networks, the factorization is an $I$-map of the policy, and the resulting paths embed as unique fingerprints in a metric space whose closeness lets each new problem learn from similar past ones. On canonical physics-informed machine learning (PIML) benchmarks, GRAFT-ATHENA improves over human and prior agentic baselines, and on production solvers, it tackles complex engineering problems such as reconstructing Mach-10 flow over the Apollo Command Module from a 1968 report and recovering shear-thinning blood-cell rheology. Notably, the system grows its own knowledge substrate, autonomously proposing regularization constraints for ill-posed inverse problems and discovering new numerical methods such as a spectral PINN with exponential convergence. These results provide a foundation for autonomous laboratories that grow more capable with every problem they solve.

</details>


### [101] [Language Modeling with Hyperspherical Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11125)
*Justin Deschenaux,Caglar Gulcehre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为了解决现有流语言模型因使用 One-hot 向量带来的计算瓶颈和语义模糊问题，本研究提出了 $\mathbb{S}$-FLM，即一种在超球面上运行的潜流模型，提升了复杂推理任务中的生成质量和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离散扩散模型采样受限且表达能力不足；而现有的流模型（FLMs）依赖 One-hot 向量，导致在大词汇表下训练代价高昂，且高斯噪声缺乏语义解释性。

Method: 引入 $\mathbb{S}$-FLM（一种超球面潜流语言模型），通过在 $\mathbb{S}^{d-1}$ 超球面上学习速度场（而非在 One-hot 空间），结合交叉熵损失函数进行训练，避免了 One-hot 向量带来的高算力开销。

Result: $\mathbb{S}$-FLM 在大规模词汇推理任务中显著提升了生成表现，在标准温度（T=1）下缩小了与掩码扩散模型的性能差距。但在优化后的低温度（T=0.1）解码下，仍存在一定性能滞后。

Conclusion: $\mathbb{S}$-FLM 通过在超球面上定义潜空间流，有效解决了基于 One-hot 向量的流模型在处理大规模词汇表时的计算效率问题，并在推理任务中展现了优于现有流模型的性能。

Abstract: Discrete Diffusion Language Models progressed rapidly as an alternative to autoregressive (AR) models, motivated by their parallel generation abilities. However, for tractability, discrete diffusion models sample from a factorized distribution, which is less expressive than AR. Recent Flow Language Models (FLMs) apply continuous flows to language, transporting noise to data with a deterministic ODE that avoids factorized sampling. FLMs operate on one-hot vectors whose dimension scales with the vocabulary size, making FLMs costly to train. Moreover, since all distinct one-hot embeddings are equidistant in $\ell_2$, adding Gaussian noise does not have a clear semantic interpretation (unlike images, where Gaussian noise progressively degrades structure). We introduce $\mathbb{S}$-FLM, a latent FLM in the hypersphere. $\mathbb{S}$-FLM generates sequences by rotating vectors in $\mathbb{S}^{d-1}$ along a velocity field learned with cross-entropy, avoiding the overhead of materializing one-hot vectors. Previous FLMs match AR in Generative Perplexity (Gen.\ PPL), but samples with high likelihood are not necessarily correct in verifiable domains such as math and code. $\mathbb{S}$-FLM substantially improves continuous flow language models on large-vocabulary reasoning and closes the gap to masked diffusion under standard-temperature sampling ($T=1$), while a gap remains under optimized low-temperature ($T=0.1$) decoding.

</details>


### [102] [HEPA: A Self-Supervised Horizon-Conditioned Event Predictive Architecture for Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11130)
*Jonas Petersen,Gian-Alessandro Lombardi,Riccardo Maggioni,Camilla Mazzoleni,Federico Martelli,Philipp Petersen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HEPA 是一种面向稀有事件预测的预测架构，通过 JEPA 自监督预训练捕捉时间动态，并采用冻结编码器配合轻量化微调策略，在极低标注数据需求下实现了超越现有主流时序模型的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元时间序列中的关键事件（如设备故障、心律失常）具有高危害性但稀有且标注成本极高，现有监督学习模型难以在数据匮乏条件下实现精准预测。

Method: 1. 预训练阶段：使用基于 JEPA（联合嵌入预测架构）的因果 Transformer 编码器，通过预测未来表征而非具体数值学习 temporal dynamics；2. 微调阶段：冻结编码器，仅微调预测头，输出针对不同预测时域的单调生存累积分布函数（CDF）。

Result: 在 11 个领域的 14 个基准测试中，HEPA 在 10 项测试上优于 PatchTST、iTransformer、MAE 和 Chronos-2 等主流模型，同时显著降低了所需调整的参数量及标注数据量。

Conclusion: HEPA 证明了利用无标签数据预训练（JEPA）结合针对性预测头微调，是解决稀有事件预测任务的高效方案，在参数效率和数据效率上均优于现有的前沿时间序列模型。

Abstract: Critical events in multivariate time series, from turbine failures to cardiac arrhythmias, demand accurate prediction, yet labeled data is scarce because such events are rare and costly to annotate. We introduce HEPA (Horizon-conditioned Event Predictive Architecture), built on two key principles. First, a causal Transformer encoder is pretrained via a Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA): a horizon-conditioned predictor learns to forecast future representations rather than future values, forcing the encoder to capture predictable temporal dynamics from unlabeled data alone. Second, we freeze the encoder and finetune only the predictor toward the target event, producing a monotonic survival cumulative distribution function (CDF) over horizons. With fixed architecture and optimiser hyperparameters across all benchmarks, HEPA handles water contamination, cyberattack detection, volatility regimes, and eight further event types across 11 domains, exceeding leading time-series architectures including PatchTST, iTransformer, MAE, and Chronos-2 on at least 10 of 14 benchmarks, with an order of magnitude fewer tuned parameters and, on lifecycle datasets, an order of magnitude less labeled data.

</details>


### [103] [Pion: A Spectrum-Preserving Optimizer via Orthogonal Equivalence Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12492)
*Kexuan Shi,Hanxuan Li,Zeju Qiu,Yandong Wen,Simon Buchholz,Weiyang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Pion 是一种通过正交等价变换进行权重更新的新型优化器，它在训练中保持权重矩阵的奇异值不变，为 LLM 的训练提供了一种稳定且高效的新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的加法型优化器（如 Adam、Muon）在处理权重更新时可能无法最优地利用矩阵的几何性质，作者寻求一种能够保持权重矩阵谱范数（spectral norm）不变的优化方法。

Method: 提出了 Pion 优化器，其核心机制是利用左、右正交变换对权重矩阵进行更新，确保在训练过程中矩阵的奇异值（谱）保持不变。

Result: 实验表明，Pion 在大型语言模型（LLM）的预训练和微调任务中表现出良好的稳定性，并具备与标准优化器相当的竞争力。

Conclusion: Pion 是一种通过正交等价变换更新权重矩阵的优化器，证明了在保持奇异值不变的情况下调整矩阵几何形状是 LLM 训练的一种有效且稳定的替代方案。

Abstract: We introduce Pion, a spectrum-preserving optimizer for large language model (LLM) training based on orthogonal equivalence transformation. Unlike additive optimizers such as Adam and Muon, Pion updates each weight matrix through left and right orthogonal transformations, preserving its singular values throughout training. This yields an optimization mechanism that modulates the geometry of weight matrices while keeping their spectral norm fixed. We derive the Pion update rule, systematically examine its design choices, and analyze its convergence behavior along with several key properties. Empirical results show that Pion offers a stable and competitive alternative to standard optimizers for both LLM pretraining and finetuning.

</details>


### [104] [Steerable Neural ODEs on Homogeneous Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11133)
*Emma Andersdotter,Daniel Persson,Fredrik Ohlsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文引入了“可操纵神经常微分方程（Steerable NODEs）”，通过将特征视为齐性空间上的向量丛截面，结合平行移动机制，实现了在对称群作用下具有 $G$-等变性的连续时间动态建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流形神经常微分方程（NODEs）在处理具有局部对称性特征的几何数据时缺乏正式的等变性框架，且难以直接处理齐性空间上的向量值特征演化。

Method: 在齐性空间 $M=G/H$ 上构建神经常微分方程，将特征解释为关联向量丛的截面，通过流方程（在 $M$ 上）与转向方程（特征演化，视为平行移动）构成的耦合系统来实现演化，并确保在向量场和连接均为 $G$-不变时实现 $G$-等变性。

Result: 成功推导了可操纵 NODEs 的 $G$-等变性条件，表明该框架能够包含现有的 NODEs 模型及李群上的连续归一化流，验证了其在处理复杂几何动态学习任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出了可操纵神经常微分方程（Steerable NODEs），为在齐性空间上学习向量值特征的连续时间等变动力学提供了一个通用的几何框架。

Abstract: We introduce steerable neural ordinary differential equations on homogeneous spaces $M=G/H$. These models constitute a novel geometric extension of manifold neural ordinary differential equations (NODEs) that transport associated feature vectors transforming under the local symmetry group $H$. We interpret features as sections of associated vector bundles over $M$, and describe their evolution as parallel transport. This results in a coupled system of ODEs consisting of a flow equation on $M$ and a steering equation acting on features. We show that steerable NODEs are $G$-equivariant whenever the vector field generating the flow and the connection governing parallel transport are both $G$-invariant. Furthermore, we demonstrate how steerable NODEs incorporate existing NODE models and continuous normalizing flows on Lie groups. Our framework provides the geometric foundation for learning continuous-time equivariant dynamics of general vector-valued features on homogeneous spaces.

</details>


### [105] [CORE: Cyclic Orthotope Relation Embedding for Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11159)
*Yingqi Zeng,Luying Wang,Huiling Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为解决知识图谱嵌入中区域模型受限于绝对边界的问题，本文提出了CORE模型，利用环面流形和循环正交体实现无边界的平滑建模，有效提升了关系推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于区域的知识图谱嵌入模型在优化过程中常受限于绝对边界约束，如果不加限制则会导致区域无限扩张。为了解决这一矛盾，旨在寻找一种既能避免空间边界限制又能维持区域稳定性的方法。

Method: 提出了一种名为CORE（Cyclic Orthotope Relation Embedding）的知识图谱补全模型。该模型将实体和关系嵌入到无边界的环面流形空间，并将关系建模为环面上的循环正交体，同时引入自适应宽度正则化以控制区域扩展。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的实验结果表明，CORE模型在链路预测任务中取得了具有竞争力的表现，特别是在密集语义环境下显著提升了预测精度。

Conclusion: CORE模型通过在环面流形上使用循环正交体，有效解决了基于区域嵌入方法在处理边界约束时的困难，并在各类关系建模任务中表现出色。

Abstract: Knowledge graph completion (KGC) aims to automatically infer missing facts in multi-relational data by mapping entities and relations into continuous representation spaces. Recent region-based embedding models have shown great promise in capturing complex logical patterns by representing relations as geometric regions. However, these models inevitably suffer from absolute boundary constraints during optimization. Conversely, without such constraints, relation regions expand indefinitely. To address the limitation, we propose \textbf{CORE} (Cyclic Orthotope Relation Embedding), a novel KGC model that embeds entities and relations onto a boundary-less torus manifold.CORE represents relations as cyclic orthotopes on the torus manifold, allowing regions to seamlessly wrap around spatial boundaries to ensure smooth gradient conduction. Furthermore, an adaptive width regularization is introduced to prevent unconditional region expansion. Theoretical analysis proves that CORE can capture various complex relation patterns such as subsumption and intersection. Extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets demonstrate that CORE achieves highly competitive performance, significantly improving link prediction accuracy in dense semantic environments.

</details>


### [106] [Interpretability Can Be Actionable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11161)
*Hadas Orgad,Fazl Barez,Tal Haklay,Isabelle Lee,Marius Mosbach,Anja Reusch,Naomi Saphra,Byron Wallace,Sarah Wiegreffe,Eric Wong,Ian Tenney,Mor Geva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对人工智能可解释性研究缺乏实际应用价值的问题，提出以“可行动性”作为评估核心，并构建了相应的分析维度与评估框架，以确保解释性研究能真正指导现实决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可解释性研究虽然快速增长，但在实际应用中影响力不足，缺乏明确的评估标准，导致研究成果难以转化为实际决策或干预措施。

Method: 文章定义了可行动可解释性（Actionable Interpretability）的两个维度——具体性（Concreteness）与验证（Validation）；分析了制约现实影响力的障碍；识别了五个具有高杠杆作用的潜在应用领域；并建立了一套与实际结果挂钩的评估框架。

Result: 通过界定可行动性定义并构建评估框架，为可解释性研究从“解释性”向“实用性”转型提供了指导路径，旨在解决研究与实践脱节的问题。

Conclusion: 研究提出应将“可行动性”（Actionability）作为可解释性研究的核心目标，通过建立具体的评估标准来弥合学术研究与实际应用之间的鸿沟，而非仅仅局限于探索性研究。

Abstract: Interpretability aims to explain the behavior of deep neural networks. Despite rapid growth, there is mounting concern that much of this work has not translated into practical impact, raising questions about its relevance and utility. This position paper argues that the central missing ingredient is not new methods, but evaluation criteria: interpretability should be evaluated by actionability--the extent to which insights enable concrete decisions and interventions beyond interpretability research itself. We define actionable interpretability along two dimensions--concreteness and validation--and analyze the barriers currently preventing real-world impact. To address these barriers, we identify five domains where interpretability offers unique leverage and present a framework for actionable interpretability with evaluation criteria aligned with practical outcomes. Our goal is not to downplay exploratory research, but to establish actionability as a core objective of interpretability research.

</details>


### [107] [Unlearning with Asymmetric Sources: Improved Unlearning-Utility Trade-off with Public Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11170)
*Ahmed Mehdi Inane,Vincent Quirion,Gintare Karolina Dzugaite,Ioannis Mitliagkas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出ALU框架，通过引入公共数据来抵消机器遗忘中的噪声惩罚，在维持模型高性能的同时，实现了大规模、高安全的机器遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于噪声的机器遗忘方法在处理大规模数据删除时，为达到隐私认证所需的噪声量通常会严重损害模型效用。论文旨在探索利用公共数据来缓解这一“隐私保护与模型效用”之间的权衡矛盾。

Method: 提出了非对称朗之万遗忘（Asymmetric Langevin Unlearning, ALU）框架。利用公共数据对非对称噪声项进行重加权，从而减轻遗忘过程中的效用损失，在数学上证明了公共数据量与遗忘成本下降的关系（O(1/n_pub^2)级别）。

Result: 理论证明了公共数据能够显著降低遗忘成本；实验表明ALU在处理大规模数据删除请求场景下优于标准对称方法，并能抵御变分Rényi散度和成员推理攻击，即使在存在数据分布偏移的情况下仍表现良好。

Conclusion: ALU框架成功打破了传统基于噪声的机器遗忘在处理大规模请求时的效用瓶颈。通过引入公共数据，该方法能够在保证隐私安全（防御成员推理攻击）的同时，维持模型的高效用，并适应实际应用中的数据分布偏移。

Abstract: Noise-based certified machine unlearning currently faces a hard ceiling: the noise magnitude required to certify unlearning typically destroys model utility, particularly for large-scale deletion requests.
  While leveraging public data is a standard technique in differential privacy to relax this tension, its role in unlearning remains unexplored. We address this gap by introducing Asymmetric Langevin Unlearning (ALU), a framework that uses public data to mitigate privacy costs. We prove that public data injection suppresses the unlearning cost by a factor of $O(1/n_{\mathrm{pub}}^2)$, guaranteeing a strict computational advantage over retraining. This establishes a new control mechanism: practitioners can mitigate the need for high noise-and the associated utility loss-by increasing the volume of public data. Crucially, we analyze the realistic setting of distribution mismatch, explicitly characterizing how shifts between public and private sources impact utility.
  We show that ALU enables mass unlearning of constant dataset fractions -- a regime where standard symmetric methods become impractical -- while maintaining high utility. Empirical evaluations using variational Rényi divergence and membership inference attacks confirm that ALU effectively thwarts privacy attacks while preserving utility under reasonable distribution shifts.

</details>


### [108] [Optimistic Dual Averaging Unifies Modern Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11172)
*Thomas Pethick,Wanyun Xie,Roman Machacek,Volkan Cevher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SODA 提供了一个统一的优化器理论框架，通过 1/k 权重衰减调度消除了对 weight decay 超参数的调节需求，并在多种实验设置中表现出优异的通用性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的高效优化器（如 Lion, Muon 等）各具特色，缺乏统一的理论框架；同时，深度学习训练中权重衰减（weight decay）的超参数调节往往费时费力。

Method: 提出 SODA（Optimistic Dual Averaging 的泛化版本），将 Muon、Lion、AdEMAMix 和 NAdam 等主流优化器统一下纳入该框架；引入基于 1/k 调度策略的 SODA 包装器，自动处理权重衰减。

Result: 实证表明，SODA 在不同模型规模和训练跨度下，无需额外超参数调优即可持续提升基准优化器的表现。

Conclusion: SODA 作为一个通用的优化框架，通过理论支撑的 1/k 权重衰减策略，能够有效简化超参数调节（消除 weight decay 调参），并在多种规模和训练任务中实现零调优下的性能提升。

Abstract: We introduce SODA, a generalization of Optimistic Dual Averaging, which provides a common perspective on state-of-the-art optimizers like Muon, Lion, AdEMAMix and NAdam, showing that they can all be viewed as optimistic instances of this framework. Based on this framing, we propose a practical SODA wrapper for any base optimizer that eliminates weight decay tuning through a theoretically-grounded $1/k$ decay schedule. Empirical results across various scales and training horizons show that SODA consistently improves performance without any additional hyperparameter tuning.

</details>


### [109] [Oversmoothing as Representation Degeneracy in Neural Sheaf Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11178)
*Arif Dönmez,Axel Mosig,Ellen Fritsche,Katharina Koch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过颤结理论（quiver theory）为神经层扩散（NSD）提供了坚实的代数基础，将过平滑重新定义为一种在表示空间中的退化现象，并提出了通过调整层茎（stalk）维数和几何稳定性正则化来缓解该问题的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经层扩散（NSD）虽然比传统图扩散更强大，但其谐波空间（harmonic space）的表示论结构尚不明确。“过平滑”（oversmoothing）问题在层论（sheaf theory）架构下的代数本质也缺乏深入解释。

Method: 1. 将图上的胞腔层（cellular sheaves）映射为关联颤结（incidence quiver）的表示；2. 利用表示论中的直接和分解（direct-sum decomposition）分析谐波空间（harmonic space）；3. 引入基于矩映射（moment-map）的正则化项，引导层几何向平衡状态演化；4. 通过实验验证异构图任务中打破类对称性（stalk symmetry）的有效性。

Result: 1. 建立了NSD与颤结表示论的理论对应；2. 揭示了过平滑的机制：即学习到的层会向低复杂度的和项退化，导致全局截面失去判别信息；3. 证明了在$d_v = d_e$架构下存在结构性障碍，而非均匀维数架构能有效解除该限制。

Conclusion: 将过平滑视为一种表示退化现象。通过引入颤结（quiver）理论与几何不变论，证明了非对称茎维数（non-uniform stalk dimensions）能有效缓解表示退化，并提升模型在异构图（heterophilic graphs）上的稳定性和判别力。

Abstract: Neural Sheaf Diffusion (NSD) generalizes diffusion-based Graph Neural Networks by replacing scalar graph Laplacians with sheaf Laplacians whose learned restriction maps define a task-adapted geometry. While the diffusion limit of NSD is known to be the space of global sections, the representation-theoretic structure of this harmonic space remains largely implicit. We develop a quiver-theoretic interpretation of NSD by identifying cellular sheaves on graphs with representations of the associated incidence quiver. Under this correspondence, learned sheaf geometries become points in a finite-dimensional representation space. We show that direct-sum decompositions of the underlying incidence-quiver representation induce decompositions of the harmonic space reached in the diffusion limit. This gives an algebraic interpretation of oversmoothing as representation degeneration: learned sheaves may collapse toward low-complexity summands whose global sections fail to preserve discriminative information. Building on this viewpoint, we connect sheaf diffusion to stability and moment-map principles from Geometric Invariant Theory. We introduce moment-map-inspired regularizers that bias restriction maps toward balanced representation geometries, and identify a structural obstruction in equal-stalk architectures: when $d_v = d_e$, admissibility for learnable stability parameters forces the trivial all-object summand onto a stability wall. Non-uniform stalk dimensions remove this obstruction, making adaptive stability meaningful. Experiments on heterophilic benchmarks are consistent with this mechanism: breaking stalk symmetry can reduce variance or improve validation behavior, and adaptive stability becomes more effective in selected rectangular settings. Overall, our framework reframes oversmoothing as a degeneration phenomenon in the representation geometry underlying learned sheaf diffusion.

</details>


### [110] [Behavioral Mode Discovery for Fine-tuning Multimodal Generative Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11387)
*Alberta Longhini,David Emukpere,Jean-Michel Renders,Seungsu Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对生成策略的RL微调常导致模式崩溃问题，本文提出了一种基于互信息正则化的无监督模式发现框架，实现了任务性能提升与行为多样性保持的兼得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RL微调方法（如针对扩散策略的微调）在优化奖励时会导致策略的多模态分布崩溃，向单一行为模式靠拢，从而丧失了生成模型原本具备的多样性行为能力。

Method: 提出了一种基于无监督学习的模式发现框架（Unsupervised Mode Discovery），通过提取生成策略中的潜在行为模式，并利用互信息（Mutual Information）作为内部奖励（Intrinsic Reward）来正则化RL微调过程。

Result: 在机器人操作任务中，该方法相比传统微调方法展现出更高的成功率，同时能够保留更丰富、更多样化的动作输出。

Conclusion: 该研究通过引入无监督模式发现框架，成功解决了强化学习在微调生成式策略时容易导致的“模式崩溃”问题，在提升任务成功率的同时有效保持了动作的多模态特性。

Abstract: We address the problem of fine-tuning pre-trained generative policies with reinforcement learning (RL) while preserving the multimodality of their action distributions. Existing methods for RL fine-tuning of generative policies (e.g., diffusion policies) improve task performance but often collapse diverse behaviors into a single reward-maximizing mode. To mitigate this issue, we propose an unsupervised mode discovery framework that uncovers latent behavioral modes within generative policies. The discovered modes enable the use of mutual information as an intrinsic reward, regularizing RL fine-tuning to enhance task success while maintaining behavioral diversity. Experiments on robotic manipulation tasks demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms conventional fine-tuning approaches, achieving higher success rates and preserving richer multimodal action distributions.

</details>


### [111] [CATS: Cascaded Adaptive Tree Speculation for Memory-Limited LLM Inference Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11186)
*Yuning Han,Yangchenchen Jin,Dylan Zhao,Jingwei Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对边缘设备内存受限导致投机解码难以部署的问题，提出CATS框架，通过无需额外内存的“自投机”与级联验证机制，显著提升了LLM在移动端的推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投机解码方法通常假设内存充足，可同时容纳目标模型和辅助草稿模型，但在内存受限的边缘设备上该假设不成立，导致推理速度严重受限于内存带宽，亟需一种低内存占用、高效率的加速方案。

Method: 提出CATS（Cascaded Speculative Decoding）框架，这是一种自投机解码方法，通过级联验证和修正机制，在不增加峰值内存占用的前提下，利用目标模型自身进行投机采样，并针对内存受限场景优化了参数卸载模式。

Result: CATS在多个边缘设备实验中表现优秀，最高实现了5.08倍的实际墙上时间（wall-clock）加速，且无生成质量损耗；在内存受限约束下，性能相比SOTA方法提升最高达1.45倍。

Conclusion: 在内存受限的边缘设备上，通过CATS框架（级联验证与修正）可以在无需额外内存开销的前提下，显著提升大模型的推理吞吐量与生成速度，证明了该方法在资源受限场景下的有效性与优越性。

Abstract: Auto-regressive decoding in Large Language Models (LLMs) is inherently memory-bound: every generation step requires loading the model weights and intermediate results from memory (e.g., High-Bandwidth Memory (HBM) for GPU servers), making throughput bottlenecked by memory bandwidth rather than compute. Speculative decoding addresses this by enabling parallel verification of multiple draft tokens, effectively amortizing the cost of each target-model call. However, existing speculative decoding methods are designed under the assumption that HBM is sufficiently large to hold both the target model and an auxiliary draft model simultaneously -- an assumption that breaks down on memory-constrained devices such as edge platforms with limited DRAM. We analyze the inference bottleneck in this memory-limited regime and propose CATS, a self-speculative decoding framework that conducts cascaded verification and correction based on the memory budget and parameter offloading patterns on memory-limited devices. This design maximizes token acceptance rate and end-to-end speedup while keeping the peak memory footprint on the device equal to that of the target model alone. We evaluate CATS on different models across five benchmarks on real edge devices. CATS can achieve a wall-clock speedup of up to 5.08x with no degradation in generation quality, outperforming the SOTA method by up to 1.45x under edge memory constraints.

</details>


### [112] [Deep Learning for Protein Complex Prediction and Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11189)
*Ziwei Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种结合层级结构深度学习模型与高效搜索算法的方法，旨在解决蛋白质复合物建模与序列设计中的核心计算挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确建模和设计蛋白质复合物结构对于理解细胞功能和药物研发至关重要，但目前在处理结构层级建模和高维序列搜索方面仍存在挑战，需要更高效的计算方法。

Method: 1. 开发了能够捕获蛋白质结构层级特征的特定领域深度学习架构；2. 设计了高效的搜索算法，用于在大规模序列空间中寻找相互作用的同源序列，以辅助复合物结构建模和序列设计。

Result: 该研究通过整合层级建模架构与高效搜索策略，改善了蛋白质复合物结构预测的性能，并为蛋白质序列设计提供了新的计算工具。

Conclusion: 通过引入领域特定的深度学习架构和优化搜索算法，可以有效提升蛋白质复合物结构预测的准确性及蛋白质序列设计的效率。

Abstract: Accurately modeling and designing protein complex structures is a central problem in computational structural biology, with broad implications for understanding cellular function and developing therapeutics. This thesis investigates two fundamental aspects of this problem using deep learning: domain-specific architectures that capture the hierarchical nature of protein structures, and search algorithms that efficiently navigate the vast sequence spaces of protein complexes to identify interacting homologs for improving complex structure prediction and to design protein sequences.

</details>


### [113] [Variational Linear Attention: Stable Associative Memory for Long-Context Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11196)
*Vishal Pandey,Gopal Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Variational Linear Attention (VLA) 通过正则化最小二乘法重构内存更新机制，解决了线性注意力中记忆状态爆炸的问题，实现了长序列下的稳定存储、高效计算与高精度关联检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统线性注意力虽然将复杂度降低至线性，但其记忆状态的Frobenius范数随序列长度呈线性增长（O(T)），导致存储的关联信息出现严重干扰。

Method: 提出变分线性注意力（VLA），将内存更新建模为带自适应惩罚矩阵的在线正则化最小二乘问题，利用Sherman-Morrison公式维护状态，并通过归一化写入方向保持雅可比矩阵谱范数为1，确保了状态范数的自限制。

Result: VLA将$T=1000$时的记忆状态范数减少了109倍；在多查询关联检索任务中表现接近完美，显著优于DeltaNet和标准线性注意力；Triton融合内核实现了14倍的加速，并在长序列中优于Softmax注意力。

Conclusion: VLA通过将线性注意力重构为在线正则化最小二乘问题，有效地解决了传统线性注意力中记忆状态范数无界增长及由此产生的干扰问题，在大规模序列处理中展现了卓越的记忆存储与检索能力，且具备高计算效率。
