Abstract: Linear attention reduces the quadratic cost of softmax attention to $\mathcal{O}(T)$, but its memory state grows as $\mathcal{O}(T)$ in Frobenius norm, causing progressive interference between stored associations. We introduce \textbf{Variational Linear Attention} (VLA), which reframes the memory update as an online regularised least-squares problem with an adaptive penalty matrix maintained via the Sherman-Morrison rank-1 formula. We prove that normalising the write direction to unit length gives the recurrence Jacobian spectral norm exactly $1$ for all sequence lengths and head dimensions (Proposition 2), and that the state norm is self-limiting under bounded inputs (Proposition 1). Empirically, VLA reduces $\|S_t\|_F$ by $109\times$ relative to standard linear attention at $T{=}1{,}000$, achieves near-perfect exact-match accuracy on multi-query associative recall within the effective per-head memory regime ($n_\text{pairs} < d_h$), maintaining substantially higher retrieval performance than DeltaNet and standard linear attention under increasing memory load, and maintains 62\% accuracy at the per-head capacity boundary. A Triton-fused kernel achieves $14\times$ speedup over sequential Python and $\mathcal{O}(T)$ scaling, crossing below softmax attention latency at approximately 43\,000 tokens.

</details>


### [114] [Enforcing Constraints in Generative Sampling via Adaptive Correction Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11214)
*Noah Trupin,Yexiang Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种自适应投影调度方法，通过在采样过程中智能分配约束校正时机，而非简单的全量或终点投影，显著提升了受限生成采样在保真度与计算效率方面的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的硬约束执行方式（最终投影或每步投影）过于二元化。忽视了投影会改变后续更新所依赖的状态分布，导致采样结果虽可行但偏差较大。

Method: 将约束执行建模为生成回放过程中的“校正调度问题（correction scheduling problem）”。利用“一步约束缺陷（one-step constraint defect）”作为几何失配的局部信号，提出了一种自适应调度策略，根据轨迹扰动程度动态分配投影预算。

Result: 在流形采样和投影扩散模型中，该方法改善了成本-准确率平衡。在相同投影预算下，仅需现有方法25%的校正次数即可恢复71.2%的性能收益。

Conclusion: 约束执行的时机（Constraint timing）是生成式采样中的关键设计变量；仅确保可行性是不够的，必须通过优化校正调度来保持分布的动力学一致性。

Abstract: Hard constraints in generative sampling are typically enforced by projection, applied either once at the end of sampling or after every update. This binary framing overlooks a fundamental issue: projection changes the distribution of states which future updates depend on. As a result, delayed projection can produce samples that are feasible but inconsistent with the intended sampling dynamics, even after final projection. We formalize constraint enforcement as a correction scheduling problem over the generative rollout. Using one-step constraint defect as a local signal of geometric mismatch, we introduce adaptive correction scheduling, a state-dependent policy that allocates projection budget to the steps that most strongly perturb the trajectory. Terminal and stepwise projection arise as limiting cases of this family. Across controlled manifold rollouts and a learned projected diffusion sampler, adaptive scheduling improves the cost-accuracy frontier at matched projection budgets, recovering 71.2% of full stepwise benefit with 75% fewer corrections. These results show that constraint timing is a first-class design variable in generative sampling, and that enforcing feasibility alone is insufficient to preserve the intended constrained sampling dynamics.

</details>


### [115] [Leveraging RAG for Training-Free Alignment of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11217)
*John T. Halloran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有对齐算法在应对智能体攻击时效率低、效果差的问题，本文提出了 RAG-Pref。这是一种无需训练的在线方法，通过在推理时引入检索到的对比偏好信息，显著提升了模型的安全拒绝防护能力，同时兼顾了通用任务表现和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 对齐算法（通常基于训练）计算开销大，且在应对针对智能体的攻击时拒绝能力不足。

Method: 提出一种基于 RAG（检索增强生成）的对齐算法 RAG-Pref。该方法在推理阶段利用偏好样本对（preferred/dispreferred samples）进行对比信息提取，实现“在线”（无需训练）对齐，且可与离线训练方法结合。

Result: 在五种主流 LLM 上测试显示，RAG-Pref 在应对智能体攻击时的拒绝率提升了 3.7 倍（相比之下其他在线方法为 2.9 倍，纯离线方法为 1.5 倍），同时在通用偏好任务中也表现良好，且计算开销可控。

Conclusion: RAG-Pref 是一种无需训练的高效对齐方法，在不显著增加计算开销的前提下，显著增强了 LLM 对智能体攻击的拒绝能力，并能同步提升通用人类偏好对齐表现。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) alignment algorithms typically consist of post-training over preference pairs. While such algorithms are widely used to enable safety guardrails and align LLMs with general human preferences, we show that state-of-the-art alignment algorithms require significant computational resources while being far less capable of enabling refusal guardrails for recent agentic attacks. Thus, to improve refusal guardrails against such attacks without drastically increasing computational overhead, we introduce Retrieval Augmented Generation for Pref erence alignment (RAG-Pref), a simple RAG-based alignment algorithm which conditions on preferred and dispreferred samples to leverage contrastive information during inference. RAG-Pref is online (training-free), compatible with off-the-shelf packages, and, when combined with offline (training-based) alignment algorithms, enables more than an average 3.7 factor improvement in agentic attack refusals across five widely used LLMs, compared to 2.9 for other online alignment algorithms and 1.5 for offline alignment alone. We conclude by showing that, in stark contrast to other online alignment methods, RAG-Pref similarly increases performance on general human-preference alignment tasks and does not drastically increase overall computational requirements.

</details>


### [116] [ADMM-Q: An Improved Hessian-based Weight Quantizer for Post-Training Quantization of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11222)
*Ryan Lucas,Mehdi Makni,Xiang Meng,Adam Deng,Rahul Mazumder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 ADMM-Q，一种基于 ADMM 的新型 LLM 后训练量化算法。它通过分层优化和多种改进策略，在极低比特量化条件下显著提升了模型性能，且具有良好的兼容性和模块化特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型后训练量化方法（如 GPTQ、RTN）在极低比特（sub-4-bit）量化下模型性能下降严重，亟需一种能更好保持模型精度的量化策略。

Method: 提出 ADMM-Q 算法，采用基于交替方向乘子法（ADMM）的组合变体进行分层权重量化。通过算子分裂程序持续更新权重，以最小化层级重构误差并逐步强制执行量化约束，同时引入惩罚调度、预处理和局部搜索后处理等增强机制。

Result: 在使用 Qwen3-8B 实验中，ADMM-Q 取代 GPTQ 后在多项指标上表现优异：W3A16 困惑度从 12.85 降至 10.06；W4A8（SmoothQuant）从 9.29 降至 8.68；W2A4KV4（SpinQuant）从 66.11 降至 19.42。

Conclusion: ADMM-Q 是一种模块化、可组合且高效的后训练量化（PTQ）算法，通过基于 ADMM 的权重优化显著提升了在大规模量化（尤其是 4-bit 以下）下的模型性能，在多个基准测试中优于现有的 GPTQ 等方法。

Abstract: Quantization is an effective strategy to reduce the storage and computation footprint of large language models (LLMs). Post-training quantization (PTQ) is a leading approach for compressing LLMs. Popular weight quantization procedures, including GPTQ and RTN, suffer in model utility, especially at aggressive quantization levels (sub-4-bit). We propose ADMM-Q, a novel weight quantization algorithm that considers the layer-wise quantization problem. Our algorithm is based on a combinatorial variant of the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM). Our operator-splitting procedure updates weights continuously to minimize the layer-wise reconstruction error, while gradually enforcing the quantization constraints with convergence guarantees. We propose additional algorithmic enhancements (e.g., penalty scheduling, preconditioning, and a local search post-processing step) to make ADMM-Q efficient at LLM scale. ADMM-Q is modular and can be used as a drop-in replacement for any weight quantizer within existing quantization pipelines: ADMM-Q is fully composable with existing techniques including range clipping, learned or random rotations, and activation scaling. Using ADMM-Q in place of GPTQ on Qwen3-8B, we decrease WikiText-2 perplexity in: (i) the W3A16 weight-only setting (12.85 $\rightarrow$ 10.06); (ii) the W4A8 SmoothQuant procedure (9.29 $\rightarrow$ 8.68); and (iii) the W2A4KV4 SpinQuant procedure (66.11 $\rightarrow$ 19.42).

</details>


### [117] [LiBaGS: Lightweight Boundary Gap Synthesis for Targeted Synthetic Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11231)
*Abhishek Moturu,Anna Goldenberg,Babak Taati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LiBaGS 是一种轻量级、与生成器无关的合成数据筛选方法，通过综合评估合成样本的质量和分布位置，精准选择有助于提升模型决策边界性能的样本，从而显著提高模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据仅在填补训练分布的缺失部分时才有效；然而，现有方法往往盲目增加数据或仅关注不确定性，缺乏对数据的针对性筛选，导致效率低下或冗余。

Method: LiBaGS（Lightweight, generator-agnostic selection）：1. 评分机制：结合决策边界邻近度、预测不确定性、真实数据密度及支持有效性来评估合成样本；2. 分配规则：采用边界间隙分配规则（boundary-gap allocation rule）专注于决策边界处的稀疏区域；3. 辅助机制：包含边际价值停止规则（marginal-value stopping rule）以优化数据量，并引入软标签标注与多样性目标以提升质量。

Result: 在实验中，LiBaGS 在分类准确率上超过了经典的过采样、硬增强、不确定性与密度消融实验，以及其他的目标生成选择标准。

Conclusion: LiBaGS 是一种高效且通用的合成数据筛选方法。通过关注决策边界附近的稀疏区域，并结合多样性和样本质量约束，该方法能有效提升模型在下游任务中的精度，优于传统的过采样、硬增强及现有的选择策略。

Abstract: Synthetic data is useful only when the added samples fill missing parts of the training distribution that matter for the downstream task. We introduce LiBaGS, a lightweight, generator-agnostic method for targeted synthetic training data selection. LiBaGS scores candidate synthetic samples by combining decision-boundary proximity, predictive uncertainty, real-data density, and support validity, so that selected samples are both informative and likely to remain on the real data manifold. We then use a boundary-gap allocation rule that targets sparse but realistic decision-boundary neighborhoods, rather than simply adding more data or selecting only the most uncertain candidates. LiBaGS also learns when enough synthetic samples have been added through a marginal-value stopping rule, assigns softer labels near ambiguous boundaries, and uses a diversity objective to avoid redundant near-duplicate selections. Experiments show that LiBaGS improves accuracy over classical oversampling, hard augmentation, uncertainty and density ablations, and targeted-generation selection criteria.

</details>


### [118] [A Comparative Study of Model Selection Criteria for Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11233)
*Ali Soltani,Gabriel Kronberger,Fabricio Olivetti de Franca,Mattia Billa,Alessandro Lucantonio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估了多种模型选择准则（AIC, AICc, BIC, MDL, Bootstrap）在符号回归中的表现，研究发现MDL与BIC在实现高泛化能力和准确识别真实模型方面最有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号回归（SR）中，模型选择对于平衡准确性与复杂性并确保在未见数据上具有低期望误差至关重要。尽管遗传规划常产生帕累托最优候选解，但如何自动选择泛化能力强的模型仍是一个难题，且现有研究缺乏对不同信息论和贝叶斯准则的全面性能对比。

Method: 对七个带有高斯噪声的合成数据集进行系统性实证比较；使用遗传规划（GP）生成帕累托最优候选解；比较的准则包括：AIC、AICc、BIC、MDL以及Efron的自助法（Bootstrap）估计。通过扰动真实函数生成候选表达式，评估其泛化误差和选择真实表达式的概率。

Result: MDL表现出了最强的综合性能，在识别最低测试误差模型和选择真实底层函数方面具有优势；BIC在选择真实表达式方面表现同样出色。

Conclusion: MDL在大多数数据集上始终能识别出具有最低测试误差和最短长度的模型。尽管没有单一标准在所有情况下都占主导地位，但MDL和BIC在选择真实表达式（ground-truth expressions）方面表现出最高的概率。

Abstract: Effective model selection is critical in symbolic regression (SR) to identify mathematical expressions that balance accuracy and complexity, and have low expected error on unseen data. Many modern implementations of genetic programming (GP) for SR generate a set of Pareto optimal candidate solutions, but reliable automatic selection of solutions that generalize well remains an open issue. Current literature offers various information-theoretic and Bayesian approaches, yet comprehensive comparisons of their performance across different data regimes are limited. This study presents a systematic empirical comparison of widely used selection criteria: the Akaike information criterion (AIC), the corrected AIC (AICc), the Bayesian information criterion (BIC), minimum description length (MDL), as well as Efron's bootstrap estimate for the in-sample prediction error on seven synthetic datasets with Gaussian noise. We rank candidate expressions generated by perturbing ground-truth functions to assess generalization error and selection probability of the ground-truth expression. Our findings reveal that MDL consistently identifies models with the lowest test error and the shortest length across most datasets. While no single criterion dominates all results, MDL and BIC produced the highest probability of selecting the ground-truth expressions.

</details>


### [119] [Internalizing Curriculum Judgment for LLM Reinforcement Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11235)
*Han Zheng,Yining Ma,Karthick Gunasekaran,Bharathan Balaji,Zheng Du,Shiv Vitaladevuni,Cathy Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: METIS 是一种 LLM 强化微调的元认知课程学习框架，通过内在的自我判断取代外部规则，实现资源分配优化与性能大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有 LLM 强化学习微调（RFT）中课程学习依赖手动启发式规则或外部辅助模型，导致与策略训练动态不匹配的问题。

Method: 提出 METIS 框架，通过预测提示词内的奖励方差（within-prompt reward variance）来衡量信息量（informativeness），并利用轻量级上下文学习（In-context learning）实现训练资源的动态分配，同时联合优化 RFT 奖励与自我判断奖励，从而构建闭环学习机制。

Result: 在数学推理、代码生成及工具调用等多个领域表现优异，收敛速度提升高达 67%。

Conclusion: METIS 框架通过元认知式的自我判断机制，无需人工启发式规则即可实现高效的课程学习，显著提升了 LLM 在 RFT 任务中的性能与收敛速度。

Abstract: In LLM Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (RFT), curriculum learning drives both efficiency and performance. Yet, current methods externalize curriculum judgment via handcrafted heuristics or auxiliary models, risking misalignment with the policy's training dynamics. In this paper, we introduce METIS (METacognitive Internalized Self-judgment), a novel framework that internalizes curriculum judgment as a native capability. Leveraging a critical observation that within-prompt reward variance effectively gauges prompt informativeness, METIS predicts this metric based on recent training outcomes as lightweight in-context learning examples. This intrinsic self-judgment then dynamically dictates the training allocation. Moreover, METIS closes the loop between judgment and optimization by jointly optimizing the standard RFT rewards and a self-judgment reward. This allows the policy to learn what to learn next, as a form of metacognition. Across extensive discrete and continuous RFT benchmarks from mathematical reasoning, code generation, to agentic function-calling, METIS consistently delivers superior performance while accelerating convergence by up to 67%. By bypassing handcrafted heuristics and auxiliary models, our work establishes a simple, closed-loop, and highly efficient curriculum internalization paradigm for LLM reinforcement fine-tuning.

</details>


### [120] [DeconDTN-Toolkit: A Library for Evaluation and Enhancement of Robustness to Provenance Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11237)
*Yongsen Tan,Zhecheng Sheng,Xiruo Ding,Serguei V. S. Pakhomov,Trevor Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过形式化定义“溯源偏移”，揭示了其对传统学习方法的破坏力，并推出了 \textsc{DeconDTN-Toolkit} 用于系统的评估与修复，从而提升模型在溯源变化下的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然现有研究探讨了多种分布偏移，但关于“溯源偏移”（数据源与标签之间的关系在部署时发生改变）的研究仍然不足，缺乏系统的理解与应对手段。

Method: 1. 建立溯源偏移（provenance shift）、反事实不变性与不变学习之间的形式化联系。 2. 推导出一个旨在实现鲁棒性的学习目标。 3. 开发 \textsc{DeconDTN-Toolkit}，用于模拟不同程度的溯源偏移并评估现有算法的鲁棒性。

Result: 1. 证实了ERM在面临溯源偏移时模型性能会显著下降。 2. 提出了一个鲁棒的分布外（OOD）性能指标。 3. 通过 \textsc{DeconDTN-Toolkit} 对现有主流算法进行了全面的基准测试与评估。

Conclusion: 本文将“溯源偏移”形式化，揭示了经验风险最小化（ERM）在此类偏移下的脆弱性，并提供了一套兼顾理论基础与实践工具的解决方案，为解决溯源引起的混淆问题奠定了基础。

Abstract: Despite the burgeoning body of work on distribution shifts, provenance shift-where the relationship between data source and label changes at deployment-remains poorly understood and under-addressed. In this paper, we establish a formal connection between provenance shift, counterfactual invariance, and invariant learning to derive a learning objective for robustness. We then introduce \textsc{DeconDTN-Toolkit}, a specialized evaluation and remediation suite designed to simulate provenance shifts of varying degrees while maintaining the training protocol and the infrastructure of existing benchmarks. We reveal the vulnerability of Empirical Risk Minimization under provenance shift, introduce a robust out-of-distribution performance indicator, and conduct a comprehensive evaluation on existing algorithms. Our work provides both the theoretical grounding and the practical tools necessary to characterize the problem of confounding by provenance, and implementations of methods to mitigate it.

</details>


### [121] [Support-Proximity Augmented Diffusion Estimation for Offline Black-Box Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11246)
*Yonghan Yang,Ye Yuan,Zipeng Sun,Linfeng Du,Bowei He,Haolun Wu,Can Chen,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPADE 是一种创新的黑盒优化框架，通过扩散模型建模前向似然，并引入校准与正则化约束，解决了 OOD 问题，刷新了多项优化基准记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决离线黑盒优化中严重的分布外（OOD）外推问题；现有的逆向方法存在映射不适定性，而现有的正向方法缺乏有效的分布表达能力和不确定性量化。

Method: 提出 SPADE（支撑邻近增强扩散估计）框架，利用条件生成扩散模型对前向似然 p(y|x) 进行建模，包含两个核心增强：1. 校准扩散估计模块（强制统计矩和成对排名的全局一致性）；2. 支撑邻近正则化机制（通过 kNN 密度估计隐式地将数据分布 p(x) 约束纳入框架）。

Result: SPADE 在 Design-Bench 任务以及大语言模型（LLM）数据混合优化基准测试中均达到了最先进（SOTA）的性能。

Conclusion: SPADE 通过结合校准扩散估计和支撑邻近正则化，有效解决了离线黑盒优化中的分布外（OOD）泛化问题，在多项基准测试中表现出色。

Abstract: Offline black-box optimization aims to discover novel designs with high property scores using only a static dataset, a task fundamentally challenged by the out-of-distribution (OOD) extrapolation problem. Existing approaches typically bifurcate into inverse methods, which struggle with the ill-posed nature of mapping scores to designs, and forward methods, which often lack the distributional expressivity to quantify uncertainty effectively. In this work, we propose SPADE (Support-Proximity Augmented Diffusion Estimation), a novel framework that reimagines forward surrogate modeling through the lens of conditional generative modeling. SPADE models the forward likelihood p(y|x) using a diffusion model, but with two critical enhancements to tailor it for optimization: (1) a Calibrated Diffusion Estimation module that enforces global consistency in statistical moments and pairwise rankings, and (2) a Support-Proximity Regularization mechanism that implicitly internalizes the data manifold constraint p(x) via kNN-based density estimation. Theoretically, we prove that our regularization is first-order equivalent to maximizing a Bayesian posterior with a valid design prior. Empirically, SPADE achieves state-of-the-art performance across Design-Bench tasks and an LLM data mixture optimization benchmark.

</details>


### [122] [A Proof-of-Concept Simulation-Driven Digital Twin Framework for Decision-Aware Diabetes Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11247)
*Zarrin Monirzadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于糖尿病建模的数字孪生概念框架，通过合成数据增强实现可解释的动态轨迹仿真和干预分析，为医疗决策支持系统提供了基础研究模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预测模型往往侧重于结果预测，缺乏对可解释的动态轨迹和决策干预影响的模拟。研究旨在构建一个能生成可解释仿真轨迹的数字孪生框架，以支持更深层的决策分析。

Method: 提出了一种概念验证（proof-of-concept）的数字孪生框架，利用基准临床数据进行仿真驱动的糖尿病建模，结合了合成时间序列增强技术，并对连续血糖监测（CGM）数据进行分析与反事实场景仿真。

Result: 通过公共数据集和受控合成场景的联合评估，展示了该框架在模拟时间行为和干预效果方面的能力，验证了其在复杂临床分析中的应用潜力。

Conclusion: 该框架验证了将预测模型与反事实仿真整合用于决策分析的可行性，为医疗领域仿真驱动的数字孪生系统研究奠定了基础，但目前尚不具备临床应用水平。

Abstract: This paper presents a proof-of-concept digital twin framework for simulation-driven diabetes modeling using benchmark clinical data, synthetic temporal augmentation, and illustrative continuous glucose monitoring (CGM) analysis. Unlike traditional predictive models, the framework focuses on generating interpretable simulated trajectories rather than clinically validated outcomes. Evaluation is conducted using a public dataset combined with controlled synthetic scenarios to illustrate temporal behavior and intervention effects. Results illustrate the feasibility of integrating prediction with counterfactual simulation for decision-aware analysis. This work does not claim clinical readiness but provides a foundation for future research on simulation-driven digital twin systems in healthcare.

</details>


### [123] [Curriculum Learning-Guided Progressive Distillation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11260)
*Jincheng Cao,Fanzhi Zeng,Leqi Liu,Aryan Mokhtari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大模型蒸馏中小模型效果不佳的问题，CLPD框架通过整合“数据循序渐进”与“教师能力递进”两种核心策略，显著提升了蒸馏质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的知识蒸馏方法忽视了训练数据的学习顺序及教师与学生模型之间的容量失配问题，导致“强教师未能带来更好学生”的反直觉现象，限制了小模型的性能。

Method: 提出CLPD（Curriculum Learning-Guided Progressive Distillation）框架：1. 显性课程：按难度由易到难组织训练数据；2. 隐性课程：在训练过程中逐步引入能力更强的教师模型。这两个维度协同作用，通过引导学生模型从简单到复杂逐步学习来优化蒸馏过程。

Result: 在推理基准测试中，CLPD的表现持续优于标准蒸馏、单一数据排序策略及单一教师调度策略，证明了联合优化数据顺序与教师能力的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过联合考量数据难度编排（Curriculum Learning）与教师模型能力递进（Progressive Scheduling），可以有效缓解蒸馏中的“强教师未必能产出优秀学生”的问题，从而显著提升小模型的推理能力。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation is a key technique for transferring the capabilities of large language models (LLMs) into smaller, more efficient student models. Existing distillation approaches often overlook two critical factors: the learning order of training data and the capacity mismatch between teacher and student models. This oversight limits distillation performance, as manifested by the counter-intuitive phenomenon where stronger teachers fail to produce better students. In this work, we propose Curriculum Learning-Guided Progressive Distillation (CLPD), a unified framework that explicitly accounts for both factors by aligning data difficulty with teacher strength. CLPD constructs an explicit curriculum by organizing training examples from easy to hard, while simultaneously applying an implicit curriculum over supervision signals by progressively scheduling teachers of increasing capacity. Our framework is modular and can be integrated into standard distillation algorithms with minimal overhead. Empirical results on the reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that CLPD consistently outperforms standard distillation, data ordering alone, and teacher scheduling alone across multiple settings. These findings highlight the importance of jointly considering data ordering and teacher capacity when distilling reasoning abilities into small language models.

</details>


### [124] [Latent Chain-of-Thought Improves Structured-Data Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11262)
*Carson Dudley,Samet Oymak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对结构化数据的“潜在思维链”方法，通过在Transformer中引入循环反馈机制增加测试时计算量，实验表明该方法能有效提升模型在时序和表格数据上的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受大语言模型中通过思维链（Chain-of-thought）及增加测试时计算量来提升推理能力的启发，探究这种机制在处理时序和表格数据时的有效性。

Method: 提出一种循环方案：结构化数据Transformer在初始前向计算后，将查询位置的隐藏状态压缩为“反馈令牌（feedback tokens）”并附加回输入中，通过多轮迭代实现潜在思维链计算。对比了普通基线、等效深度基线以及权重共享的循环Transformer。

Result: 在36个数据集上进行评估，其中8/9的时序数据集和22/27的表格数据集表现提升，平均收益分别为10.99%和5.31%，相较于同等深度的对比模型表现最优。

Conclusion: 实验证明，“潜在思维链”（latent chain-of-thought）是针对结构化数据（时序与表格数据）扩展测试时计算能力的一项有效策略，显著优于传统的静态模型结构。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought and more broadly test-time compute are known to augment the expressive capabilities of language models and have led to major innovations in reasoning. Motivated by this success, this paper explores latent chain-of-thought as well as the impact of depth and looping for time-series and tabular data. We propose a recurrent scheme in which a structured-data transformer, after an initial forward pass, compresses its query-position hidden states into feedback tokens that are appended to the input and processed again, allowing multiple rounds of latent computation before prediction. We compare CoT models against a same-depth no-CoT baseline, a deeper baseline matched to the CoT model in effective depth, and a looped transformer with weight-tied recurrence but no additional chain-of-thought tokens. Across 36 datasets in time-series forecasting and tabular prediction, latent chain-of-thought improves over the baseline on 8/9 time-series datasets (+10.99\% average gain) and 22/27 tabular datasets (+5.31\% average gain). Across both settings, the CoT models perform the best on average. These results demonstrate that chain-of-thought is a useful axis for scaling test-time compute for structured data.

</details>


### [125] [Quotient-Categorical Representations for Bellman-Compatible Average-Reward Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11289)
*Ege C. Kaya,Aliasghar Pourghani,Vijay Gupta,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于商空间的分布强化学习框架，有效解决了平均回报RL中偏好平移不确定性导致的病态问题，并证明了相应算法的收敛性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均回报强化学习中，偏差仅定义在加性常数上，导致直接应用分布强化学习方法时出现病态（ill-posed），需要一种能够处理该平移对称性的数学框架。

Method: 1. 构建商空间（Quotient-space）并将偏好函数在平移不变的条件下进行定义；2. 采用分类参数化（Categorical parameterization）来保持这种对称性；3. 定义投影平均回报分布算子（Projected average-reward distributional operator）；4. 在均值场意义下分析异步迭代，并针对未知增益场景引入在线增益估计器。

Result: 1. 证明了投影平均回报分布算子在坐标Cramér度量下是良定义的且是非扩展的（non-expansive）；2. 在理想中心化奖励设置下，证明了 temporal-difference 更新在 i.i.d. 和马尔可夫采样下的几乎处处收敛性；3. 证明了耦合增益估计方案在马尔可夫采样下的收敛性。

Conclusion: 文章提出了商空间表征与分类参数化方法，成功解决了平均回报强化学习中偏差函数的平移不确定性问题，并证明了该框架下的算子性质及收敛性。

Abstract: Average-reward reinforcement learning requires estimating the gain and the bias, which is defined only up to an additive constant. This makes direct distributional analogues ill-posed on the real line. We introduce a quotient-space formulation in which state-indexed bias laws are identified up to a common translation, together with a categorical parameterization that respects this symmetry. On this quotient-categorical space, we define a projected average-reward distributional operator and show that it is well-defined, non-expansive in a coordinate Cramér metric, and admits fixed points. We then study sampled recursions whose mean-field maps are asynchronous relaxations of this operator. In an idealized centered-reward setting, a one-state temporal-difference update enjoys almost sure convergence together with finite-iteration residual bounds under both i.i.d. and Markovian sampling. When the gain is unknown, we augment the recursion with an online gain estimator, and prove non-expansiveness and Markovian convergence of the resulting coupled scheme. Finally, we show that synchronous exact updates are gain-independent at the quotient-law level, isolating a structural contrast between ideal quotient distributions and practical fixed-grid categorical representations.

</details>


### [126] [Optimal Representations for Generalized Contrastive Learning with Imbalanced Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11291)
*Thuan Nguyen,Shuchin Aeron,D. Richard Brown,Prakash Ishwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文理论分析了对比学习中类别不平衡带来的几何扭曲，揭示了从类均值坍缩演变为“少数类坍缩”（MC）的机制及阈值条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注类别平衡情况下的“神经坍缩”（Neural Collapse, NC），但缺乏对类别不平衡（Class Imbalance）情形下对比学习表示几何结构的理论刻画。

Method: 利用数学证明方法，从理论上推导对比学习在类不平衡条件下的最优表示几何特征，分析了广义对比学习损失函数的结构及其对负样本数量的依赖，并通过数值模拟验证了理论推导。

Result: 1. 证明了在不平衡类中，同类样本仍会坍缩至类均值；2. 揭示了最优类别间几何结构受类比例对称性控制；3. 提出了“少数类坍缩”（MC）现象，并给出了触发该现象的不平衡度阈值条件。

Conclusion: 在对比学习中，类别不平衡会导致表示几何结构的改变。随着不平衡程度加剧，会触发“少数类坍缩”（Minority Collapse, MC）现象，即少数类样本收敛至同一个向量。这种几何形态可通过凸优化问题求解。

Abstract: In this paper, we provide a computable characterization of the geometry of optimal representations in Contrastive Learning (CL) when the classes are imbalanced. When classes are balanced and the representation dimension is greater than the number of classes, it is well-known that the optimal representations exhibit Neural Collapse (NC), i.e., representations from the same class collapse to their class means and the class means form an Equiangular Tight Frame (ETF). For imbalanced classes and a large, generalized family of CL losses, we prove that the optimal representations of all samples from the same class collapse to their class means and their geometry exhibits an angular symmetry structure that is determined by the relative class proportions. In general, we show that the geometry can be determined by solving a convex optimization problem. Exploiting this symmetry structure, we analytically investigate a special case where class imbalance is extreme and prove that CL exhibits a phenomenon called Minority Collapse (MC) where all samples from the minority classes (classes with small probabilities) collapse into a single vector, whenever the class imbalance exceeds a threshold, which in turn depends on the regularity properties of the CL loss used and on the number of negative samples. Numerical results are provided to illustrate these phenomena and corroborate the theoretical results. We conclude by identifying a number of open problems.

</details>


### [127] [Primal Generation, Dual Judgment: Self-Training from Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11299)
*Yizhu Jiao,Ruixiang Zhang,Richard Bai,Jiawei Han,Ronan Collobert,Yizhe Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DuST 提出了一种通过学习候选程序间相对正确性的代码生成训练框架，利用 GRPO 从对偶判断空间中提取信息，显著提升了模型的代码生成与判断能力，使得单次采样推理达到多次采样的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的代码生成通常仅依赖原初空间中的稀疏执行反馈（Pass/Fail），浪费了推理阶段采样过程中产生的丰富比较信息（对偶判断空间）。研究旨在利用这些信息作为更强的训练信号，提升模型的推理与纠错能力。

Method: 提出 DuST（Dual Self-Training）框架：1. 从模型分布中采样多个候选程序；2. 通过沙箱执行获取执行结果；3. 保留包含成功与失败的样本组；4. 使用 GRPO 算法对模型进行辨别式训练，使其学习根据执行正确性对候选程序进行排序。

Result: 在 LiveCodeBench 上对 5 个不同规模的模型进行测试，DuST 实现了显著提升：Qwen3-30B-Thinking 的判断质量提升 +6.2 NDCG，Pass@1 提升 +3.1，Best-of-4 准确率提升 +4.1。训练后的单次采样性能等同于基准模型的 Best-of-4 水平。

Conclusion: DuST 框架通过强化对偶判断空间（候选程序间的相对正确性）的学习，能够有效提升模型的生成与判断能力。实验证明，基于 GRPO 的强化学习是实现对偶空间知识迁移至原初生成过程的关键机制，单次推理性能即可达到基准模型多次采样的水平。

Abstract: Code generation is typically trained in the primal space of programs: a model produces a candidate solution and receives sparse execution feedback, often a single pass/fail bit. Test-time scaling enriches the inference procedure by sampling multiple candidates and judging among them, but the comparative information this process reveals is discarded after inference. We argue that this information defines a dual judgment space that provides a far richer training signal: the model learns not from an isolated success or failure, but from the relative correctness structure across its own plausible attempts, identifying which succeed, which fail, and what distinguishes them. We introduce DuST (Dual Self-Training), a framework for self-training from the dual judgment space. DuST samples candidate programs from the model's own distribution, labels them through sandbox execution, retains groups containing both successes and failures, and trains the model to rank candidates by execution correctness using GRPO. The objective is purely discriminative: the model is never directly rewarded for generating correct programs. Dual self-training improves both judgment and generation. Across five models spanning two families and three scales (4B to 30B), DuST consistently improves Best-of-4 test-time scaling on LiveCodeBench. For Qwen3-30B-Thinking on LiveCodeBench v6, judgment quality improves by +6.2 NDCG, single-sample pass@1 improves by +3.1, and Best-of-4 accuracy improves by +4.1. The trained model's single rollout matches the base model's Best-of-4 performance. SFT on the same ranking data improves judgment without improving generation, confirming that on-policy RL is the mechanism that transfers dual-space learning back into primal generation.

</details>


### [128] [A Theory of Time-Sensitive Language Generation: Sparse Hallucination Beats Mode Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11302)
*Atul Ganju,Travis McVoy,Shaddin Dughmi,Shang-Hua Teng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言生成中的“时效性”问题，证明了仅凭最终一致性无法实现及时生成，通过允许随时间消失的幻觉率，可在超线性时间阈值内实现最优的字符串生成顺序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在语言模型中，除追求生成内容的广度（breadth）外，还应重视生成的时间顺序（timeliness），即高排名的字符串应更早被生成，以符合机器学习中优先输出“更简单”或“更合理”结果的归纳偏置。

Method: 基于 Kleinberg 和 Wei 提出的全局偏序排序，引入“及时性（timeliness）”约束。通过数学建模分析最终一致性生成器（eventually consistent generators）的局限性，并探索允许幻觉率随时间消失（vanishing hallucination rate）作为放宽方案，证明了超线性截止时间下的最优性。

Result: 证明了在严格的生成时限要求下，最终一致的生成器无法满足及时性；通过引入消失的幻觉率，成功在超线性截止时间函数下实现了最优生成密度；最后证明了该结论在线性截止时间下失效，确立了边界条件。

Conclusion: 对于最终一致的生成器，及时生成（timely generation）在强意义下是不可能的；但在允许幻觉率随时间消失的放宽条件下，对于任何超线性截止时间函数，均可实现最优密度；这一结果已达到理论紧度，即线性截止时间无法实现。

Abstract: We study language generation in the limit under a global preference ordering on strings, as introduced by Kleinberg and Wei. As in [arXiv:2504.14370, arXiv:2511.05295], we aim for \emph{breadth}, but impose an additional requirement of timeliness: higher-ranked strings should be generated earlier. A string is then only credited if it is generated before a deadline, where its deadline is defined by a function that maps a string's rank in the target language to the time by which it must be produced. This is in keeping with a central consideration in machine learning, where inductive bias favors ``simpler'' or ``more plausible'' outputs, all else being equal. We show that timely generation is impossible in a strong sense for eventually consistent generators -- the protagonists of most prior related work. Under what is perhaps the mildest natural relaxation of consistency, a hallucination rate that vanishes over time, we show that we can circumvent our impossibility result. In particular, we can achieve optimal density with respect to any superlinear deadline function. We also show this is tight by ruling out timely generation with linear deadlines and vanishing hallucination rate.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [ECHO: Continuous Hierarchical Memory for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10993)
*Yanbin Hu,Jin Cui,Jiayi Lu,Ruixuan Yang,Jun Ye,Boran Zhao,Xingyu Chen,Xuguang Lan,Pengju Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ECHO 是一种新型视觉-语言-动作（VLA）记忆架构，通过在双曲空间内分层组织和持续巩固经验，显著提升了机器人处理复杂长程任务的检索效率与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型的记忆架构多为扁平化存储，缺乏结构先验，导致在处理长程操作任务时检索效率低下且对未见任务的组合泛化能力不足。

Method: 1. 利用双曲自编码器将 VLA 的隐藏状态映射至连续的双曲空间；2. 结合双曲度量和蕴含约束机制，构建语义记忆树支持分层检索；3. 设计“后台巩固机制”（通过几何插值和结构分裂）对记忆树进行实时优化和虚拟记忆合成。

Result: 在 LIBERO 基准测试中，相较于 π₀ 基准模型，执行成功率提升了 12.8%，并在跨套件未见长程任务上表现出更强的组合泛化能力。

Conclusion: ECHO 通过在双曲空间中构建分层结构，有效解决了 VLA 模型在长程任务中记忆存储效率低和组合泛化能力弱的问题，为机器人操作中的记忆增强提供了高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Memory capacity is a critical factor determining the performance of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models in long-horizon manipulation tasks. Existing memory-augmented architectures primarily rely on linear or flat storage, lacking structural priors for manipulation categories and hierarchical organization. This deficiency hinders efficient experience retrieval and limits generalization to unseen long-horizon task compositions. Inspired by the hierarchical organization of human experience, we propose ECHO (Experience Consolidation and Hierarchical Organization), a novel memory framework operating within a Continuous Hierarchical Space. By employing a hyperbolic autoencoder, ECHO maps VLA hidden states into this space. Leveraging hyperbolic metrics and entailment constraint mechanisms, experience vectors are organized into a semantic memory tree that supports efficient top-down retrieval. In parallel, a background consolidation mechanism continuously refines the memory tree through geometric interpolation and structural splitting, supporting virtual memory synthesis in the continuous space. We integrate ECHO into the $π_0$ foundation model. Evaluations on LIBERO and preliminary real-world experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, notably achieving a 12.8% absolute improvement in execution success rate over the $π_0$ baseline on LIBERO-Long, while improving compositional generalization on cross-suite unseen long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [130] [ForceFlow: Learning to Feel and Act via Contact-Driven Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11048)
*Shuoheng Zhang,Yifu Yuan,Hongyao Tang,Yan Zheng,Qiaojun Yu,Pengyi Li,Guowei Huang,Helong Huang,Xingyue Quan,Jianye Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForceFlow是一种基于流匹配的力感知机器人模仿学习框架，通过视觉与力的层级解耦及非对称多模态融合机制，显著提升了机器人在接触密集型任务中的执行能力与泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模仿学习方法在处理接触密集型（contact-rich）操作任务时，难以应对复杂的接触动力学，缺乏一种简单且稳健的、能同时处理多模态观测并具备泛化能力的力控框架。

Method: 1. 提出基于流匹配（Flow Matching）的ForceFlow框架；2. 设计非对称多模态融合架构（将力信号作为全局调节信号）；3. 采用联合预测范式增强力理解；4. 实施层级任务分解：视觉主导的接近阶段与力主导的接触交互阶段，并通过“视觉到力（V2F）”交接机制进行解耦。

Result: 在六项真实世界接触密集型任务中，ForceFlow的成功率较基准模型ForceVLA提升了37%，同时显著降低了实现成本，并表现出优秀的力信号预测能力及零样本（OOD）泛化性能。

Conclusion: ForceFlow通过将接触任务解耦为视觉引导和接触执行阶段，并利用流匹配（Flow Matching）架构和非对称多模态融合，有效解决了接触密集型任务中的力控难题，在真实机器人操作中展现了高成功率和强大的泛化能力。

Abstract: Existing imitation learning methods enable robots to interact autonomously with the physical environment. However, contact-rich manipulation tasks remain a significant challenge due to complex contact dynamics that demand high-precision force feedback and control. Although recent efforts have attempted to integrate force/torque sensing into policies, how to build a simple yet effective framework that achieves robust generalization under multimodal observations remains an open question. In this paper, we propose ForceFlow, a force-aware reactive framework built upon flow matching. For contact-stage policy design, we investigate force signal fusion mechanisms and adopt an asymmetric multimodal fusion architecture that treats force as a global regulatory signal, combined with a joint prediction paradigm that enhances the policy's understanding of instantaneous force and historical information, thereby achieving deep coupling between force and motion. For task-level hierarchical decomposition, we divide manipulation into a vision-dominant approach stage (VLM-based pointing for target localization) and a touch-dominant interaction stage (force-driven contact execution), with a Vision-to-Force (V2F) handover mechanism that explicitly decouples spatial generalization from contact regulation. Experimental results across six real-world contact-rich tasks demonstrate that ForceFlow achieves a 37% success rate improvement over the strong baseline ForceVLA while maintaining significantly lower cost. Moreover, ForceFlow exhibits accurate force signal prediction and demonstrates superior performance in contact force self-regulation and zero-shot out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization.

</details>


### [131] [SEVO: Semantic-Enhanced Virtual Observation for Robust VLA Manipulation via Active Illumination and Data-Centric Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11114)
*Tianchonghui Fang,Yuan Zhuang,Fei Miao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为解决低成本机器人模型泛化能力差的问题，SEVO通过改进观测设计（RGB预处理+环境语义增强）和强化数据采集的多样性，显著提升了机器人在新环境下的操作稳健性，无需更改模型架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有的VLA和模仿学习模型在低成本硬件上训练时，在受控环境下表现良好，但部署到新环境时泛化能力极差（甚至近乎失效），现有评估基准未能反映真实部署的困难。

Method: 提出SEVO（语义增强虚拟观测）方法，通过三个机制处理原始RGB流：1. 使用覆盖全工作空间的身体固定相机；2. 采用主动红光谱照明归一化物体外观；3. 叠加实时YOLO语义分割以提供背景不变线索。此外，强调在数据采集时系统性改变灯光、背景和干扰物的重要性。

Result: 在训练环境下，SEVO使ACT和SmolVLA策略的成功率分别达到95%和83%；在未见过的测试环境中，泛化成功率分别保持在85%和75%。相比之下，未采用SEVO的基线模型在测试环境中的成功率会从训练时的70%-75%剧降至30%-35%。

Conclusion: 相比单纯的模型规模化，更有原则的观测设计（SEVO）和训练过程中的环境多样性对于提升低成本机器人模型的泛化能力更为关键。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) and imitation-learning policies trained via community toolchains on low-cost hardware frequently fail when deployed outside the training environment. Existing evaluations, including the original ACT and SmolVLA benchmarks, demonstrate high success rates under controlled, fixed backgrounds, yet community practitioners report near-zero transfer to new environments. We present SEVO (Semantic-Enhanced Virtual Observation), a data-centric approach that improves cross-environment manipulation robustness without modifying the policy architecture. SEVO transforms the raw RGB camera stream through three mechanisms: (1) body-fixed cameras whose combined fields of view cover the full manipulation workspace, (2) active red-spectrum illumination that physically normalizes object appearance, and (3) real-time YOLO segmentation overlay that provides a background-invariant semantic cue. Critically, we show that a diversified data collection protocol (systematically varying lighting, backgrounds, and distractors during teleoperation) is the single most important factor for generalization. We target transparent water bottles, objects that visually blend with their surroundings, and select a simple pick-and-place task to enable hundreds of controlled real-robot trials across two mobile platforms. The full pipeline achieves 95% grasp success with ACT and 83% with SmolVLA in the training environment, transferring to novel environments at 85% and 75%. Without SEVO, the same policies achieve only 75%/70% in training and collapse to 30-35% in novel environments. Our results demonstrate that principled observation design and environmental diversity during data collection, not model scaling, enable low-cost robots to operate reliably in everyday household environments.

</details>


### [132] [ASIP-Planner: Adaptive Planning for UAV Surface Inspection in Partially Known Indoor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11119)
*Hanyu Jin,Zhefan Xu,Haoyu Shen,Xinming Han,Kanlong Ye,Kenji Shimada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对室内环境巡检中先验地图不准确导致的遮挡问题，本文提出了一种融合全局路径规划与局部自适应视角调整的无人机巡检框架，在提升覆盖完整性的同时优化了巡检效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室内无人机巡检依赖先验模型，但实际环境常存在临时障碍物导致遮挡和覆盖质量下降，且传统方法在部分已知场景下缺乏应对环境差异的动态调整能力。

Method: 提出了一种自适应无人机巡检框架，包含：1. 基于线段的全局覆盖规划器（将巡检目标聚类为平面，生成优化的观测序列）；2. 面向巡检的局部视角自适应模块（在线调整视图方向并生成无碰撞轨迹，以缓解遮挡导致的覆盖缺失）。

Result: 仿真实验表明，该方法相比基准方案在实现近乎完整覆盖的同时，显著缩短了巡检轨迹长度；真实场景飞行实验验证了其产出数据可有效支持后续分析。

Conclusion: 该框架通过融合全局规划与局部自适应调整，显著提升了在部分已知室内环境下的无人机巡检效率、适应性和覆盖完整性，减少了因环境差异导致的遮挡问题。

Abstract: Indoor infrastructure inspection, such as tunnels and industrial facilities, requires systematic surface coverage to ensure that all inspection targets are properly observed. Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) offer an alternative to manual inspection by conducting map-guided surface inspection using prior structural models. However, in practice, indoor inspection often relies on floorplan-derived reference maps that may not reflect unforeseen obstacles, such as temporary structures or equipment, leading to occluded viewpoints and degraded inspection quality. Existing coverage planning methods typically assume a fully known inspection environment and perform deterministic global viewpoint optimization based on accurate prior maps, making them vulnerable to environmental discrepancies during execution. This work presents an adaptive UAV inspection framework for partially known structured indoor environments. The proposed method integrates a segment-based global coverage planner with an inspection-oriented local view-angle adaptation module. The global planner organizes planar inspection targets into surface-aligned clusters to generate compact viewpoint sequences with improved orientation consistency. The local planner generates collision-free trajectories and adjusts the viewing direction online to mitigate occlusion-induced coverage loss while preserving the planned trajectory structure. The simulation results across randomized scene configurations demonstrate that the proposed global planner achieves near-complete coverage while reducing trajectory length compared to representative baselines. Real-world flight experiments further validate that the framework produces usable inspection data for downstream analysis. These results indicate that the proposed framework improves inspection efficiency and adaptability in partially known structured indoor environments.

</details>


### [133] [Forecast-aware Gaussian Splatting for Predictive 3D Representation in Language-Guided Pick-and-Place Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11144)
*Kaixin Jia,Jiacheng Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Forecast-GS通过预测任务完成后的3D状态，增强了机器人对动作效果的评估能力，在复杂机器人操作任务中表现优于现有方法，提升了任务执行的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的机器人操作系统通常仅基于当前场景进行推理，缺乏对“任务完成状态”的显式建模，导致在面对局部观测及复杂的空间/语义目标时，难以可靠地判断候选动作是否能达成目标。

Method: 提出Forecast-GS（Forecast-aware Gaussian Splatting），一种用于语言条件化机器人操作的预测性3D表示框架。通过显式建模任务完成后的目标状态，而非仅基于当前场景进行推理，从而辅助机器人评估候选动作的有效性。

Result: 在真实的拾取-放置任务（Cutter-to-Box, Apple-to-Bowl, Sponge-to-Tray）测试中，Forecast-GS的自动候选选择成功率分别达到21/25、23/25和16/25，显著优于基准方法ReKep；人类辅助选择设置下的成功率进一步提升，证明了该方法在目标预测方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过显式预测任务完成后的3D状态，能有效提升机器人操作评估的可靠性；同时，如何实现自动化的最终状态排序仍是实现完全自主操纵的核心挑战。Forecast-GS为语言理解、3D感知和机器人操作规划之间提供了可解释的桥梁。

Abstract: We introduce Forecast-aware Gaussian Splatting (Forecast-GS), a predictive 3D representation framework for language-conditioned robotic manipulation. While recent manipulation systems have made progress by grounding language instructions into robot affordances, value maps, or relational keypoint constraints, they usually reason over the current scene and do not explicitly model the task-completed state. This limitation is critical when success depends on satisfying spatial and semantic goals under partial observations, where the robot must evaluate whether a candidate action leads to a feasible task-consistent outcome.
  We validate Forecast-GS on real-world pick-and-place manipulation tasks, including Cutter-to-Box, Apple-to-Bowl, and Sponge-to-Tray. For each task, we conduct 25 real-world trials under varied initial object configurations using the same robot platform and sensing setup. Forecast-GS with automatic candidate selection achieves success rates of 21/25, 23/25, and 16/25 on the three tasks, respectively, outperforming the ReKep baseline, which achieves 15/25, 19/25, and 10/25. A diagnostic human-assisted setting further improves success rates to 23/25, 24/25, and 19/25, suggesting that candidate generation is effective while automatic ranking remains imperfect. These results suggest that explicitly forecasting task-completed 3D states enables more reliable action evaluation, while the gap between automatic and human-assisted selection indicates that robust final-state ranking remains an important challenge for fully autonomous manipulation. Overall, Forecast-GS provides an interpretable bridge between language understanding, 3D perception, and robotic manipulation planning.

</details>


### [134] [Distributed Pose Graph Optimization via Continuous Riemannian Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11210)
*Jaeho Shin,Maani Ghaffari,Yulun Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将PGO问题建模为李群上阻尼动力学系统的分布式优化框架，通过半隐式几何积分和基于速度的邻居预测，实现了在延迟通信环境下高性能、高收敛性的分布式位姿估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分布式姿态图优化（PGO）算法在处理复杂环境、大规模协同及通信延迟（非同步）时，收敛速度和鲁棒性仍有提升空间，缺乏一种能够将连续时间物理动力学原理与离散分布式优化有效结合的统一框架。

Method: 1. 将PGO问题建模为李群上的二阶连续时间动力学系统（具有阻尼的质点系统）；2. 导出阻尼欧拉-庞加莱方程（Euler--Poincaré equations）；3. 采用半隐式几何积分器进行优化求解；4. 在多机器人场景下，使用块对角质量和阻尼矩阵实现完全分布式计算，并引入基于状态和速度的邻居预测机制以应对通信延迟。

Result: 该方法在实验中证明了优于当前最先进分布式基准算法的性能，特别是在同步和异步（延迟）通信环境下表现出更强的收敛性和稳定性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于黎曼动力学的分布式PGO求解框架，利用阻尼欧拉-庞加莱方程实现了高效、鲁棒且能够处理延迟通信的分布式优化，在基准测试中优于现有的分布式算法。

Abstract: We present a framework for distributed Pose Graph Optimization (PGO) by formulating the problem as a second-order continuous-time dynamical system evolving on Lie groups. By modeling pose variables as massive particles subject to damping, the equilibrium points of the resulting Riemannian dynamics coincide with first-order critical points of the original PGO problem. Using the governing damped Euler--Poincaré equations and a semi-implicit geometric integrator, we design an optimization algorithm that generalizes existing algorithms such as Riemannian gradient descent and Gauss--Newton. In multi-robot settings, we present a fully distributed and parallel method based on block-diagonal mass and damping matrices, where each robot solves an ordinary differential equation for its own poses with minimal communication overhead. Moreover, modeling both state and velocity enables principled neighbor prediction that significantly improves convergence under delayed communication. Theoretically, we present an analysis and establish sufficient condition that ensures energy dissipation under the employed geometric discretization scheme. Experiments on benchmark PGO datasets demonstrate that the proposed solver achieves superior performance compared to state-of-the-art distributed baselines in both synchronous and asynchronous regimes.

</details>


### [135] [Computational Design of a Low-Visibility UAV Using a Human-Aligned Perceptual Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11296)
*Jingxian Wang,Chen Yu,David Matthews,Emma Alexander,Sam Kriegman,Michael Rubenstein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 Phantom Twist 的单桨无人机设计方案，通过自动化设计流水线布局组件以最大化运动模糊效果，从而在确保飞行性能的前提下显著降低了无人机的视觉可见度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决传统多旋翼无人机在执行任务时易被肉眼识别的问题，通过利用高速旋转产生的运动模糊效应，设计出一种低可见度的单旋翼无人机。

Method: 构建了两阶段自动化设计流水线：第一阶段负责优化功能组件（电池、控制板、电机等）的布局，第二阶段针对稳定飞行所需的惯性和空气动力学约束进行验证；引入 LPIPS 度量标准来衡量并最小化飞行器的视觉感知度。

Result: 通过原型机的制造与飞行测试验证，结果表明设计方案在保持飞行稳定性和可控性的同时，其视觉可见度远低于传统四轴无人机。

Conclusion: Phantom Twist 的自动化设计流水线能够有效产出稳定、可控且具有极低视觉感知度的单桨无人机，证明了通过运动模糊和结构优化实现低可见度无人机的可行性。

Abstract: We introduce Phantom Twist, a type of single-propeller UAV designed to achieve low visibility through high-speed spinning and the exploitation of motion blur. We develop a two-stage automated design pipeline that optimizes the placement of functional components including batteries, control PCB, motor-propeller assembly, and counterweights. The pipeline minimizes visibility as measured by a human-aligned perceptual metric (LPIPS) while strictly satisfying inertial and aerodynamic constraints required for stable flight. We validate this approach through fabrication and flight testing of multiple prototypes. These tests confirm that our pipeline produces stable, controllable designs and that the optimized UAV exhibits significantly reduced visual perceptibility compared to conventional quadcopters.

</details>


### [136] [Kairos: A Scalable Serving System for Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11381)
*Yinwei Dai,Ganesh Ananthanarayanan,Landon Cox,Xenofon Foukas,Bozidar Radunovic,Ravi Netravali*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对物理 AI 推理与执行异步交错的任务特性，Kairos 成为首个将“生成-执行”循环作为系统核心的基础架构，在多机器人应用中显著降低了任务延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的针对数字 AI 设计的推理系统无法满足物理 AI 的独特需求，即多轮推理与执行、分块动作生成以及推理与执行的异步交错，这阻碍了物理 AI 在大规模机器人集群中的应用。

Method: 提出并设计了名为 Kairos 的多机器人推理服务系统，该系统将“生成-执行（generate-execute）”循环作为核心处理单元，优化了推理与动作执行的交错逻辑，实现执行阶段的主动参与。

Result: 在多种物理 AI 模型和机器人硬件上进行了测试，结果显示 Kairos 将端到端任务延迟降低了 31.8% 至 66.5%，且性能优势随机器人集群规模的扩大而增强。

Conclusion: Kairos 通过将“生成-执行”循环作为核心架构设计，成功适应了物理 AI 的异步交互特性，显著提升了多机器人系统的任务响应效率，为大规模物理 AI 的部署提供了有效的系统支撑。

Abstract: Physical AI is experiencing rapid growth with frontier foundation models increasing its capabilities across general environments. Physical AI tasks are characterized by inference properties that are markedly different from digital AI. They consist of multiple rounds of inference and action execution, generating a chunk of actions in each inference round, and asynchronously interleaving inference and execution. This makes existing digital AI serving systems unsuited for physical AI; a shortcoming that is critical for enabling their wide adoption, considering their size and the scale of the robot fleets they have to serve. To fill this gap, we design Kairos, the first multi-robot serving system that makes the generate-execute loop a first-class citizen, with active involvement in the execution phase. Across a wide range of physical AI models and robots, Kairos reduces the average end-to-end task latency by 31.8--66.5% over state-of-the-art digital AI serving practices, with gains scaling with the robot fleet size.

</details>


### [137] [Overcoming Dynamics-Blindness: Training-Free Pace-and-Path Correction for VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11459)
*Yanyan Zhang,Chaoda Song,Vikash Singh,Xinpeng Li,Kai Ye,Zhe Hu,Zhongzhu Pu,Yu Yin,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对现有单帧观测 VLA 模型在非平稳环境下缺乏动态感知的问题，本文提出了一种名为 Pace-and-Path Correction 的训练无关推理算子，通过二次代价函数分解实现动作节奏与路径的实时动态修正，显著提升了机器人动作的成功率与时序鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型多采用单帧观测，导致其缺乏对时序动态的感知能力，在非平稳环境中的控制性能大幅下降；而现有改进方法往往面临重训练开销大、推理延迟高或时序一致性差等问题。

Method: 提出 Pace-and-Path Correction 框架。这是一种训练无关的闭式推理算子，将动作分块过程建模为二次代价函数最小化问题。通过正交分解，将模型输出拆解为“节奏（Pace）”和“路径（Path）”两个通道，分别负责执行速度压缩与空间偏移修正，从而在不进行重训练的前提下吸收时序动态。

Result: 在 MoveBench 基准测试中，该方法优于现有的训练无关封装器及动态自适应方法。在纯动态环境和混合环境下，相较于基准 VLA 模型，成功率分别提升了 28.8% 和 25.9%。

Conclusion: 提出了一种无需训练、闭式推理时的操作算子 Pace-and-Path Correction。该方法能有效修正现有分块动作 VLA 模型在非平稳环境下的时序动态感知偏差，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升模型在动态场景下的作业成功率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve remarkable flexibility and generalization beyond classical control paradigms. However, most prevailing VLAs are trained under a single-frame observation paradigm, which leaves them structurally blind to temporal dynamics. Consequently, these models degrade severely in non-stationary scenarios, even when trained or finetuned on dynamic datasets. Existing approaches either require expensive retraining or suffer from latency bottlenecks and poor temporal consistency across action chunks. We propose Pace-and-Path Correction, a training-free, closed-form inference-time operator that wraps any chunked-action VLA. From a single quadratic cost, joint minimization yields a unified solution that decomposes orthogonally into two distinct channels. The pace channel compresses execution along the planned direction, while the path channel applies an orthogonal spatial offset, jointly absorbing the perceived dynamics within the chunk window. We evaluate our approach on a comprehensive diagnostic benchmark MoveBench designed to isolate motion as the sole controlled variable. Empirical results demonstrate that our framework consistently outperforms state-of-the-art training-free wrappers and dynamic-adaptive methods and improves success rates by up to 28.8% and 25.9% in absolute terms over foundational VLA models in dynamic-only and static-dynamic mixed environments, respectively.

</details>


### [138] [Offline Policy Evaluation for Manipulation Policies via Discounted Liveness Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11479)
*Hao Wang,Joshua Bowden,Colton Crosby,Somil Bansal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对机器人离线策略评估中稀疏奖励和有限视野截断带来的问题，本文提出了一种基于活性（liveness）的贝尔曼算子，实现了无截断偏差的鲁棒价值评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作中的稀疏奖励、非单调的任务进展以及有限评估周期导致的截断偏差（打破了经典贝尔曼方程的无限视野假设）使得传统的离线策略评估方法失效。

Method: 提出了一种基于活性（liveness）的贝尔曼算子，将策略评估重构为任务完成问题，产生一个对有限视野截断具有鲁棒性的保守不动点价值函数。

Result: 在模拟操作任务（VLA模型与扩散策略）及真实世界布料折叠任务中，该方法准确反映了任务进度，显著优于 TD(0) 和蒙特卡洛评估等传统基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于活性（liveness）的贝尔曼算子提供了一种稳健的离线策略评估方法，能够有效解决任务完成过程中的截断偏差问题，并通过理论证明了其收敛性。

Abstract: Policy evaluation is a fundamental component of the development and deployment pipeline for robotic policies. In modern manipulation systems, this problem is particularly challenging: rewards are often sparse, task progression of evaluation rollouts are often non-monotonic as the policies exhibit recovery behaviors, and evaluation rollouts are necessarily of finite length. This finite length introduces truncation bias, breaking the infinite-horizon assumptions underlying standard methods relying on Bellman equations/principle of optimality. In this work, we propose a framework for offline policy evaluation from sparse rewards based on a liveness-based Bellman operator. Our formulation interprets policy evaluation as a task-completion problem and yields a conservative fixed-point value function that is robust to finite-horizon truncation. We analyze the theoretical properties of the proposed operator, including contraction guarantees, and show how it encodes task progression while mitigating truncation bias. We evaluate our method on two simulated manipulation tasks using both a Vision-Language-Action model and a diffusion policy, and a cloth folding task using human demonstrations. Empirical results demonstrate that our approach more accurately reflects task progress and substantially reduces truncation bias, outperforming classical baselines such as TD(0) and Monte Carlo policy evaluation.

</details>


### [139] [Coordinated Diffusion: Generating Multi-Agent Behavior Without Multi-Agent Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11485)
*Lasse Peters,Laura Ferranti,Javier Alonso-Mora,Andrea Bajcsy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CoDi框架，通过仅利用单智能体数据和引导项，实现了无需协同演示的多智能体策略学习，有效解决了多智能体模仿学习中的数据稀缺问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体模仿学习面临“数据瓶颈”问题，即随着智能体数量增加，收集高质量、大规模的协同演示数据变得极为昂贵且困难，现有方法难以有效扩展。

Method: 提出Coordinated Diffusion (CoDi)框架：将独立训练的单智能体扩散策略通过用户定义的代价函数进行耦合；推导了一种扩散采样方案，将得分函数分解为单智能体策略与代价驱动的引导项；利用无需梯度的引导项实现对黑盒代价函数的支持。

Result: 在仿真和真实机械臂协作任务中，CoDi无需协作演示数据即实现了鲁棒的协作行为，展示了比传统多智能体模仿学习方法更高的数据效率。

Conclusion: CoDi通过利用单智能体演示数据和辅助代价函数，成功实现了无需协作演示的多智能体协调策略学习，在效率和鲁棒性上均优于现有的多智能体模仿学习基准。

Abstract: Imitation learning powered by generative models has proven effective for modeling complex single-agent behaviors. However, teaching multi-agent systems, like multiple arms or vehicles, to coordinate through imitation learning is hindered by a fundamental data bottleneck: as the joint state-action space grows exponentially with the number of agents, collecting a sufficient amount of coordinated multi-agent demonstrations becomes extremely costly. In this work, we ask: how can we leverage single-agent demonstration data to learn multi-agent policies? We present Coordinated Diffusion (CoDi), a framework that couples independently trained single-agent diffusion policies through a user-defined multi-agent cost function, without requiring any coordinated demonstrations. We derive a new diffusion-based sampling scheme wherein the diffusion score function decomposes into independent, single-agent pre-trained base policies plus a cost-driven guidance term that coordinates these base policies into cohesive multi-agent behavior. We show that this guidance term can be estimated in a gradient-free manner, making CoDi applicable to black-box, non-differentiable cost functions without additional training. Theoretically and empirically, we analyze the conditions under which this composition can faithfully approximate a target multi-agent behavior. We find a complementary role for demonstration data versus the cost function: single-agent demonstrations must cover the support of the desired multi-agent behavior, while the cost function must promote desired behavior from this product of single-agent policies. Our results in simulation and hardware experiments of a two-arm manipulation task show that CoDi discovers robust coordinated behavior from single-agent data, is more data-efficient than multi-agent baselines, and highlights the importance of joint guidance, base policy support, and cost design.

</details>


### [140] [RIO: Flexible Real-Time Robot I/O for Cross-Embodiment Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11564)
*Pablo Ortega-Kral,Eliot Xing,Arthur Bucker,Vernon Luk,Junseo Kim,Owen Kwon,Angchen Xie,Nikhil Sobanbabu,Yifu Yuan,Megan Lee,Deepam Ameria,Bhaswanth Ayapilla,Jaycie Bussell,Guanya Shi,Jonathan Francis,Jean Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对机器人领域基础设施碎片化问题，本文推出了开源框架 RIO，为多形态、跨平台的机器人学习提供标准化的底层架构，有效提升了 VLA 模型的开发效率与数据复用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人代码高度依赖特定硬件配置，导致数据共享、复用和多形态跨设备协同开发成本高昂，严重阻碍了通用具身智能（VLA）模型的研究进展。

Method: 提出 RIO (Robot I/O) 框架，通过引入灵活且轻量级的组件（涵盖控制、遥操作、数据格式化、传感器配置及策略部署），构建了一套标准化的抽象接口，支持在不同机器人形态（单臂、双臂、人形）和硬件平台之间无缝切换。

Result: 在三种形态（单臂、双臂、人形）和四种硬件平台上验证了 RIO 的有效性；利用 RIO 采集的数据成功微调了 π0.5 和 GR00T 等前沿 VLA 模型，完成了多项复杂任务（如拾取、折叠、清洗）。

Conclusion: RIO 是一个旨在解决机器人学习领域基础设施碎片化问题的开源框架，通过提供高度灵活的抽象层，降低了跨硬件平台进行机器人控制、遥操作和策略部署的门槛，有助于加速通用机器人学习的社区研究。

Abstract: Despite recent efforts to collect multi-task, multi-embodiment datasets, to design recipes for training Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs), and to showcase these models on different robot platforms, generalist cross-embodiment robot capabilities remains a largely elusive ideal. Progress is limited by fragmented infrastructure: most robot code is highly specific to the exact setup the user decided on, which adds major overhead when attempting to reuse, recycle, or share artifacts between users. We present RIO (Robot I/O), an open source Python framework that provides flexible, lightweight components for robot control, teleoperation, data formatting, sensor configuration, and policy deployment across diverse hardware platforms and morphologies. RIO provides abstractions that enable users to make any choice and to switch between them, with minimal reconfiguration effort. We validate RIO on VLA deployment workflows across three morphologies (single-arm, bimanual, humanoid) and four hardware platforms with varying grippers and cameras. Using teleoperated data collected with RIO, we fine-tune state-of-the-art VLAs including $π_{0.5}$ and GR00T on household tasks such as pick-and-place, folding, and bowl scrubbing. By open sourcing all our efforts, we hope the community can accelerate their pace of robot learning on real-world robot hardware. Additional details at: https://robot-i-o.github.io

</details>


### [141] [Sampling-Based Follow-the-Leader Motion Planning for Manipulator-Mounted Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11618)
*Chengnan Shentu,Nicholas Baldassini,Oluwagbotemi D. Iseoluwa,Radian Gondokaryono,Jessica Burgner-Kahrs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对安装在机械臂上的连续体机器人，提出了一种基于采样的FTL运动规划算法，通过几何解耦计算大幅提升了规划效率，并在实验中取得了高精度的路径跟踪效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的FTL方法通常假设机器人基座固定或仅具有单自由度插入机构，无法满足将连续体机器人安装在具有全自由度（SE(3)）基座的机械臂上的实际应用需求。

Method: 提出了一种基于采样的运动规划器，核心思想是通过闭式几何构造（closed-form geometric construction）来确定基座姿态，从而将全局形状搜索与基座姿态确定解耦，避免了在线规划中的迭代优化，并将大部分计算任务前置到离线阶段。

Result: 在120条模拟路径的测试中实现了0%的尖端误差和1.9%的平均形状偏差，成功率达100%；并通过6自由度肌腱驱动CR在串联机械臂上的实验验证了方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种针对安装在机械臂上的连续体机器人（CR）的跟随（FTL）运动规划方法，通过闭式几何构造解耦了全局形状搜索与基座姿态确定，实现了高效、高精度的路径跟踪，并在仿真和实际实验中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Follow-the-leader (FTL) motion exploits the unique morphology of continuum robots (CRs) to navigate confined spaces by having the body retrace the path of the tip. While extensively studied, existing FTL methods typically assume a fixed base or a single degree-of-freedom insertion mechanism, limiting their applicability to practical systems in which CRs are mounted on robotic manipulators with fully actuated SE(3) base pose. This paper presents a sampling-based motion planner for FTL motion of manipulator-mounted CRs that jointly considers robot configuration and base pose. The key idea is to decouple global shape search from base pose determination by computing the base pose through a closed-form geometric construction, thereby avoiding iterative optimization during online planning. The approach supports general forward models and enables efficient planning by shifting the majority of computation offline. We establish theoretical guarantees including resolution complete shape search and converging tip tracking throughout waypoint traversal and interpolation. Experiments on 120 simulated paths over 3 test classes demonstrate 0% tip error and 1.9% mean shape deviation (w.r.t. robot length) at 100% success rate. We validate the practicality of our approach on a 6-DOF tendon-driven CR mounted on a serial manipulator. Code and visualization available at https://continuumroboticslab.github.io/sb-ftl-cr-planner/.

</details>


### [142] [A Proprioceptive-Only Benchmark for Quadruped State Estimation: ATE, RPE, and Runtime Trade-offs Between Filters and Smoothers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11674)
*Ylenia Nisticò,João Carlos Virgolino Soares,Joan Solà,Claudio Semini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对比了三种先进四足机器人本体状态估计器（MUSE, IEKF, IS），通过GrandTour数据集评估发现精度与延迟存在显著权衡，并开源了评估框架以供从业者快速针对应用约束进行选型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前四足机器人领域缺乏对主流本体感知状态估计器的客观对比评价，从业者难以针对特定应用场景和计算预算做出准确的评估与选择。

Method: 在GrandTour数据集的CYN-1序列上，对比了三种先进的状态估计器（MUSE, IEKF, IS）；评估指标包括长时精度（ATE）、短时精度（RPE）以及在统一硬件/软件平台下的单次更新计算耗时。

Result: IEKF和IS展现出更优的长距离轨道估计精度；三种算法在短时位姿误差（RPE）上差异不显著；揭示了不同算法在精度与延迟之间的折中表现。

Conclusion: 本研究为四足机器人本体感知状态估计器的选择提供了清晰的性能参考。IEKF和IS在长距离精度（ATE）上优于MUSE，但三种方法在短时精度（RPE）上表现相近；研究同时也突出了精度与计算延迟之间的权衡，并提供了开源基准测试代码以支持可复现性。

Abstract: We compare three state-of-the-art proprioceptive state estimators for quadruped robots: MUSE [1], the Invariant Extended Kalman Filter (IEKF) [2], and the Invariant Smoother (IS) [3], on the CYN-1 sequence of the GrandTour Dataset [4]. Our goal is to give practitioners clear guidance on accuracy and computation time: we report long-term accuracy (Absolute Trajectory Error, ATE), short-term accuracy (translational and rotational Relative Pose Error, RPE), and per-update computation time on a fixed hardware/software stack. On this dataset, RPEs are broadly similar across methods, while IEKF and IS achieve a lower ATE than MUSE. Runtime results highlight the accuracy-latency trade-offs across the three approaches. In the discussion, we outline the evaluation choices used to ensure a fair comparison and analyze factors that influence short-horizon metrics. Overall, this study provides a concise snapshot of accuracy and cost, helping readers choose an estimator that fits their application constraints, with all evaluation code and documentation released open-source at https://github.com/iit-DLSLab/state_estimation_benchmark for full reproducibility.

</details>


### [143] [Rainbow Deep Q-Learning with Kinematics-Aware Design for Cooperative Delta and 3-RRS Parallel Robot Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11697)
*Hassen Nigatu,Gaokun Shi,Jituo Li,Wang Jin,Lu Guodong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种协同并联机器人的装配框架，通过预先对3-RRS机械臂进行几何优化以增大可用工作空间，并结合基于Rainbow DQN的运动学感知强化学习策略，实现了高效、稳定的自动装配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对Delta并联机器人与3-RRS并联机械臂协同装配任务，旨在利用几何优化扩展操作空间，结合深度强化学习提高协作装配的成功率与安全性。

Method: 采用包含几何设计优化（最大化无奇异工作空间并改善条件数）与运动学感知Rainbow DQN（结合Double Q-learning、Dueling、PR、多步回报、Noisy Nets及分布值函数）的两阶段学习课程，构建马尔可夫决策过程。

Result: 在模拟器中实现了稳定的策略收敛和可靠的装配结果；相比于传统的DQN智能体和采样规划器，显著降低了违反运动学及工作空间约束的频率。

Conclusion: 通过几何设计优化与运动学感知DRL框架的协同，有效解决了并联机器人协作装配任务中的空间约束与路径规划问题，证明了协同设计比单一优化更具优势。

Abstract: This paper presents a kinematics-aware deep reinforcement learning framework based on Rainbow Deep Q-Networks (DQN) for cooperative peg-in-hole manipulation by a Delta parallel robot and a 3-RRS (Revolute--Revolute--Spherical) parallel manipulator. A key contribution is the integration of a geometric design-optimization stage that precedes learning: the 3-RRS geometry is tuned to maximize the singularity-free workspace and improve conditioning, which in turn enlarges the safe region in which the reinforcement learning policy can explore. Together the two manipulators expose a 6~degree-of-freedom (DoF) controllable subspace (three Delta translations, two 3-RRS rotations, and one 3-RRS vertical translation); the peg-in-hole task is invariant to rotation about the peg axis, so the task-relevant manifold is five dimensional. The cooperative insertion problem is cast as a Markov Decision Process with a 12-dimensional state vector and a discrete action set containing $6 \times 2 = 12$ incremental commands (one positive and one negative per controlled DoF). A shaped reward combines dense proximity guidance, penalties for kinematic and workspace violations, and sparse bonuses for successful insertions. The Rainbow DQN -- integrating double Q-learning, dueling architecture, prioritized replay, multi-step returns, noisy linear layers for exploration, and a distributional value head -- is trained with a two-stage curriculum. The co-designed framework is validated in a high-fidelity kinematic simulator, where it achieves stable policy convergence, reliable insertions, and reduced constraint violations compared against a vanilla DQN agent and a classical sampling-based planner.

</details>


### [144] [Introducing Environmental Constraints to Grasping Strategies for Paper-Like Flexible Materials Using a Soft Gripper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11714)
*Yi Dong,Yang Li,Jinjun Duan,Zhendong Dai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用环境约束抓取纸质类柔性材料的策略，通过建立力学模型与实验评估，为机器人抓取平面柔性物体提供了系统化的方法论与应用参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纸类材料在压缩应力下表现出与织物不同的力学特性，且物理属性的微小变化会对抓取产生显著影响。然而，现有的机器人抓取策略在处理此类高度灵活性物体时仍面临挑战，亟需专门的策略研究。

Method: 1. 基于现有操控原语，提出了一套利用环境约束抓取柔性材料的系统化策略；2. 建立了相关抓取过程的力学与运动学模型；3. 开发了一套评估系统，用于测量抓取力与成功率，并通过实验验证了材料属性及工况对抓取的影响。

Result: 通过实验证明了所提策略的有效性，量化了材料性质和环境条件对抓取成功率的影响，并总结了不同环境约束下抓取策略的具体适用场景。

Conclusion: 通过对环境约束的利用，为柔性片状物（如纸张）提供了一套系统化的抓取策略。该研究明确了不同策略的适用工作空间与特性，为家用服务机器人处理平面柔性物体提供了实用的指导方案。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation of flexible objects is widely required in both industrial and service applications. Among such objects, paper-like materials exhibit distinct mechanical characteristics compared to cloth, being more sensitive to compressive stress, where minor variations in physical properties can significantly affect grasping. This study systematically investigates grasping strategies for paper-like materials using a universal soft gripper by exploiting environmental constraints. Based on manipulation primitives employed in existing grasping strategies, we proposed systematic grasping strategies for flexible materials by exploiting environmental constraints and analyzed their mechanical and kinematic models. To investigate the influence of materials and working conditions on grasping, an evaluation system for measuring grasping force and success rate was defined and experimentally evaluated. Finally, we summarized the specific workspaces and characteristics of different strategies that can satisfy various task requirements and lead to potential applications in household service robots for grasping planar flexible objects.

</details>


### [145] [DreamAvoid: Critical-Phase Test-Time Dreaming to Avoid Failures in VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11750)
*Xianzhe Fan,Yuxiang Lu,Shenyuan Gao,Xiaoyang Wu,Ruihua Han,Manling Li,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对 VLA 模型在精细操作中容易因累积误差导致失败的问题，本文提出了 DreamAvoid 框架。通过在执行关键阶段“想象”不同动作产生的未来结果并进行评估，实现了对操作失败的预见与规避，极大地提升了机器人任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视-语-行（VLA）模型主要基于成功演示进行训练，缺乏对细粒度操作关键阶段失败风险的感知，导致微小的动作误差极易引发整体任务的不可逆失败。

Method: 提出了一种测试时“梦境”（Test-time Dreaming）框架，包含三个核心组件：1. Dream Trigger（感知关键阶段）；2. Action Proposer（生成多种候选动作）；3. Dream Evaluator（预测并评估生成动作的短期未来效果，以此选择最优路径）。同时引入了自主边界学习范式以区分成功与失败边界。

Result: 在真实世界操作任务和模拟基准测试中，DreamAvoid 显著降低了任务失败率，提升了机器人操作的稳健性。

Conclusion: DreamAvoid 通过在执行关键阶段引入预测机制，有效增强了 VLA 模型对失败的预判与规避能力，显著提升了机器人操作任务的成功率。该框架成功克服了传统模型训练数据集中缺乏失败样本导致的泛化局限性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are often brittle in fine-grained manipulation, where minor action errors during the critical phases can rapidly escalate into irrecoverable failures. Since existing VLA models rely predominantly on successful demonstrations for training, they lack an explicit awareness of failure during these critical phases. To address this, we propose DreamAvoid, a critical-phase test-time dreaming framework that enables VLA models to anticipate and avoid failures. We also introduce an autonomous boundary learning paradigm to refine the system's understanding of the subtle boundary between success and failure. Specifically, we (1) utilize a Dream Trigger to determine whether the execution has entered a critical phase, (2) sample multiple candidate action chunks from the VLA via an Action Proposer, and (3) employ a Dream Evaluator, jointly trained on mixed data (success, failure, and boundary cases), to "dream" the short-horizon futures corresponding to the candidate actions, evaluate their values, and select the optimal action. We conduct extensive evaluations on real-world manipulation tasks and simulation benchmarks. The results demonstrate that DreamAvoid can effectively avoid failures, thereby improving the overall task success rate. Our code is available at https://github.com/XianzheFan/DreamAvoid.

</details>


### [146] [NavOL: Navigation Policy with Online Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11762)
*Xiaofei Wei,Chun Gu,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NavOL通过在线交互仿真器并不间断利用全局规划器修正轨迹，有效克服了传统模仿学习的分布偏移问题，实现了高效、稳健的机器人导航策略训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决离线模仿学习中分布偏移与累积误差的问题，以及强化学习中奖励函数设计困难与学习效率低下的瓶颈。

Method: 提出了一种在线模仿学习框架（NavOL），基于预训练的导航扩散策略，通过在模拟器中运行策略并利用全局规划器即时获取专家路径作为监督信号，循环更新策略以消除分布偏移。

Result: 在IsaacLab平台上实现了高效扩展（每小时采集2000条轨迹），在多个仿真基准测试及真实世界实验中，NavOL在导航成功率和泛化能力上均优于现有的离线及在线方案。

Conclusion: NavOL通过在线模仿学习范式，证明了其在解决机器人导航中分布偏移及学习效率问题上的有效性，在仿真与真实场景中均表现出稳健的泛化性能。

Abstract: Learning robust navigation policies remains a core challenge in robotics. Offline imitation learning suffers from distribution shift and compounding errors at rollout, while reinforcement learning requires reward engineering and learns inefficiently. In this paper, we propose NavOL, an online imitation learning paradigm that interacts with a simulator and updates itself using expert demonstrations gathered online. Built upon a pretrained navigation diffusion policy that maps local observations to future waypoints, NavOL trains in a rollout update loop: during rollout, the policy acts in the simulator and queries a global planner which has privileged access to the global environment for the optimal path segment as ground truth trajectory labels; during update, the policy is trained on the online collected observation trajectory pairs. This online imitation loop removes the need for reward design, improves learning efficiency, and mitigates distribution shift by training on the policy own explored rollouts. Built on IsaacLab with fast, high-fidelity parallel rendering and domain randomization of camera pose and start-goal pairs, our system scales across 50 scenes on 8 RTX 4090 GPUs, collecting over 2,000 new trajectories per hour, each averaging more than 400 steps. We also introduce an indoor visual navigation benchmark with predefined start and goal positions for zero-shot generalization. Extensive evaluations on simulation benchmarks, including the NavDP benchmark and our proposed benchmark, as well as carefully designed real-world experiments, demonstrate the effectiveness of NavOL, showing consistent performance gains in online imitation learning.

</details>


### [147] [See What Matters: Differentiable Grid Sample Pruning for Generalizable Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11817)
*Yixu Feng,Zinan Zhao,Yanxiang Ma,Chenghao Xia,Chengbin Du,Yunke Wang,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 GridS 的可微网格采样器，通过几何感知的视觉 Token 连续重采样，在极高压缩比（保留不足 10% 的 Token）下实现了 VLA 模型的高效加速，且不牺牲任务执行性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 VLA 模型计算成本过高，而传统的 Token 剪枝方法会丢失关键的几何信息（如接触点），导致性能显著下降，限制了进一步的压缩和加速。

Method: 提出了一种名为“可微网格采样器（GridS）”的即插即用模块。该模型将压缩重新定义为视觉编码器中的任务感知、连续 Token 重采样，通过自适应预测显著坐标，并利用可微插值提取特征。

Result: 在 LIBERO 基准测试和真实机器人平台上，GridS 实现了 76% 的 FLOPs 降低，同时在视觉 Token 数量减少 90% 以上的情况下，机器人操作成功率未受损。

Conclusion: GridS 成功打破了 VLA 模型中“压缩率”与“任务性能”之间的权衡，证明了通过几何感知的连续 Token 重采样，可以在保留关键空间特征的同时实现极高的压缩比，为实时机器人部署提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown remarkable promise in robotics manipulation, yet their high computational cost hinders real-time deployment. Existing token pruning methods suffer from a fundamental trade-off: aggressive compression using pruning inevitably discards critical geometric details like contact points, leading to severe performance degradation. This forces a compromise, limiting the achievable compression rate and thus the potential speedup. We argue that breaking this trade-off requires rethinking compression as a geometry-aware, continuous token resampling in the vision encoder. To this end, we propose the Differentiable Grid Sampler (GridS), a plug-and-play module that performs task-aware, continuous resampling of visual tokens in VLA. By adaptively predicting a minimal set of salient coordinates and extracting features via differentiable interpolation, GridS preserves essential spatial information while achieving drastic compression (with fewer than 10% original visual tokens). Experiments on both LIBERO benchmark and a real robotic platform demonstrate that validating the lowest feasible visual token count reported to date, GridS achieves a 76% reduction in FLOPs with no degradation in the success rate. The code is available at https://github.com/Fediory/Grid-Sampler.

</details>


### [148] [Mapping Embodied Affective Touch Strategies on a Humanoid Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11825)
*Qiaoqiao Ren,Omar Eldardeer,Francesca Cocchella,Rea Francesco,Alessandra Sciutti,Tony Belpaeme*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究探索了人形机器人全身的情感触觉，发现触觉交互特征高度依赖于身体部位与环境约束，且触觉交互能有效促进人机亲密度及社会化关系的感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多集中于特定身体部位的触觉交互，缺乏对人形机器人全身情感触觉的全面理解，且忽视了触觉表达如何随机器人具身特征（身体部位、空间约束、社会角色）而改变。

Method: 采用人机交互实验法，招募32名被试与配有全身体表传感器（E-skin）的iCub人形机器人进行交互。实验设计了三种触觉约束条件（自由触、仅限手臂触、仅限躯干触），要求被试通过触觉表达八种不同情绪，并收集触觉轨迹及动力学数据。

Result: 1. 自由触条件下，被试偏好上身易触区域；2. 较少触碰的区域展现了更强的情绪表达选择性；3. 触觉动力学特征（运动vs压力）在不同身体部位的表现存在显著差异；4. 实验有效提升了参与者与机器人之间的亲密度。

Conclusion: 情感触觉的表达高度依赖于机器人身体部位，且受到具身约束的影响。研究表明，触觉交互策略在不同约束条件下无法直接迁移，交互过程显著提升了人机亲密度及社会感知。

Abstract: Affective touch in human-robot interaction is shaped not only by emotional intent, but also by robot embodiment, including touch location, physical constraints, and perceived agency or social role. Existing HRI studies typically focus on one or two isolated body parts, limiting understanding of how affective touch generalises across the full humanoid body. We present a study with 32 participants interacting with the iCub robot, which is equipped with full-body distributed tactile sensors. Participants expressed eight emotions under three conditions: free touch, arm-only touch, and torso-only touch. Results show that body region and spatial constraints jointly shaped both touch location and dynamics. In free touch, participants preferred socially accessible upper-body regions, while less frequently touched areas showed stronger emotion-specific selectivity. Emotion-related variation was more evident in motion features for arm-only touch and pressure features for torso-only touch. Touch strategies also did not transfer directly between free and constrained conditions, even within the same coarse body region. Participants reported increased closeness to the robot after interaction, with around 30 percent reporting a change in perceived social relationship. Together, these findings show that affective touch expression is strongly body-region dependent and shaped by embodiment constraints.

</details>


### [149] [Learning Action Manifold with Multi-view Latent Priors for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11832)
*Junjin Xiao,Dongyang Li,Yandan Yang,Shuang Zeng,Tong Lin,Xinyuan Chang,Feng Xiong,Mu Xu,Xing Wei,Zhiheng Ma,Qing Zhang,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对VLA模型深度感知与动作学习难题，本文提出结合多视角扩散生成和几何引导Transformer的架构，配合动作流形学习技术，实现了空间感知增强与鲁棒的端到端机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在处理单目视角时存在深度感知不足（深度歧义）的问题，且在动作预测过程中回归非结构化噪声导致训练效率低下。

Method: 1. 多视角合成：利用预训练的多视角扩散模型生成隐含的新视角以缓解深度歧义；2. G3T（几何引导门控Transformer）：在3D几何约束下对齐多视角特征并过滤遮挡噪声；3. AML（动作流形学习）：通过定义有效的动作流形，直接在目标空间预测动作而非对噪声/速度进行回归。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0及真实机器人实验中，该方法在任务成功率和鲁棒性方面均优于当前的主流基准模型。

Conclusion: 该方法通过引入多视角扩散模型与几何引导Transformer，有效解决了VLA模型在单目输入下的深度感知难题，并通过动作流形学习提升了策略鲁棒性，在模拟器与真实机器人任务中均超越了现有SOTA。

Abstract: This paper tackles spatial perception and manipulation challenges in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. To address depth ambiguity from monocular input, we leverage a pre-trained multi-view diffusion model to synthesize latent novel views and propose a Geometry-Guided Gated Transformer (G3T) that aligns multi-view features under 3D geometric guidance while adaptively filtering occlusion noise. To improve action learning efficiency, we introduce Action Manifold Learning (AML), which directly predicts actions on the valid action manifold, bypassing inefficient regression of unstructured targets like noise or velocity. Experiments on LIBERO, RoboTwin 2.0, and real-robot tasks show our method achieves superior success rate and robustness over SOTA baselines. Project page: https://junjxiao.github.io/Multi-view-VLA.github.io/.

</details>


### [150] [Cooperative Robotics Reinforced by Collective Perception for Traffic Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11972)
*Mohammad Khoshkdahan,John Pravin Arockiasamy,Andy Flores Comeca,Alexey Vinel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种在NLOS路口部署协同类人机器人的创新方案，通过融合视觉感知与V2X通信，利用机器人进行主动物理干预（STOP手势与合流阻挡），有效弥补了现有交通预警系统的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非视距（NLOS）路口因驾驶员视野受限导致的碰撞风险，现有V2X技术在渗透率不足或驾驶员忽略警示时存在局限性，且无法直接干预未配装V2X车辆，需寻求一种能够通过物理手段主动干预交通的协同补偿机制。
