Method: 构建了一套包含双摄像头基础设施单元（用于环境感知与生成CPM）与协同类人机器人（具备V2X接收与路测环境通知DENM中继能力）的系统；采用两路并行感知路径融合，通过设定危险区（ZoD）预测碰撞风险，并通过机器人的类人STOP手势及物理阻挡实现主动交通干预。

Result: 在鹿特丹未来交通公园（FMP）的实地部署测试结果表明，该系统通过融合视觉与V2X感知，能够早期检测车辆并可靠预测隐患，显著提升了NLOS条件下的行车安全性，有效防止了危险合流。

Conclusion: 通过引入具备物理交通管制能力的协同类人机器人，该研究验证了结合V2X信息流与基础设施视觉感知系统在处理非视距路口（NLOS）安全问题上的有效性，证明该方案能有效预防不安全合流行为。

Abstract: Collisions at non-line-of-sight (NLOS) intersections remain a major safety concern because drivers have limited visibility of approaching traffic. V2X based warnings can reduce these risks, yet many vehicles are not equipped with V2X and drivers may ignore in vehicle alerts. Collective perception (CP) can compensate for low V2X penetration by extending the awareness of connected vehicles, but it cannot influence unconnected vehicles. To fill this gap, our work introduces a complementary concept that adds a cooperative humanoid robot as an active traffic moderator capable of physically stopping a vehicle that attempts to merge into an unseen traffic stream. The system operates on two parallel perception pathways. A dual camera infrastructure unit detects the position, speed and motion of approaching vehicles and transmits this information to the robot as a collective perception message (CPM). The robot also receives cooperative awareness messages (CAM) from connected vehicles through its onboard V2X unit and can act as a relay for decentralized environmental notification messages (DENM) when safety events originate elsewhere along the road. A fusion module combines these streams to maintain a robust real time view of the main road. A Zone of Danger (ZoD) is defined and used to predict whether an approaching vehicle creates a collision risk for a merging road user. When such a risk is detected, the robot issues a human-like STOP gesture and blocks the merging path until the hazard disappears. The full system was deployed at the Future Mobility Park (FMP) in Rotterdam. Experiments show that the combined vision and V2X perception allows the robot to detect approaching vehicles early, predict hazards reliably and prevent unsafe merges in real world NLOS conditions.

</details>


### [151] [Closing the Motion Execution Gap: From Semantic Motion Task Constraints to Kinematic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12053)
*Simon Stelter,Vanessa Hassouna,Malte Huerkamp,Michael Beetz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Giskard 框架，通过引入运动状态机和可微运动学模型，解决了高层任务规划与底层运动控制之间的鸿沟，实现了具备跨平台通用性的复杂机器人运动执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决“运动执行鸿沟”（Motion Execution Gap）问题，即高层语义任务规划与底层具体机器人运动执行之间存在的脱节，使机器人能够处理复杂的限制条件并实现跨平台的通用执行。

Method: 提出了“运动状态机”（Motion Statecharts）作为可执行的符号化表示；利用统一的可微运动学世界模型实现世界中心（world-centric）的运动规范化；采用基于 lMPC（线性模型预测控制）的任务函数方法，并通过加加速度（jerk）限制确保任务切换时的平滑性。

Result: 成功在 8 个不同的机器人平台上进行了部署和验证，展示了框架的跨平台迁移能力与在复杂环境下的执行效果。开源代码已发布。

Conclusion: Giskard 提供了一种鲁棒且可移植的机器人运动执行框架，通过运动状态机（Motion Statecharts）和可微运动学模型，有效弥合了高层任务规划与底层运动控制之间的鸿沟，并实现了多机器人平台的跨硬件通用性。

Abstract: This paper addresses the Motion Execution Gap, the disconnect between high-level symbolic task descriptions using semantic constraints and executable robot motions. Motion Statecharts are introduced as an executable symbolic representation for complex motions. They allow the arbitrary arrangement of motion constraints, monitors or nested statecharts in parallel and sequence. World-centric motion specification and generalization across embodiments are enabled through the use of a unified differentiable kinematic world model of both, robots and environments. Motion execution is realized through a lMPC-based implementation of the task-function approach, in which smooth transitions during task switches are ensured using jerk bounds. Cross-platform transferability was demonstrated by deploying the method on eight robot platforms, operating in diverse environments. The proposed framework is called Giskard and is available open source: https://github.com/cram2/cognitive_robot_abstract_machine.

</details>


### [152] [Control of Fully Actuated Aerial Vehicles: A Comparison of Model-based and Sensor-based Dynamic Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12071)
*Ali Sidar Yilmaz,Buday Turan,Lukas Pries,Markus Ryll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究对比了全驱动无人机上的几何 NDI 与 INDI 控制策略，发现 INDI 具有更强的抗扰动和参数鲁棒性，而 NDI 在特定低频环境下姿态控制更稳，为全驱动无人机的控制设计提供了选型依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全驱动多旋翼平台允许位置与姿态的解耦控制，但其性能上限取决于控制策略及执行器动态。旨在通过对比两种不同的动态逆策略，探索如何在全驱动无人机中实现更稳健的控制及更快速的部署。

Method: 在固定倾角全驱动六旋翼无人机上，对比了基于模型的非线性动态逆控制器（几何 NDI）与基于传感器的增量动态逆控制器（INDI）。两者均采用相同的外环架构，运行频率同为 500 Hz，通过五个实验（包含参数失配、外部动态载荷、阵风扰动、降频及传感器降级测试）进行性能评估。

Result: 实验数据表明，INDI 在面对模型参数误差（如旋翼力系数失配）和外部扰动时表现出更显著的鲁棒性，并在全频率范围内的位置误差表现更稳健；而几何 NDI 在控制频率降低的情况下，姿态保持能力优于 INDI。

Conclusion: INDI 在参数不匹配、阵风干扰和传感器降级等鲁棒性测试中表现更优。但在较低控制频率下，几何 NDI 能提供更好的姿态跟踪性能。研究揭示了基于模型（NDI）与基于传感器（INDI）控制策略在鲁棒性与性能间的权衡，并首次验证了全姿态跟踪 INDI 在全驱动多旋翼上的有效性。

Abstract: Fully actuated multirotor platforms decouple translational force generation from vehicle attitude, enabling independent control of position and orientation and shifting performance limitations from attitude authority to actuator dynamics and control effectiveness. This paper compares a model-based nonlinear dynamic inversion controller (geometric NDI) with a sensor-based incremental dynamic inversion controller (INDI) on a fixed-tilt fully actuated hexarotor. Both controllers share an identical outer-loop structure and are both executed at 500 Hz; therefore, performance differences can be attributed primarily to the inversion strategy. Controller performance is evaluated in five experiments covering attitude step tracking under nominal conditions and under a 50% mismatch in the rotor force coefficient, hover disturbance rejection under an external lateral load, waypoint tracking in the presence of wind gust disturbances, reduced control frequency, and injected sensor degradation. The results show that INDI offers clear advantages under parameter mismatch, gust disturbances, and sensor degradation, and maintains lower position errors across the controller-frequency sweep. However, its advantages are not universal: geometric NDI yields better attitude tracking at reduced control frequencies. To the authors' best knowledge, this work presents the first experimental validation of a full pose tracking INDI controller with decoupled translational and rotational dynamics. These findings highlight the trade-off between measurement-based and model-based inversion for robust control and rapid deployment of fully actuated UAVs.

</details>


### [153] [Learning What Matters: Adaptive Information-Theoretic Objectives for Robot Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12084)
*Youwei Yu,Jionghao Wang,Zhengming Yu,Wenping Wang,Lantao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对机器人高维系统探索中参数不可辨识及干扰项影响的问题，提出了 QOED 方法，通过对费舍尔信息矩阵进行子空间分析并抑制非关键参数，实现了更高效的自适应探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人探索任务中，基于信息论的目标设计面临挑战：高维系统中许多参数难以观测或不可辨识，传统的探索目标往往因忽略或受到这些干扰项的影响而失效。

Method: 提出了准最优实验设计 (QOED) 方法：1) 通过对费舍尔信息矩阵 (Fisher Information Matrix) 进行特征空间分析，识别可辨识的参数子空间；2) 修改信息论探索目标，强化可辨识方向并抑制非关键参数带来的干扰。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界的导航与操作任务中，识别可辨识方向与抑制干扰分别带来了 35.23% 和 21.98% 的性能提升；集成到基于模型的强化学习策略中，相比基准方法表现更优。

Conclusion: QOED 通过识别可观测参数子空间并抑制不相关参数（nuisance effects）的干扰，为高维机器人系统提供了一种近似最优且鲁棒的信息论探索策略，在导航和操作任务中显著提升了模型参数学习效率与策略性能。

Abstract: Designing learnable information-theoretic objectives for robot exploration remains challenging. Such objectives aim to guide exploration toward data that reduces uncertainty in model parameters, yet it is often unclear what information the collected data can actually reveal. Although reinforcement learning (RL) can optimize a given objective, constructing objectives that reflect parametric learnability is difficult in high-dimensional robotic systems. Many parameter directions are weakly observable or unidentifiable, and even when identifiable directions are selected, omitted directions can still influence exploration and distort information measures. To address this challenge, we propose Quasi-Optimal Experimental Design (Q{\footnotesize OED}), an adaptive information objective grounded in optimal experimental design. Q{\footnotesize OED} (i) performs eigenspace analysis of the Fisher information matrix to identify an observable subspace and select identifiable parameter directions, and (ii) modifies the exploration objective to emphasize these directions while suppressing nuisance effects from non-critical parameters. Under bounded nuisance influence and limited coupling between critical and nuisance directions, Q{\footnotesize OED} provides a constant-factor approximation to the ideal information objective that explores all parameters. We evaluate Q{\footnotesize OED} on simulated and real-world navigation and manipulation tasks, where identifiable-direction selection and nuisance suppression yield performance improvements of \SI{35.23}{\percent} and \SI{21.98}{\percent}, respectively. When integrated as an exploration objective in model-based policy optimization, Q{\footnotesize OED} further improves policy performance over established RL baselines.

</details>


### [154] [World Action Models: The Next Frontier in Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12090)
*Siyin Wang,Junhao Shi,Zhaoyang Fu,Xinzhe He,Feihong Liu,Chenchen Yang,Yikang Zhou,Zhaoye Fei,Jingjing Gong,Jinlan Fu,Mike Zheng Shou,Xuanjing Huang,Xipeng Qiu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次对“世界动作模型 (WAMs)”进行了系统的学术综述，定义了这一将预测性世界模型与动作生成相结合的新范式，对其架构、方法论、数据源和评估协议进行了全面分类与分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉-语言-动作 (VLA) 模型仅学习从观测到动作的反应式映射，缺乏对物理环境演变规律的理解，无法支持复杂的长时决策与规划。

Method: 提出了世界动作模型 (WAMs) 的统一概念定义，构建了包含“级联式”(Cascaded) 与“联合式”(Joint) 的分类法，并从生成模态、条件化机制和动作解码策略等维度对现有研究进行了系统梳理与评估方案综述。

Result: 定义并澄清了 WAMs 的概念框架，构建了结构化的研究分类体系，梳理了涵盖远程操作、仿真及互联网视频等数据的全生态系统，并指出了该领域面临的核心挑战。

Conclusion: WAMs 是具身智能领域的重要演进方向，通过整合预测性世界模型与动作生成，克服了传统 VLA 模型缺乏物理世界规律建模的缺陷。未来需重点关注数据集的多样性、评估协议的标准化以及架构设计的效率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have achieved strong semantic generalization for embodied policy learning, yet they learn reactive observation-to-action mappings without explicitly modeling how the physical world evolves under intervention. A growing body of work addresses this limitation by integrating world models, predictive models of environment dynamics, into the action generation pipeline. We term this emerging paradigm World Action Models (WAMs): embodied foundation models that unify predictive state modeling with action generation, targeting a joint distribution over future states and actions rather than actions alone. However, the literature remains fragmented across architectures, learning objectives, and application scenarios, lacking a unified conceptual framework. We formally define WAMs and disambiguate them from related concepts, and trace the foundations and early integration of VLA and world model research that gave rise to this paradigm. We organize existing methods into a structured taxonomy of Cascaded and Joint WAMs, with further subdivision by generation modality, conditioning mechanism, and action decoding strategy. We systematically analyze the data ecosystem fueling WAMs development, spanning robot teleoperation, portable human demonstrations, simulation, and internet-scale egocentric video, and synthesize emerging evaluation protocols organized around visual fidelity, physical commonsense, and action plausibility. Overall, this survey provides the first systematic account of the WAMs landscape, clarifies key architectural paradigms and their trade-offs, and identifies open challenges and future opportunities for this rapidly evolving field.

</details>


### [155] [Premover: Fast Vision-Language-Action Control by Acting Before Instructions Are Complete](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12160)
*Joonha Park,Jiseung Jeong,Taesik Gong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Premover 通过在用户输入指令的空闲时间内进行预计算，实现了机器人动作执行的“抢跑”，在不牺牲任务成功率的前提下显著缩短了响应时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 VLA 模型评估往往假设输入已即时完成，但在实际部署中，用户输入指令需要数秒时间，导致机器人处理闲置，浪费了计算/反应时间。

Method: 提出了一个轻量级模块 Premover：1. 保持 VLA 主干冻结，添加两个小型投影头将中间层特征映射到共享空间；2. 利用仿真渲染的分割掩膜监督生成的“关注图”（focus map），对下一帧图像 Token 进行重加权；3. 训练一个标量“就绪阈值”（readiness threshold）来动态决定何时触发动作执行。

Result: 在 LIBERO 基准测试中，Premover 将平均执行时长从 34.0 秒缩短至 29.4 秒（减少了 13.6%），同时保持了与全提示基线一致的成功率（95.1% vs 95.0%），而未经优化的初步预计算方法成功率仅为 66.4%。

Conclusion: Premover 证明了 VLA 模型在用户输入期间利用空闲时间进行预计算是可行的。通过引入轻量级关注机制和就绪阈值，该方法在保持任务成功率不变的情况下，有效缩短了机器人的实际执行时间，为实现更高效的人机协作提供了范式。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies are typically evaluated as if the user had finished typing or speaking before the robot begins acting. In real deployment, however, users take several seconds to enter a request, leaving the policy idle for a substantial fraction of the interaction. We introduce Premover, a lightweight module that converts this idle window into useful precomputation. Premover keeps the VLA backbone frozen and attaches two small projection heads, one for image patches, one for language tokens, that map an intermediate layer of the backbone into a shared space. The resulting focus map is supervised by simulator-rendered target-object segmentation masks and applied as a per-patch reweighting of the next step's image tokens. A single scalar readiness threshold, trained jointly from streaming prefixes, decides when the policy should begin acting. On the LIBERO benchmark suite, Premover reduces mean wall-clock time from 34.0 to 29.4 seconds, a 13.6% reduction, while matching the full-prompt baseline's success rate (95.1% vs. 95.0%); naive premoving, by contrast, collapses to 66.4%.

</details>


### [156] [X-Imitator: Spatial-Aware Imitation Learning via Bidirectional Action-Pose Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12162)
*Kai Xiong,Hongjie Fang,Lixin Yang,Cewu Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: X-Imitator 是一种将机器人空间感知与动作生成紧密耦合的双向反馈框架，通过模拟人类前向模型，显著提升了机器人复杂操作任务的执行效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人操作方法多将空间感知与动作执行视为解耦或单向的过程，限制了机器人处理复杂操作任务的能力。

Method: 提出 X-Imitator，一种双路径（dual-path）框架，将空间感知与动作生成建模为紧密耦合的双向循环。通过历史动作条件化当前姿态预测，并实现空间推理与动作生成的互惠细化（mutual refinement），模拟人类的内化前向模型（internal forward models）。

Result: 在 24 个仿真任务和 3 个真实世界任务的实验中，证明了该框架显著优于普通策略及现有显式位姿引导方法。

Conclusion: X-Imitator 通过构建感知与动作的双向耦合机制，克服了传统机器人操作中感知与执行脱节的瓶颈，为提升复杂操作任务的性能提供了一种通用的模块化方案。

Abstract: Effectively handling the interplay between spatial perception and action generation remains a critical bottleneck in robotic manipulation. Existing methods typically treat spatial perception and action execution as decoupled or strictly unidirectional processes, fundamentally restricting a robot's ability to master complex manipulation tasks. To address this, we propose X-Imitator, a versatile dual-path framework that models spatial perception and action execution as a tightly coupled bidirectional loop. By reciprocally conditioning current pose predictions on past actions and vice versa, this framework enables continuous mutual refinement between spatial reasoning and action generation. This joint modeling exactly mimics human internal forward models. Designed as a modular architecture, the system can be seamlessly integrated into various visuomotor policies. Extensive experiments across 24 simulated and 3 real-world tasks demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms both vanilla policies and prior methods utilizing explicit pose guidance. The code will be open sourced.

</details>


### [157] [From Imagined Futures to Executable Actions: Mixture of Latent Actions for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12167)
*Yajie Li,Bozhou Zhang,Chun Gu,Zipei Ma,Jiahui Zhang,Jiankang Deng,Xiatian Zhu,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MoLA 提出了一种将视频生成模型转化为机器人控制的关键接口，通过混合逆动力学模型将视觉想象转化为结构化的潜在动作，提升了长周期机器人任务的执行精度与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频生成机器人模型侧重于视觉逼真度，导致生成的轨迹在缺乏动作逻辑控制的情况下显得间接且不稳定，难以转化为有效的机器人动作。

Method: 提出 MoLA（Mixture of Latent Actions）框架：不直接使用生成的视频帧，而是通过混合预训练的逆动力学模型（Inverse Dynamics Models）提取视频中的多模态语义、深度和光流信息，将其转化为可执行的潜在动作表示。

Result: 在 LIBERO、CALVIN 和 LIBERO-Plus 等仿真基准测试以及真实硬件操作任务中，MoLA 在任务成功率、时间一致性和泛化性能方面均表现出色。

Conclusion: MoLA 通过引入面向控制的潜在动作接口，有效缓解了视频生成模型在机器人操作任务中视觉逼真度与控制相关性之间的脱节，实现了更稳定、更鲁棒的动作执行。

Abstract: Video generation models offer a promising imagination mechanism for robot manipulation by predicting long-horizon future observations, but effectively exploiting these imagined futures for action execution remains challenging. Existing approaches either condition policies on predicted frames or directly decode generated videos into actions, both suffering from a mismatch between visual realism and control relevance. As a result, predicted observations emphasize perceptual fidelity rather than action-centric causes of state transitions, leading to indirect and unstable control. To address this gap, we propose MoLA (Mixture of Latent Actions), a control-oriented interface that transforms imagined future videos into executable representations. Instead of passing predicted frames directly to the policy, MoLA leverages a mixture of pretrained inverse dynamics models to infer a mixture of latent actions implied by generated visual transitions. These modality-aware inverse dynamics models capture complementary semantic, depth, and flow cues, providing a structured and physically grounded action representation that bridges video imagination and policy execution. We evaluate our approach on simulated benchmarks (LIBERO, CALVIN, and LIBERO-Plus) and real-world robot manipulation tasks, achieving consistent gains in task success, temporal consistency, and generalization.

</details>


### [158] [DexTwist: Dexterous Hand Retargeting for Twist Motion via Mixed Reality-based Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12182)
*Dongmyoung Lee,Chengxi Li,Dongheui Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexTwist 是一种用于混合现实（MR）远程操作的重定向框架，它通过实时优化旋转轴一致性和三指接触稳定性，解决了机器人手在进行复杂旋转任务时的操作失稳问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于运动学相似度（如关节角或指尖位置误差）的重定向方法在处理旋转操作（如开盖、拧螺丝）时，因操作者手部与机器人手的运动学差异，容易产生指尖滑动、接触丢失及旋转轴偏移，导致任务失败。

Method: 提出 DexTwist 框架：1. 检测三指捏合动作；2. 估计操作者的意图旋转轴（screw axis）与扭转幅度；3. 实施实时残差关节空间细化（residual joint-space refinement），在保持机器人三指几何形状的同时，通过优化旋转角度、旋转轴一致性、指尖闭合度及三指稳定性等目标函数，实现对旋转任务的精确跟踪。

Result: 仿真及真实世界实验表明，与基于向量的传统重定向基准相比，DexTwist 在执行旋转任务时取得了更高的旋转角度跟踪精度，并具备更好的旋转轴稳定性。

Conclusion: DexTwist 框架通过引入功能性扭转重定向机制，成功解决了由于机器人手与人类手在物理结构（ embodiment gap）上的差异导致的复杂旋转操作失稳问题，显著提升了电传操作在接触丰富任务中的精度与稳定性。

Abstract: Dexterous teleoperation via Mixed Reality (MR)-based interfaces offers a scalable paradigm for transferring human manipulation skills to dexterous robot hands. However, conventional retargeting approaches that minimize kinematic dissimilarity (e.g., joint angle or fingertip position error) often fail in contact-rich rotational manipulation, such as cap opening, key turning, and bolt screwing. This failure stems from the embodiment gap: mismatched link lengths, joint axes/limits, and fingertip geometry can cause direct pose imitation to induce tangential fingertip sliding rather than stable object rotation, resulting in screw axis drift, contact slip, and grasp instability. To address this, we propose DexTwist, a functional twist-retargeting framework for MR-based dexterous teleoperation. DexTwist detects a tripod pinch, estimates the operator's intended screw axis and twist magnitude, and applies a real-time residual joint-space refinement that tracks turning progress while regularizing the robot tripod geometry. The refinement minimizes a virtual-object objective defined by turning angle, screw axis consistency, fingertip closure, and tripod stability. Simulation and real-world experiments show that DexTwist improves turning angle tracking and screw axis stability compared with a vector-based retargeting baseline.

</details>


### [159] [TMRL: Diffusion Timestep-Modulated Pretraining Enables Exploration for Efficient Policy Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12236)
*Matthew M. Hong,Jesse Zhang,Anusha Nagabandi,Abhishek Gupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为解决机器人策略预训练后在强化学习微调中探索不足的问题，研究者提出了TMRL框架，结合“上下文平滑预训练”与“时间步调制强化学习”，实现了高效的真实世界机器人策略迁移与学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于行为克隆(BC)预训练的机器人策略通常存在动作分布过窄的问题，导致在下游强化学习(RL)微调阶段缺乏必要的探索能力，限制了微调的效率与性能。

Method: 1. Context-Smoothed Pre-training (CSP)：在行为克隆(BC)预训练阶段向策略输入注入前向扩散噪声，使策略在精准模仿与广泛动作覆盖之间取得平衡；2. Timestep-Modulated Reinforcement Learning (TMRL)：在强化学习微调阶段引入 timestep 调制机制，允许智能体根据任务需求动态调节扩散 timestep，从而精确控制探索范围。

Result: TMRL框架能够无缝集成到多种策略输入（如状态、点云、视觉语言模型VLA）中；在复杂机器人操纵任务中，实现了在一小时内的快速真实场景微调，显著提升了样本效率。

Conclusion: 本文提出的TMRL框架有效解决了预训练策略在强化学习微调阶段探索能力不足的问题，通过在预训练阶段引入随机噪声并在微调阶段动态调整，显著提升了机器人策略在复杂操作任务中的微调效率与成功率。

Abstract: Fine-tuning pre-trained robot policies with reinforcement learning (RL) often inherits the bottlenecks introduced by pre-training with behavioral cloning (BC), which produces narrow action distributions that lack the coverage necessary for downstream exploration. We present a unified framework that enables the exploration necessary to enable efficient robot policy finetuning by bridging BC pre-training and RL fine-tuning. Our pre-training method, Context-Smoothed Pre-training (CSP), injects forward-diffusion noise into policy inputs, creating a continuum between precise imitation and broad action coverage. We then fine-tune pre-trained policies via Timestep-Modulated Reinforcement Learning (TMRL), which trains the agent to dynamically adjust this conditioning during fine-tuning by modulating the diffusion timestep, granting explicit control over exploration. Integrating seamlessly with arbitrary policy inputs, e.g., states, 3D point clouds, or image-based VLA policies, we show that TMRL improves RL fine-tuning sample efficiency. Notably, TMRL enables successful real-world fine-tuning on complex manipulation tasks in under one hour. Videos and code available at https://weirdlabuw.github.io/tmrl/.

</details>


### [160] [SI-Diff: A Framework for Learning Search and High-Precision Insertion with a Force-Domain Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12247)
*Yibo Liu,Stanko Oparnica,Simon Shewchun-Jakaitis,Guoyi Fu,Jie Wang,Jun Yang,Anand Jagannathan,Tony Hong-Yau Lo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SI-Diff是一种基于力域扩散策略的统一框架，通过引入模式条件化机制和新型教师策略，实现了在单一模型中同时处理机器人接触式装配任务中的“搜索”与“高精度插入”过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的装配任务（如销孔插入）存在姿态不确定性和小间隙等挑战。现有方法通常将搜索和高精度插入视为独立任务，缺乏能够在一个模型内无缝处理这两种不同动作模式的统一方案。

Method: 提出了一种基于力域扩散策略（force-domain diffusion policy）的框架，核心创新包括：1. 模式条件化机制（mode-conditioning mechanism），用于在同一模型中捕捉搜索与插入的不同动作模式；2. 开发了一种能够生成多样化轨迹的搜索教师策略，用于引导模型学习。

Result: 实验结果表明，与当前SOTA方法TacDiffusion相比，SI-Diff将x-y轴向的对准容差从2mm提升至5mm，并表现出强大的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的SI-Diff框架通过单一模型成功统一了搜索与高精度插入任务，显著提升了面对位姿对准误差的鲁棒性以及对未知形状的泛化能力。

Abstract: Contact-rich assembly is fundamental in robotics but poses significant challenges due to uncertainties in relative poses, such as misalignments and small clearances in peg-in-hole tasks. Existing approaches typically address search and high-precision insertion separately, because these tasks involve distinct action patterns. However, supporting both tasks within a single model, without switching models or weights, is desirable for intelligent assembly systems. In this work, we propose SI-Diff, a framework that learns both search and high-precision insertion through a force-domain diffusion policy. To this end, we introduce a new mode-conditioning mechanism that enables the policy to capture distinct action behaviors under a single framework. Moreover, we develop a new search teacher policy that can generate diverse trajectories. By training on successful and efficient demonstrations provided by the teacher policy, the model learns the mapping from tactile and end-effector velocity observations to effective action behaviors. We conduct thorough experiments to show that SI-Diff extends the tolerance to x-y misalignments from 2 mm to 5 mm compared to the state-of-the-art baseline, TacDiffusion, while also demonstrating strong zero-shot transferability to unseen shapes.

</details>


### [161] [Real-Time Whole-Body Teleoperation of a Humanoid Robot Using IMU-Based Motion Capture with Sim2Sim and Sim2Real Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12347)
*Hamza Ahmed Durrani,Suleman Khan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文开发并验证了一套用于Unitree G1人形机器人的全身远程操作系统，通过优化运动学重定向与处理流水线，实现了无需学习模型的低延迟、高稳定性实时遥控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有人形机器人全身远程操作中存在的低延迟、稳定性差、形态差异（Kinematic Mismatches）、传感器噪声累积、控制延迟以及模拟到现实（Sim-to-Real）迁移鸿沟等技术挑战。

Method: 提出了一套自定义的运动处理、运动学重定向（Kinematic Retargeting）及控制流水线。该系统直接对接Virdyn IMU全身动捕套装数据，通过直观的映射方式实时驱动Unitree G1人形机器人，并全程摒弃了离线缓冲与学习型组件。

Result: 通过Sim-to-Sim（MuJoCo仿真）与Sim-to-Real（实机部署）验证，系统在不进行任何预训练修改的情况下，成功实现了包括行走、站立、坐下、转身、鞠躬及复杂全身表达动作的实时同步还原。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一套基于商用穿戴式动作捕捉硬件的、可扩展的全身拟人机器人远程操作框架，证明了无需离线缓冲或基于学习的方法即可实现实时、稳定的全身控制。

Abstract: Stable, low-latency whole-body teleoperation of humanoid robots is an open research challenge, complicated by kinematic mismatches between human and robot morphologies, accumulated inertial sensor noise, non-trivial control latency, and persistent sim-to-real transfer gaps. This paper presents a complete real-time whole-body teleoperation system that maps human motion, recorded with a Virdyn IMU-based full-body motion capture suit, directly onto a Unitree G1 humanoid robot. We introduce a custom motion-processing, kinematic retargeting, and control pipeline engineered for continuous, low-latency operation without any offline buffering or learning-based components. The system is first validated in simulation using the MuJoCo physics model of the Unitree G1 (sim2sim), and then deployed without modification on the physical platform (sim2real). Experimental results demonstrate stable, synchronized reproduction of a broad motion repertoire, including walking, standing, sitting, turning, bowing, and coordinated expressive full-body gestures. This work establishes a practical, scalable framework for whole-body humanoid teleoperation using commodity wearable motion capture hardware.

</details>


### [162] [GuidedVLA: Specifying Task-Relevant Factors via Plug-and-Play Action Attention Specialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12369)
*Xiaosong Jia,Bowen Yang,Zuhao Ge,Xian Nie,Yuchen Zhou,Cunxin Fan,Yufeng Li,Yilin Chai,Chao Jing,Zijian Liang,Qingwen Bu,Haidong Cao,Chao Wu,Qifeng Li,Zhenjie Yang,Chenhe Zhang,Hongyang Li,Zuxuan Wu,Junchi Yan,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GuidedVLA通过为动作解码器的注意力头引入辅助监督，显式引导模型聚焦于任务关键因子，从而克服了端到端学习中常见的过拟合问题，显著提升了机器人的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端VLA模型缺乏显式指导，容易过度拟合视觉捷径或噪声等虚假相关性，限制了模型在不同环境下的泛化能力。

Method: 提出GuidedVLA框架，将动作解码器拆解为具有特定功能的组件，利用手动定义的辅助信号（如物体定位、空间几何、时间技能逻辑）监督解码器中不同的注意力头，以强制模型关注任务相关的核心因素。

Result: 在模拟和真实机器人实验中，GuidedVLA在领域内和跨领域任务上的成功率均优于现有的强基准模型；实验证明了专题因子的质量与任务性能呈正相关，且实现了特征的有效解耦。

Conclusion: 明确引导行动解码器的学习过程（而非端到端盲目学习）是构建更鲁棒、更具泛化能力的VLA模型的有效途径，能够产生解耦且高质量的特征表达。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models aim for general robot learning by aligning action as a modality within powerful Vision-Language Models (VLMs). Existing VLAs rely on end-to-end supervision to implicitly enable the action decoding process to learn task-relevant features. However, without explicit guidance, these models often overfit to spurious correlations, such as visual shortcuts or environmental noise, limiting their generalization. In this paper, we introduce GuidedVLA, a framework designed to manually guide the action generation to focus on task-relevant factors. Our core insight is to treat the action decoder not as a monolithic learner, but as an assembly of functional components. Individual attention heads are supervised by manually defined auxiliary signals to capture distinct factors. As an initial study, we instantiate this paradigm with three specialized heads: object grounding, spatial geometry, and temporal skill logic. Across simulation and real-robot experiments, GuidedVLA improves success rates in both in-domain and out-of-domain settings compared to strong VLA baselines. Finally, we show that the quality of these specialized factors correlates positively with task performance and that our mechanism yields decoupled, high-quality features. Our results suggest that explicitly guiding action-decoder learning is a promising direction for building more robust and general VLA models.

</details>


### [163] [SafeManip: A Property-Driven Benchmark for Temporal Safety Evaluation in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12386)
*Chengyue Huang,Khang Vo Huynh,Sebastian Elbaum,Zsolt Kira,Lu Feng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了SafeManip，这是一个利用LTLf定义时序安全属性的基准框架，填补了机器人操作中对执行过程安全性评估的空白，研究发现现有主流策略模型在处理复杂任务时仍存在严重的时序安全违例问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人操作评估主要关注“任务是否完成”，忽略了“操作过程是否安全”——特别是时序相关的安全（如交叉污染或过早释放物体），导致即使任务成功也可能伴随不安全行为。

Method: 1. 引入SafeManip基准，定义了基于有限迹线性时序逻辑（LTLf）的可重用安全模板；2. 将机器人执行轨迹映射为符号谓词迹，并利用监控器对8类操作安全属性（碰撞、抓取/释放稳定性、交叉污染等）进行评估。

Result: 在50个RoboCasa365家庭任务上对6种主流视觉-语言-动作模型进行评估，发现许多任务成功但过程不安全的案例；发现模型任务成功率的提升并不直接对应执行安全性的提高，尤其是在复杂任务中。

Conclusion: 任务成功并不等同于执行安全。SafeManip通过引入一种基于LTLf的通用评估层，克服了现有基准在评估时序安全属性方面的不足，揭示了即使是高性能的视觉-语言-动作模型在复杂任务中仍存在显著的安全隐患。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation is typically evaluated by task success, but successful completion does not guarantee safe execution. Many safety failures are temporal: a robot may touch a clean surface after contamination or release an object before it is fully inside an enclosure. We introduce SafeManip, a property-driven benchmark to explicitly evaluate temporal safety properties in robotic manipulation, moving beyond prior evaluations that largely focus on task completion or per-state constraint violations. SafeManip defines reusable safety templates over finite executions using Linear Temporal Logic over finite traces (LTLf). It maps observed rollouts to symbolic predicate traces and evaluates them with LTLf-based monitors. Its property suite covers eight manipulation safety categories: collision and contact safety, grasp stability, release stability, cross-contamination, action onset, mechanism recovery, object containment, and enclosure access. Templates can be instantiated with task-specific objects, fixtures, regions, or skills, allowing the same safety specifications to generalize across tasks and environments. We evaluate SafeManip on six vision-language-action policies, including $π_0$, $π_{0.5}$, GR00T, and their training variants, across 50 RoboCasa365 household tasks. Results show that even strong models often behave unsafely. Task-success gains do not reliably translate into safer execution: many successful rollouts remain unsafe, while longer-horizon or more complex tasks expose more violations. SafeManip provides a reusable evaluation layer for diagnosing temporal safety failures and measuring safe success beyond task completion.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [Uniform Scaling Limits in AdamW-Trained Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11059)
*William Gibson,Christoph Reisinger*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文通过将深层Transformer的注意力机制建模为交互粒子系统，证明了其训练过程在深度趋于无穷大时收敛至McKean-Vlasov ODE系统，且误差界不随Token数量或嵌入维度增长，为模型动力学的尺度无关性分析提供了数学基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分析方法在处理大深度Transformer时常受限于Token数量或模型维度，导致在大规模模型上的分析失效。本文旨在建立一种在大深度及多头注意力设置下，关于Transformer动力学的统一、与模型规模解耦的数学解释框架。

Method: 1. 将Transformer的隐藏状态动力学建模为交互粒子系统（IPS）；2. 在适当的缩放条件下，利用前向-后向常微分方程系统推导隐藏状态及反向传播变量的联合收敛；3. 结合MVODE流映射与测度集中不等式（Concentration of Measure）导出收敛界，避免了覆盖参数的误差分析。

Result: 给出了隐藏状态与反向传播变量联合动力学的L^2收敛界，收敛速率为O(L^-1 + L^-1/3 H^-1/2)；证明了该收敛界与Token数量无关，并在适配AdamW优化器后与Token嵌入维度无关。

Conclusion: 该论文成功证明了Transformer在大深度和大注意力头数限制下，其隐藏状态动力学可收敛至McKean-Vlasov常微分方程（MVODE）系统，该收敛性边界与Token数量及嵌入维度无关，提供了对深层Transformer训练动力学的理论理解。

Abstract: We study the large-depth limit of transformers trained with AdamW, by modelling the hidden-state dynamics as an interacting particle system (IPS) coupled through the attention mechanism. Under appropriate scaling of the attention heads, we prove that the joint dynamics of the hidden states and backpropagated variables converge in $L^2$, uniformly over the initial condition, to the solution of a forward--backward system of ODEs at rate $\mathcal O(L^{-1}+L^{-1/3}H^{-1/2})$. Here, $L$ and $H$ denote the depth and number of heads of the transformer, respectively. The limiting system of ODEs can be identified with a McKean--Vlasov ODE (MVODE) when the attention heads do not incorporate causal masking. By using the flow maps associated with this MVODE and applying concentration of measure techniques, we obtain bounds on the difference between the discrete and continuous models that are uniform over compact sets of initial conditions. As this is achieved without resorting to a covering argument, the constants in our bounds are independent of the number of tokens. Furthermore, under a suitable adaptation to AdamW, the bounds become independent of the token embedding dimension.

</details>


### [165] [Interpretable Machine Learning for Spatial Science: A Lie-Algebraic Kernel for Rotationally Anisotropic Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11179)
*Kane Warrior,Dalia Chakrabarty*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种可解释的各向异性高斯过程核，通过直接参数化主长度尺度和旋转矩阵（SO(3)），有效解决了标准核函数无法捕捉非轴对齐各向异性的局限性，并在保持预测性能的同时提升了模型的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的ARD各向异性核仅能捕捉轴对齐的各向异性，而通用的SPD矩阵参数化方法虽然能表示旋转各向异性但缺乏直观的几何解释（如长度尺度和方向）。因此，需要一种既能捕捉任意方向的各向异性，又具备参数可解释性的GP核。

Method: 提出了一种全新的各向异性高斯过程（GP）核函数。该方法通过三个主长度尺度和一个显式的SO(3)旋转来参数化三维SPD（对称正定）协方差度量。利用轴角向量（axis-angle vector）经由Lie代数指数映射（Lie-algebra exponential map）表示旋转，从而将旋转约束在SO(3)流形上，并使用MCMC方法进行贝叶斯推断。

Result: 在合成数据上，该方法能准确恢复生成度量，且在旋转各向异性任务中优于轴对齐ARD核；在轴对齐场景下，性能与ARD持平。在纳米砖材料密度数据集的实际应用中，成功揭示了传统轴对齐核无法捕捉的旋转各向异性。

Conclusion: 该研究通过直接参数化主长度尺度和SO(3)旋转，成功构建了一个既具备可解释性又能描述任意旋转各向异性的高斯过程核函数。实验证明该方法在处理旋转各向异性数据时表现优于传统的轴对齐ARD核，并与通用SPD参数化方法性能相当。

Abstract: Many three-dimensional spatial fields are anisotropic, with directions of rapid and slow variation that need not align with the coordinate axes. Standard Gaussian process kernels with Automatic Relevance Determination (ARD) capture only axis-aligned anisotropy, while generic full symmetric positive definite (SPD) metrics can represent rotated anisotropy but do not parameterise principal length-scales and directions directly. We introduce an interpretable rotationally anisotropic GP kernel that parameterises a three-dimensional SPD covariance metric using three principal length-scales and an explicit SO(3) rotation. The rotation is represented by an axis-angle vector and mapped to SO(3) via the Lie-algebra exponential map, giving unconstrained Euclidean coordinates for inference while always inducing a valid SPD metric.
  The construction spans the same family of three-dimensional SPD covariance metrics as a generic full-SPD parameterisation, but exposes the geometry differently: length-scales and orientation are explicit, interpretable, and directly available for prior specification and posterior summaries. We perform Bayesian inference on these quantities using Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC), and characterise the resulting symmetries and weakly identified regimes.
  On synthetic data with rotated anisotropy, the posterior recovers the generating metric and improves prediction relative to an axis-aligned ARD baseline, while matching the predictive performance of a generic full SPD baseline. When the ground truth is axis-aligned, posterior mass concentrates near the identity rotation and predictive performance matches ARD. On a material-density dataset from a laboratory-fabricated nano-brick, the inferred metric reveals rotated anisotropy that is not captured by axis-aligned kernels.

</details>


### [166] [Exact Stiefel Optimization for Probabilistic PLS: Closed-Form Updates, Error Bounds, and Calibrated Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11607)
*Haoran Hu,Xingce Wang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文改进了概率偏最小二乘（PPLS）的拟合框架，通过噪声子空间估计和精确流形优化解决了模型瓶颈，在提供高精度预测的同时实现了理论支撑的有效不确定性校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统概率偏最小二乘法（PPLS）在联合EM/ECM更新时存在噪声-信号耦合问题，且处理正交约束较为复杂，限制了该模型在需要高确定性与可解释性场景下的应用。

Method: 提出了一种端到端的PPLS拟合框架，核心改进包括：1. 采用噪声子空间估计（Noise-subspace estimation）替代全谱噪声平均；2. 使用Stiefel流形上的精确最优化替代内点惩罚法；3. 引入高斯化（Gaussianization）以扩展至次高斯设置；4. 开发了基于块结构Fisher分析的闭式标准误计算方法。

Result: 在理论上，噪声子空间估计器达到了极小极大下界；在实验上，该方法在合成数据及多组学基准测试（TCGA-BRCA, PBMC CITE-seq）中，无需事后重校准即可实现近乎标称的覆盖率，点预测精度达到Ridge回归水平，在交叉视图预测任务中表现优于PO2PLS，且提升了参数恢复的稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作通过结合噪声子空间估计与Stiefel流形上的精确优化，克服了传统PPLS模型在噪声-信号耦合和正交约束处理上的局限性，在实现高点预测精度的同时，提供了理论上更严谨且有效的校准不确定性估计。

Abstract: Probabilistic partial least squares (PPLS) is a central likelihood-based model for two-view learning when one needs both interpretable latent factors and calibrated uncertainty. Building on the identifiable parameterization of Bouhaddani et al.\ (2018), existing fitting pipelines still face two practical bottlenecks: noise--signal coupling under joint EM/ECM updates and nontrivial handling of orthogonality constraints. Following the fixed-noise scalar-likelihood line of Hu et al.\ (2025), we develop an end-to-end framework that combines noise pre-estimation, constrained likelihood optimization, and prediction calibration in one pipeline. Relative to Hu et al.\ (2025), we replace full-spectrum noise averaging with noise-subspace estimation and replace interior-point penalty handling with exact Stiefel-manifold optimization. The noise-subspace estimator attains a signal-strength-independent leading finite-sample rate and matches a minimax lower bound, while the full-spectrum estimator is shown to be inconsistent under the same model. We further extend the framework to sub-Gaussian settings via optional Gaussianization and provide closed-form standard errors through a block-structured Fisher analysis. Across synthetic high-noise settings and two multi-omics benchmarks (TCGA-BRCA and PBMC CITE-seq), the method achieves near-nominal coverage without post-hoc recalibration, reaches Ridge-level point accuracy on TCGA-BRCA at rank $r=3$, matches or exceeds PO2PLS on cross-view prediction while providing native calibrated uncertainty, and improves stability of parameter recovery.

</details>


### [167] [Learning U-Statistics with Active Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11638)
*Xiaoning Wang,Yuyang Huo,Liuhua Peng,Changliang Zou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对U-统计量的主动学习采样框架，通过最优加权采样方案在节省标注预算的同时显著优化了估计效率与统计推断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代应用中获取U-统计量所需的标签成本高昂，因此需要一种能够在固定标注预算下，既能保持统计推断有效性又能提高估计效率的主动采样策略。

Method: 提出了一种针对U-统计量的主动推理框架，采用增强逆概率加权（AIPW）方法整合采样规则与机器学习预测，推导了最小化方差的最优采样规则，并将其扩展至基于U-统计量的经验风险最小化（ERM）。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在真实数据集上相较于基准方法能显著提升估计效率，并能成功维持预定的覆盖概率（target coverage）。

Conclusion: 该论文提出的主动推理框架有效地解决了U-统计量在有限标注成本下的计算效率问题，通过结合增强逆概率加权（AIPW）和最优采样策略，实现了在保持统计推断有效性的同时显著提升估计精度。

Abstract: $U$-statistics play a central role in statistical inference. In many modern applications, however, acquiring the labels required for $U$-statistics is costly. Motivated by recent advances in active inference, we develop an active inference framework for $U$-statistics that selectively queries informative labels to improve estimation efficiency under a fixed labeling budget, while preserving valid statistical inference. Our approach is built on the augmented inverse probability weighting $U$-statistic, which is designed to incorporate the sampling rule and machine learning predictions. We characterize the optimal sampling rule that minimizes its variance and design practical sampling strategies. We further extend the framework to $U$-statistic-based empirical risk minimization. Experiments on real datasets demonstrate substantial gains in estimation efficiency over baseline methods, while maintaining target coverage.

</details>


### [168] [Minimax Rates and Spectral Distillation for Tree Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11841)
*Binh Duc Vu,David S. Watson*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文通过谱分析方法揭示了随机森林和梯度提升机的收敛理论，并基于此提出了一种高效的模型压缩框架，大幅减小了模型规模并保持了预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管随机森林（RF）和梯度提升机（GBM）在实践中广泛应用，但其理论特性（如收敛性）尚不明确，且在大规模应用中存在模型冗余，缺乏高效的压缩方法。

Method: 引入谱视角分析算法，具体包括：1.推导随机森林回归的极大极小最优收敛界；2.利用核算子或平滑矩阵的特征向量/奇异向量进行降维；3.学习非线性映射来实现模型蒸馏与压缩。

Result: 1.证明了随机森林的统计收敛速率受诱导核算子的特征值衰减控制；2.开发的压缩方案能将模型体积缩小几个数量级，同时保持竞争性的预测性能；3.在模型修剪和规则提取任务上优于现有技术，特别适用于资源受限环境。

Conclusion: 通过谱分析方法揭示了决策树集成模型的内在机制，并证明了谱表示法在模型压缩中的有效性，为理解随机森林和梯度提升机提供了新的理论和实践框架。

Abstract: Tree ensembles such as random forests (RFs) and gradient boosting machines (GBMs) are among the most widely used supervised learners, yet their theoretical properties remain incompletely understood. We adopt a spectral perspective on these algorithms, with two main contributions. First, we derive minimax-optimal convergence for RF regression, showing that, under mild regularity conditions on tree growth, the eigenvalue decay of the induced kernel operator governs the statistical rate. Second, we exploit this spectral viewpoint to develop compression schemes for tree ensembles. For RFs, leading eigenfunctions of the kernel operator capture the dominant predictive directions; for GBMs, leading singular vectors of the smoother matrix play an analogous role. Learning nonlinear maps for these spectral representations yields distilled models that are orders of magnitude smaller than the originals while maintaining competitive predictive performance. Our methods compare favorably to state of the art algorithms for forest pruning and rule extraction, with applications to resource constrained computing.

</details>


### [169] [Information-Theoretic Generalization Bounds for Sequential Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12190)
*Futoshi Futami,Masahiro Fujisawa*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 为序列决策问题（如在线学习、多臂老虎机）扩展了信息论泛化界的分析工具，引入序列条件互信息（SCMI）框架，实现了对顺序数据流下的泛化误差控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于超样本条件的互信息（CMI）泛化界仅适用于批处理独立同分布（i.i.d.）设置，无法直接处理数据按序列适应性揭示的在线学习、流式学习及多臂老虎机等顺序决策问题。

Method: 开发了“序列超样本框架”，将学习者的流（filtration）与用于比较的“影子坐标”（ghost-coordinate）分离；利用序列条件互信息（SCMI）作为泛化界的核心量度，并引入了伯恩斯坦型改进以在特定方差条件下优化收敛速度。

Result: 成功推导出适用于序列决策场景的泛化界，该界不仅能有效控制泛化间隙，且通过伯恩斯坦型改进在满足方差条件下获得了更快的收敛速率。该方法成功推广至在线学习、带重要性加权的流式主动学习及随机多臂老虎机问题。

Conclusion: 提出了一种适用于序列决策问题的通用泛化分析框架（序列超样本框架），证明了在行交换性假设下，泛化间隙可由序列条件互信息（SCMI）控制，并提供了一套适用于在线学习、流式主动学习和多臂老虎机等场景的分析工具。

Abstract: Information-theoretic generalization bounds based on the supersample construction are a central tool for algorithm-dependent generalization analysis in the batch i.i.d.~setting. However, existing supersample conditional mutual information (CMI) bounds do not directly apply to sequential decision-making problems such as online learning, streaming active learning, and bandits, where data are revealed adaptively and the learner evolves along a causal trajectory. To address this limitation, we develop a sequential supersample framework that separates the learner filtration from a proof-side enlargement used for ghost-coordinate comparisons. Under a row-wise exchangeability assumption, the sequential generalization gap is controlled by sequential CMI, a sum of roundwise selector--loss information terms. We also establish a Bernstein-type refinement that yields faster rates under suitable variance conditions. The selector-SCMI proof strategy applies to online learning, streaming active learning with importance weighting, and stochastic multi-armed bandits.

</details>


### [170] [Optimal Policy Learning under Budget and Coverage Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12235)
*Giovanni Cerulli*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了带预算与最低覆盖双重约束下的最优策略制定，提出了一种仿射阈值决策规则，并开发了 GLC 和 RC 两种优化算法，证明了其在实际应用中的接近最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在政策制定中，同时面临预算限额（budget constraint）与最低干预效果/覆盖率（minimum coverage constraint）的组合约束时，如何实现最优的资源分配或策略学习是一个极具挑战性的组合优化问题。

Method: 1. 理论推导：利用对偶理论分析问题的背包型（knapsack-type）结构与影子价格（shadow prices）；2. 复杂性分析：证明线性规划松弛具有 O(1) 的积分间隙；3. 算法实现：提出了贪婪-拉格朗日算法 (GLC) 和排序-切割算法 (RC)；4. 验证：通过蒙特卡洛模拟评估算法表现。

Result: 1. GLC 算法能够提供极其接近最优解的表现且在有限样本下稳健；2. RC 算法在覆盖约束松弛或成本同质时表现接近最优，但在成本异质性与约束收紧交互时会出现分配偏差；3. 模拟实验验证了理论分析的结论。

Conclusion: 在预算和最小覆盖双重约束下，该研究证实了最优策略遵循仿射阈值规则。基于线性规划松弛的 O(1) 积分间隙，证明了离散分配问题与连续松弛问题的渐近等价性，并为实际应用提供了有效的优化算法。

Abstract: We study optimal policy learning under combined budget and minimum coverage constraints. We show that the problem admits a knapsack-type structure and that the optimal policy can be characterized by an affine threshold rule involving both budget and coverage shadow prices. We establish that the linear programming relaxation of the combinatorial solution has an O(1) integrality gap, implying asymptotic equivalence with the optimal discrete allocation. Building on this result, we analyze two implementable approaches: a Greedy-Lagrangian (GLC) and a rank-and-cut (RC) algorithm. We show that the GLC closely approximates the optimal solution and achieves near-optimal performance in finite samples. By contrast, RC is approximately optimal whenever the coverage constraint is slack or costs are homogeneous, while misallocation arises only when cost heterogeneity interacts with a binding coverage constraint. Monte Carlo evidence supports these findings.

</details>


### [171] [Online Learning-to-Defer with Varying Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12340)
*Dang Hoang Duy,Yannis Montreuil,Maxime Meyer,Axel Carlier,Lai Xing Ng,Wei Tsang Ooi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个在线L2D算法，解决了流式数据下专家动态变动带来的挑战，并提供了强有力的理论性能保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有L2D方法主要局限于批处理设置，无法满足现实应用中对流式数据处理、专家实时可用性变化及专家群体分布漂移的需求。

Method: 提出了首个针对多分类问题的在线学习延迟（Online L2D）算法，采用带有赌博机反馈（bandit feedback）和动态专家池的处理机制，结合在线凸优化的一阶方法及在线框架下的H-一致性界（H-consistency bounds）进行分析。

Result: 理论上在一般条件下实现了 O((n+n_e)T^{2/3}) 的遗憾界，在低噪声条件下实现了 O((n+n_e)√T) 的遗憾界；实验证明该方法在处理专家可用性和可靠性波动时表现出优异性能。

Conclusion: 该算法成功将L2D框架扩展至在线环境，能够应对专家动态可用和可靠性变化的情况，并提供了理论上的遗憾界（regret bounds）支撑。

Abstract: Learning-to-Defer (L2D) methods route each query either to a predictive model or to external experts. While existing work studies this problem in batch settings, real-world deployments require handling streaming data, changing expert availability, and shifting expert distribution. We introduce the first online L2D algorithm for multiclass classification with bandit feedback and a dynamically varying pool of experts. Our method achieves regret guarantees of $O((n+n_e)T^{2/3})$ in general and $O((n+n_e)\sqrt{T})$ under a low-noise condition, where $T$ is the time horizon, $n$ is the number of labels, and $n_e$ is the number of distinct experts observed across rounds. The analysis builds on novel $\mathcal{H}$-consistency bounds for the online framework, combined with first-order methods for online convex optimization. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that our approach effectively extends standard Learning-to-Defer to settings with varying expert availability and reliability.

</details>


### [172] [Multi-Variable Conformal Prediction: Optimizing Prediction Sets without Data Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12341)
*Laura Lützow,Simone Garatti,Marco C. Campi,Lars Lindemann,Matthias Althoff*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对传统共形预测形状受限及依赖数据分割的缺陷，本文提出了多变量共形预测（MCP）框架，通过将预测集设计与校准整合为单一优化问题，在实现高效、小尺寸预测集的同时，无需数据分割并保证了理论覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统共形预测受限于标量评分函数和单一阈值，其预测集形状在校准前被固定，且通常依赖“数据分割”（Data Splitting）方法，导致数据利用效率低且结果对分割敏感。因此需要一种更灵活的方法来同时处理形状设计与校准。

Method: 提出了多变量共形预测（MCP）框架，引入了向量值评分函数和多校准变量。具体包含两种高效算法：RemMCP（基于约束移除的约束优化）和RelMCP（基于约束松弛的迭代优化）。该方法基于情境理论（Scenario Theory），将形状设计与校准结合为单一优化任务。

Result: 数值实验表明，RemMCP和RelMCP在维持目标覆盖率的同时，预测集尺寸更小或相当，且显著降低了跨校准运行的方差。

Conclusion: MCP框架通过将预测集设计与校准统一为一个优化问题，成功消除了对数据分割的需求，在保证覆盖率的同时，通过充分利用所有数据提升了预测集的紧凑性并降低了不确定性。

Abstract: Conformal prediction constructs prediction sets with finite-sample coverage guarantees, but its calibration stage is structurally constrained to a scalar score function and a single threshold variable - forcing shapes of prediction sets to be fixed before calibration, typically through data splitting. We introduce multi-variable conformal prediction (MCP), a framework that extends conformal prediction to vector-valued score functions with multiple simultaneous calibration variables. Building on scenario theory as a principled framework for certifying data-driven decisions, MCP unifies prediction set design and calibration into a single optimization problem, eliminating data splitting without sacrificing coverage guarantees. We propose two computationally efficient variants: RemMCP, grounded in constrained optimization with constraint removal, which admits a clean generalization of split conformal prediction; and RelMCP, based on iterative optimization with constraint relaxation, which supports non-convex score functions at the cost of possibly greater conservatism. Through numerical experiments on ellipsoidal and multi-modal prediction sets, we demonstrate that RemMCP and RelMCP consistently meet the target coverage with prediction set sizes smaller than or comparable to those of baselines with data split, while considerably reducing variance across calibration runs - a direct consequence of using all available data for shape optimization and calibration simultaneously.

</details>


### [173] [Model-based Bootstrap of Controlled Markov Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12410)
*Ziwei Su,Imon Banerjee,Diego Klabjan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于有限受控马尔可夫链的Bootstrap方法，解决了离线RL中行为策略未知时的转移核估计与不确定性量化问题，并提供了比现有方案更精确的离线策略评估和优化的置信区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习中，当生成数据的行为策略未知时，需要对非平稳或历史依赖策略下的转移核进行可靠的估计与不确定性量化。

Method: 提出了一种针对有限受控马尔可夫链（CMCs）模型转移核的基于模型的Bootstrap方法；利用针对访问计数的Bootstrap大数定理（LLN）和针对转移增量的鞅中心极限定理（CLT）；通过验证贝尔曼算子的哈达玛可微性（Hadamard differentiability），应用Delta方法实现策略评估和策略优化。

Result: 实现了转移核估计器的分布一致性；理论上证明了离线策略评估（OPE）和最优策略恢复（OPR）的渐进有效性；RiverSwim实验表明该方法在小样本和短视界下比传统Bootstrap和插值型CLT置信区间具有更好的覆盖率和校准度。

Conclusion: 提出的基于模型的Bootstrap方法在有限受控马尔可夫链中对转移核具有分布一致性，且通过Delta方法实现了离线强化学习中价值函数和Q函数的渐近有效置信区间，优于现有的基准方法。

Abstract: We propose and analyze a model-based bootstrap for transition kernels in finite controlled Markov chains (CMCs) with possibly nonstationary or history-dependent control policies, a setting that arises naturally in offline reinforcement learning (RL) when the behavior policy generating the data is unknown. We establish distributional consistency of the bootstrap transition estimator in both a single long-chain regime and the episodic offline RL regime. The key technical tools are a novel bootstrap law of large numbers (LLN) for the visitation counts and a novel use of the martingale central limit theorem (CLT) for the bootstrap transition increments. We extend bootstrap distributional consistency to the downstream targets of offline policy evaluation (OPE) and optimal policy recovery (OPR) via the delta method by verifying Hadamard differentiability of the Bellman operators, yielding asymptotically valid confidence intervals for value and $Q$-functions. Experiments on the RiverSwim problem show that the proposed bootstrap confidence intervals (CIs), especially the percentile CIs, outperform the episodic bootstrap and plug-in CLT CIs, and are often close to nominal ($50\%$, $90\%$, $95\%$) coverage, while the baselines are poorly calibrated at small sample sizes and short episode lengths.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [ShardTensor: Domain Parallelism for Scientific Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11111)
*Corey Adams,Peter Harrington,Akshay Subramaniam,Mohammad Shoaib Abbas,Jaideep Pathak,Mike Pritchard,Sanjay Choudhry*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对科学机器学习（SciML）处理极端分辨率数据时扩展性差的问题，本文提出了ShardTensor框架，通过域并行技术实现了输入数据与硬件约束的解耦，支持高效的大规模数据训练与推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的科学机器学习（SciML）在处理极端分辨率数据时，现有的并行方法难以扩展或导致模型精度下降，且缺乏一种通用的框架来解决 batch size 小于 1 时的输入数据并行化问题。

Method: 引入ShardTensor范式：一种域并行（domain parallelism）技术，通过解耦输入数据的空间维度与硬件约束，支持在单设备 batch size 小于 1 的情况下实现输入数据的灵活扩展与并行处理。

Result: 在训练和推理任务中均展现出良好的强扩展性（降低延迟）和弱扩展性（处理更大规模数据），验证了多维度并行能力，克服了SciML在处理极端尺度输入时的技术瓶颈。

Conclusion: ShardTensor提供了一种解耦空间维度与硬件约束的通用并行化框架，有效解决了SciML在极端尺度数据处理下的扩展性难题，实现了高保真的训练与推理。

Abstract: Scientific Machine Learning (SciML) faces unique challenges for extreme-resolution data, with mitigations that often fail to scale or degrade the accuracy of trained models. While some specialized methods have achieved remarkable results in training models or performing inference on massive spatial datasets with bespoke techniques, there is no generalized framework for parallelization over input data below batch size one per device. In this work we introduce ShardTensor: a novel paradigm of domain parallelism that enables flexible scaling of input data to arbitrary sizes. By decoupling the spatial dimensionality of input data from hardware constraints, ShardTensor enables scientific machine learning workloads to reach new levels of high fidelity training and inference. We demonstrate both strong and weak scaling of workloads during training and inference, showing improved latency with strong scaling and demonstrating the capacity to process higher data sizes with weak scaling. Additionally, we demonstrate multiple dimensions of parallelization, removing barriers to SciML on extreme-scale inputs.

</details>


### [175] [ReCoVer: Resilient LLM Pre-Training System via Fault-Tolerant Collective and Versatile Workload](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11215)
*Ziyue Liu,Zhengyang Wang,Ruijie Zhang,Avinash Maurya,Hui Zhou,Paul Hovland,Sheng Di,Franck Cappello,Bogdan Nicolae,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ReCoVer 是一种专为大规模 LLM 预训练设计的容错框架，通过保持迭代内梯度一致性，在保障训练轨迹完整的前提下，显著提升了硬件故障频发场景下的训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模 GPU 集群上训练大模型时，硬件故障已成为常态。现有的训练框架难以在维持训练轨迹（Training Trajectory）一致性的同时提供高效的容错能力。

Method: 提出了一种基于三个解耦协议层的弹性框架：(1) 故障隔离的集合通信（Fault-tolerant collectives）；(2) 迭代内细粒度恢复（In-step fine-grained recovery），防止梯度损坏；(3) 多功能的作业调度策略（Versatile-workload policy），动态重新分配微批次配额。该系统与 3D 并行和 HSDP 兼容。

Result: 在 512 个 GPU 的预训练任务中，即便丢失 256 个 GPU，ReCoVer 仍能保持与无故障基准相同的训练轨迹。相比检查点重启方案，有效吞吐量提升至 2.23 倍，在 234 GPU 小时内多处理了 74.9% 的 Token。

Conclusion: ReCoVer 通过保持迭代内微批次恒定，实现了与无故障训练轨迹等价的鲁棒性，在 GPU 故障频发环境下，其训练效率显著优于传统的检查点重启（checkpoint-and-restart）机制。

Abstract: Pre-training large language models on massive GPU clusters has made hardware faults routine rather than rare, driving the need for resilient training systems. Yet existing frameworks either focus on specific parallelism schemes or risk drifting away from a failure-free training trajectory. We propose ReCoVer, a resilient LLM pre-training system that upholds a single invariant: each iteration keeps the number of microbatches constant, ensuring per-iteration gradients remain stochastically equivalent to a failure-free run. The framework is organized as three decoupled protocol layers: (1) Fault-tolerant collectives that isolate faults from propagating across replicas; (2) in-step fine-grained recovery that preserves intra-iteration progress and prevents gradient corruption; (3) versatile-workload policy that dynamically redistributes microbatch quotas across the survivors. The design is parallelism-agnostic, integrating directly with both 3D parallelism and Hybrid Sharded Data Parallel (HSDP) as a drop-in substrate. We evaluate our implementation on end-to-end pre-training tasks for up to 512 GPUs, ReCoVer successfully preserves the training trajectory from a failure-free reference despite of 256 GPUs lost spread across the run. For comparison with checkpoint-and-restart baselines, ReCoVer demonstrates $2.23\times$ higher effective throughput after successive failures. This advantage results in ReCoVer processing 74.9% more tokens at 234 GPU-hours, with the gap widening as the training prolongs.

</details>


### [176] [MLCommons Chakra: Advancing Performance Benchmarking and Co-design using Standardized Execution Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11333)
*Srinivas Sridharan,Andy Balogh,Bradford M. Beckmann,Brian Coutinho,Louis Feng,Sheng Fu,Sanshan Gao,Mehryar Garakani,Taekyung Heo,David Kanter,Josh Ladd,Ziwei Li,Winston Liu,Changhai Man,Dan Mihailescu,Spandan More,Joongun Park,Ashwin Ramachandran,Vinay Ramakrishnaiah,Saeed Rashidi,Vijay Janapa Reddi,Puneet Sharma,Phio Tian,William Won,Hanjiang Wu,Huan Xu,Jinsun Yoo,Tushar Krishna*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Chakra 是一个支持分布式 AI 工作负载性能分析与软硬件协同设计的开源生态系统，通过基于图的标准化执行追踪（ET）格式，实现了对复杂 AI 任务行为的高效建模与模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能迭代迅速，当前生产环境缺乏敏捷的方法来高效观测、复现和优化分布式 ML 工作负载行为，这限制了高效软硬件协同设计（SW-HW Co-design）的能力。

Method: 提出了一种基于图（Graph-based）的工作负载表示方法——Chakra 执行追踪（Chakra Execution Trace, ET），并配套开发了一套用于 ET 采集、分析、生成及重放的工具链，支持模拟器和仿真器集成。

Result: 通过对生产环境 AI 集群采集的 ET 进行分析，验证了 Chakra 在实际场景下的建模价值；同时该项目已被 MLCommons 采纳，并获得 NVIDIA、AMD、Meta 等多家行业巨头支持与贡献。

Conclusion: Chakra 提供了一个开源、标准化且可互操作的 AI/ML 工作负载性能评估与软硬件协同设计生态系统，有效填补了生产环境机器学习工作负载表征的空白。

Abstract: The fast pace of artificial intelligence~(AI) innovation demands an agile methodology for observation, reproduction and optimization of distributed machine learning~(ML) workload behavior in production AI systems and enables efficient software-hardware~(SW-HW) co-design for future systems. We present Chakra, an open and portable ecosystem for performance benchmarking and co-design. The core component of Chakra is an open and interoperable graph-based representation of distributed AI/ML workloads, called Chakra execution trace~(ET). These ETs represent key operations, such as compute, memory, and communication, data and control dependencies, timing, and resource constraints. Additionally, Chakra includes a complementary set of tools and capabilities to enable the collection, analysis, generation, and adoption of Chakra ETs by a broad range of simulators, emulators, and replay tools. We present analysis of Chakra ETs collected on production AI clusters and demonstrate value via real-world case studies. Chakra has been adopted by MLCommons and has active contributions and engagement across the industry, including but not limited to NVIDIA, AMD, Meta, Keysight, HPE, and Scala, to name a few.

</details>


### [177] [ChunkFlow: Communication-Aware Chunked Prefetching for Layerwise Offloading in Distributed Diffusion Transformer Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11335)
*Han Meng,Danny Willow Liu,Dong Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ChunkFlow 是一种针对大型扩散模型推理的通信感知卸载框架，通过联合调度预取与 PCIe 通信解决带宽竞争问题，显著降低了显存需求并优化了推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分层卸载方法在小计算负载下无法隐藏预取延迟，且在 PCIe 环境下，预取与 GPU 间通信（如 all-reduce）存在带宽竞争，导致预取延迟暴露，影响推理性能。

Method: 提出了一种基于一阶分析模型的通信感知、分块粒度（chunk-granular）的卸载运行时系统 ChunkFlow；该系统能够自适应地为集体通信让路，并动态调节显存与预取量之间的平衡。

Result: 在双 H100 GPU PCIe 环境下运行扩散 Transformer 时，ChunkFlow 相比 SGLang 的分层卸载最高提升 1.28 倍的推理速度；在较大负载下，显存峰值降低了 49%，且在小负载下实现了接近零的额外耗时。

Conclusion: ChunkFlow 通过联合调度预取与通信，有效解决了扩散模型推理中分层卸载的性能瓶颈，在减少 GPU 显存占用的同时，实现了显著的速度提升和更灵活的性能权衡。

Abstract: Layerwise offloading reduces the GPU memory footprint of large diffusion transformer (DiT) inference by prefetching upcoming layers from host memory, but its effectiveness hinges on hiding prefetch latency behind per-layer computation. This assumption breaks down when the per-GPU compute workload is small. Moreover, on PCIe-only nodes, prefetch and inter-GPU collective communications such as all-reduce and all-to-all contend on the shared PCIe path, exposing prefetch latency even when compute would otherwise hide it. We revisit layerwise offloading as a co-scheduling problem between prefetch and communication, guided by a first-order analytical model that predicts when prefetch can be hidden by computation. Building on this model, we design ChunkFlow, a communication-aware, chunk-granular offloading runtime that adaptively yields to collective communication and smoothly trades GPU memory for prefetch volume. On three representative diffusion transformers running on two H100 GPUs over PCIe with Ulysses sequence parallelism, ChunkFlow delivers up to 1.28x step-time speedup over SGLang's existing layerwise offloading, reduces peak GPU memory by up to 49% over the no-offload baseline at near-identical step time once the workload is large enough, and exposes a tunable memory-latency tradeoff that recovers near-zero step-time overhead in the small-workload regime.

</details>


### [178] [GriNNder: Breaking the Memory Capacity Wall in Full-Graph GNN Training with Storage Offloading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11517)
*Jaeyong Song,Seongyeon Park,Hongsun Jang,Jaewon Jung,Hunseong Lim,Junguk Hong,Jinho Lee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: GriNNder 是首个利用高性能 NVMe SSD 实现大规模全图 GNN 训练的框架，通过结构化存储卸载（SSO）技术，克服了内存容量瓶颈，使受限资源下的单机全图训练得以实现且性能显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全图 GNN 训练需要保留完整邻域，导致高昂的分布式计算成本；而现有的单机训练方法在面对超大规模图时，受限于 GPU 和宿主机内存容量，难以进行全图训练。因此，该工作旨在利用高容量、高带宽的 NVMe SSD 作为辅助存储，实现资源受限下的全图训练。

Method: 提出了结构化存储卸载（SSO）框架，包含以下核心技术：（1）基于特征依赖性的分区级缓存策略；（2）减少冗余存储操作的梯度计算重收集策略；（3）轻量级图分区方案，以降低分区过程的内存开销。该方法通过协调 GPU-Host-SSD 三级存储层级，实现了对大规模图数据的高效流水线访问。

Result: 实验结果表明，GriNNder 在多种模型和数据集上均表现优异，最高可比当前最先进的基线方法提高 9.78 倍的加速比，且吞吐量与分布式系统相当，成功在单 GPU 环境下完成了以往需大规模算力支撑的大规模全图训练。

Conclusion: GriNNder 成功打破了单机全图 GNN 训练的内存限制，通过高效的存储层级管理，在单 GPU 上实现了可扩展、高性能的大规模全图训练。研究证明，结合高性能 NVMe SSD 和精心设计的缓存与数据调度机制，可以在大幅降低基础设施成本的同时，达到媲美分布式系统的吞吐量。

Abstract: Full-graph training of graph neural networks (GNNs) is widely used as it enables direct validation of algorithmic improvements by preserving complete neighborhood information. However, it typically requires multiple GPUs or servers, incurring substantial hardware and inter-device communication costs. While existing single-server methods reduce infrastructure requirements, they remain constrained by GPU and host memory capacity as graph sizes increase. To address this limitation, we introduce GriNNder, which is the first work to leverage storage devices to enable full-graph training even with limited memory. Because modern NVMe SSDs offer multi-terabyte capacities and bandwidths exceeding 10 GB/s, they provide an appealing option when memory resources are scarce. Yet, directly applying storage-based methods from other domains fails to address the unique access patterns and data dependencies in full-graph GNN training. GriNNder tackles these challenges by structured storage offloading (SSO), a framework that manages the GPU-host-storage hierarchy through coordinated cache, (re)gather, and bypass mechanisms. To realize the framework, we devise (i) a partition-wise caching strategy for host memory that exploits the observation on cross-partition dependencies, (ii) a regathering strategy for gradient computation that eliminates redundant storage operations, and (iii) a lightweight partitioning scheme that mitigates the memory requirements of existing graph partitioners. In experiments performed over various models and datasets, GriNNder achieves up to 9.78x speedup over state-of-the-art baselines and throughput comparable to distributed systems, enabling previously infeasible large-scale full-graph training even on a single GPU.

</details>


### [179] [State Twins: An Off-Chain Substrate for Agentic Reasoning over Decentralized Finance Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11522)
*Ian C. Moore*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 State Twin 架构，旨在将 DeFi 协议逻辑从链上时间解耦，通过在内存中创建精确的离线协议副本，使智能体能够执行高效的（如分叉、重放、回溯）推理任务，从而突破链上交互的延迟与成本限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的 DeFi 智能体过度依赖链上 RPC 查询和交易确认，导致推理受制于区块确认延迟和高昂 Gas 费用，限制了智能体的“假设性（what-if）”探索能力。

Method: 1. 将 AMM（Uniswap V2/V3, Balancer, Stableswap）形式化为离散时间受控动力系统；2. 构建 State Twin（一种类型化、内存中、可重放的 AMM 副本）；3. 量化并证明 twin 与链上实际状态之间的保真度界限；4. 开发开源工具集 DeFiPy v2，支持模型上下文协议（MCP）。

Result: 实现了在 sub-second（毫秒级）时间内基于单个链上快照同时运行 N 个独立的分叉模拟（fork-and-evaluate），证明了该 substrate 在复杂场景下的高效扩展能力。

Conclusion: State Twin 作为一种 DeFi 智能体架构的底层基元，通过解耦链上时间与推理过程，能够高效支持复杂的离线分析、回测及智能体决策，且具备数学严谨性和可移植性。

Abstract: We introduce the State Twin: a typed, in-memory, replayable replica of an on-chain automated market maker (AMM) pool that serves as a substrate for agentic reasoning over decentralized finance (DeFi) protocols. Agentic DeFi stacks today couple reasoning to chain time, since every "what if?" query incurs a new RPC read or a real transaction, so the agent's effective action space is bounded by block confirmation latency and gas. We argue this coupling is a structural problem rather than a performance one, and that the missing layer is an off-chain substrate that preserves the protocol's exact mathematics while admitting the operations on-chain state cannot: forking, replay, branching, counterfactual rollout. We formalize each AMM family (Uniswap V2, V3, Balancer, Stableswap) as a discrete-time controlled dynamical system, prove a quantitative fidelity bound on the divergence between twin and chain, and give the open architecture used in DeFiPy v2, an open-source Python toolkit that ships the State Twin substrate and a reference Model Context Protocol server exposing typed analytical primitives as LLM tools. The same primitive (i.e., one Python class, one calling pattern) serves a notebook quant, a backtest, and an LLM agent without modification. We close with a fork-and-evaluate worked example: a single live RPC read seeds N independent in-memory twins under distinct price-shock scenarios, in sub-second wall-clock time. The contribution is the substrate, not a particular agent, which is what the specification of what an agentic DeFi substrate must look like

</details>


### [180] [NAVIS: Concurrent Search and Update with Low Position-Seeking Overhead in On-SSD Graph-Based Vector Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11523)
*Jaeyong Song,Hongsun Jang,Changmin Shin,Seongyeon Park,Yong Jae Ryoo,Seo Jin Park,Jinho Lee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: NAVIS 是一种新型磁盘向量搜索系统，通过优化内存布局、动态更新入口图及智能缓存技术，克服了现有系统在高并发状态下“位置搜寻”效率低的难题，实现了向量数据库检索与插入性能的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有磁盘图向量搜索（GVS）系统在处理高维数据时，由于“位置搜寻”（Position Seeking）过程开销大，且受到“封装布局带来的冗余向量读取”和“静态入口点漂移”这两个系统性限制，导致高并发更新与检索性能不佳。

Method: 1. 引入“布局支持的选择性向量读取”：解耦封装页面，实现按需读取向量数据；2. 动态轻量级入口图更新机制：利用并发更新产生的遍历信息，避免静态入口的性能退化；3. 入口图感知的边列表缓存：专注于刷新入口点附近的高复用路径。

Result: 在多个大规模高维基准测试中，NAVIS 将平均插入吞吐量提升了最高 2.74 倍，平均并发检索吞吐量提升了最高 1.37 倍，并将平均检索延迟降低了最高 25.26%。

Conclusion: NAVIS 通过优化磁盘布局、动态更新入口图以及智能缓存策略，有效解决了大规模矢量数据库在高并发更新和检索场景下的性能瓶颈（特别是“位置搜寻”开销），显著提升了查找和插入效率。

Abstract: On-disk graph-based vector search (GVS) has become the dominant approach for serving large-scale vector databases at high recall, but prior systems struggle to sustain concurrent search and update throughput on high-dimensional workloads. We find the main cause of this in position seeking, a full graph traversal that every update performs to locate neighbors before linking the new vector into the graph. Position seeking is fundamentally heavier than a search query, and its cost is further amplified by two systemic limitations of current GVS systems, packed layouts that couple every edge fetch to a full vector load, and a static entrance graph whose entry points drift away from newly inserted regions as updates accumulate.
  We present NAVIS, an on-SSD GVS system that drives down position-seeking overhead through (i) a layout-supported selective vector read that breaks the packed-page coupling without losing its locality benefits, (ii) a dynamic lightweight entrance graph update mechanism that reuses traversal information already produced by concurrent updates, and (iii) an entrance graph-aware edgelist cache that concentrates capacity on high-reuse paths near refreshed entry points. Across multiple large-scale high-dimensional benchmarks, NAVIS enhances average insertion throughput by up to 2.74x and average concurrent search throughput by up to 1.37x while reducing average search latency by up to 25.26%.

</details>


### [181] [GraphFlash: Enabling Fast and Elastic Graph Processing on Serverless Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11631)
*Chen Zhao,Parsa Poorsistani,Mohammad Goudarzi,Tawfiq Islam,Adel N. Toosi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: GraphFlash是一个新型高性能无服务器图处理框架，通过创新的子图中心模型和特定系统优化，实现了超越现有无服务器系统的计算性能并大幅降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图处理系统依赖静态集群，弹性差且资源利用率低；现有的无服务器图处理系统因状态管理低效和外部存储通信开销大，导致性能受限。

Method: 1. 采用子图中心编程模型和共享外部存储实现无状态函数执行；2. 提供两种执行模式：旋转模式（资源受限）和固定模式（高性能）；3. 引入系统级优化：分区感知键聚合（partition-aware key aggregation）、函数内分区共置（intra-function partition co-location）和超步感知激活（superstep-aware activation）。

Result: 在多种图算法和数据集上，相比现有无服务器系统，执行时间最高提升127倍，资源消耗最高降低98%；在资源受限情况下，实现最高48倍加速和99.97%的成本降低，性能可媲美传统分布式框架。

Conclusion: GraphFlash通过创新的子图中心编程模型和系统级优化，成功解决了现有无服务器图处理系统的性能与成本瓶颈，验证了在无服务器基础设施上实现高性能、高弹性图计算的可行性。

Abstract: Graph processing systems are essential for analyzing large-scale data with complex relationships, yet most existing frameworks rely on statically provisioned clusters, resulting in poor elasticity and inefficient resource utilization under dynamic workloads. Serverless computing offers automatic scaling and fine-grained billing, but existing serverless graph systems suffer from performance limitations due to inefficient state management and high communication overhead through external storage. We present GraphFlash, a fast and elastic graph processing framework built on serverless infrastructure. GraphFlash adopts a subgraph-centric programming model and leverages shared external storage for coordination and communication, enabling stateless, fine-grained function execution. It supports two execution modes: rotating mode for resource-constrained environments and pinned mode for higher performance when resources are sufficient. To address serverless limitations, GraphFlash introduces system-level optimizations, including partition-aware key aggregation, intra-function partition co-location, and superstep-aware activation. Across multiple graph algorithms and datasets, GraphFlash outperforms existing serverless-compatible systems by up to 127x in execution time and reduces resource consumption by up to 98% under higher-resource configurations, while matching the performance of traditional distributed frameworks on large workloads. Even with limited resources, it achieves up to 48x speedup and 99.97% cost reduction over prior serverless solutions, demonstrating that GraphFlash makes serverless graph processing practical and performant.

</details>


### [182] [AB-Sparse: Sparse Attention with Adaptive Block Size for Accurate and Efficient Long-Context Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12110)
*Di Liu,Ruitian Wang,Chen Chen,Mingliang Gong,Yongjie Yuan,Han Zhao,Yu Feng,Quan Chen,Minyi Guo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AB-Sparse 是一种无需训练的算法-系统协同设计框架，通过为不同注意力头分配自适应的块大小并在硬件层面进行优化，解决了长上下文大模型 KV Cache 的性能与精度困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有块稀疏注意力机制采用统一的块大小，忽略了不同注意力头对块粒度敏感度不同的事实，导致模型精度受损；此外，KV Cache 随上下文长度增加导致的计算与访存瓶颈日益严重。

Method: 提出 AB-Sparse 框架，包括：1. 针对不同注意力头进行自适应块大小分配 (Adaptive Block Size Allocation)；2. 采用无损块质心量化 (Lossless Block Centroid Quantization) 以降低内存占用；3. 开发定制化 GPU 内核以支持变长块的高效执行。

Result: 在保持吞吐量不变的前提下，AB-Sparse 在现有块稀疏注意力基准测试上实现了高达 5.43% 的精度提升。

Conclusion: AB-Sparse 通过自适应块大小分配提高模型精度，并配合无损块质心量化补偿内存开销，同时通过定制 GPU 内核确保系统吞吐量不受影响，有效缓解了长上下文 KV Cache 的计算瓶颈。

Abstract: As large language models scale to longer contexts, loading the growing KV cache during attention computation becomes a critical bottleneck. Previous work has shown that attention computation is dominated by a small subset of tokens. This motivates block sparse attention methods that partition the KV cache into fixed-size blocks and selectively compute attention over those blocks exhibiting high importance. However, these methods assign a uniform block size across all attention heads, implicitly assuming homogeneous behavior throughout the model. Our analysis reveals that this assumption is flawed: attention heads exhibit widely varying sensitivity to block granularity, and uniformity leads to suboptimal accuracy. We present AB-Sparse, a training-free algorithm-system co-designed framework that improves accuracy while preserving throughput. AB-Sparse introduces lightweight adaptive block size allocation across attention heads to improve accuracy. To compensate for the additional memory overhead, it further employs lossless block centroid quantization. In addition, custom GPU kernels are developed to support efficient execution with variable block sizes. Evaluation results demonstrate that AB-Sparse achieves an accuracy improvement of up to 5.43% over existing block sparse attention baselines without throughput overhead.

</details>


### [183] [NCCLZ: Compression-Enabled GPU Collectives with Decoupled Quantization and Entropy Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12396)
*Jiamin Wang,Zhijing Ye,Xiaodong Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: NCCLZ 是一种针对多节点 GPU 计算的压缩通信库，通过解耦量化/熵编码并将其深度整合入 NCCL，实现了高效的压缩通信重叠，显著降低了多节点通信瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压缩通信库存在局限性：一是依赖 MPI 栈无法充分发挥 NCCL 性能；二是缺乏熵编码导致压缩比受限；三是压缩算法与通信原语耦合严重，限制了通信与计算的重叠效率。

Method: 提出了 NCCLZ 框架：1. 解耦量化（接口层）与熵编码（NCCL 层）；2. 在 NCCL 基本操作中嵌入熵编码；3. 开发设备端轻量级策略选择器；4. 实现压缩与通信的流水线化重叠（Overlap）。

Result: 在科学计算、深度学习梯度及合成负载下，相比标准 NCCL 实现了高达 9.65 倍的加速，相比现有压缩辅助通信库性能提升高达 3.34 倍。

Conclusion: NCCLZ 通过解耦量化与熵编码，并将其深度集成到 NCCL 原始操作中，证明了在有限带宽环境下，通过流水线化的压缩与通信重叠，可显著提升分布式多节点 GPU 任务的性能。

Abstract: Collective communication is a major bottleneck for multi-node GPU workloads in scientific computing and distributed deep learning, especially when inter-node bandwidth is limited. Although NCCL provides optimized GPU-centric collectives, large messages can still dominate end-to-end performance. Existing compression-enabled collective libraries either rely on MPI-based stacks that cannot fully exploit NCCL, omit entropy coding, or tightly couple full compressors with communication primitives, limiting compression ratio, flexibility, and communication-computation overlap. This paper presents NCCLZ, a compression-enabled GPU collectives that decouples quantization and entropy coding and integrates them at different layers of the stack. NCCLZ places quantization at the interface, embeds entropy coding into NCCL primitives, uses a lightweight device-side selector to choose coding strategies, and overlaps compression with communication to reduce exposed overhead. Experiments on scientific datasets, training gradients, and synthetic workloads show up to 9.65x speedup over NCCL and up to 3.34x improvement over prior compression-assisted collective libraries.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [Debiasing Message Passing to Mitigate Popularity Bias in GNN-based Collaborative Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11145)
*Md Aminul Islam,Ahmed Sayeed Faruk,Sourav Medya,Elena Zheleva*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了 DPAA 框架，通过在 GNN 消息传递的聚合阶段直接引入嵌入感知的交互加权和层级加权，有效解决了流行度偏差被反复传播和放大的痛点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 GNN 的协同过滤模型存在流行度偏差问题，现有的去偏方法（如重加权或因果推断）无法在 GNN 的消息传递聚合过程中有效缓解偏差，且既有聚合加权方法往往依赖不稳定的静态启发式规则。

Method: 提出 DPAA (Debiasing Popularity Amplification in Aggregation) 框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 基于嵌入感知的自适应交互加权，利用从预训练到动态训练平滑过渡的权重来稳定信号；(2) 层级加权机制，通过放大高阶邻域信号来平衡长尾物品的交互。

Result: 在真实世界及半合成数据集上的实验表明，DPAA 在处理流行度偏差纠正方面优于现有的 SOTA 方法。

Conclusion: DPAA 框架通过在消息传递阶段引入自适应的交互级和层级加权，有效缓解了基于 GNN 的协同过滤模型中的流行度偏差问题，并在实验中优于现有的前沿去偏方法。

Abstract: Collaborative filtering (CF) models based on graph neural networks (GNNs) achieve strong performance in recommender systems by propagating user-item signals over interaction graphs. However, they are highly susceptible to popularity bias, since skewed interaction distributions and repeated message passing across high-order neighborhoods amplify the influence of popular items while suppressing long-tail ones. Existing debiasing approaches, including re-weighting objectives, regularization, causal methods, and post-processing, are less effective in GNN-based settings because they do not directly counteract bias propagated through the aggregation process, and recent in-aggregation weighting methods often rely on static heuristics or unstable embedding estimates. We propose Debiasing Popularity Amplification in Aggregation (DPAA), a popularity debiasing framework for GNN-based CF that integrates adaptive, embedding-aware interaction weighting and layer-wise weighting directly into message passing. DPAA assigns interaction-level weights from a representation-aware popularity signal, stabilized by a smooth transition from pre-trained to evolving model embeddings during training. It further introduces a layer-wise weighting that amplifies higher-order neighborhoods, surfacing long-range interactions with diverse and underexposed items. Experiments on real-world and semi-synthetic datasets show that DPAA outperforms state-of-the-art popularity-bias correction methods for GNN-based CF.

</details>


### [185] [MIRA: An LLM-Assisted Benchmark for Multi-Category Integrated Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11254)
*Mehmet Deniz Türkmen,Suchana Datta,Dwaipayan Roy,Daniel Hienert,Philipp Mayr,Derek Greene*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对现有信息检索基准缺乏多源异构数据支持的问题，本文提出了MIRA基准测试。该工具通过整合四类学术资源并利用大语言模型降低构建成本，为研究多面、类别感知的综合信息检索系统提供了重要的评估资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代检索系统需要统一接口处理多源异构信息，但现有的信息检索（IR）评价基准缺乏代表当代检索领域多样性的测试集，无法满足这一趋势的需求。

Method: 1. 构建了一个包含“出版物、科研数据、变量、工具与仪器”四类异构数据的真实社交科学搜索平台数据集；
2. 收集用户真实查询作为任务基础；
3. 引入大语言模型（LLM）自动化生成主题描述、叙述语境以及辅助进行相关性判断，大幅降低了基准测试构建的成本与人力需求。

Result: 构建了一个名为MIRA的综合性评价基准，支持类别感知的跨源排序任务，提供了一个比传统数据集更贴近真实用户场景、具备多维度评估能力的科研实验平台。

Conclusion: MIRA填补了当前信息检索评价中缺乏多元化、跨类别测试集的空白，为开发和评估能够处理异构数据源的现代搜索系统提供了重要的基准测试工具，验证了利用LLM构建高质量评测数据集的可行性。

Abstract: Users increasingly expect modern search systems to offer a unified interface that seamlessly retrieves information from diverse data sources and formats. However, current information retrieval (IR) evaluation benchmarks have not kept pace with this development, primarily due to the lack of test collections that represent the diversity of contemporary search domains. We address this critical gap with MIRA, a novel benchmark based on a large-scale social science search platform. MIRA is designed for category-aware ranking across heterogeneous categories - Publications, Research Data, Variables, and Instruments & Tools - within a single, unified evaluation framework. The proposed collection is distinctive in several ways: (1) it is built upon real user queries, providing a more realistic basis for evaluation; (2) it covers scholarly items from four distinct categories, enabling multi-faceted evaluation; and (3) it leverages a Large Language Model to generate topic descriptions and narratives, as well as for relevance assessment with respect to these topics, substantially reducing the labor and cost of test collection generation. We release this resource to benefit the community by providing a foundational testbed for the research on multi-faceted, category-aware, integrated, or cross-category information retrieval.

</details>


### [186] [Much of Geospatial Web Search Is Beyond Traditional GIS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11336)
*Ilya Ilyankou,Stefano Cavazzi,James Haworth*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究通过大规模数据挖掘发现地理空间查询在Web搜索中的比例被显著低估（18%），指出搜索需求正从传统的空间定位转向动态、实用的 transactional 查询，建议地理系统从静态数据库向LLM驱动的实时生成系统转型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标注方案低估了Web搜索中地理查询的比例，且缺乏对地理空间搜索查询内容及其分布特征的大规模、细粒度刻画。

Method: 利用稠密句子嵌入（Dense Sentence Embeddings）、轻量级 SetFit 分类器及基于密度的聚类算法，对包含101万条真实查询的MS MARCO语料库进行非地理词汇预筛选挖掘；构建了包含88个类别的空间查询分类法。

Result: 从MS MARCO中识别出181,827条地理空间查询（占比18.0%，远高出原标注的6.17%）；揭示了价格（15.3%）、开业时间、天气等实用查询占据主导地位，远超地理基础常识类查询。

Conclusion: 地理空间Web搜索不仅规模远超预期，且高度偏向事务性和实用性需求。传统的GIS和知识图谱难以覆盖这些动态或评估类请求，这对构建混合检索架构和评估LLM的地理推理能力提出了新要求。

Abstract: Web search queries concern place far more often than existing labelling schemes suggest, yet the landscape of geospatial web search queries - what people ask of place, and how often - remains poorly characterised at scale.
