We apply dense sentence embeddings, a lightweight SetFit classifier, and density-based clustering to the full MS MARCO corpus of 1.01 million real Bing queries without prior filtering for toponyms or spatial keywords, identifying 181,827 geospatial queries (18.0%), nearly threefold the 6.17% labelled as Location in the original annotations. The resulting taxonomy of 88 query categories reveals that geospatial web search is dominated by transactional and practical lookups: costs and prices alone account for 15.3% of geospatial queries, nearly twice the size of the entire physical geography theme. Much of this activity - costs, opening hours, contact details, weather, travel recommendations - falls outside the scope traditional GIS systems and knowledge graphs are built to serve. The categories vary substantially in the kind of answer they admit, from deterministic lookups answerable from spatial databases or knowledge graphs to evaluative or temporally volatile queries that require generative or real-time systems. We discuss implications for hybrid retrieval architectures and for benchmarks of geographic reasoning in large language models. We openly release the labelled dataset, classifier, and taxonomy.

</details>


### [187] [FedMM: Federated Collaborative Signal Quantization for Multi-Market CTR Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11433)
*Jun Zhang,Dugang Liu,Xing Tang,Xiuqiang He,Zhong Ming*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对多市场推荐中隐私保护与 ID 空间不重叠的挑战，FedMM 利用分层量化技术实现了跨市场的协同信号迁移，在保障隐私的同时提升了 CTR 预测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多市场推荐 (MMR) 范式在处理跨市场数据时面临两大痛点：1. 集中式训练忽略了市场数据隐私；2. 联邦学习方法在处理市场间异构性及不重叠 ID 空间（ID 空间割裂）时效果较差。

Method: 提出联邦协同信号量化方法 (FedMM)，引入残差量化变分自编码器 (RQ-VAE) 和双层代码本（全局共享代码本 + 本地特异性代码本）机制，对协同嵌入进行量化从而规避原始数据传输并对齐不重叠的 ID 空间。

Result: 在 Benchmark 数据集上的实验结果表明，FedMM 在提供隐私保障的前提下，显著提升了 CTR 预测的准确度，优于现有的联邦学习及传统推荐基准。

Conclusion: FedMM 成功解决了多市场推荐中的数据隐私、市场异构性以及 ID 空间不重叠的问题，通过层次化代码本设计在保护隐私的同时有效提升了推荐系统的 CTR 预测性能。

Abstract: Online platforms such as Amazon and Netflix serve users across multiple countries and regions, underscoring the importance of multi-market recommendation (MMR). Most MMR methods adopt a pre-training and fine-tuning paradigm, in which a unified model is first trained on centralized, global data and subsequently adapted to specific markets. However, this approach ignores the privacy of market data. While traditional federated learning preserves privacy, it typically aims to obtain a global model by aggregating model parameters and does not account for significant market heterogeneity. Additionally, because ID spaces are disjoint across markets, embedding-based aggregation strategies become ineffective. To overcome these challenges, we propose a federated collaborative signal quantization (FedMM) method for multi-market click-through rate (CTR) prediction. Our core idea leverages a discrete codebook mechanism to achieve privacy-preserving transmission and align disjoint ID spaces. We further employ a hierarchical codebook structure to capture cross-market shared patterns and market-specific characteristics. Specifically, we deploy a residual quantized variational autoencoder (RQ-VAE) with a dual-layer codebook mechanism for each market to quantize collaborative embeddings. The first layer utilizes a global federated codebook, updated via aggregation to capture universally shared collaborative patterns, while the second layer maintains a local codebook to learn market-specific semantics. Finally, the learned discrete codes, which integrate both general and specific collaborative signals, are incorporated into downstream CTR models to enhance prediction accuracy across all markets. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that FedMM significantly improves recommendation performance with privacy guarantees.

</details>


### [188] [Conditional Memory Enhanced Item Representation for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11447)
*Ziwei Liu,Yejing Wang,Shengyu Zhou,Xinhang Li,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对生成式推荐中SID表征构建的局限性，本文提出了ComeIR框架，通过模态引导的评分、双层Engram记忆和记忆恢复预测头，有效解决了身份信息的结构保持问题与颗粒度匹配挑战，实现了更精准的项生成推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐（GR）方法中，直接合并SID-token嵌入会导致量化信息丢失和ID冲突，而引入外部输入的方法则难以保持生成所需的SID结构化证据，导致“身份-结构保持冲突”及“输入输出粒度失配”问题。

Method: 提出ComeIR (Conditional Memory enhanced Item Representation) 框架：1. 采用多模态（MM）引导的Token评分机制，自适应评估SID内各代码的贡献度；2. 构建双层Engram记忆模块，分别捕捉项内代码组合与项间转移模式；3. 设计记忆恢复预测头，在SID解码过程中重用记忆信息。

Result: 大量实验表明，ComeIR在推荐任务中表现出优越的有效性和灵活性，且随着条件记忆规模扩大，模型性能表现出显著的扩展收益。

Conclusion: 本文提出的ComeIR框架有效解决了生成式推荐中信息丢失、ID冲突及输入输出粒度不匹配的问题，通过引入条件记忆增强了表征能力，显著提升了推荐性能。

Abstract: Generative recommendation (GR) has emerged as a promising paradigm that predicts target items by autoregressively generating their semantic identifiers (SID). Most GR methods follow a quantization-representation-generation pipeline, first assigning each item a SID, then constructing input representations from SID-token embeddings, and finally predicting the target SID through autoregressive generation. Existing item-level representation constructions mainly take two forms: directly merging SID-token embeddings into a compact vector, or enriching item-level representations with external inputs through additional networks. However, these item-level constructors still expose two practical challenges: direct merging may amplify the information loss caused by quantization and ID collision while obscuring SID code relations, whereas external-input-based methods can strengthen item semantics but cannot reliably preserve the SID-structured evidence required for token-level generation. These limitations make representation construction an underexplored bottleneck, leading to two severe problems, \ie{} the Identity-Structure Preservation Conflict and Input-Output Granularity Mismatch. To this end, we propose ComeIR, a Conditional Memory enhanced Item Representation framework that reconstructs SID-token embeddings into item-aware inputs and restores the token granularity during SID decoding. Specifically, MM-guided token scoring adaptively estimates the contribution of each code within the SID, dual-level Engram memory captures intra-item code composition and inter-item transition patterns, and a memory-restoring prediction head reuses the memories during SID decoding. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and flexibility of ComeIR, and further reveal scalable gains from enlarging conditional memory.

</details>


### [189] [TwiSTAR:Think Fast, Think Slow, Then Act,Generative Recommendation with Adaptive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11553)
*Shiteng Cao,Kaian Jiang,Yunlong Gong,Zhiheng Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对生成式推荐中推理效率与精度的权衡问题，本文提出了一种自适应动态分配推理策略的框架，通过智能体按需调度快速检索和慢速推理资源，实现了计算效率与推荐质量的双重优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐方法在推理策略上不够灵活，统一使用快速生成或慢速思维链（CoT）推理会导致在困难样本上精度不足，或在简单样本上造成不必要的计算浪费。

Method: 提出了“Think Fast, Think Slow, Then Act”框架。系统包含三个工具：基于语义ID（SID）的快速检索器、轻量级候选排序器以及引入了协作常识的慢速推理模型。一个通过有监督预训练和智能体强化学习训练的规划器，负责根据用户历史序列的难度动态选择工具。

Result: 在三个数据集上的实验结果表明，该框架在保持较低延迟的同时，显著提升了推荐准确率，优于多种强基线模型。

Conclusion: 通过引入动态决策机制，该研究有效平衡了推荐系统在精度与计算延迟之间的权衡，证明了自适应推理策略在生成式推荐任务中的优越性。

Abstract: Generative recommendation with Semantic IDs (SIDs) has emerged as a promising paradigm, yet existing methods apply a fixed inference strategy, either fast direct generation or slow chain-of-thought reasoning, uniformly across all user histories. This approach creates a trade-off: fast recommendation model produces suboptimal accuracy on hard samples, while always invoking slow reasoning incurs prohibitive latency and wastes computation on easy cases. To address this, we propose Think Fast, Think Slow, Then Act, a framework that learns to adaptively allocate reasoning effort per user sequence. Our system equips an LLM with three complementary tools: a fast SID-based retriever, a lightweight candidate ranker, and a slow reasoning model that generates explicit rationales before recommending. Crucially, we inject collaborative commonsense into the slow model by transforming item-to-item knowledge into natural language explanations. A planner, trained through supervised warm-up followed by agentic reinforcement learning, dynamically decides which tool to invoke. Experiments on three datasets demonstrate that our method outperforms strong baselines, achieving consistent accuracy gains while reducing inference latency compared to uniform slow reasoning.

</details>


### [190] [HSUGA: LLM-Enhanced Recommendation with Hierarchical Semantic Understanding and Group-Aware Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11662)
*Guorui Li,Dugang Liu,Lei Li,Xing Tang,Zhong Ming*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 序列推荐中长序列语义提取困难及用户差异忽略问题，提出 HSUGA 框架，通过“分层理解”与“群体感知对齐”插件，显著提升了推荐准确性与自适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 推荐方法存在两大局限：一是直接处理长序列导致推断不准；二是通用语义利用策略未考虑用户差异（活跃水平）。

Method: 提出 HSUGA 框架，包含两个插件：1) 分层语义理解（HSU）：采用两阶段偏好挖掘并利用受限编辑操作建模偏好演化；2) 群体感知对齐（GAA）：根据用户活跃度动态调整语义利用强度。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验结果表明，HSUGA 能有效提升序列推荐性能。

Conclusion: HSUGA 通过 HSU 和 GAA 插件解决了 LLM 推荐中语义提取不可靠和利用策略单一的问题，经实验验证其有效性和兼容性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-enhanced sequential recommendation typically aims to improve two core components: user semantic embedding extraction and utilization. Despite promising results, existing methods still have two limitations: 1) In the extraction stage, most methods directly input long interaction sequence fragments into LLM for preference summarization. However, excessively long sequences increase inference difficulty, making it challenging to reliably infer accurate user embeddings. 2) In the utilization stage, most methods employ the same semantic embedding utilization strategy for all users, neglecting the differences caused by user activity levels, leading to suboptimal performance. To address these issues, we propose HSUGA, which introduces a simple yet effective plugin for each of the two core components: Hierarchical Semantic Understanding (HSU) and Group-Aware Alignment (GAA). HSU performs a staged two-phase preference mining and models preference evolution through constrained editing operations, thereby improving the reliability of user semantic extraction. GAA adjusts the intensity of semantic utilization based on user activity levels, providing weaker alignment for active users and stronger guidance for users with sparse historical data. Finally, extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate the effectiveness and compatibility of HSUGA.

</details>


### [191] [Quality-Aware Collaborative Multi-Positive Contrastive Learning for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11707)
*Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对序列推荐中对比学习视图构造质量与多样性的矛盾，本文提出了 QCMP-CL，通过协同上下文增强生成多样化视图，并利用置信度驱动的质量感知机制进行动态加权，提升了推荐的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比学习序列推荐方法依赖启发式增强，易导致信息丢失和语义漂移；现有可学习增强多样性不足；且各视图间的质量差异缺乏显式建模，导致模型易受低质量视图干扰。

Method: 1. 引入协同序列增强模块，通过“相同目标”与“相似序列”两种互补上下文生成增强视图；2. 设计质量感知机制，根据增强操作的置信度估计视图质量，并据此对对比学习损失进行动态加权。

Result: 在三个真实数据集上的实验结果表明，QCMP-CL 有效提升了模型性能，优于当前的对比学习序列推荐基准。

Conclusion: 提出的 QCMP-CL 框架通过协同增强与质量感知机制，有效解决了序列推荐中对比学习存在的视图质量差、语义漂移及假阴性问题，在实验中表现优于现有最优基准。

Abstract: The effectiveness of contrastive learning in sequential recommendation hinges on the construction of contrastive views, which ideally should be both semantically consistent and diverse. However, most existing CL-based methods rely on heuristic augmentations that are prone to removing crucial items or disrupting transition patterns, leading to semantic drift. While a few studies have explored learnable augmentations to improve view quality, they often suffer from limited diversity and still necessitate heuristic aids. Furthermore, the quality differences across views are rarely modeled explicitly and adaptively, aggravating the false-positive issue. To address these issues, we propose Quality-aware Collaborative Multi-Positive Contrastive Learning for sequential recommendation. First, we introduce a learnable collaborative sequence augmentation module that generates two augmented views under two complementary collaborative contexts, one based on same-target sequences and the other on similar sequences, thereby enhancing view diversity while preserving intent consistency.Second, we design a quality-aware mechanism, tightly integrated into the model representations, which estimates each view' s quality from the confidence of its augmentation operations and assigns adaptive weights to ensure that high-confidence views contribute more supervision while low-confidence ones contribute less.Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate that QCMP-CL outperforms state-of-the-art CL-based sequential recommendation baselines.

</details>


### [192] [Very Efficient Listwise Multimodal Reranking for Long Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11864)
*Yiqun Sun,Pengfei Wei,Lawrence B. Hsieh*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ZipRerank 是一种高效的多模态列表式重排序器，通过轻量级交互和非自回归推理，在提升多模态检索速度的同时，保持了与现有先进模型相当的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态重排序器（基于 VLM）因视觉 Token 序列过长及依赖多步自回归解码，导致计算资源消耗大、推理延迟高，难以满足实际应用需求。

Method: 1. 引入轻量级查询-图像早期交互机制压缩输入长度；2. 舍弃自回归解码，采用单前向传播对所有候选对象进行评分；3. 采用两阶段训练：基于文本转图像数据的列表式预训练，以及利用 VLM 教师模型进行蒸馏的列表式微调。

Result: 在 MMDocIR 基准测试中，性能达到或超过了当前最先进的多模态重排序器，同时将 LLM 推理延迟降低了一个数量级。

Conclusion: ZipRerank 通过高效的架构设计和两阶段训练策略，在保持先进多模态重排序性能的同时，显著降低了推理延迟，是实现高性能、低延迟多模态检索与生成（M-RAG）系统的理想方案。

Abstract: Listwise reranking is a key yet computationally expensive component in vision-centric retrieval and multimodal retrieval-augmented generation (M-RAG) over long documents. While recent VLM-based rerankers achieve strong accuracy, their practicality is often limited by long visual-token sequences and multi-step autoregressive decoding. We propose ZipRerank, a highly efficient listwise multimodal reranker that directly addresses both bottlenecks. It reduces input length via a lightweight query-image early interaction mechanism and eliminates autoregressive decoding by scoring all candidates in a single forward pass. To enable effective learning, ZipRerank adopts a two-stage training strategy: (i) listwise pretraining on large-scale text data rendered as images, and (ii) multimodal finetuning with VLM-teacher-distilled soft-ranking supervision. Extensive experiments on the MMDocIR benchmark show that ZipRerank matches or surpasses state-of-the-art multimodal rerankers while reducing LLM inference latency by up to an order of magnitude, making it well-suited for latency-sensitive real-world systems. The code is available at https://github.com/dukesun99/ZipRerank.

</details>


### [193] [From Trajectories to Phenotypes: Disease Progression as Structural Priors for Multi-organ Imaging Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11958)
*Zian Wang,Lizhen Lan,Guangming Wang,Haosen Zhang,Minxuan Xu,Qing Li,Tianxing He,Mo Yang,Wenyue Mao,Yajing Zhang,Yan Li,Chengyan Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将 longitudinal EHR 疾病轨迹知识蒸馏至影像表型（IDP）的框架，通过跨模态几何对齐，有效缓解了影像学数据在疾病演变捕捉上的局限性，提升了临床预测的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成像衍生表型（IDP）仅能提供疾病的静态快照，而电子健康记录（EHR）涵盖了疾病的时序演变及共病结构，二者包含部分共享的疾病相关结构。研究旨在利用EHR丰富的时序信息增强有限影像学数据的表征学习能力。

Method: 提出了一种“轨迹感知蒸馏框架”（Trajectory-aware Distillation Framework）：1. 使用Transformer训练生成式疾病轨迹模型，提取纵向电子健康记录（EHR）的轨迹嵌入；2. 通过几何保持对齐（Geometry-preserving Alignment），将这些轨迹知识迁移至器官维度的IDP编码器；3. 在下游预测中，通过交叉注意力（Cross-attention）融合轨迹与影像学表征。

Result: 在英国生物样本库（UK Biobank）159种疾病的验证中：1. 轨迹感知预训练一致提升了疾病鉴别（AUC）和发病时间预测（MAE）的性能；2. 对低患病率疾病的增益最显著；3. IDP嵌入空间与轨迹嵌入空间的相似性结构高度一致。

Conclusion: 基于人口规模的生成式疾病轨迹模型可作为数据受限成像模态的结构先验，显著提升成像衍生表型（IDP）在疾病预测及发病时间预测任务中的性能，并在低患病率疾病上表现尤为突出，证明了轨迹与影像学表征空间的几何对齐有效性。

Abstract: Imaging-derived phenotypes (IDPs) summarize multi-organ physiology but provide only static snapshots of diseases that evolve over time. In contrast, longitudinal electronic health records encode disease trajectories through temporal dependencies among past diagnosis events and comorbidity structure. We hypothesize that IDPs and disease trajectories contain partially shared disease-relevant structure. We propose a trajectory-aware distillation framework that transfers structural knowledge from a generative disease trajectory Transformer into an organ-wise IDP encoder. A population-scale trajectory model trained on longitudinal diagnosis sequences produces subject-level embeddings that supervise IDP representation learning via geometry-preserving alignment. During downstream prediction, trajectory and imaging representations can also be fused via cross-attention. Across 159 diseases in the UK Biobank cohort, trajectory-aware pretraining consistently improves both discrimination (AUC) and time-to-onset prediction (MAE), with the largest gains for low-prevalence diseases. Similarity relationships in IDP embedding space also align with those in trajectory space, providing supportive evidence for partially aligned representation geometry. These results suggest that population-scale generative disease models can serve as structural priors for data-limited imaging modalities, improving robustness under realistic cohort constraints.

</details>


### [194] [Unlocking Crowdsourcing for Ontology Matching Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12226)
*Zhangcheng Qiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型产生的海量本体匹配结果验证困难的问题，本文提出了一套结合差异化可信度、一致性预填充和时间依赖性信念机制的众包验证系统，有效实现了人机协同匹配验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型（LLM）的进步极大提高了本体匹配的效率，但随之产生的大量匹配结果令传统的专家验证方案不堪重负。

Method: 提出了一套创新的本体匹配众包验证系统，并设计了三种机制：1. 差异化可信度（Differential Trustworthiness）；2. 一致性预填充（Coherence Pre-filling）；3. 时间依赖性信念（Time-dependent Beliefs）。

Result: 通过两个真实世界案例验证，提出的系统有效保证了验证质量，证明了其在实际应用场景中的有效性和适用性。

Conclusion: 该研究证实了众包与先进本体匹配（OM）系统的集成能够有效实现人机协同（Human-in-the-loop）验证，解决了大规模语言模型生成海量匹配结果带来的验证瓶颈。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) pose new challenges for ontology matching (OM). While OM systems built on LLMs have shown remarkable capabilities in discovering more mappings, traditional OM validation that relies on domain experts has become overwhelming. In this study, we explore the use of crowdsourcing for OM validation and introduce a novel crowdsourcing system. We propose three domain-specific mechanisms, namely differential trustworthiness, coherence pre-filling, and time-dependent beliefs, to ensure the quality of crowdsourcing for OM validation. We demonstrate that our crowdsourcing system can be integrated with state-of-the-art OM systems to enable human-in-the-loop validation. Two real-world use cases illustrate the effectiveness of our crowdsourcing system.

</details>


### [195] [EHR-RAGp: Retrieval-Augmented Prototype-Guided Foundation Model for Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12335)
*Saeed Shurrab,Mariam Al-Omari,Dana El Samad,Farah E. Shamout*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了 EHR-RAGp，一种通过原型引导检索机制动态聚合关键临床历史的检索增强框架，解决了 EHR 数据中长序列处理与相关信息提取的难题，显著提升了模型在临床任务中的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 EHR 预测模型在处理长时轨迹、异构事件、时间不规则性以及区分过去临床背景的相关性方面存在局限，传统的固定窗口或统一聚合方法往往会忽略关键的临床信号。

Method: 提出了一种名为 EHR-RAGp 的检索增强基础模型，该模型包含一个原型引导（prototype-guided）的检索模块，用于对齐临床任务并评估历史数据块（chunks）的相关性，从而实现对医疗历史记录的动态整合。

Result: 在多个临床预测任务中，EHR-RAGp 的表现均优于现有的 SOTA EHR 基础模型和基于 Transformer 的基线模型；此外，将其集成到现有临床基础模型中可带来显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: EHR-RAGp 提供了一种可扩展且高效的框架，通过动态检索和整合长时临床语境，显著提升了电子健康记录（EHR）领域的下游预测性能。

Abstract: Electronic Health Records (EHR) contain rich longitudinal patient information and are widely used in predictive modeling applications. However, effectively leveraging historical data remains challenging due to long trajectories, heterogeneous events, temporal irregularity, and the varying relevance of past clinical context. Existing approaches often rely on fixed windows or uniform aggregation, which can obscure clinically important signals. In this work, we introduce EHR-RAGp, a retrieval-augmented foundation model that dynamically integrates the most relevant patient history across diverse clinical event types. We propose a prototype-guided retrieval module that acts as an alignment mechanism and estimates the relevance of retrieved historical chunks with respect to a given prediction task, guiding the model towards the most informative context. Across multiple clinical prediction tasks, EHR-RAGp consistently outperforms state-of-the-art EHR foundation models and transformer-based baselines. Furthermore, integrating EHR-RAGp with existing clinical foundation models yields substantial performance gains. Overall, EHR-RAGp provides a scalable and efficient framework for leveraging long-range clinical context to improve downstream performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [A Cascaded Generative Approach for e-Commerce Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11118)
*Moein Hasani,Hamidreza Shahidi,Trace Levinson,Yuan Zhong,Guanghua Shu,Vinesh Gudla,Tejaswi Tenneti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对电商首页构建僵化的问题，本文提出了一种生成式级联商品化框架，通过生成主题与关键词辅助检索，配合教师-学生微调与质量过滤机制，实现了在满足生产性能要求下的大规模个性化升级，线上实验显著提升了转化率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的电商首页构建模式（静态主题+检索+点对点排序）过于僵化，难以实现页面间的个性化与语义连贯性，且难以动态满足复杂的商业化需求。

Method: 提出了一种级联商品化框架，包含两个生成式任务：1. 页面级主题生成；2. 基于约束的关键词生成（用于辅助产品检索）。利用教师-学生模型微调技术以满足生产环境下的延迟和成本限制，并将生成内容与传统排序模型相融合。设计了 AI 驱动的内容评估与质量过滤框架。

Result: 微调后的模型性能接近闭源商用大模型；在线实验显示，该框架使页面浏览后的购物车添加率（cart adds per page view）提升了约 2.7%。

Conclusion: 该论文提出的生成式级联商品化框架能够有效提升电商平台的个性化与页面语义连贯性，通过优化生成式模型与传统排序系统的融合，实现了在保持线上性能的同时显著提升用户互动指标（购物车添加率），证明了生成式 AI 在大规模电商场景中部署的可行性。

Abstract: Personalized storefronts in large e-commerce marketplaces are often assembled from many independent components: static themes per page section ("placement"), retrieval systems to fetch eligible products per placement, and pointwise rankers to order content. While effective in optimizing for aggregate preferences, this paradigm is rigid and can limit personalization and semantic cohesion across the page. This makes it poorly suited to support dynamic objectives and merchandising requirements over time. To address this, we introduce a cascaded merchandising framework that decomposes storefront construction into two generative tasks: (i) placement-level theme generation and (ii) constrained keyword generation per placement to power product retrieval. Teacher-student fine-tuning is leveraged to improve scalability of this framework under production latency and cost constraints. Fine-tuned model ablations are shown to approach closed-weight LLM performance. We further contribute frameworks for AI-driven content evaluation and quality filtering, enabling safe and automated deployment of dynamic content at scale. Generative output is fused with traditional ranking models to preserve hybrid infrastructure. In online experiments, this framework yields an estimated +2.7% lift in cart adds per page view over a strong baseline.

</details>


### [197] [EVOCHAMBER: Test-Time Co-evolution of Multi-Agent System at Individual, Team, and Population Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11136)
*Yaolun Zhang,Tianyi Xu,Shengyu Dai,Zhenwen Shao,Qingyun Wu,Huazheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EVOCHAMBER是一个无需训练的测试时演进框架，通过非对称的“协作梦境”机制和动态种群管理，实现了跨智能体的知识高效迁移，在复杂任务中成功涌现出专业化的智能体团队。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时演进方法要么局限于单体学习（缺乏跨智能体协作），要么采用对称广播（抹除协作所需的专业化分工）。研究者旨在通过多智能体演进探索协同合作、知识流向和涌现式专业化。

Method: 提出EVOCHAMBER框架，包含三个核心层面：1. CODREAM（协作梦境）：在任务失败或分歧时，通过非对称知识蒸馏从强智能体向弱智能体传输专有知识；2. 团队级操作：在线组建任务相关的团队并动态调整协作结构；3. 种群级生命周期管理：通过变异、合并、修剪和播种机制维护种群质量。该方法无需额外训练。

Result: 在数学竞赛、代码编程和多领域推理任务中，EVOCHAMBER取得了优异表现（最高达87.1%准确率），较基线模型在数学任务上有32%的相对提升；实验观察到四个到五个稳定利基专家的涌现。

Conclusion: EVOCHAMBER证明了多智能体测试时演进并非单一智能体演进的简单叠加。通过非对称的知识迁移和结构化的种群管理，该框架能够引发突现的专业化分工，显著提升复杂任务处理能力。未来应进一步研究这种演进机制在不同任务复杂度下的扩展性。

Abstract: We argue that multi-agent test-time evolution is not single-agent evolution replicated N times. A single-agent learner can only evolve its own context and memory. A multi-agent system additionally evolves who collaborates, how they collaborate, and how knowledge flows across the population. These components have no single-agent counterpart and can produce phenomena such as emergent specialization. Yet prior test-time methods either confine experiences to individual agents, forfeiting cross-agent learning, or broadcast symmetrically to all agents, erasing the specialization that makes collaboration valuable. We present EVOCHAMBER, a training-free framework that instantiates test-time evolution at three levels over a coevolving agent pool. At its core is CODREAM (Collaborative Dreaming), a post-task protocol triggered on team failure or disagreement, in which agents collaboratively reflect, distill insights, and route them asymmetrically from strong to weak agents on the failed niche, preserving specialization while filling knowledge gaps. Team-level operators assemble niche-conditioned teams and select collaboration structures online. Population-level lifecycle operators fork, merge, prune, and seed agents under performance pressure. On three heterogeneous task streams with Qwen3-8B, EVOCHAMBER reaches 63.9% on competition math, 75.7% on code, and 87.1% on multi-domain reasoning, outperforming the best baseline by 32% relative on math and confirming asymmetric cross-agent transfer as the primary driver in ablation. Starting from several identically initialized agents, four to five stable niche specialists spontaneously emerge, a structural signature of multi-agent evolution that no single-agent learner can express. See our code at: https://github.com/Mercury7353/EvoChamber

</details>


### [198] [RankQ: Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning via Self-Supervised Action Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11151)
*Andrew Choi,Wei Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RankQ 是一种新型的离线到在线强化学习框架，通过引入自监督排名损失替代传统的悲观惩罚，有效解决了离线数据质量对在线微调的限制，显著提升了策略的样本效率和最终执行性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离线到在线强化学习方法主要通过对 OOD 动作施加悲观约束来缓解价值高估，但这类似于行为克隆锚定，当离线数据集中的动作不是最优时，会限制在线阶段的策略改进能力。

Method: 提出了一种名为 RankQ 的离线到在线 Q 学习目标函数，它在时序差分（TD）学习的基础上增加了一个自监督的多项排名损失（multi-term ranking loss），用于强制执行结构化的动作排序，从而引导动作梯度指向更高质量的行为，而非简单地惩罚不在分布范围（OOD）内的动作。

Result: 在稀疏奖励的 D4RL 基准测试中表现优于多种现有方法；在基于视觉的机器人学习中，低数据量下成功率比最佳竞品高出 42.7%，高数据量下高出 13.7%，并实现了卓越的模拟到真实（sim-to-real）迁移效果。

Conclusion: RankQ 通过引入自监督排名损失有效优化了离线到在线的强化学习，能够更好地利用离线数据进行引导，并在在线阶段实现更优的策略改进，显著提升了从离线预训练到在线微调的性能，特别是在低数据量和复杂视觉任务中表现优异。

Abstract: Offline-to-online reinforcement learning (RL) improves sample efficiency by leveraging pre-collected datasets prior to online interaction. A key challenge, however, is learning an accurate critic in large state--action spaces with limited dataset coverage. To mitigate harmful updates from value overestimation, prior methods impose pessimism by down-weighting out-of-distribution (OOD) actions relative to dataset actions. While effective, this essentially acts as a behavior cloning anchor and can hinder downstream online policy improvement when dataset actions are suboptimal. We propose RankQ, an offline-to-online Q-learning objective that augments temporal-difference learning with a self-supervised multi-term ranking loss to enforce structured action ordering. By learning relative action preferences rather than uniformly penalizing unseen actions, RankQ shapes the Q-function such that action gradients are directed toward higher-quality behaviors. Across sparse reward D4RL benchmarks, RankQ achieves performance competitive with or superior to seven prior methods. In vision-based robot learning, RankQ enables effective offline-to-online fine-tuning of a pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) model in a low-data regime, achieving on average a 42.7% higher simulation success rate than the next best method. In a high-data setting, RankQ improves simulation performance by 13.7% over the next best method and achieves strong sim-to-real transfer, increasing real-world cube stacking success from 43.1% to 84.7% relative to the VLA's initial performance.

</details>


### [199] [OLIVIA: Online Learning via Inference-time Action Adaptation for Decision Making in LLM ReAct Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11169)
*Sheldon Yu,Junda Wu,Xintong Li,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Sizhe Zhou,Tong Yu,Jiawei Han,Jingbo Shang,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OLIVIA是一个创新框架，通过在LLM智能体的动作选择层引入轻量级且具备在线更新能力的线性老虎机策略，实现了推理阶段的高效自我进化与不确定性感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时适配方法（如提示工程、检索）仅通过操纵上下文间接影响行为，无法在动作层面进行精细化调整，且难以定量评估不确定性，导致在大规模部署任务中累积误差，降低了可靠性。

Method: 提出OLIVIA框架，将LLM的最终动作选择层建模为背景线性老虎机（Contextual Linear Bandit），利用冻结的隐藏状态作为决策上下文，通过上置信界（UCB）探索机制进行轻量级的在线更新。

Result: 在四个基准测试中，OLIVIA在任务性能上持续优于静态ReAct及现有的提示词驱动的推理时适配方法；该方法具备样本高效性且计算开销极小。

Conclusion: 在ReAct风格的LLM智能体中，引入显式的在线决策层（如背景线性老虎机）相比纯提示词或检索式方法，能更高效、更具可解释性地实现部署阶段的性能提升和不确定性建模。

Abstract: Large language model agents interleave reasoning, action selection, and observation to solve sequential decision-making tasks. In deployed settings where agents repeatedly handle related multi-step tasks, small action-selection errors can accumulate into wasted tool calls, latency, and reduced reliability. Despite this need for deployment-time improvement, existing inference-time adaptation methods for LLM agents mainly rely on prompting or retrieval, which influence behavior indirectly through context manipulation. For ReAct-style agents, such approaches do not expose an explicit decision layer that can score candidate actions, represent uncertainty, or be updated online from action-level feedback. As a result, they provide limited support for trackable, fine-grained, and uncertainty-aware adaptation during deployment. We propose OLIVIA, an inference-time action adaptation framework for ReAct-style agents. OLIVIA models the LLM's final action-selection layer as a contextual linear bandit over candidate actions, with frozen hidden states as decision contexts. This choice is particularly suitable for deployment because it adapts behavior directly at the action-selection interface, preserves the underlying reasoning process, and provides explicit uncertainty estimates and lightweight online updates from action-level feedback. With upper-confidence-bound exploration, OLIVIA improves the policy sample-efficiently with minimal computational overhead. We instantiate OLIVIA on four benchmarks and show that it consistently improves task performance over static ReAct and prompt-based inference-time baselines. Our results suggest that explicit online decision layers provide an effective alternative to purely prompt- or retrieval-based adaptation for LLM agents during deployment.

</details>


### [200] [The Many Faces of On-Policy Distillation: Pitfalls, Mechanisms, and Fixes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11182)
*Siqi Zhu,Xuyan Ye,Hongyu Lu,Weiye Shi,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过实证分析揭示了OP(S)D在大语言模型后训练中的局限性与失效根源，明确了其在处理不同类型特权信息时的适用性，并提出了优化策略以缓解梯度不稳定和分歧问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管OP(S)D作为大模型后训练方法具有潜力，但现有研究对其有效性评价存在争议，且缺乏对其具体失效场景及背后深层原因的系统性剖析，旨在厘清该技术的适用边界。

Method: 通过全面实证研究，深入分析OPD（有教师）和OPSD（自我蒸馏）在数学推理任务中的表现。识别并定义了三种核心故障机制：条件分歧（模型生成的上下文导致的分布不匹配）、梯度偏差（TopK反向KL引起的优化不稳定）以及PI依赖崩溃（针对实例级别PI的无效学习）。尝试了Stop-gradient、RLVR适配和SFT作为缓解策略。

Result: OPD在数学推理中对教师选择和损失函数极其敏感；OPSD在处理“实例级特权信息”时完全失效，但在处理“共享潜在规则”（如系统提示词或对齐偏好）时表现良好。揭示了导致模型失效的三大关键机制。

Conclusion: OP(S)D的有效性高度依赖于任务类型和特权信息（PI）的性质。针对PI的特性（共享规则 vs. 实例特定），模型表现出显著差异。通过应用Stop-gradient TopK目标函数、RLVR适应教师模型以及SFT预训练学生模型，可以有效缓解分布式不匹配、优化不稳定和PI依赖崩溃等关键故障机制。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) and on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) have emerged as promising post-training methods for large language models, offering dense token-level supervision on trajectories sampled from the model's own policy. However, existing results on their effectiveness remain mixed: while OP(S)D has shown promise in system prompt and knowledge internalization, recent studies also report instability and degradation. In this work, we present a comprehensive empirical study of when OPD and OPSD work, when they fail, and why. We find that OPD on mathematical reasoning is highly sensitive to teacher choice and loss formulation, whereas OPSD fails in our tested settings due to test-time absence of instance-specific privileged information (PI). In contrast, OPSD is effective when PI represents a shared latent rule, such as a system prompt or alignment preference. We identify three failure mechanisms: (1) distribution mismatch between teacher and student caused by conditioning on student-generated prefixes, (2) optimization instability from biased TopK reverse-KL gradients, and (3) an OPSD-specific limitation where the student learns a PI-free policy that aggregates PI-conditioned teachers, which is insufficient when PI is instance-specific. We further show that stop-gradient TopK objectives, RLVR-adapted teachers, and SFT-stabilized students mitigate these failures.

</details>


### [201] [Don't Look at the Numbers: Visual Anchoring Bias and Layer-wise Representation in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11218)
*M. Shalankin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 视觉语言模型（VLM）存在严重的数值定位偏见，其对图像质量的判断受无关数字干扰的程度远超常规图像退化感官的影响。该研究通过模型内部探测证明了这种偏见源于模型特定的表征动态和信息整合方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索VLM在进行图像质量评价时是否会受到嵌入数值的“定位偏见”（Anchoring Bias）影响，并试图定位该偏见在模型内部的起因。

Method: 通过嵌入数值锚点（numeric anchors）测试六个不同架构家族的VLM，使用方差分析（ANOVA）量化偏见程度，对图像质量退化进行对比，并通过层级探测（layer-wise probing）和融合分析（fusion analysis）研究模型的内部表征与信息整合。

Result: 1. 锚点效应显著存在且远大于图像质量降质的影响；2. 偏见效应与模型深度有关，特定层级对质量预测至关重要；3. 不同模型架构下的信息融合模式存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 研究证实了视觉语言模型（VLM）中存在因果性的视觉定位偏见，且这种偏见与模型的表示动态和架构特征有直接联系，而非简单的视觉降质干扰。

Abstract: Embedded numeric anchors on images systematically bias Vision-Language Model quality judgments across six VLMs from five architectural families (ANOVA eta^2 = 0.18-0.77, all p < 0.001). Anchor effects are 2.5x larger than severe image quality degradation, confirming bias is not reducible to visual changes. Layer-wise probing reveals consistent dissociation: layers where anchor classification saturates (L12-L34) are suboptimal for quality prediction, with optimal layers deeper (R^2 = 0.69-0.91). Fusion analysis identifies architecture-dependent integration -- instant fusion at L1-L2 in two models versus partial or no fusion in three others. These results establish a causal account of visual anchoring bias, linking behavioral susceptibility to representation dynamics.

</details>


### [202] [Do Vision-Language-Models show human-like logical problem-solving capability in point and click puzzle games?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11223)
*Dominik Helfenstein,Marco Menner,Maximilian Triebel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了VLATIM基准，旨在评估VLM在复杂物理益智游戏中的推理与交互能力。研究发现，当前模型在逻辑推理与高精度操作执行之间存在“鸿沟”，无法实现人类水平的解谜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VL(A)M（视觉-语言-动作模型）基准测试往往忽视了复杂物理推理和精细动作交互（如点击式解谜）的重要性，导致模型在处理此类任务时表现欠佳。

Method: 构建了名为VLATIM的基准测试，涵盖五个循序渐进的难度阶段，要求模型在物理益智游戏《The Incredible Machine 2》中进行逻辑推理、视觉定位及精准的点击交互任务。

Result: 实验表明，大型专有模型虽具备一定的逻辑规划能力，但因视觉定位精度不足，无法有效完成物理谜题，导致整体解题成功率与人类存在巨大差距。

Conclusion: 当前主流大模型在处理需要物理推理和精准交互的复杂任务时表现不足，特别是高层逻辑规划能力与底层视觉定位及动作执行能力之间存在显著割裂，离具备类人级问题解决能力仍有较大差距。

Abstract: Vision-Language(-Action) Models (VLMs) are increasingly applied to interactive environments, yet existing benchmarks often overlook the complex physical reasoning required for point-and-click puzzle games. This paper introduces Vision-Language Against The Incredible Machine (VLATIM), a benchmark designed to evaluate human-like logical problem-solving capabilities within the classic physics puzzle game The Incredible Machine 2 (TIM). Unlike existing benchmarks, VLATIM specifically targets the critical gap between high-level logical reasoning and continuous action spaces requiring precise mouse interactions. This benchmark is structured into five progressive parts, assessing capabilities that range from basic visual grounding and domain understanding to multi-step manipulation and full puzzle solving. Our results reveal a significant disparity between reasoning and execution. While large proprietary models demonstrate superior planning abilities, they struggle with precise visual grounding. Consequently, they do not yet show human-like problem-solving capabilities.

</details>


### [203] [A CAP-like Trilemma for Large Language Models: Correctness, Non-bias, and Utility under Semantic Underdetermination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11672)
*Vinu Ellampallil Venugopal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 受CAP定理启发，本文提出了大模型的“正确性-无偏性-实用性”三难困境：在语义未定的复杂任务中，大模型无法同时满足这三点，其表现不佳往往是结构性的权衡而非单纯的技术缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对语言模型在处理“语义未定”问题（即前提不足以支撑唯一答案）时，难以同时满足正确、客观且有用这一现实难题，探究其背后的理论限度。

Method: 通过类比分布式系统中的CAP定理，构建了大型语言模型（LLM）的“正确性-无偏性-实用性”三难困境（Trilemma）数学框架；通过语义未定（semantic underdetermination）理论进行形式化建模，并提供典型案例分析进行验证。

Result: 形式化定义了该三难困境，证明了当用户给出的前提无法锁定唯一答案时，模型若追求高实用性（给出明确回答）则必然引入偏见；若追求无偏见/正确则必须进行拒绝或模糊处理，从而损害实用性。

Conclusion: LLM的某些失败并非单纯源于模型自身的局限性，而是由“语义未定”决策请求的底层结构所导致的必然权衡。任何LLM在面对此类问题时，必须在正确性、无偏见和高实用性之间做出取舍，无法三者兼得。

Abstract: The CAP theorem states that a distributed system cannot simultaneously guarantee consistency, availability, and partition tolerance under network partition. Inspired by this result, this paper formulates a CAP-like conjecture for Large Language Models (LLMs). The proposed trilemma states that, under semantic underdetermination, an LLM cannot always simultaneously guarantee strong correctness, strict non-bias, and high utility. A prompt is semantically underdetermined when the given premises do not determine a unique answer. In such cases, a useful and decisive response requires the model to introduce a selection criterion, preference, prior, or value ordering. If this criterion is not supplied by the user or justified by the available premises, the response becomes biased in a broad selection-theoretic sense. Conversely, if the model avoids unsupported preferences, it may preserve correctness and non-bias but may reduce utility through refusal, hedging, or clarification. The paper formalizes this correctness--non-bias--utility trilemma, develops examples, and argues that certain LLM failures arise not merely from model limitations but from the structure of underdetermined decision requests.

</details>


### [204] [PIVOT: Bridging Planning and Execution in LLM Agents via Trajectory Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11225)
*Tuo Zhang,Alin-Ionut Popa,Yan Xu,Rui Song,Dimitrios Dimitriadis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PIVOT框架通过将轨迹视为可迭代优化的对象，利用环境交互获得的反馈梯度，有效解决了LLM智能体在长跨度任务中的执行失败与约束违规问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型（LLM）的智能体生成的计划虽然表面连贯，但在实际执行中常因动作不可行、违反约束或长跨度下的错误累积而失败（即“规划-执行脱节”问题）。

Method: 提出了PIVOT（Plan-Inspect-eVOlve Trajectories）框架，包含四个阶段：1. PLAN（生成候选轨迹）；2. INSPECT（执行并计算文本梯度损失）；3. EVOLVE（基于反馈迭代改进轨迹）；4. VERIFY（对任务约束进行全局核查）。通过单调接受过程保证解质量不下降。

Result: 在DeepPlanning和GAIA数据集上达到SOTA性能。人工辅助（HITL）变体使约束满足度提升高达94%；完全自主变体同样表现优异。在计算上，相比同类方法减少了3-5倍的Token消耗。

Conclusion: PIVOT证明了基于反馈的轨迹优化（无论是自监督还是人类监督）是解决自主智能体中规划与执行脱节问题的有效方法。该方法在显著提升约束满足度的同时，保持了极高的计算效率。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents frequently generate seemingly coherent plans that fail upon execution due to infeasible actions, constraint violations, and compounding errors over extended horizons. PIVOT (Plan-Inspect-eVOlve Trajectories) addresses this plan-execution misalignment through a self-supervised framework that treats trajectories as optimizable objects iteratively refined via environment interaction. The framework comprises four stages: PLAN generates candidate trajectories; INSPECT executes them and computes structured losses with textual gradients encoding plan-execution discrepancies; EVOLVE applies these signals to produce improved trajectories; and VERIFY performs a final global check against task constraints. A monotonic acceptance process ensures a non-decreasing solution quality. Empirical evaluations on DeepPlanning and GAIA demonstrate state-of-the-art performance: with human-in-the-loop (HITL) feedback, PIVOT establishes a strong upper bound up to 94% relative improvement in constraint satisfaction, while its fully autonomous variant retains substantial gains, showing that the core trajectory-refinement mechanism remains effective without external supervision. At the same time, PIVOT remains computationally efficient, requiring up to 3x to 5x fewer tokens than competing refinement methods. These findings establish that (self- or human-supervised) feedback-based trajectory optimization is a principled methodology for mitigating plan-execution gaps in autonomous agent systems.

</details>


### [205] [Unlocking LLM Creativity in Science through Analogical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11258)
*Andrew Shen,Shaul Druckmann,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 为解决大模型在科学发现中生成方案单一的问题，本文提出了类比推理（AR）方法，通过跨领域借鉴启发，显著提升了科研方案的创新性和多样性，并在多个生物医药实际任务中达到了SOTA效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动科学研究需要AI系统能够稳定生成多样且新颖的解决方案，但现有的LLM在处理开放式问题时容易出现“模式崩溃”（mode collapse），导致生成内容单一。

Method: 提出了一种类比推理（Analogical Reasoning, AR）架构：通过在跨领域问题间建立基于共享关系结构的类比，引导模型搜索更具新颖性和多样性的方案，而非直接进行常规生成。

Result: AR法使方案多样性指标提升了90-173%，新颖解生成率超过50%（基线仅为1.6%）。在四个生物医学实验中，不仅性能优于基线，还在扰动效应预测、细胞通信、脑区交互推理及寡核苷酸性质预测任务上表现卓越（如特定指标达到13倍提升）。

Conclusion: 类比推理（AR）通过利用跨领域的共享关系结构克服了大语言模型在解决开放式问题时的模型崩溃问题，显著提升了方案的多样性、创新性及在生物医药领域任务中的实际性能。

Abstract: Autonomous science promises to augment scientific discovery, particularly in complex fields like biomedicine. However, this requires AI systems that can consistently generate novel and diverse solutions to open-ended problems. We evaluate LLMs on the task of open-ended solution generation and quantify their tendency to mode collapse into low-diversity generations. To mitigate this mode collapse, we introduce analogical reasoning (AR) as a new approach to solution generation. AR generates analogies to cross-domain problems based on shared relational structure, then uses those analogies to search for novel solutions. Compared to baselines, AR discovers significantly more diverse generations (improving solution diversity metrics by 90-173%), generates novel solutions over 50% of the time (compared to as little as 1.6% for baselines), and produces high-quality analogies. To validate the real-world feasibility of AR, we implement AR-generated solutions across four biomedical problems, yielding consistent quantitative gains. AR-generated approaches achieve a nearly 13-fold improvement on distributional metrics for perturbation effect prediction, outperform all baselines on AUPRC when predicting cell-cell communication, infer brain region interactions with a high Spearman correlation ($ρ$=0.729) to published methods, and establish state-of-the-art performance on 2 datasets for oligonucleotide property prediction. The novel and diverse solutions produced by AR can be used to augment the search space of existing solution generation methods.

</details>


### [206] [LatentRouter: Can We Choose the Right Multimodal Model Before Seeing Its Answer?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11301)
*Xueqi Cheng,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LatentRouter 是一种利用潜空间通信和反事实效用预测的多模态大模型智能路由框架，能根据查询需求自动选择最合适的模型，显著提升了多模态任务的性能与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 MLLM 在不同任务（如 OCR、图表理解、空间推理等）上具有异构性能，简单的难度估计不足以实现最优路由，需要实现查询需求与模型能力的精准匹配。

Method: 提出了 LatentRouter：1. 提取多模态路由胶囊（routing capsules）；2. 使用模型能力表征（capability tokens）；3. 实现潜空间通信（latent communication）以预测反事实性能；4. 采用分布式的效用预测头及有界胶囊校正。

Result: 在 MMR-Bench 和 VL-RouterBench 上表现超越了固定模型、特征级路由及现有的学习型路由器基线；证明了潜空间通信是性能提升的核心贡献。

Conclusion: LatentRouter 通过潜空间通信和反事实效用预测，有效地解决了多模态大模型路由中模型能力与查询需求不匹配的问题，优于现有的各类路由策略。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have heterogeneous strengths across OCR, chart understanding, spatial reasoning, visual question answering, cost, and latency. Effective MLLM routing therefore requires more than estimating query difficulty: a router must match the multimodal requirements of the current image-question input with the capabilities of each candidate model. We propose LatentRouter, a router that formulates MLLM routing as counterfactual multimodal utility prediction. Given an image-question query, LatentRouter extracts learned multimodal routing capsules, represents each candidate MLLM with a model capability token, and performs latent communication between these states to estimate how each model would perform if selected. A distributional outcome head predicts model-specific counterfactual quality, while a bounded capsule correction refines close decisions without allowing residual signals to dominate the prediction. The resulting utility-based policy supports performance-oriented and performance-cost routing, and handles changing candidate pools through shared per-model scoring with availability masking. Experiments on MMR-Bench and VL-RouterBench show that LatentRouter outperforms fixed-model, feature-level, and learned-router baselines. Additional analyses show that the gains are strongest on multimodal task groups where model choice depends on visual, layout-sensitive, or reasoning-oriented requirements, and that latent communication is the main contributor to the improvement. The code is available at: https://github.com/LabRAI/LatentRouter.

</details>


### [207] [Constraint-Data-Value-Maximization: Utilizing Data Attribution for Effective Data Pruning in Low-Data Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11312)
*Danilo Brajovic,David A. Kreplin,Marco F. Huber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对现有数据价值评估方法在极端数据剪枝场景下的局限性，本文提出了CDVM方法，通过约束优化显著提升了模型在少量数据下的训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于Shapley的数据价值评估方法在数据保留比例较低时，无法有效识别并准确剪枝低价值数据，导致模型性能下降。

Method: 提出了“约束数据价值最大化”（CDVM）方法，该方法在最大化总影响力的同时，引入惩罚机制约束单个测试样本的贡献度，从而在数据剪枝过程中平衡了模型性能与数据分布的稳定性。

Result: 实验表明，在OpenDataVal基准测试中，CDVM在低数据场景下比现有方法具有更稳健的性能，且运行效率具有竞争力。

Conclusion: CDVM通过将数据剪枝建模为约束优化问题，有效解决了现有基于Shapley数据价值方法在低数据保留率下性能不佳的问题，在OpenDataVal基准上表现出极强的鲁棒性和竞争力。

Abstract: Attributing model behavior to training data is an evolving research field. A common benchmark is data removal, which involves eliminating data instances with either low or high values, then assessing a model's performance trained on the modified dataset. Many existing studies leverage Shapley-based data values for this task. In this paper, we demonstrate that these data values are not optimally suited for pruning low-value data when only a limited amount of data remains. To address this limitation, we introduce the Constraint-Data-Value-Maximization (CDVM) approach, which effectively utilizes data attributions for pruning in low-data scenarios. By casting pruning as a constrained optimization that both maximizes total influence and penalizes excessive per-test contributions, CDVM delivers robust performance when only a small fraction of the data is retained. On the OpenDataVal benchmark, CDVM shows strong performance and competitive runtime.

</details>


### [208] [Rethinking Evaluation for LLM Hallucination Detection: A Desiderata, A New RAG-based Benchmark, New Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11330)
*Wenbo Chen,Veena Padmanabhan,Tootiya Giyahchi,Elaine Wong,Leman Akoglu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出了现有大模型幻觉检测基准在长上下文RAG场景和标签噪声模拟上的缺失，并提出了一个新的开源基准T RIVIA+进行弥补，同时通过实验揭示了当前主流幻觉检测器在应对这些挑战时的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测基准缺乏RAG场景下的长上下文支持，且未考虑真实应用中常见的标签噪声问题，导致评估结果无法准确反映检测器在复杂实际场景下的真实性能。

Method: 1. 建立幻觉检测基准（HDB）的理想属性指标（Desiderata）；2. 对现有HDB进行对比分析以识别差距；3. 构建并开源T RIVIA+数据集，包含超长上下文及四种不同类型的噪声标签；4. 在RAG任务中对现有主流检测器进行实验评估。

Result: 提出T RIVIA+数据集，它是目前 literature 中上下文最长的RAG幻觉检测基准，并提供了包含样本相关与非相关的多种噪声标签，填补了当前研究的空白。

Conclusion: 当前大模型幻觉检测器在RAG场景下仍有较大提升空间；LLM-as-a-Judge基线表现依然具有竞争力；标签噪声显著影响检测效能。T RIVIA+为未来该领域的研究提供了更严谨的基准评估工具。

Abstract: Hallucination, broadly referring to unfaithful, fabricated, or inconsistent content generated by LLMs, has wide-ranging implications. Therefore, a large body of effort has been devoted to detecting LLM hallucinations, as well as designing benchmark datasets for evaluating these detectors. In this work, we first establish a desiderata of properties for hallucination detection benchmarks (HDBs) to exhibit for effective evaluation. A critical look at existing HDBs through the lens of our desiderata reveals that none of them exhibits all the properties. We identify two largest gaps: (1) RAG-based grounded benchmarks with long context are severely lacking (partly because length impedes human annotation); and (2) Existing benchmarks do not make available realistic label noise for stress-testing detectors although real-world use-cases often grapple with label noise due to human or automated/weak annotation. To close these gaps, we build and open-source a new RAG-based HDB called T RIVIA+ that underwent a rigorous human annotation process. Notably, our benchmark exhibits all desirable properties including (1) T RIVIA+ contains samples with the longest context in the literature; and (2) we design and share four sets of noisy labels with different, both sample-dependent and sampleindependent, noise schemes. Finally, we perform experiments on RAG-based HDBs, including our T RIVIA+, using popular SOTA detectors that reveal new insights: (i) ample room remains for current detectors to reach the performance ceiling on RAG-based HDBs, (ii) the basic LLM-as-a-Judge baseline performs competitively, and (iii) label noise hinders detection performance. We expect that our findings, along with our proposed benchmark 1 , will motivate and foster needed research on hallucination detection for RAG-based tasks.

</details>


### [209] [Causal Bias Detection in Generative Artifical Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11365)
*Drago Plecko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对生成式AI中的公平性挑战，提出了统一的因果公平性理论框架，推导了新的因果分解方法用以量化模型内部机制对公平性的影响，并为评估大语言模型中的社会偏见提供了有效工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前因果公平性研究多集中于标准预测任务，而生成式AI能够学习并重构所有变量间的因果机制，其复杂性使得现有方法不再适用，因此需要针对生成式AI特性开发新的因果公平性方法。

Method: 1. 建立了一个统一的理论框架，将生成式AI的因果公平性问题与标准机器学习设定相结合；2. 推导了因果分解结果，以量化因果路径及生成式模型机制替换带来的公平性影响；3. 建立了识别条件（Identification conditions）并提出了高效的估计量（Estimators）。

Result: 1. 开发了一套可用于量化生成式AI公平性影响的严谨方法论；2. 在大语言模型（LLM）的实证分析中，成功识别并量化了跨不同数据集的种族和性别偏差。

Conclusion: 该研究将生成式AI的因果公平性正式化，提供了一个统一的框架，并证明了通过分解因果路径与模型机制替换，可以量化生成式模型中的公平性偏差，为评估和减轻大模型偏见提供了理论和工具支撑。

Abstract: Automated systems built on artificial intelligence (AI) are increasingly deployed across high-stakes domains, raising critical concerns about fairness and the perpetuation of demographic disparities that exist in the world. In this context, causal inference provides a principled framework for reasoning about fairness, as it links observed disparities to underlying mechanisms and aligns naturally with human intuition and legal notions of discrimination. Prior work on causal fairness primarily focuses on the standard machine learning setting, where a decision-maker constructs a single predictive mechanism $f_{\widehat Y}$ for an outcome variable $Y$, while inheriting the causal mechanisms of all other covariates from the real world. The generative AI setting, however, is markedly more complex: generative models can sample from arbitrary conditionals over any set of variables, implicitly constructing their own beliefs about all causal mechanisms rather than learning a single predictive function. This fundamental difference requires new developments in causal fairness methodology. We formalize the problem of causal fairness in generative AI and unify it with the standard ML setting under a common theoretical framework. We then derive new causal decomposition results that enable granular quantification of fairness impacts along both (a) different causal pathways and (b) the replacement of real-world mechanisms by the generative model's mechanisms. We establish identification conditions and introduce efficient estimators for causal quantities of interest, and demonstrate the value of our methodology by analyzing race and gender bias in large language models across different datasets.

</details>


### [210] [Causal Algorithmic Recourse: Foundations and Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11373)
*Drago Plecko,Collin Wang,Elias Bareinboim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对现有算法补偿方法忽略动态决策场景的缺陷，提出了一种基于因果框架和 Copula 模型的回溯分析方法，能够有效评估重复决策环境下的补偿措施效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的算法补偿（algorithmic recourse）方法主要将决策视为静态、孤立的反事实问题，忽略了现实中针对同一对象在不同潜在条件下反复决策的动态特性，导致对补偿措施的评估不够准确。

Method: 1. 建立了一个基于过程的因果框架，将补偿建模为干预前后的变化过程；2. 引入“后补偿稳定性”条件，允许仅通过观测数据进行推理；3. 开发了基于 Copula 的算法用于参数推断；4. 在满足“补偿数据”可用时，提供了针对 Copula 模型的拟合度测试及分布无关（distribution-free）的备选算法。

Result: 研究提出的方法能够在观测数据及补偿数据集上有效推断补偿效果，通过实证与半合成数据集测试，验证了其在处理潜在变量重采样和动态决策环境下的鲁棒性与准确性。

Conclusion: 该研究通过引入因果框架和基于 Copula 的算法，成功实现了对算法补偿效果的有效评估。实验证明，该方法在处理重复性决策场景下，能够比现有静态反事实方法更准确地量化补偿措施的长期影响。

Abstract: The trustworthiness of AI decision-making systems is increasingly important. A key feature of such systems is the ability to provide recommendations for how an individual may reverse a negative decision, a problem known as algorithmic recourse. Existing approaches treat recourse outcomes as counterfactuals of a fixed unit, ignoring that real-world recourse involves repeated decisions on the same individual under possibly different latent conditions. We develop a causal framework that models recourse as a process over pre- and post-intervention outcomes, allowing for partial stability and resampling of latent variables. We introduce post-recourse stability conditions that enable reasoning about recourse from observational data alone, and develop a copula-based algorithm for inferring the effects of recourse under these conditions. For settings where paired observations of the same individual before and after intervention are available (called recourse data), we develop methods for inferring copula parameters and performing goodness-of-fit testing. When the copula model is rejected, we provide a distribution-free algorithm for learning recourse effects directly from recourse data. We demonstrate the value of the proposed methods on real and semi-synthetic datasets.

</details>


### [211] [Revisiting Privacy Preservation in Brain-Computer Interfaces: Conceptual Boundaries, Risk Pathways, and a Protection-Strength Grading Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11386)
*Lei Sun,Xiuqing Mao,Shuai Zhang,Qingyu Zeng,Min Zhao,Jiyuan Li,Wenle Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对BCI技术日益增长的隐私风险，提出了一个综合性的分类评估框架，强调在保障模型可用性的前提下，解耦任务无关的敏感神经信息，以保护用户隐私与神经伦理安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着BCI技术从实验室向临床及现实环境迁移，其带来的隐私风险已从原始神经信号泄露扩大到派生数据、模型资产及解码输出的关联风险，亟需一套针对BCI系统的系统性隐私保护框架。

Method: 在该BCI范式基础上，定义隐私保护边界和对象，构建了一个包含“保护对象、生命周期阶段、保护强度等级”的三维分类框架，并据此对现有工作进行分级，引入了任务无关敏感信息解耦的概念。

Result: 提出了新的BCI隐私保护三维评估框架，将现有研究划分为四个保护强度等级，并明确了用户数据隐私与模型隐私之间的共享风险路径。

Conclusion: BCI隐私保护需超越单纯的数据加密，应重点关注任务无关敏感信息的解耦，同时兼顾下游任务的可用性。未来研究必须将神经伦理风险和心理隐私作为核心议题。

Abstract: Brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) are moving rapidly from laboratory research into clinical, edge, and real-world settings. Under ISO/IEC 8663:2025, a BCI is a direct communication link between central nervous system activity and external software or hardware systems. This link expands privacy risk beyond raw neural-signal leakage: neural data, derived representations, model assets, and decoded outputs can be re-associated with individuals across collection, transmission, storage, training, inference, and feedback, or used to infer information beyond what a task requires. Starting from the general BCI paradigm, this review deffnes privacy-protection boundaries, protection objects, and the relationship between user data privacy and model privacy within a shared risk pathway. It then proposes a three-dimensional framework - protection object, lifecycle stage, and dominant protection-strength level - to classify existing work into four levels of protection strength. Finally, mental privacy and neuroethical risks are treated as open issues, emphasizing that BCI privacy protection should not only obscure data but also disentangle task-irrelevant sensitive information while preserving downstream utility. Keywords: Brain-computer interface, Neural data privacy, User data privacy, Model privacy, Disentanglement of task-irrelevant sensitive information, Protection-strength grading, Neuroethical risks

</details>


### [212] [Transformer Interpretability from Perspective of Attention and Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11392)
*Yongjin Cui,Xiaohui Fan,Huajun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过引导注意力梯度来提升Transformer可解释性的方法，并揭示了ViT模型存在人类难以察觉的“类别重写”安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前学术界过于关注Transformer的性能而忽视了其可解释性；现有的基于梯度的解释方法存在局限，需要更精细的机制来理解Transformer的决策过程。

Method: 提出了一种基于引导梯度方向（特别是注意力方向）的方法，用于深入解析Transformer的特征区域及内部机制。

Result: 所提方法能够更全面地解释Transformer的特征关注区域；实验发现ViT对图像的分类感知与人类存在显著差异，并能通过微小的扰动实现隐蔽的“类别重写”。

Conclusion: 该研究通过引导注意力梯度，提出了一种增强Transformer模型可解释性的方法；同时发现ViT模型存在“类别重写（class rewriting）”现象，揭示了该类模型在特定场景下的安全风险。

Abstract: Although researchers' attention is more focused on the performance of Transformer models, the interpretation of Transformer can never be ignored. Gradient is widely utilized in Transformer interpretation. From the perspective of attention and gradient, we conduct an in-depth study of Transformer interpretation and propose a method to achieve it by guiding the gradient direction, or more precisely, the attention direction. The method enables more comprehensive interpretation of feature regions, offers detail interpretation, and helps to better understand Transformer mechanism. Leveraging the difference in how Vision Transformer (ViT) and humans perceive images, we alter the class of an image in a way that is almost imperceptible to the human eye. This class rewriting phenomenon may potentially pose security risks in certain scenarios.

</details>


### [213] [AcuityBench: Evaluating Clinical Acuity Identification and Uncertainty Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11398)
*Robin Linzmayer,Georgianna Lin,Di Coneybeare,Jason Chu,Trudi Cloyd,Manish Garg,Miles Gordon,Elizabeth Hartofilis,Benjamin Hong,Ashraf Hussain,Eugene Y. Kim,Oluchi Iheagwara King,Ross McCormack,Erica Olsen,John K. Riggins,Mustafa N. Rasheed,Dana L. Sacco,Vinay Saggar,Osman R. Sayan,Amit Shembekar,Janice Shin-Kim,Wendy W. Sun,Bernard P. Chang,David Kessler,Noémie Elhadad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究团队提出了一个医疗导诊评估基准 AcuityBench，通过整合多类医疗文本，系统评估大模型对患者医疗紧迫性的判断能力，并揭示了不同交互范式在安全性与医疗质量控制方面的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有健康类基准侧重于医学问答或特定工作流，缺乏对复杂医疗场景中“护理紧迫性识别”这一关键安全能力的统一评估机制。为了确保模型能够准确引导患者寻求适当级别的医疗护理，需要一个能够跨场景评估紧迫性判别的通用基准。

Method: 1. 整合五个公开数据集，涵盖会话、论坛、临床案例及患者消息，建立统一的四级护理紧迫性框架；2. 构建包含 914 个案例的基准（697 个共识案例用于准确性评估，217 个歧义案例用于不确定性评估）；3. 采用两种评估方式：四分类 QA 任务和基于准则的自由文本对话评估；4. 在 12 个前沿模型上进行横向测试，并与医师判断进行对比。

Result: 1. 模型间紧迫性识别准确率差异巨大；2. 任务形式存在系统性权衡：对话式交互相比 QA 能减少过度医疗建议，但在高紧迫性情况下增加了漏诊风险；3. 在处理模糊案例时，模型无法模拟临床医师的判断分布，且缺乏与专家对等的临床不确定性认知。

Conclusion: AcuityBench 是评估语言模型在医疗场景中识别护理紧迫性（acuity）的关键工具。研究表明该能力在当前模型中存在显著差异，且任务形式（QA vs. 对话）会影响过诊（over-triage）与漏诊（under-triage）的平衡。该基准为提升医疗大模型在真实临床导诊应用中的安全性提供了重要的评估标准。

Abstract: We introduce AcuityBench, a benchmark for evaluating whether language models identify the appropriate urgency of care from user medical presentations. Existing health benchmarks emphasize medical question answering, broad health interactions, or narrow workflow-specific triage tasks, but they do not offer a unified evaluation of acuity identification across these settings. AcuityBench addresses this gap by harmonizing five public datasets spanning user conversations, online forum posts, clinical vignettes, and patient portal messages under a shared four-level acuity framework ranging from home monitoring to immediate emergency care. The benchmark contains 914 cases, including 697 consensus cases for standard accuracy evaluation and 217 physician-confirmed ambiguous cases for uncertainty-aware evaluation. It supports two complementary task formats: explicit four-way classification in a QA setting, and free-form conversational responses evaluated with a rubric-based judge anchored to the same framework. Across 12 frontier proprietary and open-weight models, we find substantial variation in clear-case acuity accuracy and error direction. Comparing task formats reveals a systematic tradeoff: conversational responses reduce over-triage but increase under-triage relative to QA, especially in higher-acuity cases. In ambiguous cases, no model closely matches the distribution of physician judgments, and model predictions are more concentrated than expert clinical uncertainty. We also compare expert and model adjudication on a subset of maximally ambiguous cases, using those cases to examine the role of clinical uncertainty in label disagreement. Together, these results position acuity identification as a distinct safety-critical capability and show that AcuityBench enables systematic comparison and stress-testing of how well models guide users to the right level of care in real-world health use.

</details>


### [214] [Attributing Emergence in Million-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11404)
*Ling Tang,Jilin Mei,Qian Chen,Qihan Ren,Linfeng Zhang,Quanshi Zhang,Jing Shao,Xia Hu,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文解决了LLM多智能体系统难以在大规模下进行归因的瓶颈，通过路径积分归因法证明：仅靠小规模样本研究宏观现象会导致归因结论严重扭曲，且无法通过重标定修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究LLM多智能体系统社会现象的归因方法在计算上呈组合爆炸，导致只能在小规模（$N \lesssim 10^3$）下进行，无法探究真实社会现象（$N \geq 10^6$）中微观智能体对宏观涌现的真实贡献。

Method: 将Aumann--Shapley路径积分归因法（Path-Integral Attribution）适配于百万级智能体规模的LLM多智能体系统；对比全规模与小规模（$N=10^2$）的归因结果；提出“归因缩放偏差定理”进行理论证明。

Result: 新方法比采样Shapley值快4-5个数量级；全规模数据（Bluesky，约167万用户）显示贡献主要来自长尾和中间层用户，而小样本偏差会错误地将贡献归结为少数大V；理论证明了无法通过事后缩放来消除这种偏差。

Conclusion: 在大规模多智能体系统中，全面归因分析是理论上的必要而非可选，因为小规模样本存在“归因缩放偏差”（Attribution Scaling Bias），即便通过简单的重标定也无法校正这种结构性差异。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can simulate human-like reasoning and decision-making in individual agents. LLM-powered multi-agent systems (MAS) combine such agents to simulate population-scale social phenomena such as polarization, information cascades, and market panics. Such studies require attributing macro emergence to individual agents, but existing axiomatic methods scale combinatorially in $N$ and have been confined to $N \lesssim 10^3$, while the phenomena they explain occur at $N \geq 10^6$. We address this gap by adapting Aumann--Shapley path-integral attribution to LLM-powered MAS at million-agent scale; the resulting method satisfies all four axioms, runs four to five orders of magnitude faster than sampled Shapley on the same hardware. We use this method to test the scale gap empirically: across 14 days of public Bluesky data ($1{,}671{,}587$ active users), we compute the attribution at both full scale and the visibility-biased $N = 10^2$ convenience sample used by small-scale studies, and the two disagree structurally. At full scale the long tail and middle tier jointly carry the majority; the biased small panel attributes almost everything to a few high-follower accounts. We then prove that under any nonlinear macro indicator the disagreement cannot be reduced by post-hoc rescaling: an Attribution Scaling Bias theorem shows that no global rescaling factor can reconcile small-scale and full-scale attribution. Full-scale attribution is therefore not a methodological choice but a theoretical requirement for any nonlinear macro indicator.

</details>


### [215] [TOPPO: Rethinking PPO for Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning with Critic Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11473)
*Yuanpeng Li,Gefei Lin,Annie Qu,Rui Miao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了PPO在多任务强化学习中表现不佳的原因是批评者侧的梯度病态，并提出了TOPPO，通过优化梯度更新平衡，证明了On-policy方法在MTRL中同样高效，打破了对Off-policy方法的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多任务强化学习（MTRL）中，尽管Off-policy方法（如SAC）占主导，但On-policy方法（如PPO）尚表现不佳。研究发现问题根源在于PPO在MTRL中存在批评者侧的梯度病态（gradient ill-conditioning），导致简单任务主导更新，而难任务被忽略。

Method: 提出TOPPO（Tail-Optimized PPO），通过引入“批评者平衡（Critic Balancing）”模块来优化PPO的价值函数更新，解决多任务学习中因梯度条件不良导致的“尾部任务（tail tasks）”停滞问题，而非依赖模块化架构或超大规模参数。

Result: 在Meta-World+基准测试中，TOPPO的表现优于SAC和ARS系列基准，实现了更好的平均任务和尾部任务性能，且参数量更少、采样效率更高。在训练早期即可与强SAC基准持平或超越。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过优化批评者（critic）侧的梯度条件，在线策略（on-policy）方法（如PPO）在多任务强化学习中可以媲美甚至超越离线策略（off-policy）方法（如SAC），并指出梯度病态问题是制约PPO在多任务场景发挥的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) and its variants dominate Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning (MTRL) due to their off-policy sample efficiency, while on-policy methods such as Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) remain underexplored. We diagnose that PPO in MTRL suffers from a previously overlooked issue: critic-side gradient ill-conditioning, which may cause tail tasks to stall while easy tasks dominate the value function's updates. To address this, we propose TOPPO (Tail-Optimized PPO), a reformulation of PPO via Critic Balancing -- a set of modules that improve gradient conditioning and balance learning dynamics across tasks. Unlike prior approaches that rely on modular architectures or large models, TOPPO targets the optimization bottleneck within PPO itself. Empirically, TOPPO achieves stronger mean and tail-task performance than published SAC-family and ARS-family baselines while using substantially fewer parameters and environment steps on Meta-World+ benchmark. Notably, TOPPO matches or surpasses strong SAC baselines early in training and maintains superior performance at full budget. Ablations confirm the effectiveness of each module in TOPPO and provide insights into their interactions. Our results demonstrate that, with proper optimization, on-policy methods can rival or exceed off-policy approaches in MTRL, challenging the prevailing reliance on SAC and highlighting critic-side gradient conditioning as the central bottleneck.

</details>


### [216] [What Do EEG Foundation Models Capture from Human Brain Signals?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11410)
*Ling Tang,Qian Chen,Jilin Mei,Houshi Xu,Quanshi Zhang,Jing Shao,Na Zou,Xia Hu,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过系统审计阐明了EEG基础模型内部表示与传统临床特征之间的深层联系，证明了深度学习模型在很大程度上捕获了人类已知的临床特征，并量化了这种解释力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代EEG基础模型在无需传统手工特征的情况下表现优异，但其内部表征与传统临床特征体系（如频带功率、连接性等）的关系尚不明确，需要揭示这种“黑箱”模型究竟学到了什么。

Method: 提出了一个包含三个维度的审计架构：1. 使用层级岭回归探测（layer-wise ridge probing）分析学到的信息；2. 使用LEACE风格的交叉协方差子空间擦除（cross-covariance subspace erasure）分析使用的特征；3. 通过透明分类器与随机特征基准进行对比。覆盖了3个基础模型、5个临床任务及63个临床特征词典。

Result: 1. 审计发现68.6%的(模型, 任务, 特征)单位具有表示因果性；2. 频域特征主导了模型表现，但其他五类特征也贡献显著；3. 确认的特征平均恢复了模型相对于随机基准性能优势的79.3%。

Conclusion: 现代EEG基础模型学习到的特征与临床传统手工特征高度重合。实验确认68.6%的特征具有表征因果性（representation-causal），且这些特征解释了模型优于随机基准的大部分性能（平均79.3%）。难以完全解释的残留部分为未来的概念发现指明了方向。

Abstract: Clinical electroencephalogram (EEG) analysis rests on a hand-crafted feature catalog refined over decades, \emph{e.g.,} band power, connectivity, complexity, and more. Modern EEG foundation models bypass this catalog, learn directly from raw signals via self-supervised pretraining, and match or outperform feature-engineered baselines on most clinical benchmarks. Whether the two representations align is an open question, which we decompose into three sub-questions: \emph{what does the model learn}, \emph{what does the model use}, and \emph{how much can be explained}. We answer them with layer-wise ridge probing, LEACE-style cross-covariance subspace erasure, and a transparent classifier benchmarked against a random-feature baseline. The audit covers three foundation models (CSBrain, CBraMod, LaBraM), five clinical tasks (MDD, Stress, ISRUC-Sleep, TUSL, Siena), and a 6-family 63-feature lexicon. Of the $945$ (model, task, feature) units, $648$ ($68.6\%$) are representation-causal and $199$ ($21.1\%$) are encoded-only. Across tasks, $50$ features qualify as universal candidates with strong support (all three architectures RC) in two or more tasks. Frequency-domain features dominate, but the other five families each contribute substantial causal mass. Confirmed features recover, on average, $79.3\%$ of the foundation model's advantage over the random baseline, with a clean task gradient (MDD $\approx 0.99$ down to Stress $\approx 0.56$): tasks near ceiling are almost fully recovered by the lexicon, while harder tasks leave a non-trivial residual that pinpoints a concrete target for future concept discovery.

</details>


### [217] [FibQuant: Universal Vector Quantization for Random-Access KV-Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11478)
*Namyoon Lee,Yongjune Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对大模型长上下文推理中的 KV 缓存瓶颈，FibQuant 利用径向-角度向量量化替代传统标量量化，在维持高效随机访问的同时，显著提升了高压缩比下的模型保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理受到 KV 缓存引起的内存带宽限制，现有标量量化方法因忽略了旋转变换后的几何特性而导致性能受限。

Method: 提出 FibQuant，这是一种通用的定长向量量化器。它沿用了“归化-旋转-存储”架构，但将标量查表优化替换为共享的径向-角度码本，该码本融合了 Beta 分布半径、Fibonacci/Roberts-Kronecker 准均匀方向及多重重启 Lloyd-Max 精炼优化。

Result: 在 GPT-2 和 TinyLlama-1.1B 上验证，FibQuant 实现了 5x 至 34x 的压缩比，在 4x 压缩下困惑度仅偏离 fp16 0.10，且在 8x 压缩下表现显著优于标量 TurboQuant。

Conclusion: FibQuant 是一种高性能的 KV 缓存压缩方案，通过引入向量量化替代标量量化，有效解决了长上下文推理中的内存带宽瓶颈，在保持高保真度的同时实现了极高的压缩比。

Abstract: Long-context inference is increasingly a memory-traffic problem. The culprit is the key--value (KV) cache: it grows with context length, batch size, layers, and heads, and it is read at every decoding step. Rotation-based scalar codecs meet this systems constraint by storing a norm, applying a shared random rotation, and quantizing one coordinate at a time. They are universal and random-access, but they discard the geometry created by the normalization step. After a Haar rotation, a block of $k$ consecutive coordinates is not a product source; it is a spherical-Beta source on the unit ball. We introduce \textsc{FibQuant}, a universal fixed-rate vector quantizer that keeps the same normalize--rotate--store interface while replacing scalar tables by a shared radial--angular codebook matched to this canonical source. The codebook combines Beta-quantile radii, Fibonacci\,/\,Roberts--Kronecker quasi-uniform directions, and multi-restart Lloyd--Max refinement. We prove that the resulting vector code strictly improves on its scalar product specialization at matched rate, with a high-rate gain that separates into a cell-shaping factor and a density-matching factor. The same construction gives a dense rate axis, including fractional-bit and sub-one-bit operating points, without calibration or variable-length addresses. On GPT-2 small KV caches, \textsc{FibQuant} traces a memory--fidelity frontier from $5\times$ compression at $0.99$ attention cosine similarity to $34\times$ at $0.95$. End-to-end on TinyLlama-1.1B, it is within $0.10$ perplexity of fp16 at $4\times$ compression and has $3.6\times$ lower perplexity than scalar \textsc{TurboQuant} at $b = 2$ ($8\times$ compression), where scalar random-access quantization begins to fail.

</details>


### [218] [Under the Hood of SKILL.md: Semantic Supply-chain Attacks on AI Agent Skill Registry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11418)
*Shoumik Saha,Kazem Faghih,Soheil Feizi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了 AI 智能体技能系统中的语义供应链漏洞，表明攻击者可以通过操纵技能元数据（SKILL.md）诱导智能体发现、选择并运行恶意技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主 AI 智能体通过模块化的 Agent Skills 扩展能力，这些技能的元数据（SKILL.md）可能被恶意操纵，导致智能体不当地加载或使用恶意技能，从而引入了语义供应链安全风险。研究旨在揭示这种“指令式”元数据带来的潜在威胁。

Method: 研究人员通过三个针对 Agent Skill 生命周期（即发现、选择、治理阶段）的攻击场景，利用真实 ClawHub 技能和模拟的注册表机制进行实验：1. 发现阶段：利用短文本触发器操纵基于嵌入（Embedding）的检索；2. 选择阶段：通过描述性框架（Description-based framing）诱导智能体选择对抗性变体；3. 治理阶段：采用语义逃避策略规避自动化审核。

Result: 实验结果显示：在发现阶段，攻击者能实现高达 86% 的配对胜率和 80% 的 Top-10 曝光率；在选择阶段，77.6% 的对比试验中智能体偏向于选择对抗性变体；在治理阶段，恶意技能通过语义逃避成功绕过 36.5% 到 100% 的阻止决策。

Conclusion: SKILL.md 不仅仅是被动的文档，而是决定智能体如何发现、信任和使用第三方能力的“操作性文本”。目前的智能体技能生态系统在语义层面上存在严重的供应链安全风险，亟需引入更严谨的治理和防御机制。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents increasingly extend their capabilities through Agent Skills: modular filesystem packages whose SKILL.md files describe when and how agents should use them. While this design enables scalable, on-demand capability expansion, it also introduces a semantic supply-chain risk in which natural-language metadata and instructions can affect which skills are admitted, surfaced, selected, and loaded. We study SKILL.md - only attacks across three registry-facing stages of the Agent Skill lifecycle, using real ClawHub skills and realistic registry mechanisms. In Discovery, short textual triggers can manipulate embedding-based retrieval and improve adversarial skill visibility, achieving up to 86% pairwise win rate and 80% Top-10 placement. In Selection, description-only framing biases agents toward functionally equivalent adversarial variants, which are selected in 77.6% of paired trials on average. In Governance, semantic evasion strategies cause malicious skills to avoid a blocking verdict in 36.5%-100% of cases. Overall, our results show that SKILL.md is not passive documentation but operational text that shapes which third-party capabilities agents find, trust, and use.

</details>


### [219] [A Mechanistic Investigation of Supervised Fine Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11426)
*Ruhaan Chopra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用预训练稀疏自编码器（SAE）分析大模型精调（SFT）机制的方法，发现尽管SFT后高层激活高度相似，但底层的稀疏语义特征已发生显著重组，揭示了模型精调与安全对齐的深层动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们观察到SFT后模型隐藏层的余弦相似度依然很高，常被误认为模型行为未发生显著改变；本文旨在通过SAE揭示这类“高相似度”掩盖下的底层表征演变，探究精调对模型行为的真正机制影响。

Method: 利用预训练的稀疏自编码器（SAE）将SFT前后的隐藏层激活值映射到稀疏潜在空间；通过对比投影后的稀疏潜变量，量化并分析模型表征的差异；建立诊断流水线，识别特定任务和层级的语义特征变化及安全对齐的更新模式。

Result: 证实了SFT会导致底层稀疏潜变量的显著偏离；发现了SFT引起改变的特定任务与层级分布规律；明确了安全对齐过程中具有层级特性的更新剖面。

Conclusion: 尽管SFT前后模型隐藏层激活值的余弦相似度很高，但这掩盖了底层稀疏特征的显著变化。预训练的稀疏自编码器（SAE）能够有效揭示SFT导致的深层表征重组机制，为理解模型精调和安全对齐提供了高分辨率的观测窗口。

Abstract: The cosine similarity between a large language model's hidden activations before and after Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) remains very high. This, at first glance, suggests that SFT leaves the model's activation geometry largely undisturbed. However, projecting both sets of activations through a Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) pretrained on the base model reveals that the underlying sparse latents diverge significantly. We introduce a novel investigative pipeline which utilizes these pretrained SAEs as a high-resolution diagnostic tool to mechanistically investigate the drivers of this representational divergence. Through our analytical pipeline, we discover task-specific and layer-specific distributions of the precise semantic features that are systematically altered during supervised fine-tuning. We additionally identify a layer-wise update profile specific to safety alignment. All code, experimental scripts, and analysis files associated with this work are publicly available at: https://github.com/ruhzi/sae-investigation.

</details>


### [220] [Adaptive Teacher Exposure for Self-Distillation in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11458)
*Zihao Han,Tiangang Zhang,Huaibin Wang,Yilun Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 推理任务中的自我蒸馏，提出了一种自适应调整教师参考逻辑曝光度的方法 ATESD，解决了因为参考路径过深导致的训练困难，实现了更稳健的模型推理能力提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出当前主流思维链自训练（On-policy self-distillation）存在“教师曝光失配”问题：当教师提供的参考推理逻辑远超学生现有能力时，强加的 token 目标反而难以被学生吸收（训练信号过强导致难以优化）。

Method: 提出“自适应教师曝光自我蒸馏”（ATESD）。使用轻量级 Beta 策略控制器根据训练状态动态决定教师的“曝光比例”（reveal ratio）；引入基于折扣学习进度（discounted learning-progress）的奖励机制，用于评估曝光决策对学生未来性能提升的影响，从而解决延迟信用分配问题。

Result: 在 Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B 模型上对比 AIME 24/25 和 HMMT 25 数据集，ATESD 表现显著优于传统的 OPSD 方法，平均准确度分别提升了 0.95、2.05 和 2.33 个点。

Conclusion: ATESD 通过将教师曝光率转化为可学习的控制变量，解决了推理任务中教师引导过强造成的“曝光失配”问题，被证明是一种更优的自我蒸馏范式。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation has become a strong recipe for LLM reasoning, where a privileged teacher supervises the student's own rollouts while conditioning on the reference solution. A design choice shared by nearly all such methods, however, has gone unquestioned: the teacher always sees the full reference reasoning. We argue that this default itself is part of the problem and identify a teacher-side exposure mismatch: when the teacher conditions on reasoning far beyond the student's current competence, the resulting token targets become too strong to absorb. A controlled fixed-exposure sweep makes this concrete on two fronts: 1) full exposure is not reliably the best choice, and 2) student-teacher mismatch grows monotonically as the teacher sees more privileged reasoning. This motivates treating teacher exposure not as a fixed hyperparameter but as a learnable training-time control variable. We therefore propose Adaptive Teacher Exposure for Self-Distillation (ATESD). ATESD models the reveal ratio with a lightweight Beta-policy controller conditioned on compact training-state statistics, and uses one sampled exposure for a short hold window of student updates. To make this exposure controller learnable, we optimize it with a discounted learning-progress reward that scores each held decision by its effect on the student's future improvement rather than its immediate loss change, addressing the delayed credit assignment induced by on-policy distillation. Experiments on AIME 24, AIME 25, and HMMT 25 across Qwen3-{1.7B, 4B, 8B} show that ATESD consistently outperforms competitive self-distillation and RL baselines, improving over OPSD by +0.95, +2.05, and +2.33 Average@12 points respectively, and establishing adaptive teacher exposure as an effective new axis for reasoning self-distillation.

</details>


### [221] [Breaking $\textit{Winner-Takes-All}$: Cooperative Policy Optimization Improves Diverse LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11461)
*Haoxuan Chen,Tianming Liang,Wei-Shi Zheng,Jian-Fang Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GCPO通过引入“团队合作”机制替代“个体竞争”，利用行列式点过程量化多样性，促使模型学习多样化的正确推理路径，从而解决了强化学习训练中的探索崩溃问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于验证器的强化学习（如GRPO）存在“赢家通吃”现象，导致模型陷入局部最优（探索崩溃），且简单的熵正则化无法根本解决轨迹竞争导致的同质化问题。

Method: 提出了组协同策略优化（GCPO）：1. 引入团队级信用分配机制，奖励贡献于团队有效覆盖范围而非个体准确性的路径；2. 使用行列式点过程（determinant volume）衡量语义嵌入空间的覆盖多样性；3. 通过计算边际贡献将集体奖励分配给单个轨迹。

Result: 在多个推理基准测试中，GCPO在推理准确率和解决方案的多样性方面均显著优于现有主流的优化方法。

Conclusion: GCPO通过将学习目标从个体竞争转向团队合作，有效解决了GRPO等算法中的探索崩溃问题，在提升大语言模型推理准确性的同时显著增强了解决方案的多样性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiers (RLVR) has become a central paradigm for improving LLM reasoning, yet popular group-based optimization algorithms like GRPO often suffer from exploration collapse, where the models prematurely converge on a narrow set of high-scoring patterns, lacking the ability to explore new solutions. Recent efforts attempt to alleviate this by adding entropy regularization or diversity bonus. However, these approaches do not change the \textit{winner-takes-all} nature, where rollouts still compete for individual advantage rather than cooperating for maximizing global diversity. In this work, we propose Group Cooperative Policy Optimization (GCPO), which shifts the training paradigm from rollout competition to team cooperation. Specifically, GCPO replaces independent rollout scoring with team-level credit assignment: a rollout is rewarded by how much it contributes to the team's valid solution coverage, rather than its individual accuracy. This coverage is described as a determinant volume over reward-weighted semantic embeddings, where only correct and non-redundant rollouts contribute to this volume. During advantage estimation, GCPO redistributes the collective team reward to each single rollout according to its average marginal contribution to the team. This cooperative training paradigm routes optimization toward non-redundant correct reasoning paths. Experiments across multiple reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that GCPO significantly improves both reasoning accuracy and solution diversity over existing approaches. Code will be released at $\href{https://github.com/bradybuddiemarch/gcpo}{this}$.

</details>


### [222] [Engagement Process: Rethinking the Temporal Interface of Action and Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11484)
*Jialian Li,Yuchen Cao,Junhong Liu,Weiran Guo,Xutao Wang,Jiaming Song,Jiahao Zhang,Jie Chen*

Main category: cs.AI
