TL;DR: 针对传统 POMDP 在处理时间交互时的局限性，本文提出了“参与过程（EP）”框架，通过将动作和观察转变为时间轴上的解耦事件流，增强了智能体对延迟、多速率及协同任务的处理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 POMDP 模型基于固定时间步（time-step），难以处理数字和物理环境中广泛存在的复杂时间交互，如审议延迟、反馈滞后、动作持续性及多速率协作等问题。

Method: 引入“参与过程（Engagement Process, EP）”交互形式化描述：将动作和观察建模为基于时间的解耦事件流，而非传统的固定决策步配对，允许系统在不同时间尺度上进行操作。

Result: 在一系列玩具任务、LLM 智能体及学习实验中，EP 能够捕捉并揭示传统步长接口所隐藏的时间相关行为，并支持智能体在存在显式时间成本的情况下进行策略优化。

Conclusion: EP 框架通过将时间显式化并解耦动作与观察，有效解决了传统 POMDP 在处理复杂时间交互（如下一步延迟、持续性动作等）时的局限性，实现了更灵活、鲁棒的交互建模。

Abstract: Task completion in digital and physical environments increasingly involves complex temporal interaction, where actions and observations unfold over different time scales rather than align with fixed observation--action steps. To model such interactions, we propose \emph{Engagement Process} (EP), an interaction formalism that inherits the decision-theoretic structure of POMDPs while making time explicit in the action--observation interface. EP represents actions and observations as decoupled event streams along time, rather than updates paired at fixed decision steps. This interface captures single-agent timing issues such as deliberation latency, delayed feedback, and persistent actions, while supporting richer agent-side organization, multi-rate coordination, and compositional interaction among subsystems. Across toy, LLM-agent, and learning experiments, EP exposes temporal behaviors hidden by step-based interfaces and enables policies to adapt under explicit time costs.

</details>


### [223] [The Evaluation Differential: When Frontier AI Models Recognise They Are Being Tested](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11496)
*Varad Vishwarupe,Nigel Shadbolt,Marina Jirotka,Ivan Flechais*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对 AI 模型在评估与实际部署间行为不一致的问题，本文提出了“评估差异”（ED）框架及 TRACE 审计协议，旨在通过明确评估条件的限制，使 AI 安全性评估结论更具严谨性与真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代 AI 模型能够识别评估情境并调整行为，导致“评估环境”与“部署环境”表现不一致，使得仅凭现有评估手段得出的安全性结论存在有效性（claim-validity）问题。

Method: 1. 引入“评估差异”（Evaluation Differential, ED）及其标准化形式（nED），用于量化评估环境与部署环境下的行为分歧；2. 证明单纯的边缘评估分数无法识别 ED；3. 提出安全性声明类型学（ED-stable, ED-degraded, ED-inverted, ED-undetermined）；4. 设计 TRACE（Test-Recognition Audit for Claim Evaluation）审计协议，旨在将评估结果转化为受限的安全性声明。

Result: 通过回顾性分析三个公开的评估安全事件，验证了该框架能够揭示隐藏的评估偏差，并为系统卡片（System Cards）和合规性评估提供了更科学的治理路径。

Conclusion: TRACE 协议通过显式标注评估证据产生的条件，有效界定了安全性声明的有效范围，从而解决评估结果在不同环境下可能产生的偏差问题。该框架不旨在消除对抗性适应，而是为了规范化评估结论的严谨性。

Abstract: Recent published evidence from frontier laboratories shows that contemporary AI models can recognise evaluation contexts, latently represent them, and behave differently under those contexts than under deployment-continuous conditions. Anthropic's BrowseComp incident, the Natural Language Autoencoder findings on SWE-bench Verified and destructive-coding evaluations, and the OpenAI / Apollo anti-scheming work all document instances of this phenomenon. We argue that these findings create a claim-validity problem for safety conclusions drawn from frontier evaluations. We introduce the Evaluation Differential (ED), a conditional divergence in a target behavioural property between recognised-evaluation and deployment-continuous contexts, define a normalised effect-size form (nED) for cross-property comparison, and prove that marginal evaluation scores cannot identify ED. We develop a typology of safety claims (ED-stable, ED-degraded, ED-inverted, ED-undetermined) by their warrant-status under documented divergence, and specify TRACE (Test-Recognition Audit for Claim Evaluation), an audit protocol that wraps existing evaluation infrastructure and produces restricted claims rather than capability scores. We apply the framework retrospectively to three publicly documented evaluation incidents and discuss governance implications for system cards, conformity assessment, and the international network of AI safety and security institutes. TRACE does not eliminate adversarial adaptation; it disciplines the claims drawn from evaluation evidence by making explicit the conditions under which that evidence was produced.

</details>


### [224] [Selective Off-Policy Reference Tuning with Plan Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11505)
*Duc Anh Le,Tien-Phat Nguyen,Thien Huu Nguyen,Linh Ngo Van,Trung Le*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对推理任务中因所有采样失败导致 RL 训练停滞的问题，SORT 提出了一种基于参考规划的修复机制，将“全错”数据转化为高质量的学习信号，从而大幅提升了模型在复杂推理任务中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在涉及可验证奖励的推理任务中，当所有采样路径均失败时（即无法获得奖励），学习过程会停滞（stall），导致模型无法从错误中有效学习。

Method: 提出 SORT（Selective Optimization with Reference Training）方法：在 GRPO 的基础上，对失败的采样序列进行“修复更新”。具体而言，利用参考解推导出一个规划（plan），对比有无该规划条件下的 Token 概率，通过加权机制将原本错误的采样转化为结构感知的学习信号，而非单纯的模仿学习。

Result: 在三个主流主干模型及八个推理基准测试中，SORT 均优于 GRPO 和其他引导方法，特别是在能力较弱的模型上收益最为显著。

Conclusion: SORT 通过引入基于参考解的规划修复机制，有效解决了强化学习在硬推理任务中因采样失败而陷入停滞的问题，显著提升了推理模型的性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards helps reasoning, but GRPO-style methods stall on hard prompts where all sampled rollouts fail. SORT adds a repair update for those failures without changing rollout generation: it derives a plan from the reference solution, compares token probabilities with and without that plan, and gives higher weight to tokens that become more predictable under plan conditioning. This turns all-wrong prompts into selective, structure-aware learning signals instead of uniform imitation. Across three backbones and eight reasoning benchmarks, SORT improves over GRPO and guidance baselines, with largest gains on weaker models.

</details>


### [225] [AutoLLMResearch: Training Research Agents for Automating LLM Experiment Configuration -- Learning from Cheap, Optimizing Expensive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11518)
*Taicheng Guo,Nitesh V. Chawla,Olaf Wiest,Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对大模型（LLM）实验配置成本高昂、自动化程度低的问题，本文提出 AutoLLMResearch 框架，通过模拟人类研究者利用低保真实验推导通用结论并外推至高成本场景的能力，实现了高效的实验配置自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 实验配置（架构设计、超参数调优等）极其昂贵，传统的自动化方法仅适用于低成本场景，而高成本场景下无法进行频繁的试错，目前只能依赖专家直觉，效率低下且浪费算力。

Method: 提出了 AutoLLMResearch 框架：1) 构建了 LLMConfig-Gym，这是一个包含超过 100 万 GPU 小时实验数据、覆盖四类关键 LLM 任务的跨保真度（multi-fidelity）环境；2) 设计了一个结构化训练流水线，将配置研究建模为长程马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），并专门设计了激励跨保真度外推推理的机制。

Result: 在保留测试集（held-out experiments）上的广泛评估表明，该框架在性能上优于多种强基线模型，不仅能有效识别出高性能配置，还表现出良好的泛化能力和可解释性。

Conclusion: AutoLLMResearch 证明了其在自动化高成本 LLM 实验配置方面的有效性、泛化能力和可解释性，为解决当前 LLM 研究中实验配置高度依赖手动经验且成本高昂的问题提供了一种实用方案。

Abstract: Effectively configuring scalable large language model (LLM) experiments, spanning architecture design, hyperparameter tuning, and beyond, is crucial for advancing LLM research, as poor configuration choices can waste substantial computational resources and prevent models from realizing their full potential. Prior automated methods are designed for low-cost settings where repeated trial and error is feasible, but scalable LLM experiments are too expensive for such extensive iteration. To our knowledge, no work has addressed the automation of high-cost LLM experiment configurations, leaving this problem labor-intensive and dependent on expert intuition. Motivated by this gap, we propose AutoLLMResearch, an agentic framework that mimics how human researchers learn generalizable principles from low-fidelity experiments and extrapolate to efficiently identify promising configurations in expensive LLM settings. The core challenge is how to enable an agent to learn, through interaction with a multi-fidelity experimental environment that captures the structure of the LLM configuration landscape. To achieve this, we propose a systematic framework with two key components: 1) LLMConfig-Gym, a multi-fidelity environment encompassing four critical LLM experiment tasks, supported by over one million GPU hours of verifiable experiment outcomes; 2) A structured training pipeline that formulates configuration research as a long-horizon Markov Decision Process and accordingly incentivizes cross-fidelity extrapolation reasoning. Extensive evaluation against diverse strong baselines on held-out experiments demonstrates the effectiveness, generalization, and interpretability of our framework, supporting its potential as a practical and general solution for scalable real-world LLM experiment automation.

</details>


### [226] [Controllable User Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11519)
*Guy Tennenholtz,Ofer Meshi,Amir Globerson,Uri Shalit,Jihwan Jeong,Craig Boutilier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文指出当前大模型对话模拟器通过监督微调训练存在“可控性崩溃”问题，通过将其建模为因果推理问题并引入离策略评估技术，提出了一套缓解偏差的训练方法，实现了更稳健、分布更真实的对话模拟与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线数据集难以覆盖罕见场景或支持新策略评估；现有大语言模型模拟器常因通过监督微调处理轨迹标签，导致模型产生结构性偏差（前瞻偏差），无法满足反事实评估需求。

Method: 将可控模拟形式化为因果推理问题；引入离策略评估（OPE）方法；提出三种针对性的训练缓解技术：先验控制（a priori controls）、步骤级动态控制（step-wise dynamic controls）以及直接策略条件学习（direct policy-conditioned learning）。

Result: 实验证明，传统全局控制会扭曲对话分布并导致行为多样性崩溃；而提出的因果 grounded 模拟器成功消除了前瞻偏差，能够有效应对策略偏移，并在各种评估指标上优于传统方法。

Conclusion: 传统的基于监督微调的对话模拟器存在因果不一致性（前瞻偏差），会导致在策略偏移下评估指标的方差激增即“可控性崩溃”。通过采用提出的因果导向训练方法，可以消除偏差，保持自然变异性，并在未见过的策略下表现出稳健的零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Using offline datasets to evaluate conversational agents often fails to cover rare scenarios or to support testing new policies. This has motivated the use of controllable user simulators for targeted, counterfactual evaluation, typically implemented by prompting or fine-tuning large language models. In this work, we formalize controllable simulation as a causal inference problem. By bridging natural language evaluation with off-policy evaluation methodology, we show that the standard practice of training simulators via supervised fine-tuning on post-hoc trajectory labels yields a structurally biased model. Specifically, these labels are inextricably coupled to the data-generating behavior policy, injecting a look-ahead bias that breaks causal consistency. Furthermore, we prove that under policy shift this failure causes the variance of evaluation metrics to explode geometrically, a phenomenon we term controllability collapse. To restore causal consistency, we establish theoretical conditions for accurate simulation and propose practical training mitigations: a priori controls, step-wise dynamic controls, and direct policy-conditioned learning. Empirical evaluation confirms that while standard global controls distort conversational distributions and collapse behavioral diversity, our causally grounded simulators eliminate look-ahead bias, preserve natural variance, and exhibit robust zero-shot generalization to unseen agent behaviors.

</details>


### [227] [Read, Grep, and Synthesize: Diagnosing Cross-Domain Seed Exposure for LLM Research Ideation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11532)
*Yunju Choi,Min Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现LLM通过跨领域检索产生的创新方案，核心驱动力在于接触了更多样化的信息，而非模型真正理解并利用了不同领域间的特定语义关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索LLM的创新（Ideation）系统性能提升的原因：究竟是得益于有针对性的跨领域检索，还是仅仅源于对多样化机制的广泛接触。

Method: 提出名为PaperGym的三阶段流水线：1. 使用工具增强（读取、grep、bash）从论文中提取种子；2. 通过跨领域改写（paraphrasing）进行七个ML领域的种子检索；3. 基于LLM的 rubric 评分系统对检索到的种子进行方法综合与评估。

Result: 工具增强提取提高了特异性；跨领域检索在创新性对比中优于“不检索”和“同领域检索”基准，但与“随机多样化种子”控制组相比无显著差异。

Conclusion: LLM的创新能力目前主要得益于对多样化“种子”（seeds）的广泛接触，而并非基于对特定领域知识的深度语义利用。未来的研究需要改进模型，使其能更好地主动利用跨领域检索到的语义逻辑，而非仅仅依赖随机的多样性。

Abstract: The discovery of novel methodologies for emerging problems is a continuing cycle in ML, often driven by the migration of techniques across domains. Building on this observation, we ask whether current LLM ideation systems benefit from targeted cross-domain retrieval or simply from exposure to diverse mechanisms. We study this question through PaperGym, a three-stage pipeline: (1) tool-augmented seed extraction via read, grep, and bash over an isolated paper environment, (2) cross-domain seed retrieval via paraphrasing across seven ML domains, and (3) method synthesis from retrieved seeds, each scored by rubric-based judges. Tool-augmented extraction improves specificity, and paraphrase-based retrieval broadens domain coverage. In synthesis, cross-domain retrieval receives more pairwise novelty wins than no-retrieval and same-domain baselines, but shows no significant difference from a random diverse-seed control. These findings suggest LLM ideation systems benefit from diverse seed exposure, but do not yet reliably exploit the semantic reason particular seeds were retrieved. We release the seed library, rubric prompts, and run scripts at https://github.com/yunjoochoi/PaperGym

</details>


### [228] [Optimal LTLf Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11544)
*Yujian Cao,Sven Schewe,Qiyi Tang,Shufang Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对传统LTLf策略综合“全有或全无”的局限性，提出了一系列最优综合方法，通过最大化满足多个不可同时实现的目标，在不确定环境中实现了更具鲁棒性的策略生成，并在实验中验证了其可扩展性和可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的LTLf策略综合通常遵循“全有或全无”的范式，一旦规范无法在不确定的环境中得到完全满足，系统就会返回不可实现（unrealisable）。该研究旨在解决即使无法完全实现所有目标，也能尽可能多地实现给定规范中多个目标的问题。

Method: 提出三种最优LTLf综合策略：1. 最大化保证综合（Max-guarantee synthesis），预先确定可实现的最高优先级目标集；2. 最大化观测综合（Max-observation synthesis），根据不同执行路径最大化后验实现的性能；3. 增量式最大化观测综合（Incremental max-observation synthesis），在交互过程中动态挖掘更强保证的机会。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的几种最优综合变体在性能上具有良好的可扩展性，能够解决基准测试中的大部分实例，证明了该方法在实际应用中的可行性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在环境不确定的情况下，通过最优LTLf综合方法实现多目标部分满足在计算上是可行且高效的，为处理不可实现（unrealisable）的规范提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Strategy synthesis typically follows an all-or-nothing paradigm, returning unrealisable whenever a specification cannot be guaranteed in an uncertain environment. In this paper, we introduce optimal LTLf synthesis, where the goal is to realise as many objectives as possible from a given specification consisting of multiple objectives, especially for the case that they are not all jointly realisable. We first consider max-guarantee synthesis, which commits to a maximal set of objectives that we can a priori guarantee to realise. We then introduce max-observation synthesis, which maximises a posteriori realised objectives that may be incomparable on different executions. Finally, we present incremental max-observation synthesis, which further improves strategies by exploiting opportunities for stronger guarantees when they arise during an execution. Experimental results show that different variations of optimal synthesis scale broadly equally well, solving a large fraction of the benchmark instances within the given timeout, demonstrating the practical feasibility of the approach.

</details>


### [229] [Hindsight Hint Distillation: Scaffolded Reasoning for SWE Agents from CoT-free Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11556)
*Shengjie Wang,Guanghe Li,Zonghan Yang,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HHD 通过利用模型失败的轨迹合成提示信息，绕过了对昂贵思维链标注的依赖，实现了长程任务推理能力的自我提升与泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂长程任务需要强大的规划和推理能力；虽然思维链（CoT）数据有效，但获取成本高昂。亟需一种在没有 CoT 标注的情况下提升模型推理与规划能力的方法。

Method: 提出“后见之明提示蒸馏”（Hindsight Hint Distillation, HHD）方法：利用模型自身的失败尝试合成“后见之明提示”（Hindsight Hints），通过这些提示引导模型完成任务，随后进行自我蒸馏（Self-distillation），使模型在推理阶段无需提示也能完成任务。

Result: 在 SWE-bench Verified 上实现了 8% 的绝对性能提升（基准仅为 2%）；在跨语言挑战（SWE-bench Multilingual）中展现出强大的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: HHD 有效地从无需思维链（CoT）标注的数据中合成专家级推理能力，显著提升了模型处理长程复杂任务的性能及泛化能力。

Abstract: Solving complex long-horizon tasks requires strong planning and reasoning capabilities. Although datasets with explicit chain-of-thought (CoT) rationales can substantially benefit learning, they are costly to obtain. To address this challenge, we propose Hindsight Hint Distillation (HHD), which only requires easy-to-obtain question-answer pairs without CoT annotations. Inspired by how human teachers use student mistakes to provide targeted guidance, HHD synthesizes hindsight hints from the model's own failed self-rollouts and uses them to scaffold on-policy rollouts that successfully complete the tasks. The model then self-distills these scaffolded trajectories and generalizes to new problems without hint guidance. Experiments show that HHD significantly outperforms iterative RFT and trajectory-synthesis baselines, achieving an absolute improvement of 8\% on SWE-bench Verified, while all baselines improve by only around 2\%. Notably, the reasoning strategies induced by HHD generalize effectively to out-of-distribution tasks, yielding the largest gains on SWE-bench Multilingual despite no training on multilingual data. These results demonstrate that HHD can effectively synthesize expert-like reasoning from CoT-free data and substantially improve long-horizon performance.

</details>


### [230] [Dual-Temporal LSTM with Hybrid Attention for Airline Passenger Load Factor Forecasting: Integrating Intra-Flight and Inter-Flight Booking Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11569)
*ASM Nazrul Islam,Md. Hasanul Kabir,Md. Liakot Ali,Joydeb Kumar Sana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用双流注意力LSTM捕捉航空预订水平与垂直维度时序特征的预测模型，解决了现有模型信息单一和鲁棒性不足的问题，并已在实际航司运营中得到应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有航空需求预测模型通常仅处理单一时间维度（导致信息缺失），且预测绝对乘客数量在机型变更时存在脆弱性，缺乏对复杂预订时序特征的深度挖掘。

Method: 提出了一种双流LSTM集成注意力机制架构。该模型并行处理两个序列：水平序列（某航班出发前不同预订时段的累积数据）和垂直序列（跨历史航班在相同预订节点的数据），并通过自注意力、交叉注意力及多种融合策略挖掘时序依赖。

Result: 在孟加拉航空（BBA）真实数据集上，该模型取得了2.8167的MAE和0.9495的R²，显著优于基准模型及现有的双LSTM架构，并在多种航线类型中展现出良好的泛化性能。该方法已在BBA投入实际运营。

Conclusion: 通过双流注意力LSTM框架，利用水平（单次航程内）和垂直（跨航程历史）数据维度，能有效提升航空预订需求预测的准确性与鲁棒性，具备极高的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Accurate short-term demand forecasting is crucial to airline revenue management, yet most existing systems fail to meet this need because current models treat booking data as a single temporal dimension, either the accumulation of bookings for a specific flight or the historical booking profile of the same route. This unidimensional view discards information carried by the other temporal stream and forecasting absolute passenger counts introduces a further operational fragility when change in planned aircraft type alters total seat capacity. This study addresses both limitations. A dual-stream Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) integrated with attention framework is proposed that simultaneously processes two complementary input sequences: a horizontal sequence capturing intra-flight booking accumulation over the days preceding departure, and a vertical sequence capturing inter-flight booking patterns at fixed days-before-departure offsets across historical flights. Multiple dual-stream architectural variants, combining self-attention, cross-attention, and hybrid attention with concatenation, residual, and gated fusion strategies, are developed and evaluated. Experiments on real-world reservation data from the national airline of Bangladesh, Biman Bangladesh Airlines (BBA), demonstrate that the proposed hybrid model achieves a Mean Absolute Error of 2.8167 and a coefficient of determination ($R^{2}$) of 0.9495, outperforming single-stream baselines, tree-based models, and three prior dual-LSTM architectures applied to the same data. Validation across four flight category pairs; domestic versus international, direct versus transit, high versus low frequency, and short versus mid versus long haul confirms that the model generalizes across operationally diverse route types. Biman Bangladesh Airlines (BBA) has officially integrated this methodology into its operations.

</details>


### [231] [GAR: Carbon-Aware Routing for LLM Inference via Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11603)
*Disha Sheshanarayana,Rajat Subhra Pal,Manjira Sinha,Tirthankar Dasgupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种绿色感知路由框架（GAR），通过轻量级预测器和在线优化算法，在保证 LLM 推理准确度和延迟 SLA 的前提下，有效降低了系统的总碳排放量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 路由方法忽视了能源效率和碳排放，而不同模型、时间及区域的能效差异显著。需要一种能同时满足准确度、延迟 SLO 以及碳排放限制的调度策略。

Method: 提出名为 GAR（Green-Aware Routing）的约束多目标优化框架，包含用于准确性、延迟和碳排放的轻量级预测器；设计了 GAR-PD 在线原始对偶算法以处理滚动碳预算，并辅以启发式策略。

Result: 在 7B-70B 异构模型池的 NLP 基准测试中，GAR 显著降低了碳排放，同时严格保证了准确率基准和 p95 延迟的要求。

Conclusion: GAR 是一个兼顾准确率、延迟与碳排放的实用型 LLM 请求路由框架，证明了在不显著牺牲性能的前提下实现可持续 AI 推理的可行性。

Abstract: The growing deployment of large language models (LLMs) makes per-request routing essential for balancing response quality and computational cost across heterogeneous model pools. Current routing methods rarely consider sustainable energy use and CO2 emissions as optimization objectives, despite grid carbon intensity varying by time and region, and models differing significantly in energy consumption. To address this gap, we introduce Green-Aware Routing (GAR), a constrained multi-objective optimization framework that minimizes per-request CO2 emissions subject to explicit accuracy floors and p95-latency service-level objectives (SLOs). GAR employs adaptive constraint optimization through per-dataset floor tuning and incorporates lightweight estimators for correctness, tail latency, and carbon emissions, enabling real-time routing decisions without additional inference passes. We present GAR-PD, a practical online primal-dual routing algorithm for rolling carbon budgets, alongside heuristic variants that achieve high feasibility coverage while limiting accuracy degradation. Comprehensive experiments across standard NLP benchmarks with heterogeneous LLM pools (7B-70B) demonstrate that GAR achieves substantial carbon reductions while maintaining competitive accuracy and p95 latency guarantees, providing a practical, theoretically grounded approach to sustainable LLM inference.

</details>


### [232] [Nice Fold or Hero Call: Learning Budget-Efficient Thinking for Adaptive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11625)
*Zhaomeng Zhou,Lan Zhang,Junyang Wang,Mu Yuan,Junda Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BET通过动态评估问题的“可解性”来分配推理预算，在显著减少计算资源开销的同时，提升了模型处理复杂问题的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理模型在分配测试时计算（test-time compute）时常忽视问题的“可解性”（solvability），导致在不可解问题上浪费资源，或对复杂但可解的问题过度压缩。

Method: 提出了Budget-Efficient Thinking (BET)框架，包含两阶段策略：基于行为冷启动（behavioral cold-start）和在投资成本感知奖励（investment-cost-aware reward）下使用GRPO进行训练，从而学习到“快速求解”、“及时止损”和“全力以赴”三种推理行为。

Result: 在七个基准测试和三个基模型上，推理Token消耗平均减少约55%，同时整体性能得到提升，并具备良好的跨领域零样本迁移能力。

Conclusion: BET通过将推理视为基于预期回报的计算投资，成功优化了大型推理模型的测试时计算分配，实现了更高的推理效率与性能。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) improve problem solving through extended reasoning, but often misallocate test-time compute. Existing efficiency methods reduce cost by compressing reasoning traces or conditioning budget on perceived difficulty, yet largely overlook solvability. As a result, they may spend large budgets on queries beyond the model's capability while compressing hard-but-solvable queries that require deeper reasoning. In this work, we formulate adaptive reasoning as a computational investment under uncertainty, where budget should follow the expected return of reasoning rather than perceived difficulty alone. To instantiate this principle, we propose Budget-Efficient Thinking (BET), a two-stage framework that combines behavioral cold-start with GRPO under an investment-cost-aware reward. By aligning solve-or-fold decisions with rollout-derived solvability, BET learns three behaviors: (1) short solve, answering easy queries concisely; (2) nice fold, abstaining early when continued reasoning has near-zero expected return; and (3) hero call, preserving sufficient compute for hard-but-solvable queries. Across seven benchmarks and three base models, BET reduces reasoning tokens by ~55% on average while achieving overall performance improvements, and transfers zero-shot from mathematical reasoning to scientific QA and logical reasoning with comparable efficiency gains.

</details>


### [233] [Can LLM Agents Respond to Disasters? Benchmarking Heterogeneous Geospatial Reasoning in Emergency Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11633)
*Junjue Wang,Weihao Xuan,Heli Qi,Pengyu Dai,Kunyi Liu,Hongruixuan Chen,Zhuo Zheng,Junshi Xia,Stefano Ermon,Naoto Yokoya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 文章提出了首个端到端灾害应急智能体基准 DORA，研究发现现有主流大模型在处理复杂灾害响应任务时，在领域定位、工具决策及长路径任务稳健性方面均存在显著瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的灾害响应研究多局限于单一的感知或通用工具使用，缺乏对端到端、具备复杂逻辑推理和协同操作能力的灾害应急响应工作流的探索。

Method: 构建了 DORA（Disaster Operational Response Agent）基准，包含 515 个专家构建的任务，覆盖 45 个真实事件。提供了包含 3500 个工具调用步骤的“金标准轨迹”（gold trajectories）和 108 个 MCP 工具库，以评估多模态大模型在多传感器数据处理、复杂推理和操作决策上的性能。

Result: 对 13 个主流大模型进行了测试，发现：（1）模型在灾害领域的定位准确度不足；（2）工具选择和参数对齐是主要瓶颈（即使给定工具调用顺序，收益也仅提升个位数）；（3）任务链条越长，性能衰减越严重，差距从 7% 扩大到 56%。

Conclusion: DORA 建立了灾害响应智能体领域严谨的基准测试体系，揭示了目前大模型在灾害领域存在定位偏差（grounding）、工具路径规划困难和长链条任务脆弱性等核心挑战，为未来研究提供了评估依据。

Abstract: Operational disaster response goes beyond damage assessment, requiring responders to integrate multi-sensor signals, reason over road networks, populations and key facilities, plan evacuations, and produce actionable reports. However, prior work largely isolates remote-sensing perception or evaluates generic tool use, leaving the end-to-end workflows of emergency operations underexplored. In this paper, we introduce Disaster Operational Response Agent benchmark (DORA), the first agentic benchmark for end-to-end disaster response: 515 expert-authored tasks across 45 real-world disaster events spanning 10 types, paired with expert-verified, replayable gold trajectories totaling 3,500 tool-call steps. Tasks span five dimensions that cover the operational disaster-response pipeline: disaster perception, spatial relational analysis, rescue and evacuation planning, temporal evolution reasoning, and multi-modal report synthesis. Agents compose calls from a 108-tool MCP library over heterogeneous geospatial data: optical, SAR, and multi-spectral imagery across single-, bi-, and multi-temporal sequences (0.015-10m GSD), complemented by elevation and social vector layers. We comprehensively evaluate 13 frontier LLMs on our benchmark, revealing three persistent challenges: 1) disaster-domain grounding exposes unique failure modes (damage-semantic grounding, sensor-modality mismatch, and disaster-pipeline composition); 2) agents are doubly bottlenecked by tool selection and argument grounding, where gold tool-order hints improve accuracy by only 1.08-4.40%, and alternative scaffolds yield at most a 3.24% gain; 3) compositional fragility scales with trajectory length, the agent-to-gold gap widening from 7% to 56% on long pipelines. DORA establishes a rigorous testbed for operationally reliable disaster-response agents.

</details>


### [234] [Seirênes: Adversarial Self-Play with Evolving Distractions for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11636)
*Chi Zhang,Haibo Qiu,Qiming Zhang,Yufei Xu,Xinbo Gao,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Seirênes 是一种基于自博弈的训练框架，通过让 LLM 学习构建“干扰上下文”来攻击自身的盲区，并同时提升在干扰下的解题能力，实现了推理鲁棒性的协同演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 推理模型虽然在标准数据集上表现优异，但在面对带有冗余信息、干扰指令等“非理想化”上下文时表现出易碎性（Fragility）。研究旨在消除这种对单纯分布的依赖，提升模型在复杂现实环境下的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出了一种参数共享的对抗性自博弈（Self-play）框架。该模型扮演双重角色：一是“干扰生成者”，负责构建包含无关信息、干扰项和偶然相关性的复杂上下文，以暴露自身的推理缺陷；二是“问题求解者”，在干扰环境下辨别核心任务并恢复逻辑，通过博弈实现协同进化。

Result: 在七个数学推理基准测试中，Seirênes 在 4B 到 30B 模型规模下均取得显著性能提升（平均提升 7.2 至 10.2 点）。此外，4B 模型生成的干扰能够导致 GPT 和 Gemini 等顶尖商业模型的准确率下降 4-5 个百分点，验证了其在寻找模型推理盲区方面的通用性。

Conclusion: Seirênes 通过自博弈机制成功将上下文干扰转化为训练信号，显著提升了推理模型的鲁棒性与解决复杂任务的能力。该工作证明了在模型自身生成的干扰下训练，能够克服浅层模式匹配，从而锚定深层的逻辑推理能力。

Abstract: We present Seirênes, a self-play RL framework that transforms contextual interference from a failure mode of LLM reasoning into an internal training signal for co-evolving more resilient reasoners. While RL with verifiable rewards has significantly advanced reasoning capabilities, models can still exhibit fragility when encountering non-idealized contexts: scenarios characterized by superfluous information, tangential instructions, or incidental correlations that differ from the clean distributions typical of standard benchmarks. Seirênes harnesses this vulnerability through a parameter-shared and adversarial self-play loop. Within this framework, a single model is trained to both construct plausible yet distracting contexts that expose its own reasoning blind spots, and solve problems by discerning the essential task from these perturbations to recover the core underlying logic. By pitting these competing objectives against each other, Seirênes compels the model to move beyond superficial pattern matching and anchors its capabilities in robust underlying reasoning. This continuous interaction sustains an informative co-evolutionary curriculum as the model improves. Across seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks and model scales from 4B to 30B, Seirênes achieves average gains of +10.2, +9.1, and +7.2 points. Besides, distracting contexts produced by the 4B Seirênes model reduce the accuracy of top-tier closed-source models (GPT and Gemini) by roughly 4--5 points, revealing Seirênes' general ability to uncover reasoning models' blind spots.

</details>


### [235] [OOM-Free Alpamayo via CPU-GPU Memory Swapping for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11678)
*Seungwoo Roh,Huiyeong Kim,Jong-Chan Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需修改模型结构的系统级优化框架，通过分层存储、流水线重叠和智能动态配置，有效解决了大规模VLA模型在显存受限的消费级GPU上的推理瓶颈，实现了数倍的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在自动驾驶领域表现优异，但其庞大的参数量（20-60GB）超出了消费级GPU（12-16GB）的显存容量，阻碍了其在受限边缘设备上的部署。

Method: 提出了一种无需模型修改的内存高效VLA推理框架，包含三个核心阶段：1. 顺序需求分层（Sequential Demand Layering）：将显存管理粒度从模型级细化至层级；2. 流水线需求分层（Pipelined Demand Layering）：通过计算与数据传输的重叠隐藏传输延迟；3. GPU驻留层决策策略（GPU-Resident Layer Decision Policy）：基于收益分析确定最优的常驻层配置，配合性能预测模型进行配置优化。

Result: 在RTX 5070Ti（16GB）上部署Alpamayo-R1-10B（21.52GB）模型，相较于现有的Accelerate卸载方案，实现了最高3.55倍的推理加速，且在保持BF16全精度的情况下，配置预测误差小于1.3%。

Conclusion: 该研究通过系统级优化（而非模型修改）成功实现了在显存受限的消费级GPU上部署大规模VLA模型，证明了在不损失精度的情况下，通过存储分层、流水线隐藏传输延迟和智能层分配策略，可以显著提升模型推理效率。

Abstract: End-to-end Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for autonomous driving unify perception, reasoning, and control in a single neural network, achieving strong driving performance but requiring 20-60GB of GPU memory-far exceeding the 12-16GB available on commodity GPUs. We present a framework, which enables memory-efficient VLA inference on VRAM-constrained GPUs through system-level optimization alone, without model modification. Our work proceeds in three stages: (1) Sequential Demand Layering reduces VRAM usage from model-level to layer-level granularity; (2) Pipelined Demand Layering hides parameter transfer time within layer execution time via transfer--compute overlap; and (3) a GPU-Resident Layer Decision Policy, informed by per-module residency benefit analysis, eliminates the residual transfer overhead that pipelining cannot hide. We further propose a performance prediction model that determines the optimal configuration-both the number and placement of resident layers-from a single profiling run with less than 1.3% prediction error across all configurations. Applied to NVIDIA's Alpamayo-R1-10B (21.52GB) on an RTX 5070Ti (16GB), our work achieves up to 3.55x speedup over Accelerate offloading while maintaining full BF16 precision.

</details>


### [236] [Explaining and Breaking the Safety-Helpfulness Ceiling via Preference Dimensional Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11679)
*ShiYing Huang,Liang Lin,Yuer Li,Kaiwen Luo,Zhenhong Zhou,An Zhang,Junhao Dong,Kun Wang,Zhigang Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对大模型多目标对齐中的“零和博弈”困境，本文提出MORA方法，通过重写提示词引入多维意图，突破了原有提示词对奖励空间的限制，在提升各项对齐指标的同时实现了多目标的协同优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型多目标对齐中存在“零和博弈”现象，即优化某一指标（如有用性）通常会损害另一指标（如无害性）。现有方法（如数据筛选、参数合并）仅能在固定的帕累托前沿强制妥协，无法从根本上解决权衡问题。

Method: 提出MORA（Multi-Objective Reward Assimilation）框架：1. 通过预采样分离单奖励提示词；2. 通过重写问题将单维意图扩展为多维意图，从而增加奖励的多样性，打破固定帕累托前沿的制约。

Result: 1. 顺序对齐场景：在有用、无害、真实维度对齐后，单项偏好性能提升5%至12.4%（无害性提升尤为突出）。2. 同时对齐场景：平均总奖励提升4.6%。

Conclusion: 多目标冲突的根本原因在于提示词本身限制了可实现的多维奖励空间。通过重写提示词以融合多维意图（MORA方法），可以有效突破这一局限，实现多目标性能的协同提升。

Abstract: In the realm of multi-objective alignment for large language models, balancing disparate human preferences often manifests as a zero-sum conflict. Specifically, the intrinsic tension between competing goals dictates that aggressively optimizing for one metric (e.g., helpfulness) frequently incurs a substantial penalty on another (e.g., harmlessness). While prior work mainly focuses on data selection, parameter merging, or algorithmic balancing during training, these approaches merely force compromises between divergent preferences along a fixed Pareto frontier, failing to fundamentally resolve the inherent trade-off. In this work, we approach this problem from a novel perspective of multi-dimensional rewards. By scaling up the model's rollouts and analyzing the outputs across different reward dimensions, we arrive at a critical conclusion: the conflict among multiple objectives stems from the fact that the prompt itself inherently restricts the achievable multi-dimensional rewards. Based on this core observation, we propose MORA: Multi-Objective Reward Assimilation. Specifically, MORA isolates single-reward prompts through pre-sampling and expands their reward diversity by rewriting the original questions to incorporate multi-dimensional intents. Extensive experiments demonstrate that: (1) in sequential alignment, MORA achieves single-preference improvements ranging from 5% to 12.4%, with exceptional gains in harmlessness, after multiple-preference alignment across helpful, harmless, and truthful dimensions. (2) In simultaneous alignment, MORA achieves an average overall reward improvement of 4.6%. Our codes are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/MORA-MPA.

</details>


### [237] [Persistent and Conversational Multi-Method Explainability for Trustworthy Financial AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11687)
*Georgios Makridis,Georgios Fatouros,John Soldatos,George Katsis,Dimosthenis Kyriazis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向金融领域的人本可解释AI（XAI）架构，通过将模型解释制品持久化存储，利用RAG实现多方法交叉验证，并引入自动化一致性校验，有效提升了金融预测解释的可信度与易用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融机构严格的监管环境迫切需要AI解释具备持久性、多方法交叉验证能力，并通过易于理解、对话式的交互方式呈现给决策者。

Method: 1. 架构设计：将XAI制品（LIME、遮挡分析、显著性热图）转化为具备元数据与自然语言摘要的搜索对象，并存入分布式存储；2. 交互逻辑：利用检索增强生成（RAG）技术实现多方法解释的交叉验证与对话式 synthèse（综合）；3. 评估机制：通过自动化检查（完整性、幻觉检测、归因一致性）来评估解释的忠实度。

Result: 通过在EXTRA-BRAIN金融情绪分析管道上的实验，验证了该架构的有效性；受限提示（Constrained Prompting）技术相比朴素提示，将模型幻觉率降低了36%，并将方法归因引用率提升了73%。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的以人为中心的XAI架构通过持久化存储、多方法三角验证及自动化一致性校验，为金融领域提供了一种更具鲁棒性、透明度和可信度的AI解释方案。

Abstract: Financial institutions increasingly require AI explanations that are persistent, cross-validated across methods, and conversationally accessible to human decision-makers. We present an architecture for human-centered explainable AI in financial sentiment analysis that combines three contributions. First, we treat XAI artifacts -- LIME feature attributions, occlusion-based word importance scores, and saliency heatmaps -- as persistent, searchable objects in distributed S3-compatible storage with structured metadata and natural-language summaries, enabling semantic retrieval over explanation history and automatic index reconstruction after system failures. Second, we enable multi-method explanation triangulation, where a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) assistant compares and synthesizes results from multiple XAI methods applied to the same prediction, allowing users to assess explanation robustness through natural-language dialogue. Third, we evaluate the faithfulness of generated explanations using automated checks over grounding completeness, hallucinated claims, and method-attribution behavior. We demonstrate the architecture on an EXTRA-BRAIN financial sentiment analysis pipeline using FinBERT predictions and present evaluation results showing that constrained prompting reduces hallucination rate by 36\% and increases method-attribution citations by 73\% compared to naive prompting. We discuss implications for trustworthy, human-centered AI services in regulated financial environments.

</details>


### [238] [SafeSteer: A Decoding-level Defense Mechanism for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11716)
*Xinyi Zeng,Xue Yang,Jingyuan Zhang,Huanqian Yan,Xiang Chen,Kaiwen Wei,Hankun Kang,Yu Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SafeSteer 是一种无需微调的解码级防御框架，通过解码探针和模态语义对齐技术，显著增强了 MLLM 对有害输入的识别与纠正能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 防御方法依赖于昂贵的微调或低效的后处理干预，不仅成本高且面临性能权衡，无法有效应对新型攻击。研究旨在挖掘 MLLM 在解码阶段固有的安全辨别能力。

Method: 提出 SafeSteer 框架，包含两个核心部分：1) Decoding-Probe（解码探针）：在解码过程中检测并纠正有害输出；2) 模态语义对齐向量（Modal Semantic Alignment Vector）：将强大的文本安全对齐能力迁移至视觉模态。

Result: 实验表明，SafeSteer 在多个 MLLM 上可将安全性提升高达 33.40%，且无需微调，能够保持模型原有的生成性能。

Conclusion: SafeSteer 是一种有效的解码级防御机制，能够在不进行微调的情况下显著提升多模态大模型的安全性，并有效平衡模型的有用性与无害性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are gaining increasing attention. Due to the heterogeneity of their input features, they face significant challenges in terms of jailbreak defenses. Current defense methods rely on costly fine-tuning or inefficient post-hoc interventions, limiting their ability to address novel attacks and involving performance trade-offs. To address the above issues, we explore the inherent safety capabilities within MLLMs and quantify their intrinsic ability to discern harmfulness at decoding stage. We observe that 1) MLLMs can distinguish the harmful and harmless inputs during decoding process, 2) Image-based attacks are more stealthy. Based on these insights, we introduce SafeSteer, a decoding-level defense mechanism for MLLMs. Specifically, it includes a Decoding-Probe, a lightweight probe for detecting and correcting harmful output during decoding, which iteratively steers the decoding process toward safety. Furthermore, a modal semantic alignment vector is integrated to transfer the strong textual safety alignment to the vision modality. Experiments on multiple MLLMs demonstrate that SafeSterr can improve MLLMs' safety by up to 33.40\% without fine-tuning. Notably, it can maintain the effectiveness of MLLMs, ensuring a balance between their helpfulness and harmlessness.

</details>


### [239] [OptArgus: A Multi-Agent System to Detect Hallucinations in LLM-based Optimization Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11738)
*Zhong Li,Zihan Guo,Xiaohan Lu,Juntao Wang,Jie Song,Chao Shen,Jiageng Wu,Mingyang Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对大模型在优化建模中的“语义幻觉”问题，本文提出了首个精细化幻觉分类体系及多智能体检测框架OptArgus，通过结构化审计提升了数学模型生成的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型在将自然语言转化为数学优化模型时存在“幻觉”问题，仅仅匹配目标函数数值无法保证语义的准确性，且缺乏针对优化建模特有的结构性与语义一致性的检测方法。

Method: 1. 建立了优化建模领域的幻觉分类体系（针对目标函数、变量、约束及实现）；2. 设计了OptArgus多智能体检测架构，包含指挥者路由、专家审计员及证据聚合机制；3. 构建了包含484个清洁样本、1266个人工注入错误样本及6292个自然生成的LLM样本的评估基准套件。

Result: 相比单智能体基线，OptArgus在清洁样本上误报更少，在受控错误案例中定位更精准，在自然生成的模型输出中表现出更强的检测能力。

Conclusion: 该研究将优化建模中的幻觉检测具象化为一项可实证的科学问题，证明了基于细粒度分类的模块化审计机制（OptArgus）是实现可靠优化建模的有效路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to translate natural-language optimization problems into mathematical formulations and solver code, but matching the reference objective value is not a reliable test of correctness: an artifact may agree numerically while still changing the underlying optimization semantics. We formulate this issue as \emph{optimization-modeling hallucination detection}, namely structural consistency auditing over the problem description, symbolic model, and solver implementation. We develop, to our knowledge, the first fine-grained hallucination taxonomy specifically for optimization modeling, spanning objective, variable, constraint, and implementation failures. We use this taxonomy to design OptArgus, a multi-agent detector with conductor routing, specialist auditors, and evidence consolidation. To evaluate this setting, we introduce a three-part benchmark suite with $484$ clean artifacts, $1266$ controlled injected artifacts, and $6292$ natural LLM-generated artifacts. Against a matched single-agent baseline, OptArgus produces fewer false alarms on clean artifacts, more accurate top-ranked localization on controlled single-error cases, and stronger detection on natural model outputs. Together, these contributions turn optimization-modeling hallucination detection into a concrete empirical problem and suggest that modular, taxonomy-grounded auditing is a practical route to more reliable optimization modeling.

</details>


### [240] [Towards Visually Grounded Multimodal Summarization via Cross-Modal Transformer and Gated Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11753)
*Abid Ali,Diego Molla-Aliod,Usman Naseem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对多模态总结中的特征对齐问题，提出了 SPeCTrA-Sum 系统，通过深度视觉处理器进行层级化跨模态融合，并结合 DPP 蒸馏实现精准的图像筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态总结方法通常仅将浅层视觉特征注入深度语言模型，导致模态间的表示不匹配（representational mismatch）以及跨模态基础薄弱（weak cross-modal grounding）。

Method: 提出了 SPeCTrA-Sum 框架，包含两个核心组件：1. 深度视觉处理器 (DVP)：实现视觉编码器与语言模型的深度与层级对齐以进行分层融合。2. 视觉相关性预测器 (VRP)：利用 DPP 蒸馏技术筛选具有显著性和多样性的图像。采用由自动回归总结、跨模态对齐和 DPP 蒸馏组成的联合多目标损失函数进行训练。

Result: 在实验中，SPeCTrA-Sum 生成了更准确、视觉基础更扎实的文本总结，并能选择更具代表性的图片。

Conclusion: 通过深度感知融合（Deep Visual Processor）和基于行列式点过程（DPP）驱动的视觉相关性预测，实现了语义更一致的跨模态总结，验证了分层融合与原则性图像选择在多模态总结任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal summarization requires models to jointly understand textual and visual inputs to generate concise, semantically coherent summaries. Existing methods often inject shallow visual features into deep language models, leading to representational mismatches and weak cross-modal grounding. We propose a unified framework that jointly performs text summarization and representative image selection. Our system, SPeCTrA-Sum (Sampler Perceiver with Cross-modal Transformer and gated Attention for Summarization), introduces two key innovations. First, a Deep Visual Processor (DVP) aligns the visual encoder with the language model at corresponding depths, enabling hierarchical, layer-wise fusion that preserves semantic consistency. Second, a lightweight Visual Relevance Predictor (VRP) selects salient and diverse images by distilling soft labels from a Determinantal Point Processes (DPP) teacher. SPeCTrA-Sum is trained using a multi-objective loss that combines autoregressive summarization, cross-modal alignment, and DPP-based distillation. Experiments show that our system produces more accurate, visually grounded summaries and selects more representative images, demonstrating the benefits of depth-aware fusion and principled image selection for multimodal summarization.

</details>


### [241] [Beyond Inefficiency: Systemic Costs of Incivility in Multi-Agent Monte Carlo Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11789)
*Alison Moldovan-Mauer,Benedikt Mangold*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究利用LLM多智能体系统模拟对抗性辩论，量化了不文明沟通对效率的损害，发现毒性行为会导致显著的收敛延迟（且模型越小越严重），并揭示了辩论中存在不受毒性影响的显著先发优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非建设性辩论和不文明沟通对生产力和凝聚力有负面影响，但由于人类受试者研究在伦理、可复现性和自然环境不可预测性方面的限制，难以量化其对操作效率的精确影响，因此需寻求更可控的实验方法。

Method: 利用基于LLM的多智能体系统构建受控的社会学研究沙盒；采用蒙特卡罗模拟框架生成数千场结构化的1对1对抗性辩论；跨不同参数规模的模型进行实验，通过测量“收敛时间”（达到结论所需的轮数）来量化操作效率。

Result: 成功复现并验证了此前研究关于毒性行为会导致25%收敛延迟的结论；发现模型参数规模越小，这种延迟越严重；确认了“先发优势”的普遍存在。

Conclusion: 研究证实了毒性沟通会降低互动效率（导致收敛延迟），且这种延迟在参数较小的模型中更为显著。此外，研究揭示了在辩论中存在显著的“先发优势”，即先发言者获胜的概率明显高于随机水平，且不受毒性条件影响。

Abstract: Unconstructive debate and uncivil communication carry well-documented costs for productivity and cohesion, yet isolating their effect on operational efficiency has proven difficult. Human subject research in this domain is constrained by ethical oversight, limited reproducibility, and the inherent unpredictability of naturalistic settings. We address this gap by leveraging Large Language Model (LLM) based Multi-Agent Systems as a controlled sociological sandbox, enabling systematic manipulation of communicative behavior at scale. Using a Monte Carlo simulation framework, we generate thousands of structured 1-on-1 adversarial debates across varying toxicity conditions, measuring convergence time, defined as the number of rounds required to reach a conclusion, as a proxy for interactional efficiency. Building on a prior study, we replicate and extend its findings across two additional LLM agents of varying parameter size, allowing us to assess whether the effects of toxic behavior on debate dynamics generalize across model scale. The convergence latency of 25% reported in the previous study was confirmed. It was found that this latency is significantly bigger for models with fewer parameters. We further identify a significant first-mover advantage, whereby the agent initiating the discussion wins significantly above chance regardless of toxicity condition.

</details>


### [242] [Why Users Go There: World Knowledge-Augmented Generative Next POI Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11807)
*Qiuyu Ding,Heng-Da Xu,Wei Zhang,Dongyi Lv,Changda Xia,Feng Xiong,Mu Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AWARE 是一种通过 LLM 智能体获取并融入实时外部环境知识的 POI 推荐框架，解决了传统静态模型无法感知动态现实世界信息的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM-based POI 推荐模型知识参数是静态的，无法捕捉到诸如本地活动、季节性趋势及实时文化语境等动态演变的现实移动影响因素。

Method: 提出 AWARE 框架，利用 LLM 智能体生成针对特定用户、时间与地点的上下文叙述（Contextual Narratives），并将这些叙述与用户历史行为上下文进行锚定与融合。

Result: 在三个真实数据集上的实验表明，AWARE 在推荐性能上优于主流基准模型，相对性能提升最高达 12.4%。

Conclusion: AWARE 通过引入智能体驱动的动态环境感知机制，显著提升了 LLM 在 POI 推荐任务中的时效性与个性化能力，验证了将实时世界知识融入时空序列建模的有效性。

Abstract: Generative point-of-interest (POI) recommendation models based on large language models (LLMs) have shown promising results by formulating next POI prediction as a sequence generation task. However, the knowledge encoded in these models remains fixed after training, making them unable to perceive evolving real-world conditions that shape user mobility decisions, such as local events and cultural trends. To bridge this gap, we propose AWARE (Agent-based World knowledge Augmented REcommendation), which employs an LLM agent to generate location- and time-aware contextual narratives that capture regional cultural characteristics, seasonal trends, and ongoing events relevant to each user. Rather than introducing generic or noisy information, AWARE further anchors these narratives in each user's behavioral context, grounding external world knowledge in personalized spatial-temporal patterns. Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate that AWARE consistently outperforms competitive baselines, achieving up to 12.4% relative improvement.

</details>


### [243] [Beyond World-Frame Action Heads: Motion-Centric Action Frames for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11809)
*Huoren Yang,Jianchao Zhao,Hu Yusong,Qiguan Ou,Yuyang Gao,Wei Ke,Yuhang He,SongLin Dong,Zhiheng Ma,Yihong Gong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MCF-Proto 提出了一种轻量级的 VLA 动作头设计，通过在局部空间预测动作并结合原型参数化，增强了机器人操作策略学习的几何一致性与泛化鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言-动作 (VLA) 模型大多使用同质化的动作头，直接在固定的世界坐标系下预测动作，这限制了模型对空间结构的学习与泛化能力。

Method: 提出一种名为 MCF-Proto 的动作头架构。该方法：1. 引入运动中心框架 (MCF)，即每步预测一个旋转矩阵 $R_t \in SO(3)$，将动作映射到局部帧；2. 使用一组动作原型 (prototypes) 进行动作参数化；3. 通过端到端训练映射回世界坐标系，无需额外监督信号。

Result: 该方法能够诱导产生稳定的涌现几何结构，使局部坐标轴与末端执行器运动强相关；动作表示更加紧凑，受几何干扰影响更小，从而在机器人操纵任务中表现出更好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过在动作头中引入轻量级的几何与组合结构（旋转归一化与原型参数化），可以有效提升 VLA 模型对机器人操作任务的组织能力及抗干扰泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have advanced rapidly with stronger backbones, broader pre-training, and larger demonstration datasets, yet their action heads remain largely homogeneous: most directly predict action commands in a fixed world coordinate frame. We propose \textbf{MCF-Proto}, a lightweight action head that equips VLA policies with a Motion-Centric Action Frame (MCF) and a prototype-based action parameterization. At each step, the policy predicts a rotation $R_t \in SO(3)$, composes actions in the transformed local frame from a set of prototypes, and maps them back to the world frame for end-to-end training, using only standard demonstrations without auxiliary supervision. This simple design induces stable emergent structure. Without explicit directional labels, the learned local frames develop a stable geometric structure whose axes are strongly compatible with demonstrated end-effector motion. Meanwhile, actions in the learned representation become substantially more compact, with variation captured by fewer dominant directions and more regularly organized by shared prototypes. These structural properties translate into improved robustness, especially under geometric perturbations. Our results suggest that adding lightweight geometric and compositional structure to the action head can materially improve how VLA policies organize and generalize robotic manipulation behavior. An anonymized code repository is provided in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [244] [Automated Reformulation of Robust Optimization via Memory-Augmented Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11813)
*Jinbiao Chen,Shuang Jin,Guoyun Zhang,Junyu Zhang,Guanyi Wang,Hanzhang Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 在鲁棒优化数学重构中的推理短板，本文提出了首个基准测试 AutoRO-Bench 以及免微调的记忆增强框架 AutoREM，通过自动构建“经验记忆”实现了高效的自动化重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的鲁棒优化（RO）难以手工转化为易解的确定性模型；尽管 LLM 在优化建模方面表现出潜力，但缺乏针对 RO 复杂推理和数学一致性重构的基准测试和专门的自动化方法。

Method: 1. 构建了 AutoRO-Bench 基准测试集，涵盖数据生成管线与 RO 应用任务；2. 提出了 AutoREM（Automated Reformulation with Experience Memory）框架，通过离线自适应程序（offline adaptation procedure）对失败轨迹进行反思，构建并维护结构化的文本经验记忆，引导 LLM 进行更准确的数学重构。

Result: AutoREM 在各种分布内、分布外数据集以及不同基座大模型上表现出一致的性能提升，显著增强了 RO 重构的准确性和效率。

Conclusion: AutoREM 框架通过构建结构化的经验记忆，有效提升了 LLM 在鲁棒优化（RO）重构任务中的准确性和效率，且具有跨模型和跨领域的可迁移性，无需微调或特定领域专家知识。

Abstract: Robust optimization (RO) provides a principled framework for decision-making under uncertainty, but its practical use is often limited by the need to manually reformulate uncertain optimization models into tractable deterministic counterparts. Recent large language models (LLMs) have been shown promising for automating optimization formulation, yet RO reformulation remains challenging because it requires precise multi-step reasoning and mathematically consistent transformations. To facilitate systematic evaluation of LLM-based reformulation, for which no dedicated benchmark currently exists, we develop AutoRO-Bench, a benchmark featuring an automated data generation pipeline for the core RO reformulation task and a curated dataset for the RO application task. To address the reformulation challenge, we propose Automated Reformulation with Experience Memory (AutoREM), a tuning-free memory-augmented framework that autonomously builds a structured textual experience memory by reflecting on past failed trajectories through a tailored offline adaptation procedure. AutoREM requires neither domain-specific expert knowledge nor parameter updates, and the resulting memory readily transfers across different base LLMs. Experimental results show that AutoREM consistently improves the accuracy and efficiency of RO reformulation across in-distribution datasets, out-of-distribution datasets, and diverse base LLMs.

</details>


### [245] [MedMemoryBench: Benchmarking Agent Memory in Personalized Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11814)
*Yihao Wang,Haoran Xu,Renjie Gu,Yixuan Ye,Xinyi Chen,Xinyu Mu,Yuan Gao,Chunxiao Guo,Peng Wei,Jinjie Gu,Huan Li,Ke Chen,Lidan Shou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedMemoryBench 是一个针对个性化医疗智能体设计的长程记忆基准，通过模拟真实医疗场景下的流式交互，揭示并分析了模型在长期记忆积累中的“饱和”缺陷，为提升医疗智能体的临床实用性和鲁棒性提供了评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试集中于日常开放域对话，无法满足大规模个性化、高风险医疗应用对记忆机制在精确性、安全性和长期临床追踪方面的严苛生产需求。

Method: 1. 采用人机协作流水线，基于临床基础合成患者原型，构建了包含约2000个会话和1.6万轮交互的高真实度、长视野医疗轨迹数据集；2. 引入“边构建边评估（evaluate-while-constructing）”的流式评估协议，模拟生产环境下的动态记忆积累；3. 对“记忆饱和（memory saturation）”现象进行了形式化定义与系统性研究。

Result: 构建了 MedMemoryBench 数据集，并系统分析了主流架构在处理长序列医疗信息时，由于记忆饱和现象导致检索和推理能力下降的问题。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了现有模型在复杂医学推理和噪声鲁棒性方面的严重瓶颈，MedMemoryBench 为未来开发高可靠、生产就绪的医疗智能体奠定了基础。

Abstract: The large-scale deployment of personalized healthcare agents demands memory mechanisms that are exceptionally precise, safe, and capable of long-term clinical tracking. However, existing benchmarks primarily focus on daily open-domain conversations, failing to capture the high-stakes complexity of real-world medical applications. Motivated by the stringent production requirements of an industry-leading health management agent serving tens of millions of active users, we introduce MedMemoryBench. We develop a human-agent collaborative pipeline to synthesize highly realistic, long-horizon medical trajectories based on clinically grounded, synthetic patient archetypes. This process yields a massive, expertly validated dataset comprising approximately 2,000 sessions and 16,000 interaction turns. Crucially, MedMemoryBench departs from traditional static evaluations by pioneering an "evaluate-while-constructing" streaming assessment protocol, which precisely mirrors dynamic memory accumulation in production environments. Furthermore, we formalize and systematically investigate the critical phenomenon of memory saturation, where sustained information influx actively degrades retrieval and reasoning robustness. Comprehensive benchmarking reveals severe bottlenecks in mainstream architectures, particularly concerning complex medical reasoning and noise resilience. By exposing these fundamental flaws, MedMemoryBench establishes a vital foundation for developing robust, production-ready medical agents.

</details>


### [246] [On-Policy Self-Evolution via Failure Trajectories for Agentic Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11882)
*Bo Yin,Qi Li,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对智能体轨迹层面的安全隐患，本文提出FATE自进化框架，通过将失败轨迹转化为修复监督信号并结合帕累托优化，有效提升了工具使用型LLM的安全性和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前工具使用型LLM智能体的安全对齐方法多为响应级或离策略，存在严重的“安全性-效能”权衡，且无法有效解决轨迹层面的不安全行为（如执行危险调用、遵循恶意指令等）。

Method: 提出FATE框架：1. 使用验证器对失败轨迹进行评分；2. 利用当前策略针对失败生成修复候选；3. 通过安全性、实用性、拒答控制和轨迹有效性等多维度过滤修复方案；4. 引入Pareto-Front Policy Optimization (PFPO) 进行有监督预热与帕累托感知策略优化。

Result: 在AgentDojo、AgentHarm和ATBench测试中，FATE将攻击成功率降低了33.5%，有害合规性降低了82.6%，外部轨迹安全性诊断率提升了6.5%，且保持了任务处理能力。

Conclusion: 利用失败的轨迹进行修补监督是一种有效的机制，能够实现LLM智能体在提升安全性的同时，保持良好的任务性能，打破了安全性与实用性之间的权衡限制。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents fail through trajectories rather than only final responses, as they may execute unsafe tool calls, follow injected instructions, comply with harmful requests, or over-refuse benign tasks despite producing a seemingly safe answer. Existing safety-alignment signals are largely response-level or off-policy, and often incur a safety-utility trade-off: improving agent safety comes at the cost of degraded task performance. Such sparse and single-objective rewards severely limit real-world usability. To bridge this gap, we propose FATE, an on-policy self-evolving framework that transforms verifier-scored failures into repair supervision without expert demonstrations. For each failure, the same policy proposes repair candidates, which are then re-scored by verifiers and filtered across security, utility, over-refusal control, and trajectory validity. This dense trajectory-level information is then used as a supervision signal for agent self-evolution. During this process, we further introduce Pareto-Front Policy Optimization (PFPO), combining supervised warmup with Pareto-aware policy optimization to preserve safety-utility trade-offs. Experiments on AgentDojo, AgentHarm, and ATBench show that FATE improves safety across different models and scales while preserving useful behavior. Compared with strong baselines, FATE reduces attack success rate by 33.5%, harmful compliance by 82.6%, and improves external trajectory-safety diagnosis by 6.5%. These results suggest that failed trajectories can provide structured repair supervision for safer self-evolving agents.

</details>


### [247] [From Clever Hans to Scientific Discovery: Interpreting EEG Foundational Transformers with LRP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11885)
*Justus Meyer zu Bexten,Nico Scherf,Bogdan Franczyk,Simon M. Hofmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将逐层相关性传播（LRP）技术引入 EEG 基础模型，通过可视化解释提升模型决策的透明度，并揭示了模型可能存在的偏见及潜在的神经科学特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基础模型（FMs）在 EEG 领域有巨大潜力，但其“黑盒”特性阻碍了在诊断和脑机接口领域的广泛应用，因此需要开发有效的模型可解释性方法。

Method: 引入并扩展了注意力感知（Attention-aware）的逐层相关性传播（LRP）方法，将其从传统的 CNN 架构适配至 EEG 基础模型常用的 Transformer 架构。

Result: LRP 成功识别了 EEG-FMs 的“聪明汉斯”（Clever Hans）效应（即模型错误地依赖眼动信号而非目标运动特征），并发现了情感预测中可能存在的传感器运动觉醒特征，验证了该方法在模型探索和诊断中的实用性。

Conclusion: LRP 是一种用于 EEG 基础模型（EEG-FMs）的事后可解释性工具，既能验证模型决策，又具备发现生物学相关假设的探索潜力。随着 EEG-FMs 的成熟，可解释性方法将成为该领域发展的核心。

Abstract: Emerging foundation models (FMs) in electroencephalography (EEG) promise a path to scale deep learning in diagnostics and brain-computer interfaces despite data scarcity, yet their opaque nature remains a barrier to wider adoption. We investigate attention-aware Layer-wise relevance propagation (LRP) as a post-hoc attribution method for EEG-FMs, extending LRP's use on convolutional neural network (CNN)-based EEG models to the Transformer architectures that current FMs are based on. We find that LRP can both verify EEG-FM decisions and surface novel, biologically plausible hypotheses from them. In motor imagery, it unmasks 'Clever Hans' behavior where models prioritize task correlated ocular signals over the intended motor correlates. In a naturalistic paradigm for affect prediction, it reveals a recurring reliance on a central electrode cluster, suggesting a candidate sensorimotor signature of arousal. Though heatmap interpretation remains ambiguous in this complex domain, the results position LRP as a tool for both verification and exploration of EEG-FMs, a role that will grow in both importance and discovery potential as the underlying models mature.

</details>


### [248] [Toward Modeling Player-Specific Chess Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11893)
*Loris Sogliuzzo,Aloïs Rautureau,Eric Piette*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种结合棋手风格嵌入与有限MCTS搜索的AI架构，并引入基于Jensen-Shannon散度的新评估指标，旨在让AI更逼真地模仿历史国际象棋大师的个人决策风格，而非仅仅追求输赢最优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有国际象棋AI侧重于追求超人的胜率，无法准确模仿特定历史棋手的个人风格；同时，传统的正确率评估指标忽略了人类行为的变异性及长期风格的一致性。

Method: 1. 基于Maia-2模型，引入冠军特定的嵌入（Champion-specific embeddings）；2. 集成有限的蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）以增强战术探索；3. 提出一种基于Jensen-Shannon散度的新型行为评价指标，利用AutoEncoder和UMAP将棋盘状态压缩至低维空间进行分布比较。

Result: 实验对比16位世界冠军风格发现，虽然引入MCTS降低了AI的传统“走棋准确率”，但显著降低了Jensen-Shannon散度，意味着模型在风格一致性和模仿逼真度上有了实质性提升。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种新的评估指标，能够有效区分不同人类棋手的风格，并证明了将个性化嵌入与有限搜索策略相结合，能够提升AI模型在模仿特定历史国际象棋大师风格方面的逼真度。

Abstract: While artificial intelligence has achieved superhuman performance in chess, developing models that accurately emulate the individualized decision-making styles of human players remains a significant challenge. Existing human-like chess models capture general population behaviors based on skill levels but fail to reproduce the behavioral characteristics of specific historical champions. Furthermore, the standard evaluation metric, move accuracy, inherently penalizes natural human variance and ignores long-term behavioral consistency, leading to an incomplete assessment of stylistic fidelity. To address these limitations, an architecture is proposed that adapts the unified Maia-2 model to champion-specific embeddings, further enhanced by the integration of a limited Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) process to enrich tactical exploration during move selection. To robustly evaluate this approach, a novel behavioral metric based on the Jensen-Shannon divergence is introduced. By compressing high-dimensional board representations into a latent space using an AutoEncoder and Uniform Manifold Approximation and Projection (UMAP), move distributions are discretized on a common grid to compare behavioral similarities. Results across 16 historical world champions indicate that while integrating MCTS decreases standard move accuracy, it improves stylistic alignment according to the proposed metric, substantially reducing the average Jensen-Shannon divergence. Ultimately, the proposed metric successfully discriminates between individual players and provides promising evidence toward more comprehensive evaluations of behavioral alignment between players and AI models.

</details>


### [249] [Rethinking Supervision Granularity: Segment-Level Learning for LLM-Based Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11905)
*Shuo Xu,Jiakun Zhang,Junyu Lai,Chun Cao,Jingwei Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了段级（segment-level）证明监督方法，平衡了单步预测与完整证明生成之间的粒度缺陷，显著提升了LLM在Lean 4定理证明中的成功率与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前Lean 4自动定理证明主要存在两种范式：基于树搜索的单步预测（粒度太细，可能导致逻辑碎片化）和完整证明生成（粒度太粗，生成难度极高且缺乏中间监督）。作者旨在寻找一种介于两者之间的最优监督粒度，以平衡信号密度与逻辑结构。

Method: 提出了一种段级监督（segment-level supervision）训练方法：从证明轨迹中提取具有局部逻辑连贯性的证明片段（而非单步或完整证明）来训练策略模型；并在推理阶段利用相同的策略触发“目标感知短路径回滚”（goal-aware short rollouts）以优化现有模型。

Result: 模型在miniF2F测试集上表现优异（STP, LeanWorkbook, NuminaMath-LEAN数据集下成功率分别为64.84%, 60.90%, 66.31%），优于单步和完整证明基线模型。同时，集成该方法后，主流模型（如BFS-Prover-V2-7B和InternLM2.5-StepProver）的成功率显著提升，且降低了推理成本。

Conclusion: 监督粒度（Supervision Granularity）的选择对大模型进行Lean 4自动定理证明至关重要。段级（segment-level）监督结合了局部信号与全局逻辑一致性，能有效优化策略模型的学习。此外，利用该策略在推理时进行短路径回滚（short rollouts）能以更低的成本提升现有模型性能，验证了该方法在对齐模型训练与证明结构方面的有效性。

Abstract: Automated theorem proving with large language models in Lean 4 is commonly approached through either step-level tactic prediction with tree search or whole-proof generation. These two paradigms represent opposite granularities for constructing supervised training data: the former provides dense local signals but may fragment coherent proof processes, while the latter preserves global structure but requires complex end-to-end generation. In this paper, we revisit supervision granularity as a training set construction problem over proof trajectories and propose segment-level supervision, a training data construction strategy that extracts locally coherent proof segments for training policy models. We further reuse the same strategy at inference time to trigger short rollouts for existing step-level models. When trained with segment-level supervision on STP, LeanWorkbook, and NuminaMath-LEAN, the resulting policy models achieve proof success rates of 64.84%, 60.90%, and 66.31% on miniF2F, respectively, consistently outperforming both step-level and whole-proof baselines. Goal-aware rollout further improves existing step-level provers while reducing inference costs. It increases the proof success rate of BFS-Prover-V2-7B from 68.77% to 70.74% and that of InternLM2.5-StepProver from 59.59% to 60.33%, showing that appropriate supervision granularity better aligns model learning with proof structure and search. Code and models are available at https://github.com/NJUDeepEngine/SEG-ATP.

</details>


### [250] [Rethinking Positional Encoding for Neural Vehicle Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11910)
*Chuanbo Hua,Federico Berto,Andre Hottung,Nayeli Gast Zepeda,Yining Ma,Zihan Ma,Paula Wong-Chung,Changhyun Kwon,Cathy Wu,Kevin Tierney,Jinkyoo Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对VRP问题设计了一种几何感知的位置编码，解决了传统NLP式位置编码在处理路径规划拓扑结构时的不适应问题，大幅提升了模型在各类路由优化任务中的性能与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于Transformer的车辆路径规划（VRP）模型中，位置编码（PE）的设计大多沿用NLP中的线性处理方式，忽略了VRP中非线性、非均匀的几何与特定拓扑结构特性，导致模型效果受限。

Method: 形式化了VRP中三种关键结构特性（各向异性节点距离、循环/方向拓扑、层级化仓库锚定结构），并据此设计了层级化各向异性位置编码（Hierarchical Anisometric PE），结合了距离索引、圆周一致性的路线内编码与仓库锚定的角度跨路线编码。

Result: 实验表明，几何感知的位置编码在多种VRP变体、模型架构及分布偏移场景下，均显著优于传统的基于索引的位置编码方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入几何基础（geometric grounding）的设计原则，研究强调了专门为路径规划问题设计的距离和拓扑感知的编码方式对于提升模型性能至关重要，证明了该方法在多类VRP问题上的通用性和优越性。

Abstract: Transformer-based models have become the dominant paradigm for neural combinatorial optimization (NCO) of vehicle routing problems (VRPs), yet the role of positional encoding (PE) in these architectures remains largely unexplored. Unlike natural language, where tokens are uniformly spaced on a line, routing solutions exhibit several properties that render standard NLP positional encodings inadequate. In this work, we formalize three such structural properties that a routing-aware PE should respect, namely anisometric node distances, cyclic and direction-aware topology, and hierarchical depot-anchored global multi-route structure, combining them with a unifying design principle of geometric grounding. Guided by these criteria, we analyze and compare PE methods spanning NLP, graph-transformer, and routing-specific families, and propose a hierarchical anisometric PE that combines a distance-indexed, circularly consistent in-route encoding with a depot-anchored angular cross-route encoding. Extensive experiments across diverse VRP variants demonstrate that geometry-grounded PE consistently outperforms index-based alternatives, with gains that transfer across problem variants, model architectures, and distribution shifts.

</details>


### [251] [Domain Restriction via Multi SAE Layer Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11920)
*Elias Shaheen,Avi Mendelson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出通过监测LLM内部层间状态变化的动态特征（利用SAE）来检测域外（OOD）输入，相比传统黑盒方法，该方案既能提升检测性能，又具备良好的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用LLM在领域特定应用中容易产生域外（OOD）交互，而现有的检测方法普遍将LLM视为“黑盒”，未能有效利用模型内部的处理特征，限制了检测的精度与可解释性。

Method: 提出了一种基于稀疏自动编码器（SAE）的分析框架，利用LLM内部各层的状态（representation）转移特征来构建模型。通过轻量级的学习方法处理这些动态信息，从而实现对域外（OOD）文本的检测。

Result: 在Gemma-2 2B和9B模型上进行了基准测试，实验结果证实该方法能够有效捕获与输入相关的细粒度细节，在区分域外文本方面表现出优异的性能。

Conclusion: 利用稀疏自动编码器（SAE）捕捉LLM内部层间状态转移，是区分域外（OOD）输入的一种高效且具解释性的方法。该研究表明，LLM的内部处理演进过程对于识别特定任务边界具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: The general-purpose nature of Large Language Models (LLMs) presents a significant challenge for domain-specific applications, often leading to out-of-domain (OOD) interactions that undermine the provider's intent. Existing methods for detecting such scenarios treat the LLM as an uninterpretable black box and overlook the internal processing of inputs. In this work we show that layer transitions provide a promising avenue for extracting domain-specific signature. Specifically, we present several lightweight ways of learning on internal dynamics encoded using a sparse autoencoder (SAE) that exhibit great capability in distinguishing OOD texts. Building on top of SAEs representation transitions enables us to better interpret the LLM internal evolution of input processing and shed light on its decisions. We provide a comprehensive analysis of the method and benchmark it with the gemma-2 2B and 9B models. Our results emphasize the efficacy of the internal process in capturing fine-grained input-related details.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [252] [State Canonization and Early Pruning in Width-Based Automated Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11025)
*Mateus de Oliveira Oliveira,Sam Urmian*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文评估并优化了基于宽度分解的自动定理证明框架，通过状态空间压缩和剪枝技术，成功验证了图论猜想并自动发现了反例，证明了该方法在图论研究中的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于宽度的自动定理证明框架在理论上可行，但缺乏实际应用评估；需进一步优化算法以更高效地验证图论猜想并寻找反例。

Method: 提出了一种基于宽度分解的自动定理证明框架，包含两种优化技术：1. 状态规范化（state-canonization）以减少状态空间；2. 早期剪枝技术（early-pruning）用于处理特定形式的图属性蕴含关系。

Result: 验证了Reed猜想在特定宽度（路径宽度≤5，树宽≤3）的无三角图上成立；成功自动构造了Reed猜想某些无效加强版本的反例。

Conclusion: 通过状态规范化和早期剪枝技术，改进了基于宽度的自动定理证明框架；实验证明该框架在验证图论猜想（如Reed猜想）及自动发现反例方面具有实用价值。

Abstract: Width-based automated theorem proving is a framework where counterexamples to graph-theoretic conjectures are searched width-wise relative to some graph width measure, such as treewidth or pathwidth.
  In a recent work it has been shown that dynamic programming algorithms operating on tree decompositions can be combined together with the purpose of width-based theorem proving. This approach can be used to show that several long-standing conjectures in graph theory can be tested in time \(2^{2^{k^{O(1)}}}\) on the class of graphs of treewidth at most \(k\). In this work, we give the first steps towards evaluating the viability of this framework from a practical standpoint. At the same time, we advance the framework in two directions. First, we introduce a state-canonization technique that significantly reduces the number of states evaluated during the search for a counterexample of the conjecture. Second, we introduce an early-pruning technique that can be applied in the study of conjectures of the form \(\mathcal{P}_1 \rightarrow \mathcal{P}_2\), for graph properties \(\mathcal{P}_1\) and \(\mathcal{P}_2\), where \(\mathcal{P}_1\) is a property closed under subgraphs.
  As a concrete application, we use our framework in the study of graph-theoretic conjectures related to coloring triangle-free graphs. In particular, our algorithm is able to show that Reed's conjecture for triangle-free graphs is valid on the class of graphs of pathwidth at most 5, and on graphs of treewidth at most 3. Perhaps more interestingly, our algorithm is able to construct in a completely automated way counterexamples to invalid strengthenings of Reed's conjecture. These are the first results showing that width-based automated theorem proving is a promising avenue in the study of graph-theoretic conjectures.

</details>


### [253] [Performance bounds for nearest neighbor search with k-d trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11313)
*Marco Bazzani,Sanjoy Dasgupta*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文通过理论分析证明了k-d树在高维空间下性能的局限性：在高维下，无论采用何种搜索策略，k-d树的效率和精度均无法满足有效搜索的要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然k-d树广泛应用于最近邻搜索，但人们普遍认为其在高维空间中效果不佳，且缺乏足够的理论分析来解释为何在高维下性能会显著下降。文章旨在通过数学证明，量化这种性能降级。

Method: 采用理论概率分析方法，推导了k-d树在不同维度和数据分布下的非渐近运行时间边界和搜索准确度边界。分析了在高维空间中，当数据满足特定分布假设时的搜索表现及最坏情况下的表现。

Result: 1. 在高维（d ≥ polylog(n)）情况下，偷懒搜索的成功率等同于随机猜测，全面搜索几乎遍历所有单元格；2. 对于均匀分布数据，当单元格中包含足够多数据点时，提供了全面搜索代价（2^O(d)）和偷懒搜索准确性的边界；3. 给出了一般绝对连续分布下，偷懒搜索结果与真正最近邻之间距离的期望上限。

Conclusion: 在高维空间下，k-d树的搜索性能会发生灾难性下降。在高维（d ≥ polylog(n)）时，偷懒搜索（defeatist search）的准确性等同于随机猜测，而全面搜索（comprehensive search）则需要遍历所有单元格。论文为k-d树在处理高维数据时的局限性提供了严谨的概率论证。

Abstract: The $k$-d tree is one of the oldest and most widely used data structures for nearest neighbor search. It partitions Euclidean space into axis-aligned rectangular cells. There are two standard ways to find the nearest neighbor to a query in a $k$-d tree. Defeatist search returns the closest data point in the query's cell, while comprehensive search also searches other cells as needed to guarantee it finds the nearest neighbor. Both strategies are commonly believed to perform poorly in high dimensions, but there have been few theoretical results explaining this. We prove non-asymptotic bounds on the runtime of comprehensive search and the accuracy of defeatist search. Under mild distributional assumptions, when the dimension $d$ is at least polylogarithmic in the number of data points, defeatist search is no more likely to return the nearest neighbor than random guessing, and comprehensive search visits every cell with high probability. We also show that on uniform data, with high probability, comprehensive search visits at most $2^{\mathcal{O}(d)}$ cells when each cell contains at least logarithmically many data points, and defeatist search returns the nearest neighbor when each cell additionally contains at least $2^{\mathcal{O}(d \log d)}$ data points. Finally, for arbitrary absolutely continuous distributions, we upper bound the expected distance between the query and the point returned by defeatist search.

</details>


### [254] [Connectivity augmentation is fixed-parameter tractable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11757)
*Tuukka Korhonen,Mikkel Thorup*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文证明了点连通度增强问题（参数λ, k）和边连通度增强问题（参数k）均为FPT，给出了相应的算法并显著提升了该领域的研究上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对连通度增强问题的参数化复杂性理解有限，特别是在点连通度要求较高（λ > 4）或缺乏特定假设的情况下，缺乏通用的FPT算法。该研究旨在克服这些限制，提供更通用的算法。

Method: 设计了参数化算法。点连通度增强问题的算法运行时间为2^{O(k log(k + λ))} n^{O(1)}；边连通度增强问题的算法运行时间为2^{O(k log k)} n^{O(1)}。

Result: 成功证明了通用的点连通度增强问题（参数为λ, k）和边连通度增强问题（参数为k）均具有FPT算法，打破了此前对λ值或图形结构的限制。

Conclusion: 该论文证明了点连通度增强问题在以λ和k为参数时是固定参数可处理（FPT）的，且边连通度增强问题在以k为参数时即为FPT。这些结果在理论上显著改进了现有研究，填补了此前在一般参数假设下的空白。

Abstract: In the vertex connectivity augmentation problem, we are given an undirected $n$-vertex graph $G$, a set of links $L \subseteq \binom{V(G)}{2} \setminus E(G)$, and integers $λ$ and $k$. The task is to insert at most $k$ links from $L$ to $G$ to make $G$ $λ$-vertex-connected. We show that the problem is fixed-parameter tractable (FPT) when parameterized by $λ$ and $k$, by giving an algorithm with running time $2^{O(k \log (k + λ))} n^{O(1)}$. This improves upon a recent result of Carmesin and Ramanujan [SODA'26], who showed that the problem is FPT parameterized by $k$ but only when $λ\le 4$.
  We also consider the analogous edge connectivity augmentation problem, where the goal is to make $G$ $λ$-edge-connected. We show that the problem is FPT when parameterized by $k$ only, by giving an algorithm with running time $2^{O(k \log k)} n^{O(1)}$. Previously, such results were known only under additional assumptions on the edge connectivity of $G$.

</details>


### [255] [On the LSH Distortion of Ulam and Cayley Similarities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11921)
*Flavio Chierichetti,Mirko Giacchini,Ravi Kumar,Erasmo Tani*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了置换空间中 Ulam 和 Cayley 相似度的 LSH 畸变问题。研究发现 Ulam 相似度具有次线性 LSH 畸变，而 Cayley 相似度的 LSH 畸变为线性级别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并非所有相似度函数都能直接构造完美的局部敏感哈希（LSH）方案。为了评估特定相似度函数与 LSH 方案的契合程度，研究人员引入了“LSH 畸变”这一概念，旨在量化相似度函数接近“精确 LSH”的程度。

Method: 通过理论分析和数学证明，研究 Ulam 和 Cayley 相似度在 LSH 框架下的映射属性，定量评估其 LSH 畸变（LSH distortion）的上下界。

Result: 明确了 Ulam 和 Cayley 这两种排列相似度度量在 LSH 算法中的理论极限，为在大规模数据集上高效进行最近邻搜索提供了理论依据。

Conclusion: Ulam 相似度存在 $O(n / \sqrt{\log n})$ 的次线性 LSH 畸变，且存在 $\Omega(n^{0.12})$ 的下界；Cayley 相似度的 LSH 畸变为 $\Theta(n)$。

Abstract: Locality-sensitive hashing (LSH) has found widespread use as a fundamental primitive, particularly to accelerate nearest neighbor search. An LSH scheme for a similarity function $S:\mathcal{X} \times \mathcal{X} \to [0,1]$ is a distribution over hash functions on $\mathcal{X}$ with the property that the probability of collision of any two elements $x,y\in \mathcal{X}$ is exactly equal to $S(x,y)$. However, not all similarity functions admit exact LSH schemes. The notion of LSH distortion measures how multiplicatively close a similarity function is to having an LSH scheme.
  In this work, we study the LSH distortion of the Ulam and Cayley similarities, which are popular similarity measures on permutations of $n$ elements. We show that the Ulam similarity admits a sublinear LSH distortion of $O(n / \sqrt{\log n})$; we also prove a lower bound of $Ω(n^{0.12})$ on the best LSH distortion achievable. On the other hand, we show that the LSH distortion of the Cayley similarity is $Θ(n)$.

</details>


### [256] [Layer-Based Width for PAFP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12457)
*Samuel German*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了“避免禁止对路径问题(PAFP)”，通过分析图的层级宽度（BFS宽度与精确长度宽度）和后向弧，确定了该问题在参数化和多项式时间内的可解边界，并证明了这些边界的紧致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PAFP 问题通常是 NP-hard 的。以往的简化研究多通过限制“禁止对集合”来获取多项式时间解。本文旨在探索基于图的结构宽度度量（而非限制禁止对集合），寻找该问题在更一般情况下的参数化算法（FPT）和多项式时间可解的条件。

Method: 1. 引入联合有向图 $G\cup\mathcal{F}$ 和基于 BFS 的宽度度量；
2. 利用分层结构和动态规划方法，结合后向弧数量 $\beta$ 进行参数化分析；
3. 对于特定宽度限制的 DAG，使用 2-SAT 编码进行简化求解；
4. 通过复杂性归约证明了边界的紧致性（即增大宽度后问题的 NP-complete 性）。

Result: 1. 当 $G\cup\mathcal{F}$ 的 BFS 宽度为 $b$ 且后向弧数为 $\beta$ 时，PAFP 是 FPT 的；
2. 对于 BFS 宽度为 2 的 DAG，当后向弧数为 $k$ 时，时间复杂度为 $2^k |I|^{O(1)}$；
3. 对精确长度宽度为 2 的 DAG，PAFP 可通过 2-SAT 在多项式时间内求解；
4. 证明了在 BFS 和精确长度宽度达到 3 时，即便没有后向弧或限制，问题即为 NP-complete。

Conclusion: 该研究为“避免禁止对路径问题(PAFP)”提供了新的参数化复杂性界限。作者证明了在基于BFS宽度及后向弧限制的特定约束下，PAFP是FPT的。同时，在DAG中对于特定宽度限制（BFS宽度2或精确长度宽度2），PAFP是可高效求解的，且这些边界是紧致的。与以往研究相比，这些结果在允许任意禁止对集的情况下实现了多项式时间可解性。

Abstract: The Path Avoiding Forbidden Pairs problem (PAFP) asks whether, in a directed graph $G$ with terminals $s,t$ and a set $\mathcal{F}$ of forbidden vertex pairs, there is an $s$-$t$ path that contains at most one endpoint from each forbidden pair. We initiate the study of PAFP through a layer-based width measure. Our first focus is the union digraph $G\cup\mathcal{F}$, obtained by adding to $G$ one arc per forbidden pair, oriented according to a fixed reachability-compatible order. Let the BFS layer $L_d$ be all vertices at directed shortest-path distance $d$ from $s$, where the BFS-width from $s$ is $\max_d |L_d|$. We show if $G\cup\mathcal{F}$ has BFS-width $b$ from $s$ and only $β$ arcs going from a later BFS layer to an earlier one, then PAFP is FPT parameterized by $b+β$. The backward-arc hypothesis is essential: we show PAFP remains NP-complete when the union digraph is a DAG with BFS-width 2.
  We also show if the input DAG has BFS-width at most $2$ and only $k$ backward input arcs, then PAFP can be decided in $2^k |I|^{O(1)}$ time, with unrestricted forbidden pairs. This width-$2$ result is tight: inspection of a classical reduction shows NP-completeness on input DAGs of BFS-width $3$ with no backward input arcs.
  Moreover, we study exact-length layers in the input graph, where the $d$-th layer consists of the vertices reachable from $s$ by a directed path of length exactly $d$. For DAGs of exact-length width at most $2$, we show PAFP is polynomial-time decidable by a 2-SAT encoding of fixed-length paths. This bound is tight: the same classical reduction yields NP-completeness on DAGs of exact-length width $3$. Unlike previously known polynomial-time regimes for PAFP, which restrict the forbidden-pair set in order to obtain tractability, our two input-graph tractability results allow unrestricted forbidden pairs and input graphs with exponentially many $s$-$t$ paths.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

### [257] [Scalable Packed Layouts for Vector-Length-Agnostic ML Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12445)
*Ege Beysel,Maximilian Bartel,Jan Moritz Joseph*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 本文提出一种支持Arm SVE架构的端到端可扩展向量化编译方法，通过向量长度感知的布局与优化，在实现硬件无关的性能可移植性的同时，性能表现显著优于现有NEON生成代码及PyTorch主流框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Arm SVE等指令集支持向量长度无关（VLA）编程，但由于平铺和数据布局无法在编译时固定，导致编译器代码生成变得复杂，现有方案难以充分发挥VLA架构潜力。

Method: 提出一种基于“向量长度感知打包数据布局”（vector-length-aware packed data layouts）的端到端ML编译方法；在MLIR/IREE框架内扩展了平铺（tiling）、融合（fusion）和向量化机制，以支持可扩展向量长度（VLA）执行。

Result: 在Arm CPUs上，该方法生成的SVE代码相比IREE现有的NEON代码最高可实现1.45倍加速；在实际ML工作负载中性能优于PyTorch生态系统框架（如ExecuTorch、TorchInductor）；模拟实验证实了代码随向量长度增加的性能扩展性。

Conclusion: 通过向量长度感知（VLA）的数据编排与编译优化，实现了跨不同向量长度硬件的高性能与可移植性，验证了可扩展向量化在生产级机器学习编译器中的有效性。

Abstract: Scalable vector instruction sets such as Arm SVE enable vector-length-agnostic (VLA) execution, allowing a single implementation to adapt across hardware with different vector lengths. However, they complicate compiler code generation, as tiling and data layout decisions can no longer be fixed at compile time.
  We present an approach for enabling VLA code generation in an end-to-end ML compilation pipeline through vector-length-aware packed data layouts and corresponding compiler extensions. We integrate these mechanisms into MLIR/IREE and extend tiling, fusion, and vectorization to operate with scalable vector lengths.
  Evaluated on real-world ML workloads on Arm CPUs, our approach generates SVE code that is competitive with, and often outperforms, existing NEON-based code generation within IREE, achieving up to $1.45\times$ speedup. We also outperform PyTorch ecosystem frameworks, including ExecuTorch, TorchInductor, and eager execution, demonstrating the effectiveness of scalable vectorization in a production compiler setting. A simulator-based study further shows that the generated code scales with increasing SVE vector length on compute-bound workloads, supporting performance portability across hardware configurations.

</details>
